EMHO optimizes beyond skills or recovery prompts, modifying how the agent monitors progress, uses vision tools, grounds observations, and responds to failures. To support multiple subtasks with a single harness, we introduce EMHO-Merge, which addresses trade-offs in jointly optimizing a single shared harness across subtasks by using episode-level gains and losses to guide evidence-supported refinement of when and how revised behaviors are applied. We evaluate EMHO on EmbodiedBench across navigation and manipulation tasks, and EMHO consistently improves task success for both Qwen 9B and 27B models. Qualitative analysis shows that EMHO goes beyond recovering from failures and unproductive actions to reshape how the embodied agent interprets and interacts with its environment.

</details>


### [138] [AssemState: Manual and Physical-State-Guided Reasoning for Zero-shot Furniture Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08446)
*Zhiyuan Qi,Jierui Li,Yifan Shen,Cheng Qian,Jiateng Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AssemState，一个用于家具组装的零样本框架，通过锚点引导的边界组装状态和迭代后状态反馈来恢复组装结构并生成6D位姿更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型在视觉理解上进展显著，但在物理环境中的精确3D空间推理仍然困难。家具组装任务要求既从图示手册中恢复步骤级操作，又将语义连接关系转化为6D位姿更新，使零件能与环境及已有组件进行物理交互，这一难题亟需研究。

Method: 提出AssemState零样本框架：首先采用锚点引导的边界组装状态，将手册页面分解为单零件操作并恢复组装树；然后利用迭代式后状态反馈精炼来指导连续的SE(3)更新与修正，并通过基于仿真的释放测试验证其物理合理性。

Result: 相比最强的先前基线，组装树恢复的F1从38.58%提升至62.80%，Tree Exact Match从28.24%提升至53.92%。在243个独立评估的零件级操作中，迭代精炼使judge-accepted操作从0提升至5.3%，并将平均Chamfer距离从5.4111降至1.7744。但视觉上合理的候选位姿仍可能出现碰撞、悬浮、镜像方向错误、嵌入不完全和错误侧连接等问题。

Conclusion: AssemState改善了操作-结构恢复与部分局部位姿指标，但MLLMs在空间关系推理方面仍存在局限。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have made significant progress in visual understanding, but precise 3D spatial reasoning integrated with physical environment remains difficult. Furniture assembly requires not only recovering step-level operations from diagrammatic manuals, but also translating semantic attachment relations into 6D pose updates that enable parts to physically interact with the environment and previously assembled components. To study this problem, we propose AssemState, a zero-shot framework for manual and physical-state-guided furniture assembly. It firstly employs anchor-guided boundary assembly states to decompose manual pages into single-part operations and recover an assembly-tree. Then, it uses iterative after-state feedback refinement to guide successive (SE(3)) updates and corrections, and validates their physical plausibility through simulation-based release tests. Experiments show that compared with the strongest prior baseline, AssemState improves F1 from 38.58\% to 62.80\% and Tree Exact Match from 28.24\% to 53.92\% for assembly-tree recovery. On 243 independently evaluated part-level operations, our proposed iterative refinement improves judge-accepted operations from 0 to 5.3\% and reduces mean Chamfer distance from 5.4111 to 1.7744. However, visually plausible candidate poses may still suffer from collision, floating, mirror-orientation errors, incomplete seating, and wrong-side attachment. These results show that AssemState improves operation-structure recovery and selected local pose metrics, while MLLMs remain limited for spatial relationship reasoning.

</details>


### [139] [Cylindrical Geodesic Flow Matching for Quasiperiodic Physiological Signal Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08510)
*Onur Selim Kilic,Afra Nawar,Cem Okan Yaldiz,Michael J. Cho,Ahmet Rasim Emirdagi,Demet Tangolar,Amirali Aghazadeh,Amit J. Shah,Omer T. Inan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"柱面测地线流匹配"（cylindrical geodesic flow matching），用于准周期心血管波形的成对翻译。通过将流匹配中的仿射路径替换为相位-幅值柱面上的闭式测地线，消除了插值伪影，并在零样本PPG和少样本SCG适配任务上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴设备在不同身体位置采集的心血管信号之间存在准周期波形成对翻译需求（从源信号恢复目标振荡信号）。该映射具有内在几何结构：相位沿周期环绕须作为循环变量处理，幅值严格为正，且逐拍对齐会在不同周期和受试者间不可预测地漂移。已有深度网络用于相位估计和复值信号建模，但未显式学习成对信号间的相位传输；端点监督回归和流匹配中的标准仿射路径都未考虑这种相位-幅值结构。

Method: 提出柱面测地线流匹配方法。证明流匹配中标准仿射路径在准周期信号间插值时会扭曲中间幅值和瞬时频率；将仿射路径替换为相位-幅值柱面上的闭式测地线，从而消除这些伪影，并将每个训练对转化为密集、几何一致的速率监督。

Result: 在零样本光电容积脉搏波（PPG）和有限支持心震图（SCG）适配基准上，该方法一致优于插值基线，并达到或超过直接监督预测；相比最强竞争基线，Hilbert变换、L2和动态时间规整（DTW）距离最多降低约15%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，桥接几何（bridge geometry）是振荡信号翻译中流匹配的关键归纳偏置。

Abstract: Paired translation between quasiperiodic physiological waveforms (i.e., recovering a target oscillatory signal from the source) is central to the interpretation of cardiovascular signals derived from wearables placed at different body locations. This source-to-target mapping in these problems carries inherent geometric structure: the phase wraps around the cycle and must be treated as a circular variable, the amplitude remains strictly positive, and the beat-to-beat alignment can drift unpredictably across cycles and subjects. While deep neural networks have been used for phase estimation and complex-valued signal modeling, prior work does not explicitly learn phase transport between paired signals. Consequently, neither endpoint-supervised regression nor the standard affine path used in flow matching accounts for this phase--amplitude structure. We introduce \emph{cylindrical geodesic flow matching} for paired cardiovascular waveform translation. We show that the standard affine path used in flow matching distorts intermediate amplitude and instantaneous frequency when interpolating between quasiperiodic signals; replacing it with a closed-form geodesic on the phase--amplitude cylinder eliminates these artifacts and converts each training pair into dense, geometry-consistent velocity supervision. On zero-shot photoplethysmography and limited-support seismocardiography adaptation benchmarks, our method consistently outperforms interpolation baselines and matches or exceeds direct supervised prediction, reducing Hilbert Transform, $L_2$, and Dynamic Time Warping distance by up to ${\sim}15\%$ over the strongest competing baseline. These results suggest that bridge geometry is a critical inductive bias for flow matching on oscillatory signal translation.

</details>


### [140] [How Much Evidence Should a Coding Agent's Self-Correction Carry? Adaptive Dirichlet Evidence for Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08514)
*Yunbo Long,Guangya Hao,Yuhan Liu,Yiting Duan,Longyan Tan,Yunchen Long,Hao Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Effective-Evidence Self-Distillation（EESD），将“证据支持”与“证据量”分离建模，通过归一化执行相关性产生有效伪计数质量，再用Dirichlet后验得到不确定性惩罚权重，用于KL锚定的纠错学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体可通过执行反馈修正程序并从自身纠错中学习，但纠错的学习权重应同时反映其执行所支持的转移以及该支持背后的证据量，因此需要将这两类量分开表示。

Method: EESD用归一化执行相关性决定相对转移支持和一个有效伪计数质量；Dirichlet后验据此产生不确定性惩罚权重，用于KL锚定的纠错学习。理论上，在对称先验下改变质量保持类别排序，有效质量给出的监督系数受其匹配的固定质量对应项上界约束。

Result: 在四个模型-领域历史扫描中，可见观测从1增至8使未来结果NLL降低55.0–59.3%；在8个观测时，有效质量在全部四组比较中NLL低于固定质量。主要匹配DeepSeek/RunBugRun研究中，3,000个样例的argmax预测完全一致，集中相关性下NLL增益最大。一轮纠错学习后，DeepSeek/CodeARC全测试Pass@1从15.0%升至20.4%，配对95%源bootstrap区间为[+2.8, +8.0]个百分点；十二种下游设置评估确立了该更新的模型-领域适用范围。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将证据支持与证据量分离会改变编码智能体中的概率估计和纠错学习效果。

Abstract: Execution feedback lets coding agents revise programs and learn from their own corrections. A correction's learning weight should reflect both the transitions supported by its executions and the amount of evidence behind that support. We introduce Effective-Evidence Self-Distillation (EESD), which represents these quantities separately. Normalized execution relevance determines relative transition support and an effective pseudo-count mass; a Dirichlet posterior then produces an uncertainty-penalized weight for KL-anchored correction learning. Under a symmetric prior, changing mass preserves category ordering, and effective mass yields a supervised coefficient bounded by its matched fixed-mass counterpart. Across four model-domain history sweeps, increasing visible observations from one to eight reduces future-outcome NLL by 55.0-59.3%. At eight observations, effective mass achieves lower NLL than fixed mass in all four comparisons. In the primary matched DeepSeek/RunBugRun study, argmax predictions agree on all 3,000 examples, with the largest NLL gain under concentrated relevance. After one correction-learning round, DeepSeek/CodeARC all-tests Pass@1 increases from 15.0% to 20.4%, with a paired 95% source-bootstrap interval of [+2.8, +8.0] percentage points. The twelve-setting downstream evaluation establishes the model-domain scope of this update. These results show how separating evidence support from evidence mass changes probability estimation and correction learning in coding agents.

</details>


### [141] [Toward Alignment Scaling Laws: A Framework and First Preregistered Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08540)
*Jeremy Canale*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将对齐问题形式化为可测量的标度律家族：对每个风险类别 r，维持固定安全目标所需的对齐负担建模为 B_r(N)=a_rN^α_r（N 为能力代理）。按 α_r 与 1 的关系区分「标度有益/持平/累积对齐债务」三种情形。作者给出三种负担操作化、区分观测/审计/真实对齐，证明若干定理（最大指数决定长期regime、超1时需超指数增长、小模型拟合低估大尺度指数、无假阳性审计不会低估真实对齐），并提出可预注册协议，在 Pythia 对抗训练数据、Qwen2.5/Qwen3 上进行两次预注册实证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前关于「对齐随模型规模增大是变易还是变难」的争论往往基于孤立发现，仿佛对齐是单一属性。作者主张应将对齐视为一族可测量的标度关系，从而能够按风险类别分别量化对齐负担随能力的增长速率，并据此判断标度是帮助、持平还是累积债务。

Method: 1）对每个风险类别 r 建模负担 B_r(N)=a_rN^α_r；2）提出三种负担操作化，并区分观测、审计与真实对齐；3）构建「纠错消耗能力headroom」的玩具模型；4）证明四条定理：最大指数决定长期regime、α>1 时保持headroom需超指数增长、幂律正混合下小模型拟合低估大尺度指数、无假阳性审计不低估真实对齐；5）提出可预注册协议，并进行两次缩减版应用：对 Pythia 分类器公开对抗训练数据的预注册再分析，以及 Qwen2.5 0.5B–72B 的预注册试点（在 Qwen3 0.6B–14B 上复现）。

Result: Pythia 分类器：将攻击成功率压到 10% 以下所需算力按 N^0.60 增长。Qwen 试点：真实性指数 −0.05、陈述性情指数 0.48（在缩减规则下均属标度有益，但局部斜率在最大规模处趋近1；在 Qwen3 0.6B–14B 上复现）；谄媚指数 0.89（72B 加两个种子后为 0.83）与植入后门均未确定——后门在触发器已知时可被快速移除，但在五个规模中的四个上能存活于盲安全训练。另发布四款网页游戏演绎这些规律。

Conclusion: 作者不主张当前前沿模型究竟处于哪种regime，而是提供一套可预注册、可复用的标度律框架与协议来分析对齐随能力增长的动态；同时强调长期regime由被纠正风险中的最大指数而非平均值决定。

Abstract: Whether alignment gets easier or harder as models grow is often argued from isolated findings, as if alignment were one property. We treat it as a family of measurable scaling relations: for each risk category r, the alignment burden needed to hold a fixed safety target is modeled as B_r(N)=a_rN^alpha_r, with N a capability proxy; against a budget proportional to N, scaling helps if alpha_r<1, keeps pace if alpha_r~1, and accumulates alignment debt if alpha_r>1. We give three operationalizations of burden and distinguish observed, audited and true alignment. A toy model, in which corrections consume capability headroom, makes the consequences explicit. We prove that the largest exponent among corrected risks, not an average, sets the long-run regime; that above 1 any policy holding headroom above a floor must grow super-exponentially; that, for burdens that are positive mixtures of power laws, fits on small models underestimate large-scale exponents; and that an audit that uncovers hidden failures without false positives never underestimates true alignment. We propose a pre-registrable protocol and apply reduced versions of it twice. A preregistered reanalysis of public adversarial-training data for Pythia classifiers finds that the compute needed to bring attack success under 10% grows as N^0.60. A preregistered pilot on Qwen2.5 0.5B-72B finds exponents of -0.05 for truthfulness and 0.48 for stated dispositions (both scaling helps under its reduced rule, though local slopes approach 1 at the top; replicated on Qwen3 0.6B-14B), while sycophancy (0.89, or 0.83 with two seeds added at 72B) and a planted backdoor are undetermined: the backdoor is removed quickly when its trigger is known but survives blind safety training at four of five sizes. We release four browser games that play these laws (www.aisafety.fun). We make no claim about which regime holds for current frontier models.

</details>


### [142] [AnyBottle: A Recipe to Only Keep the Concepts You Really Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08552)
*Wolfgang Stammer,Sukrut Rao,Hevra Petekkaya,David Steinmann,Bernt Schiele*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AnyBottle提出一种无需概念标注的通用方法，构建紧凑、任务特定的概念瓶颈模型：通过黑盒教师引导概念选择，结合嵌套dropout训练，使模型可用任意概念前缀推理，在视觉与文本任务上以更少概念获得更高概念一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始概念瓶颈模型（CBM）需要人工概念标注；无标注变体虽去除了标注需求，却使用大型、训练与推理中均静态的概念词表，导致瓶颈远大于任务或预测所需，难以检查与干预。作者希望实现"无标注但不必大"：构建小规模、发现式的概念词表，同时保持表达力与可解释性。

Method: AnyBottle仅假设拥有一个冻结骨干网络和一个无监督概念池（如稀疏自编码器），并引入一个在同骨干上训练的黑盒教师来引导选择。每轮加入最能解释当前瓶颈失败的概念，候选概念被限制在教师与学生分歧区域。随后按该选择顺序使用嵌套dropout训练，使最终瓶颈可从任意概念前缀进行准确预测，从而对早期置信输入使用更少概念、对困难输入使用更多概念。全流程无模态特定设计，仅需替换骨干与概念池即可迁移到新领域任务。

Result: 在六个视觉数据集和两个文本数据集、两种教师范式下，AnyBottle相较无标注基线得到概念数量更少、概念一致性更高的瓶颈，同时性能接近黑盒参考模型。

Conclusion: AnyBottle表明，无标注不必意味着大规模：一个小的、发现式学习的概念词表可以像大得多的固定词表一样具有表达力。

Abstract: Concept bottleneck models (CBMs) make predictions inspectable and intervenable by routing them through human-interpretable concepts, but originally required concept annotations. Annotation-free variants remove this requirement, but typically use large concept vocabularies, static at both training and inference, producing bottlenecks larger than any task or prediction needs and harder to inspect. We propose AnyBottle, a single recipe for building compact, task-specific CBMs. AnyBottle assumes only a frozen backbone and an unsupervised concept pool, such as a sparse autoencoder. A black-box teacher trained on the same backbone then guides selection: each round adds the concept that best explains the bottleneck's current failures, with candidates restricted to regions of teacher/student disagreement. Trained with nested dropout over this selection order, the final bottleneck predicts accurately from any concept prefix, so inference spends fewer concepts on inputs it is confident about early and more on hard ones. Since no stage is modality-specific, a new domain and task requires swapping only the backbone and concept pool. Across six vision and two text datasets and two teacher paradigms, AnyBottle yields bottlenecks with fewer concepts and higher concept consistency than annotation-free baselines, while staying close to the black-box reference. Overall, AnyBottle shows that going annotation-free need not mean going large: a small, discovered vocabulary can be as expressive as a much larger, fixed one.

</details>


### [143] [Adaptive Power Sampling for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08563)
*Bingnan Xiao,Chenhao Yang,Bingcong Li,Wei Ni,Xin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出自适应幂采样（APS），在测试时按查询动态调整锐化指数，无需训练即可提升大模型推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列级幂采样方法对基座模型分布进行统一锐化，忽略了不同查询的难度差异以及基座模型对各查询已有的处理能力差异，因此缺乏查询自适应性。

Method: 理论上证明进一步锐化的收益取决于正确与错误响应之间的自奖励差距；基于此提出APS，在测试时利用答案一致性与模型自奖励之间的关系，对每个查询单独调整锐化指数。

Result: 在MATH500、HumanEval、GPQA等多个推理任务上，APS一致优于使用固定锐化指数的幂采样方法，且无需额外训练。

Conclusion: 查询自适应的幂采样能够更有效利用模型自奖励信号，在不增加训练成本的前提下提升推理表现。

Abstract: Sequence-level power sampling has recently emerged as a training-free approach to reasoning by sampling from a sharpened output distribution of a base large language model (LLM). Nevertheless, existing methods typically sharpen the base model distribution uniformly across queries, overlooking variations in query difficulty and in how well the base model already handles each query. The goal of this work is to equip power sampling with query adaptivity. Theoretically, we show that the benefits of further sharpening are determined by the self-reward gap between correct and incorrect responses. Based on this insight, we propose \emph{Adaptive Power Sampling} (APS), which adjusts the sharpening exponent on a per-query basis at test time using the relationship between answer agreement and the model's self-reward. Experiments across diverse reasoning tasks, including MATH500, HumanEval, and GPQA, show that APS consistently outperforms power sampling with a fixed sharpening exponent, without additional training.

</details>


### [144] [MINDSET: Energy-based Schema Evolution for Long Conversational Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08586)
*Sujato Dutta,Sreekruthy Tummala,Shashank Vanga,Ayushmi Pavani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MINDSET 将长对话记忆建模为不可变情节与版本化模式的最小能量状态转移，在 LoCoMo 与 MemoryAgentBench 上取得最优或领先表现，并支持跨模型独立性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长对话代理需记住久远信息以高效完成任务，但指令与上下文随时间变化，代理必须自适应。理想记忆系统应同时保留当前与历史状态、区分过时信息与活跃知识、按查询检索证据，并避免反复调用大模型重写历史交互。

Method: 提出 MINDSET 记忆控制器：将对话存储为不可变情节，并通过最小能量状态转移组织为版本化模式；每个新情节可强化、取代、分裂或创建模式。转移决策权衡表示失真、矛盾、历史损伤、碎片化与内部不一致，并用迟滞机制防止孤立矛盾过早重写稳定记忆。

Result: 在 850 题可复现样本（700 LoCoMo + 150 MemoryAgentBench）上对比 5 种记忆系统：MINDSET 取得最高 LoCoMo 答案 F1，并显著提升检索排序（Recall@8、MRR、nDCG@8，优于次优 LightMem，Holm 校正后 p<0.01）；在 MemoryAgentBench 上得分最高但相对差异较小。消融显示受控碎片化与模式感知分配贡献最大。700 题跨模型评估（GLM-4.7 与 Gemma-4-31B）支持模型独立性。

Conclusion: 长时记忆更适合作为受约束的状态管理来处理，而非持续摘要。

Abstract: Long conversational agents have become essential in our daily lives. They must remember what was said long back in order to help us efficiently complete a task without needing the user to repeat instructions and context repeatedly. However, the main issue is that instructions and context change over time and so the agents must be able to adapt accordingly. A useful memory system should preserve both current and historical states, distinguish stale information from active knowledge, retrieve evidence appropriate to the query and avoid repeatedly invoking a large language model to rewrite prior interactions. We introduce MINDSET, a memory controller that stores a conversation as immutable episodes and organizes them into versioned schemas through minimum-energy state transitions. Each incoming episode may reinforce, supersede, split or create a schema. The transition decision balances representation distortion, contradiction, historical damage, fragmentation and internal inconsistency, while hysteresis prevents isolated contradictions from prematurely rewriting stable memory. We evaluate MINDSET against 5 memory systems on a reproducible sample of 850 questions (700 LoCoMo + 150 MemoryAgentBench). MINDSET obtains the highest observed LoCoMo answer F1 while significantly improving retrieval ranking (Recall@8, MRR and nDCG@8) over the second best method LightMem (p<0.01 after Holm correction). It obtains the highest observed scores on MemoryAgentBench although the relative difference is low. Ablations identify controlled fragmentation and schema-aware assignment as the largest contributors to answer quality. Additionally, a 700-question cross-model evaluation with GLM-4.7 and Gemma-4-31B supported model independence. These results show that long-term memory can be better handled as constrained state management rather than continual summarization.

</details>


### [145] [Recursive Game Creator: An Agentic Product-Level Experience-Oriented Game Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08621)
*Jiajun Chen,Haoyu Wu,Mingda Jia,Xihui Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Recursive Game Creator，一个以体验为导向的智能体游戏开发框架，通过 Designer、Builder、Player、Reviewer 四组件递归迭代，把游戏原型打磨成有趣可玩的游戏，并在多项基准与用户研究中取得领先表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有游戏设计智能体虽能生成可运行的游戏，但程序正确并不意味着玩家有良好体验。论文希望推动智能体游戏开发从粗糙原型走向真正有趣的游戏，弥补"能运行"与"好玩"之间的差距。

Method: 框架围绕四个组件递归展开：Designer 将用户指令与 Reviewer 反馈转化为详细计划；Builder 将计划变为候选游戏；编码原生的 Player 通过程序化接口创建并执行可复用策略，高效收集多样化的游玩轨迹，缓解基于 GUI 采集慢而导致的评估偏差；Reviewer 使用精心设计的轨迹指标推断玩家偏好，并结合视觉证据与显式文本偏好，按游戏特定标准评估游戏；最终 Reviewer 接受更优版本并给出改进意见，进入下一轮，形成递归闭环。

Result: 在 GameCraft-Bench 上取得 77.89 的最优综合表现；在 GameASG-Bench 上严格任务成功率达 53.2%，较同模型基线提升 34.1%，并以 93.4% 的平均运行时检查通过率在对比方法中最高；用户研究显示更长游戏时长和更高评分。

Conclusion: Recursive Game Creator 通过以体验为导向的递归开发闭环，有效提升了智能体生成游戏的趣味性与质量，在自动化基准与真实用户反馈上均优于现有方法，代码即将开源。

Abstract: Recent game design agents have made substantial progress in generating playable games. However, program correctness does not ensure an enjoyable experience for players. We present Recursive Game Creator, an experience-oriented harness to advance agentic game development from rough game prototypes into entertaining games. Recursive Game Creator organizes recursive development around four components: Designer, Builder, Player, and Reviewer. The Designer translates user instructions and Reviewer's feedback into detailed plans. The Builder turns these plans into candidate games. The coding-native Player creates and executes reusable policies through programmatic interfaces to efficiently collect diverse gameplay trajectories, mitigating evaluation bias caused by slow GUI-based collection. The Reviewer uses carefully designed trajectory-based metrics to induce player preferences, integrating with visual evidence and explicit textual preferences to evaluate games against game-specific criteria. Finally, the Reviewer accepts the better version and provides improvement reviews for the next round, closing the recursive loop. Our method achieves state-of-the-art overall performance of 77.89 on GameCraft-Bench. On GameASG-Bench, it achieves a strict task success rate of 53.2%, a 34.1% improvement over the same-model baseline, and the highest mean runtime-check pass rate at 93.4% among compared methods. A user study shows longer playtime and higher ratings. Code is coming soon.

</details>


### [146] [Parallel Predictive World Models for Accurate and Efficient Long-Horizon Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08627)
*Wanjin Feng,Baobin Zhang,Ao Yu,Shibo Feng,Xi Wang,Xingyu Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出并行预测世界模型（PPWM），通过并行预测有限视界轨迹并保留未来表征间的因果交互，替代状态级自回归展开，实现更准确高效的长视界世界模型规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视界世界模型规划通常依赖自回归展开，将预测状态反复反馈给模型。这虽保留时间结构，却造成与视界长度成正比的顺序计算路径，并使后期预测暴露于递归的解码状态反馈，导致误差累积与效率低下。作者希望探索能否在不进行逐步自回归的情况下实现准确高效的长视界规划。

Method: 提出并行预测世界模型（PPWM）：并行预测一个有限视界的完整轨迹，同时保留未来表征之间的因果交互。每个视界以其因果动作前缀为条件，未来表征在解码前先互相交互，从而将时间因果性与逐步输出递归解耦。作者将自回归展开形式化为因果轨迹映射，并识别出PPWM所去除的解码状态反馈路径。

Result: 在四个视觉控制任务上，PPWM在所评估的预测接口中取得最低的长视界预测误差和最高的交叉熵方法（CEM）模拟器成功率；同时相较自回归LeWM基线，CEM规划平均加速超过3倍。

Conclusion: 准确高效的长视界世界模型规划并不依赖状态级自回归，而可通过并行因果轨迹预测实现。这为世界模型规划提供了一种更优的接口设计范式。

Abstract: Long-horizon world-model planning typically relies on autoregressive rollouts, where predicted states are repeatedly fed back into the model. This preserves temporal structure but creates a horizon-length sequential path and exposes later predictions to recursive decoded-state feedback. We introduce Parallel Predictive World Models (PPWM), which predict a finite-horizon trajectory in parallel while retaining causal interaction among future representations. Each horizon is conditioned on its causal action prefix, and future representations interact before decoding, separating temporal causality from state-by-state output recursion. We formalize this distinction by viewing autoregressive rollout as a causal trajectory map and identifying the decoded-state feedback pathway removed by PPWM. Across four visual-control tasks, PPWM achieves the lowest long-horizon prediction error and the highest Cross-Entropy Method (CEM) simulator success among the evaluated predictive interfaces. Meanwhile, PPWM achieves more than a 3$\times$ average CEM planning speedup over the autoregressive LeWM baseline. These results suggest that accurate and efficient long-horizon world-model planning does not require state-by-state autoregression, but can instead be achieved through parallel causal trajectory prediction.

</details>


### [147] [SquidAgent: Parallelize Wisely, Coordinate Efficiently](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08647)
*Yexiong Lin,Shanshan Ye,Yu Yao,Zhen Fang,Bo Han,Tongliang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SquidAgent，通过基于预测token的并行决策准则，识别并消除并行多智能体中的"再探索成本"与"对齐成本"，实现超过Claude Code的2.6倍墙钟加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行多智能体本应带来近线性加速，但现有系统常比单智能体更慢；作者将差距归因于再探索成本与对齐成本两类隐藏开销。

Method: 提出基于预测token的原则性决策准则：仅当某层的关键路径成本加再探索与对齐开销低于串行成本时才并行化；据此设计SquidAgent，单步规划估算token预算、从编排者会话直接fork工作节点消除再探索、用预生成共享约定块将对齐转为有界前期成本，并由确定性调度器逐层应用准则。

Result: SquidAgent相比Claude Code实现2.2倍平均吞吐提升和2.6倍平均墙钟加速，相比最强多智能体基线实现2.0倍吞吐提升。

Conclusion: 以预测token而非墙钟时间作为成本度量更可靠，可支撑并行化的原则性决策，从而在消除隐藏开销后获得实际加速效果。

Abstract: LLM-based agents solve complex multi-step tasks, but sequential execution incurs substantial latency. In principle, parallelizing work across multiple agents should yield near-linear speedups. Yet existing parallel multi-agent systems often run slower than a single-agent baseline. We attribute this gap to two hidden costs that parallel execution incurs but a serial agent avoids. First, there is a re-exploration cost: redundant effort spent by parallel workers reconstructing context that the orchestrator already possesses, such as prior decisions, that would otherwise be inherited implicitly in a serial execution. Second, there is an alignment cost: the overhead required to reconcile inconsistencies across independently generated outputs. We thus derive a principled decision criterion: a layer should be parallelized only when its critical-path cost, plus re-exploration and alignment overheads, is lower than the corresponding serial cost. While this criterion is naturally expressed in wall-clock time, we observe that LLMs are poorly calibrated when asked to estimate task duration. To address this, we instead measure cost in predicted output tokens, which we empirically find LLMs can estimate substantially more reliably than wall-clock time. Building on this token-based criterion, we propose SquidAgent. It estimates all token budgets in a single planning step, forks each worker directly from the orchestrator's session to eliminate re-exploration cost, and replaces post-hoc reconciliation with a pre-generated shared convention block that converts alignment into a bounded upfront cost. A deterministic scheduler then applies the criterion layer by layer. Empirically, SquidAgent achieves a 2.2$\times$ mean throughput improvement and a 2.6$\times$ mean wall-time speedup over Claude Code, and a 2.0$\times$ throughput improvement over the strongest multi-agent baseline.

</details>


### [148] [ParanoiaEval: Benchmarking Unnecessary Defensive Work in Agentic Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08662)
*Hanjun Luo,Xiucheng Zhang,Zhuoning Xu,Zhimu Huang,Yingbin Jin,Xinfeng Li,Hanan Salam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ParanoiaEval，首个统一评估代码智能体风险处理能力的基准，基于软件工程风险管理中的ATMA框架，包含200个证据控制的仓库级任务对，并揭示风险处理是独立于任务能力的维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着代码智能体日益自主承担真实工作，判断其风险处理是否合理变得十分重要。现有工作从彼此分离的视角评估相关智能体行为，缺乏统一这些行为的系统性框架，因此需要构建统一评估基准。

Method: 基于软件工程风险管理中成熟的Avoidance-Transfer-Mitigation-Acceptance（规避-转移-缓解-接受）框架，将其4种基本处理方式操作化到代码智能体场景；构建200个证据控制的仓库级任务对，每对任务仅在定义处理方式的证据上存在差异；并引入针对风险处理违规和证据响应性的专门指标，使用人工校准的智能体评判器进行评估。

Result: 在8个代表性模型上的大规模实验及事后人类研究显示：(I) 即便存在明确证据，仍有11.2%-58.7%的运行出现不必要的风险处理，且不同智能体配置差异显著；(II) 更强的任务能力并不保证更恰当的风险处理，而处理违规会显著损害开发者体验，确立风险处理为独立的能力维度；(III) 智能体表现出与既有风险管理发现一致的系统性模式。

Conclusion: 风险处理应当被视为代码智能体的一项独立能力维度，人类风险管理实践中的知识可指导该能力的诊断与改进。

Abstract: As coding agents increasingly undertake real-world work autonomously, judging whether their risk treatments are warranted has become important. Existing work evaluates related agent behaviors from separate perspectives, but lacks a systematic framework for unifying these behaviors. To bridge this gap, we introduce ParanoiaEval, the first benchmark for unified evaluation of risk-treatment capabilities in coding agents. Grounded in the well-established Avoidance-Transfer-Mitigation-Acceptance framework in software engineering risk management, ParanoiaEval operationalizes its 4 fundamental treatments for coding-agent settings and contains 200 evidence-controlled repository-level task pairs, each differing only in treatment-defining evidence. We further introduce dedicated metrics for risk-treatment violations and evidence responsiveness, using a human-calibrated agentic judge for reliable evaluation. Large-scale experiments on 8 representative models and a post-hoc human study reveal that (I) unnecessary risk treatment occurs in 11.2%-58.7% of runs despite explicit evidence, with substantial variation across agent configurations; (II) stronger task capability does not ensure more appropriate risk treatment, while treatment violations substantially harm developers' experience, establishing risk treatment as an independent capability dimension; and (III) agents exhibit systematic patterns consistent with established risk-management findings, suggesting that knowledge from human practice can guide the diagnosis and improvement of this capability.

</details>


### [149] [Coupled but Late: Turn-Taking Between Full-Duplex Speech Models in Unscripted Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08683)
*Lichen Zhu,Yueqian Lin,Yiheng Wang,Hai "Helen" Li,Yiran Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 两个PersonaPlex-7B全双工语音模型在共享时钟下对话时，其轮流发言时序是耦合的，但换手明显晚于人类（中位数400–560 ms vs 137 ms），且在对方话轮末120 ms内几乎不发起抢话，表现为对感知到的结束做出被动反应，而非人类式的话轮末预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全双工语音模型正被越来越多地用于彼此对话（自博弈数据生成、智能体社会、基于模型的评测），这种环路中没有人来消化时序误差，一个模型的轮流行为直接成为另一个模型的输入。作者因此想探究这种循环会稳定到什么样的时序。

Method: 让两个PersonaPlex-7B实例在共享时钟上交换音频token进行无脚本对话，并与Switchboard语料采用同一条话轮交接规则进行对比；此外还通过延迟信道的一个方向来观察模型响应如何变化，分析话轮末的抢话分布。

Result: 模型间的时序是耦合的：跨对话重新配对说话人会破坏这种时序。但话轮交接发生得偏晚，中位数为400–560 ms，而人类为137 ms；在对方话轮最后120 ms内（人类预测性交接约十分之一发生在此）模型仅有1%的交接。延迟一个方向的信道会使响应一比一平移，且响应前的准备期为空。

Conclusion: 模型的轮流行为与"感知到话轮结束后再被动等待"一致，而非人类时序所需的"话轮末预测"，说明当前全双工语音模型缺乏人类式的前瞻性话轮预测能力。

Abstract: Full-duplex speech models are trained to converse with a person, but they are increasingly made to converse with each other, in self-play data generation, agent societies, and model-based evaluation. In that loop no human absorbs a timing error: each model's turn-taking is the other's input. We ask what timing the loop settles into. Two PersonaPlex-7B instances exchange audio tokens on a shared clock in unscripted conversation, and one floor-transfer rule is applied to them and to Switchboard. Their timing is coupled: re-pairing speakers across conversations destroys it. But the floor changes hands late, at a median of 400-560 ms against 137 ms for humans, and the last 120 ms of the partner's turn, where human projection places a tenth of its transfers, holds 1% of theirs. Delaying one direction of the channel shifts the response one-for-one and leaves the run-up to it empty, consistent with a reactive wait after the perceived end rather than the turn-end projection human timing requires.

</details>


### [150] [ScienceClaw: Benchmarking Continual Self-Evolution of AI-for-Science Agents Across the Natural and Social Sciences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08691)
*Mingda Zhang,Wenjin Liu,Tiesunlong Shen,Zikai Xiao,Zhenghong Lin,Qing Xu,Erik Cambria,Xiaoying Tang,Haoran Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出ScienceClaw，将科学自动化中的程序自我进化形式化为固定参数程序自进化，统一任务求解、科学验证与程序更新，并构建覆盖23个学科的ScienceClaw-Eval评测框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体正在加速科学自动化，但经过验证的执行很少能沉淀为持久的程序级改进，且现有评测未能在自然科学与社会科学中跨顺序任务考察这一过程。

Method: 将ScienceClaw形式化为固定参数程序自我进化；通过多轮交互修复可执行工作流，将经再执行验证的失败—成功轨迹转化为关联的Skill与Operator候选，仅当源任务重放能复现修复且独立科学任务获得提升时才保留更新；ScienceClaw-Eval覆盖23个学科，通过顺序流与独立重置评测考察科学正确性、进化增益、保持性、跨数据集迁移和进化成本。

Result: 构建了覆盖23个学科、包含顺序流与独立重置评测的ScienceClaw-Eval，用于衡量科学正确性、进化增益、保持性、跨数据集迁移与进化成本；代码已开源。

Conclusion: 该工作为科学自动化中程序级自我进化提供了统一的定义与评测框架，强调只有通过源任务重放复现与独立任务提升双重验证的更新才被保留，从而推动验证执行向持久程序改进转化。

Abstract: Large language model agents are accelerating scientific automation, yet verified executions rarely become persistent program-level improvements, and existing evaluations do not examine this process across sequential tasks in both the natural and social sciences. We formalize ScienceClaw as fixed-parameter program self-evolution that unifies task solving, scientific verification, and program updates. ScienceClaw-Eval spans 23 disciplines and measures scientific correctness, evolutionary gain, retention, cross-dataset transfer, and evolution cost through sequential streams and independent reset evaluation. Our framework repairs executable workflows through multi-turn interaction, converts re-execution-verified failure--success trajectories into linked Skill and Operator candidates, and retains an update only when source-task replay reproduces the repair and independent scientific tasks improve. Code is available at https://github.com/beita6969/ScienceClaw.

</details>


### [151] [nanoMuse: An Open-Source Personal Agent for Every Device You Own](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08699)
*Guangyi Liu,Yong Liu,Jiangning Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文回顾从2011年助手、2023年智能体到2026年Meta Muse的演进，界定"个人智能体"概念，并据Meta公开记录与生产提示词剖析Muse的实现，进而提出开源对应物nanoMuse：跨设备、共享会话、经Sentinel管控、记忆为可读文件、模型可选，并给出开放路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业个人智能体（如Meta Muse）封闭、托管于单一厂商云与单一国家，缺乏开放对应物，用户的账户、设备、长期记忆与行为问责都受制于厂商，因此需要开源替代方案。

Method: 以五个问题与三个时间视野界定个人智能体；依据Meta公开记录及其生产提示词副本，逐条标注来源地还原Muse的构建方式；随后设计并呈现遵循GPL-3.0的nanoMuse架构。

Result: 提出nanoMuse：每人所有设备各一个智能体，可操作手机屏幕与电脑；通过任何人可自建的relay共享同一段对话；所有动作经Sentinel审核；记忆为个人可读文件；模型可自选。其规模与成本以估算形式给出。

Conclusion: 开放式个人智能体尚待补齐：开放（可自建、可审计）、带来源溯源的记忆、面向"操作之手"的评测套件、以及配套开放模型，构成后续路线图。

Abstract: Assistants from 2011 answered and waited, and agents from 2023 did a task and stopped. In September 2026 Meta's Muse showed an agent for one person, with accounts, devices, memory and a conversation that lasts, closed, in a vendor's cloud, in one country. Such an agent is expected to act on a person's accounts and devices, remember them across weeks, speak first when it is worth it, and answer for what it did. It is a kind of software, not a model, and until now had no open counterpart. This report defines the personal agent in five questions and three horizons. It reads how Muse is built from Meta's public record and a copy of its production prompt, each statement marked by its source. It then presents nanoMuse, the open-source counterpart under the GPL-3.0, one agent on every device a person owns, with hands on the phone's screen and the computer's. They share one conversation over a relay anyone can run; every action goes through a Sentinel, memory is files the person can read, and the model is their choice. Its size and cost are given as estimates. What is open, memory with provenance, an evaluation suite for the hands and an open model for them, is set out as a roadmap.

</details>


### [152] [WorldSolver: Can LLM Agents Simulate the Physical Dynamics via Solver Generation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08720)
*Siru Jiang,Yongzhe Lyu,Shuo Lu,Yubin Wang,Yuxiang Zhang,Yue Liao,Bin Wang,Jian Liang,Tieniu Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出WorldSolver基准，包含168个来自61篇经典图形学论文、覆盖7个物理域的仿真求解器生成任务，从执行、视觉保真、物理合理性三维评估LLM agent，最强模型仅约48.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的agent正推进科学与工程问题求解，物理仿真是有挑战且实用的测试平台。构建求解器需要物理理解、数学推理与软件工程能力，但LLM agent这一能力尚未被充分探索，缺少相应评测基准。

Method: 构建WorldSolver基准：168个仿真任务，源自61篇经典计算机图形学论文中的物理现象，覆盖7个物理域。每个任务提供固定仿真环境的代码脚手架，求解器实现留给agent完成。评估分三个维度：可执行性检查、渲染仿真的视觉保真度、对生成动力学的物理合理性验证。

Result: 前沿agent实验表明，生成可执行求解器本身就很困难，满足视觉与物理正确性更难。GPT-5.6-Sol和Claude-Opus-5相对较好，但总分仅48.7%和46.7%。

Conclusion: WorldSolver是迈向agentic求解器生成的早期一步，期望推动agent忠实地仿真动态物理世界。代码已开源。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are increasingly advancing scientific and engineering problem solving, with physics simulation emerging as a challenging yet practical testbed for reproducing complex physical phenomena with application in embodied AI, games and films. As the workhorse of such simulation, a solver computes how the state of a dynamic system evolves over time. Building such solvers requires physical understanding to identify appropriate models, mathematical reasoning to formulate the underlying dynamics, and software engineering to implement them as executable code, yet this capability of LLM agents remains underexplored. To this end, we introduce WorldSolver, a benchmark of 168 simulation tasks derived from physical phenomena in 61 classic computer graphics papers, spanning 7 physical domains. Each task contains a code scaffold that provides a fixed simulation environment for the scene, with the solver implementation left for the agent to complete. Specifically, we evaluate them along three dimensions: Execution Checks for successful execution, Visual Fidelity for reproducing the intended dynamic behavior in the rendered simulation, and Physical Plausibility for physics-grounded verification of the generated dynamics. Experiments on frontier agents reveal that producing executable solvers is difficult itself, and satisfying visual and physical correctness is even harder. GPT-5.6-Sol and Claude-Opus-5 perform comparatively better than the other evaluated agents, yet achieve overall scores of only 48.7% and 46.7%, respectively. WorldSolver is an early step toward agentic solver generation, and we hope it helps drive progress toward agents that can faithfully simulate the dynamic physical world. Code is available at https://github.com/sirujiang/WorldSolver.

</details>


### [153] [Does an Agent's History Tell You When Compaction Will Hurt? A Modest, Bounded Effect on the TRACE Paired-Replay Corpus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08722)
*Egor Pakhomov,Erik Nijkamp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文利用TRACE公开语料（590个AppWorld压缩边界）检验：智能体近期行为能否预测压缩带来的伤害。结果显示压缩前历史对压缩后伤害的预测力很弱：预设对比为宽泛的零结果，"已写入"标签实际测量的是轨迹阶段；最佳触发规则在留出集AUROC仅0.66，而同一边界复现可达0.72。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程智能体通常按全局规则（如token预算）压缩上下文，而不考虑智能体正在做什么。作者希望探究：智能体近期行为是否能预测压缩何时会造成伤害，从而支持更行为感知的压缩触发策略。

Method: 基于TRACE公开语料库的590个harness触发的AppWorld压缩边界，从重执行的prefix状态出发，分别在压缩前上下文与摘要下重放每个边界，并记录后续动作的负担（报错或重复调用）。采用预设的prefix placement对比，建立边界内复现协议，并比较扩展协议触发规则与冻结可解释触发规则。

Result: 压缩前历史对压缩后伤害仅有弱预测力；预设对比为宽泛零结果，"已写入"标签实际测量轨迹阶段。最佳扩展协议触发规则留出集AUROC为0.66（在复现自身标签上0.64），而同一边界复现为0.72。最佳冻结可解释触发规则避免了21%的有害（正负担）边界，同时保留84%的压缩机会，在计数上超过随机规则预期，但在负担质量上未超过（事后比较）。

Conclusion: 在现有发布数据上，无法评估最佳触发规则在匹配保留率下是否优于token预算规则。作者明确提出了未来应发布哪些语料，以回答该问题。

Abstract: Many long-horizon agents compact their context on a global rule, usually a token budget, blind to what the agent was doing. We ask whether the agent's recent behaviour predicts when a compaction will hurt. TRACE's public corpus of 590 harness-triggered AppWorld compaction boundaries replays each boundary from a re-executed prefix state under the pre-compaction context and under the summary, and records the burden of the next actions: calls that error or repeat a call already made. We find that pre-boundary history predicts post-compaction harm only weakly. An internally prespecified contrast by prefix placement is a wide null, and the naive "has-written" label behind it turns out to measure trajectory phase. The best extension-protocol trigger reaches held-out AUROC 0.66 (0.64 on the replicate's own label) against a same-boundary replicate of 0.72; the best frozen, interpretable trigger avoids 21% of harmful (positive-burden) boundaries while keeping 84% of compaction opportunities, and exceeds the random-rule expectation on count but not on burden mass (a post hoc comparison). Whether the best trigger beats a token-budget rule at matched retention cannot be evaluated on the release. We state what corpora should ship to answer it.

</details>


### [154] [VeriFine: Scaling Verification for Self-Improvement in Embodied Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08761)
*Zewei Zhou,Rachel Luo,Yulong Cao,Chaowei Xiao,Chensheng Peng,Boyi Li,Thomas Tian,Zheng Lian,Yan Wang,Jiaqi Ma,Boris Ivanovic,Marco Pavone,Wenhao Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 VeriFine 框架，通过策略、课程与评判器的共同演化来扩展验证能力，实现具身推理中策略与评判器的持续自我改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我改进策略会不断暴露新的失败模式，使评判器需验证的内容不断变化；而当前固定的评判器限制了优化反馈和有用训练样本的发现，阻碍进一步自我改进。这一挑战在具身推理中尤为突出，因为可靠评估须考虑空间定位、因果推理和安全感知决策。

Method: VeriFine 是一个智能体框架，通过策略、训练课程与评判器的共同演化来扩展验证。策略改进循环使用评分标准评判器诊断反复出现的失败、构建自适应课程并优化策略；当进展停滞、验证成为瓶颈时，评判器改进循环选择性地对高信息量失败案例查询人类指导，并通过协同主动校准（人类与智能体解决分歧、趋近物理推理的客观标准）来精炼评判器，再由修订后的评判器指导下一阶段的数据选择与策略优化。

Result: 在驾驶和机器人导航任务上的实验表明，在强化学习和监督微调两种设置下，策略与评判器能力均实现了持续自我改进。

Conclusion: 结果表明，扩展验证能力可以支持策略失败模式不断演化下的持续自我改进。

Abstract: Self-improving policies continually expose new failure patterns, changing what their judges must be able to verify. However, current fixed judges constrain both optimization feedback and the discovery of useful training examples, limiting further self-improvement. This challenge is even more acute in embodied reasoning, where reliable evaluation must account for spatial grounding, causal reasoning, and safety-aware decision-making. We introduce VeriFine, an agent harness framework that scales verification through the co-evolution of the policy, training curriculum, and judge. The Policy Improvement Loop uses a rubric judge to diagnose recurring failures, construct an adaptive curriculum, and optimize the policy. When progress plateaus and verification becomes a bottleneck, the Judge Improvement Loop selectively queries human guidance on informative failure cases and refines the judge through coactive calibration, in which humans and agents resolve disagreements and converge toward the objective rubric of physical reasoning. The revised judge then guides the next stage of data selection and policy optimization. Experiments on driving and robot navigation tasks demonstrate continuous self-improvement in both policy and judge capability across reinforcement and supervised fine-tuning. These results show how scaling verification supports continuous self-improvement as policy failure patterns evolve.

</details>


### [155] [Agent in a Bottle: Can LLM Agents Turn Their Capabilities Into Cheap, Scalable Artifacts?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08775)
*Ankit Sonthalia,Haritz Puerto,Alexander Rubinstein,Martin Gubri,Seong Joon Oh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"bottling"能力（将通用能力转化为兼顾质量与摊销成本的任务专用方案）及其基准BOTTLED：智能体在固定时间、算力和API预算下完成整个无标签工作负载。结果显示强零样本性能并不能可靠转化为强bottling能力，但bottling可带来显著成本节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM虽能解决许多窄任务，但对数百万相关实例逐一查询成本过高。作者希望探究LLM智能体能否自主地为这类大规模重复性工作负载创建更廉价的解决方案，即把通用能力"装瓶"为任务专用方案。

Method: 提出BOTTLED基准：智能体接收完整无标签工作负载，须在固定的时间、计算和LLM API预算内完成，可自行选择路径（如训练小模型或编写可复用程序）。在10个模型、3项任务上进行评测，并与零样本性能及两个小模型蒸馏基线、以及专为廉价重复推理设计的"系统一"模型Jev进行比较。

Result: 强零样本性能不能可靠转化为强bottling能力：零样本分数相近的模型在bottling后差异明显；60次bottling运行中有48次低于其模型零样本性能95%置信区间下界；31次低于两个同token预算的小模型蒸馏基线中较强的一个。但bottling可带来大幅节省：在查询-商品相关性分类上，Opus 5保留约82%的零样本macro-F1，报告成本约降低657倍；与Jev相比，同任务下恢复约94%的macro-F1，成本仅为Jev预估全工作负载成本的四分之一。

Conclusion: BOTTLED为评估和改进智能体"将有限资源投入到大规模重复性工作负载的可复用解决方案"的能力提供了基础，bottling兼具挑战性与显著的成本节约潜力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can solve many narrow tasks, but querying them separately for millions of related instances can be prohibitively expensive. Can LLM agents autonomously create cheaper solutions for such workloads? We call this ability "bottling": the ability to turn general capabilities into task-specific solutions that balance answer quality and amortised cost. We introduce BOTTLED, a benchmark in which agents receive an entire unlabelled workload and must complete it under fixed time, compute and LLM API budgets. Agents choose their own approach, such as training a small model or writing a reusable program. Across ten models and three tasks, we find that strong zero-shot task performance does not reliably translate into strong bottling capabilities. Models with similar zero-shot scores can differ substantially after bottling, and 48 of 60 bottling runs score below the lower bound of the 95% confidence interval of their model's zero-shot performance. Moreover, 31 of 60 runs underperform the stronger of two small-model distillation baselines with the same token budget. Nevertheless, bottling can yield substantial savings: on query-product relevance classification, Opus 5 retains about 82% of its zero-shot macro-F1 at roughly 657 times lower reported cost. Bottling is also competitive with Jev, a "system one" model built especially for cheap, repetitive inference: Opus 5 on the same task recovers about 94% of Jev's macro-F1 at a quarter of Jev's projected full-workload cost. BOTTLED provides a basis for evaluating and improving agents' ability to invest limited resources in reusable solutions for large, repetitive workloads.

</details>


### [156] [Sherpa: Teaching LLMs to Teach Adaptively](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08778)
*Weixian Xu,Yanzhe Zhang,Zora Zhiruo Wang,Changyu Chen,Diyi Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Sherpa多轮强化学习框架，通过多个学生原型建模并直接以学生学习成效为奖励训练教师LLM，使其能自适应不同学生偏好。教师模型让学生平均提升20.5个百分点，MathTutorBench教学分数从52.5%升至79.2%，人类评估中79.6%场景优于基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM解题能力强不等于会教学。现有教师LLM训练依赖示范、偏好数据或预设教学标准，这些信号未与个体学生的学习成效挂钩，而有效教学策略因学习者而异，因此需要以学习结果为直接优化目标的训练方法。

Method: Sherpa是一个多轮强化学习框架：用LLM实例化多个具有不同学习偏好的学生原型，教师模型在多轮交互中调整教学，并以这些模拟学生的实际学习成效作为奖励直接优化教师策略。

Result: Sherpa训练的教师使各原型学生的表现平均提升20.5个百分点；在MathTutorBench上教学总分由52.5%提升到79.2%；人类成对比较中，训练后的教师有79.6%被偏好。

Conclusion: Sherpa能训练LLM教师适应多样化的模拟学生，并更好地与人类教师对齐，为AI导师教学真实学生奠定基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have become increasingly capable problem solvers, but being able to solve a problem is not the same as being able to teach it. Existing approaches to training LLMs as teachers rely on demonstrations, preference data, or predefined pedagogical criteria that specify what good teaching looks like. However, these signals are often not grounded in individual student learning outcomes, where effective teaching strategies can vary substantially across learners. To address this, we introduce Sherpa, a multi-turn reinforcement learning framework that instantiates multiple student archetypes with LLMs conditioned on distinct learning preferences and trains a teacher model to adapt its instruction by directly maximizing their learning outcomes. Teacher LLMs trained with Sherpa improve instructed students' performance across all archetypes by an average of 20.5 percentage points. Under MathTutorBench's evaluation, Sherpa raises the overall pedagogy score from 52.5% to 79.2%, indicating better teaching responses. Our human studies show that the trained teacher is preferred over the base model in 79.6% of pairwise comparisons. Together, Sherpa trains LLM teachers to adapt to diverse simulated students and become better aligned with human teachers, paving the road towards AI tutors teaching real students.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [157] [Educating future engineers about LLMs: A scalable workshop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07027)
*R. Zhang,J. C. F. de Winter,T. Dicke,D. Dodou,Y. B. Eisma*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向工程硕士生的可扩展游戏化工作坊，通过移动网页界面让226名学生为两种LLM编写提示词完成机器人导航规划任务，并最终在Spot机器人上实机演示，验证了该类仿真到现实环境在培养负责任LLM使用技能上的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型日益融入工程工作流程，学生需要动手实践以学会批判性地与LLM协作。现有教学缺乏可扩展、能培养验证与协作技能的真实场景训练环境，因此需要一个结合仿真与实机、可大规模实施的游戏化工作坊。

Method: 设计了一个面向工程硕士生的可扩展游戏化工作坊，聚焦导航规划中的人机协作。使用移动网页界面，在10期工作坊中让226名学生分别为非推理型和推理型LLM编写提示词，解决复杂度递增的网格导航任务。系统返回机器人可执行计划、轨迹可视化与自动评分，并以Boston Dynamics Spot机器人进行现场演示。最后通过问卷和提示词分析评估效果。

Result: 工作坊后问卷显示，81.5%的学生报告有实质性学习收获，91.0%报告高度参与。对提交提示词的分析发现，学生会根据LLM类型调整策略：对非推理型LLM采用逐步指令，而对复杂问题求解任务则转向提供更高层次的指导。

Conclusion: 这类交互式"仿真到现实"环境能够有效教授在工程中负责任使用LLM所需的验证与协作技能。相关代码已开源。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into engineering workflows, students require hands-on experience to learn how to collaborate with them critically. This paper presents a scalable gamified workshop designed for engineering Master's students to practice human-AI collaboration in navigation planning. Using a mobile web interface across 10 workshop sessions, a total of 226 students wrote prompts for a non-reasoning and a reasoning LLM to solve grid-based navigation tasks of increasing complexity. The system returned robot-executable plans, trajectory visualizations, and automated scoring, culminating in a live demonstration on a Boston Dynamics Spot robot. In a post-workshop questionnaire, 81.5% reported substantial learning and 91.0% reported high engagement. Analysis of the submitted prompts revealed that students changed their strategies from step-by-step instructions for the non-reasoning LLM toward providing higher-level guidance for complex problem-solving tasks. We conclude that such interactive simulation-to-reality environments are viable for teaching the verification and collaboration skills necessary for responsible LLM use in engineering. Code is available at: https://github.com/renchizhhhh/LLM-robotics-workshop

</details>


### [158] [Tellimation: Making Narrative Gaps Visible in Children's Storytelling with Just-in-Time Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07039)
*Vincent Cavez,Marielle Zheng,Momin Siddiqui,Abbie L Olszewski,Srirangaraj Setlur,Maneesh Agrawala,Hari Subramonyam*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Tellimation 系统，通过将儿童在故事讲述中遗漏或误述的场景元素进行动画化，以视觉提示叙事缺口，从而帮助有语言困难的儿童讲述更连贯的故事。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 插图场景常被用于提示儿童讲故事，但有语言困难的儿童往往会省略使故事连贯的细节、动作和关系。现有方法难以在不直接告诉儿童"该讲什么"的前提下引导其补全叙事。

Method: Tellimation 的设计空间涵盖八类叙事缺口（场景中的人物、数量、特征、正在做什么、地点、时间、人物关系，以及画面之外的言语与想法），并基于经典动画原则实例化出 20 种参数化动画。系统采用实时流水线检测话语与场景之间的差异，再依据场景的叙事潜力与儿童的历史记录，选择并参数化相应的动画进行提示。

Result: 成人被试（N=120）在无需指导的情况下理解了大多数动画的含义；儿童被试（N=12）在动画提示下解决了系统识别出的 46% 的叙事缺口，而无动画提示时仅为 13%。

Conclusion: 动画化的视觉提示可有效帮助儿童注意到并补全被遗漏的叙事要素，为支持儿童叙事表达提供了一种不直接给出答案的交互式干预思路。

Abstract: Illustrated scenes are used to prompt children's stories, yet children with language difficulties often omit the details, actions, and relationships that make a story coherent. We introduce Tellimation, which animates scene elements a child has omitted or misdescribed, drawing attention to a narrative gap without saying what to tell. Its design space covers eight kinds of gaps (who is in the scene, how many, what they are like, what they are doing, where, when, how they relate, and what lies beyond the picture, such as speech and thoughts), instantiated through 20 parameterized animations grounded in classical animation principles. A real-time pipeline detects discrepancies between utterance and scene, then selects and parameterizes an animation from the scene's narrative potential and the child's history. Adults interpreted most animations without instruction (N=120); children (N=12) resolved 46% of the gaps the system identified with animations, against 13% without.

</details>


### [159] [Towards semantic reconstruction of individual words from fnirs using clip loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07120)
*Santiago Posso-Murillo,Nathan Palladino,Ben Pyykkonen,Dan Y. Han,Luis G. Sanchez-Giraldo,Jihye Bae*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文比较了对比损失（基于CLIP）与均方误差（MSE）两种目标函数，用于从fNIRS信号重建感知词汇的语义。通过Bi-LSTM解码器，在三个共享范式的fNIRS数据集上使用GloVe-50和T5词嵌入作为目标。结果显示CLIP训练的Bi-LSTM性能最稳定，T5匹配分数更高，而显著检索结果均来自GloVe-50。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义重建将神经活动映射到词嵌入空间，可恢复感知词汇的含义而非从固定词表中选择。fNIRS含有适合此映射的语义信息，但多数fNIRS解码器使用平方误差目标，独立拟合每个词，忽略了嵌入空间的几何结构，因此需要更合适的目标函数。

Method: 评估基于CLIP损失的对比损失作为MSE的替代方案，用于从fNIRS重建感知词汇。训练Bi-LSTM解码器将fNIRS信号映射到词嵌入，使用GloVe-50和T5词嵌入作为目标，在三个共享范式（每个词图像与其口语名称配对）的fNIRS数据集上进行实验。

Result: 使用CLIP训练的Bi-LSTM在实验中是最一致的解码器。T5产生更高的匹配分数，而所有显著的检索结果均使用GloVe-50。

Conclusion: 结果支持将对比目标作为fNIRS语义解码的一个有前景的方向，并推动在更大数据集上进行验证。

Abstract: Semantic reconstruction maps neural activity to a word-embedding space, recovering the meaning of a perceived word instead of selecting it from a fixed vocabulary. Functional near-infrared spectroscopy (fNIRS) carries semantic information suitable for this mapping. However, most fNIRS decoders are trained with a squared-error objective that fits each word independently and ignores the geometry of the embedding space. To address this limitation, we evaluate a contrastive loss based on the contrastive-language-image-pretraining (CLIP) loss, as an alternative to mean-squared-error (MSE) for reconstructing perceived words from fNIRS. We compare the two objectives by training a bidirectional long short-term memory (Bi-LSTM) decoder to map fNIRS signals to word embeddings. We use GloVe-50 and T5 word embeddings as targets, across three fNIRS datasets recorded under a shared paradigm pairing each word image with its spoken name. Performance is measured with a pairwise matching score and open-vocabulary top-$k$ retrieval. The Bi-LSTM trained with CLIP is the most consistent decoder across experiments. T5 produces higher matching scores, whereas every significant retrieval result uses GloVe-50. These results support the use of contrastive objectives as a promising direction for fNIRS semantic decoding and motivate validation on larger datasets.

</details>


### [160] [SPEAR: Five Principles for Interactive Human-Agent Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07204)
*Tao Long,Lydia B. Chilton*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，主张将AI对齐从"部署前的优化问题"重新框架化为"持续的交互设计问题"，并提出由Specification、Process、Evaluation、Adaptation、Recalibration五个支柱组成的SPEAR框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI对齐研究通常把对齐视为部署前的优化问题（收集人类反馈、学习偏好或原则、微调模型、部署对齐系统）。这一框架虽取得重大进展，但未能充分刻画AI系统作为代理在情境化、长期性和社会性场景中代表用户行动后会发生什么。

Method: 这是一篇立场论文（position paper），采用概念重构与框架构建的方法。作者将对齐重新界定为人与代理之间持续进行的交互设计问题，并提出名为SPEAR的五支柱交互式对齐框架：Specification（人们如何表达意图并建立共同理解）、Process（代理如何决定何时行动、询问、推迟或暂停）、Evaluation（人们如何判断代理是否成功）、Adaptation（代理如何在重复使用中适应用户）、Recalibration（人们如何根据代理调整自身信任、期望与行为）。

Result: 论文并未报告实证结果，其主要产出是一个概念性重构与SPEAR五支柱框架，用于刻画长期、社会性交互情境下的对齐过程。

Conclusion: 作者认为，人类-代理对齐应被理解为持续的交互设计问题而非一次性的部署前优化问题。SPEAR框架的五个支柱共同为在情境化、长期性、社会性场景中实现并维持对齐提供了新的研究议程与分析视角。

Abstract: Recent AI alignment work often frames alignment as a pre-deployment optimization problem: collect human feedback, learn preferences or principles, finetune the model, and deploy an aligned system. This framing has produced major progress, but it under-specifies what happens once AI systems act as agents on users' behalf in situated, long-term, and social contexts. This position paper reframes human-agent alignment as an ongoing interaction design problem. We propose SPEAR, five pillars of interactive alignment: Specification (how people express intent and establish shared understanding), Process (how agents decide when to act, ask, defer, or pause), Evaluation (how people judge whether agents succeeded), Adaptation (how agents adapt to users over repeated use), and Recalibration (how people adapt their trust, expectations, and behavior in response to agents).

</details>


### [161] [Responsible Institutional Analytics: Interpreting Bias with AI Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07205)
*Francielle Marques,Ariel Ortiz-Beltrán,Ishari Amarasinghe,Davinia Hernández-Leo*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文提出FACTRIA框架，用于识别高校机构分析（IA）仪表盘中可能引入偏差的因素，并将其整合进生成式AI聊天机器人，以引导用户反思，从而提升对机构数据的负责任的解读。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机构分析（IA）仪表盘在高等教育决策中被广泛应用，但数据局限、分析技术约束以及缺失的情境信息往往会干扰其解读。为支持更负责任的IA解读，需要帮助用户意识到潜在的偏差因素并提供引导。

Method: 作者提出FACTRIA框架，将潜在偏差因素归纳为四个方面：分析流程、机构情境、课程层面特征以及人口统计特征。随后以该框架为基础，设计了一个生成式AI聊天机器人，在用户分析IA时提示其反思这些因素。研究采用定性研究方法，基于四个真实的IA案例与利益相关者开展研究，并使用转移网络分析（transition network analysis）进行数据分析。

Result: 转移网络分析显示，聊天机器人促使参与者意识到此前被忽视的因素如何影响了他们最初的解读。结果表明，将结构化框架与AI引导相结合，能够增强对机构数据进行情境感知和负责任解读的能力。

Conclusion: 将FACTRIA这样的结构化框架与生成式AI引导相结合，有助于提升高等教育机构分析中情境感知与负责任的解读水平。

Abstract: Institutional Analytics (IA) dashboards inform decision-making in higher education, yet data limitations, constraints in analytical techniques, and missing contextual information often affect their interpretation. To support more responsible interpretation of IA, we introduce FACTRIA, a framework that organizes potential biasing factors across four areas: the analytics pipeline, institutional context, course-level characteristics, and demographics. We used the FACTRIA framework as input to a generative-AI chatbot designed to prompt users to reflect on these factors while analyzing IA. A qualitative study with stakeholders, drawing on four authentic IA cases, and a transition network analysis showed that the chatbot prompted participants to recognize how overlooked factors influenced their initial interpretation. Findings indicated that combining a structured framework with AI-based guidance can enhance context-aware, responsible interpretation of institutional data.

</details>


### [162] [Mapping E-textiles Design Pain Points and Generative AI Opportunities: Insights from Workshops in Shanghai and Winchester](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07296)
*Zhuchenyang Liu,Nianchong Qu,Yao Zhang,Marie O'Mahony,Qi Wang,Yu Xiao*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过上海和温彻斯特两场联合设计工作坊（共20位从业者），研究者绘制了电子纺织品设计流程中的瓶颈，并将生成式AI的潜在角色对应到具体环节，指出核心障碍在于缺乏标准化、机器可读的设计表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子纺织品设计涉及材料、传感/执行结构、制造、服装集成与数据处理等多领域决策，通常需要反复原型迭代与测试，耗时耗力，且少有从业者具备跨学科专长。因此有必要识别当前设计流程的瓶颈，并探索生成式AI如何支持设计过程。

Method: 开展两场各半天的联合设计工作坊（上海、温彻斯特），参与者来自材料科学、电子、服装设计、人机交互与制造等领域。上海20人参加，其中10人有电子纺织品经验并构成分析样本；温彻斯特另有10人。参与者绘制各自的设计流程、标注瓶颈，并提出生成式AI可提供支持的环节。

Result: 两个工作坊的从业者一致识别出领域特定的操作障碍，包括数据稀缺、原型与制造之间的脱节，以及材料—硬件集成中的权衡取舍。研究者未给出机会排名清单，而是产出一份流程映射，把每个提议的GenAI角色对应到从业者所定位的流程阶段及其有用性所依赖的条件。他们强调，GenAI驱动电子纺织品设计的首要障碍并非通用模型能力，而是缺乏标准化、机器可读的电子纺织品设计表示。

Conclusion: 基于上述发现，研究者提出四类面向领域的AI工具，用以支持未来的电子纺织品设计流程。

Abstract: E-textile design involves complex decisions across materials, sensor and actuator structures, fabrication, garment integration, and data processing. It typically requires iterative prototyping and testing, which are time- and labour-intensive, while few practitioners possess cross-disciplinary expertise across all relevant domains. To identify current bottlenecks and explore how Generative AI (GenAI) might support the design process, we conducted two half-day co-design workshops, one in Shanghai and one in Winchester, with practitioners from materials science, electronics, garment design, human-computer interaction, and manufacturing. Twenty practitioners participated in the Shanghai workshop; ten of them had prior experience in e-textiles and form the contributing sample analysed here. A further ten practitioners participated in the Winchester workshop. Participants mapped their own design pipelines, annotated bottlenecks, and proposed where GenAI could provide support. Rather than presenting a ranked list of opportunities, we report a process map that indexes each proposed GenAI role to the pipeline stage at which practitioners located it, together with the conditions on which they stated its usefulness would depend. Across both sites, practitioners consistently identified domain-specific operational barriers, including data scarcity, the disconnect between prototyping and manufacturing, and trade-offs in material-hardware integration. They also emphasized that the primary barrier to GenAI-driven e-textile design is not general model capability, but the lack of standardized, machine-readable representations of e-textile designs. Based on these findings, we identify four classes of domain-tailored AI tools that could support future e-textile design processes.

</details>


### [163] [Quantum-Like Spatial Decision Dynamics: A Falsifiable Model of Cue-Order Effects in Immersive Navigation with Implications for Human-Quantum Computer Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07336)
*Aryabrata Basu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出"类量子空间决策动力学"(QSDD)框架，用完全正保迹映射、量子仪器和操作化测量刻画具身决策，并通过仿真设计分析验证其可证伪性与可判别性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单纯的顺序效应无法识别类量子认知结构，需要一个可证伪的状态空间模型来区分类量子机制与经典机制，并将沉浸式交互研究与人类-量子计算机交互联系起来。

Method: 将线索暴露建模为完全正保迹映射，中间判断为量子仪器，路线承诺为操作化定义的测量；构建一个固定决策基、非对易线索旋转、固定对称破缺初始方位角、退相干与失误的最小二元模型，五个参数须跨环境泛化。通过可复现仿真，在QSDD与七参数经典logistic真值下评估参数恢复与模型判别；并提供预注册的VR实验、遥测模式、经典对比集和失败判据。

Result: 每个设计单元40次重复：在每环境-顺序单元240个独立观测时，QSDD在85%的QSDD生成数据集中被留出环境优先选择，480个观测时达95%，但在所有样本量下经典生成数据集中均为0%；同时暴露失误参数识别较弱。

Conclusion: QSDD是一个受约束、可被数据推翻或支持的模型，其仿真仅为设计分析而非人类证据；该框架提供了从沉浸式交互研究通向人类-量子计算机交互的方法论连续路径。

Abstract: The sequence in which a person encounters spatial evidence can change a later choice, yet an order effect alone does not identify a quantum-like cognitive structure. We introduce Quantum-Like Spatial Decision Dynamics (QSDD), a falsifiable state-space account of embodied decision making in which cue exposures are completely positive trace-preserving maps, intermediate judgments are quantum instruments, and route commitment is an operationally defined measurement. We distinguish this formal claim from any assertion that cognition is microscopically quantum. We then specify a minimal binary model with a fixed decision basis, noncommuting cue rotations, a fixed symmetry-breaking initial azimuth, dephasing, and lapse; its five fitted parameters must generalize across environments rather than being refit to individual conditions. A reproducible simulation study evaluates recovery and model discrimination under both QSDD and seven-parameter classical logistic ground truths. Across 40 replicates per design cell, QSDD was preferred on held-out environments in 85% of QSDD-generated datasets at 240 independent observations per environment-order cell and 95% at 480, but in 0% of classically generated datasets at every tested sample size. The simulation also exposes weak identification of the lapse parameter and is explicitly a design analysis, not human evidence. We provide a preregistrable virtual-reality experiment, telemetry schema, classical comparison set, and failure criteria. Finally, we show how the same process-measurement logic can be transferred to human inspection and debugging of quantum circuits. QSDD is therefore offered as a constrained model to be defeated or supported by data, and as a methodologically continuous route from immersive interaction research to human-quantum computer interaction.

</details>


### [164] [Redistributing Harm: Document Transition and the Limits of Trans Inclusion in India's Identity Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07343)
*Megh Marathe,K Ranade,L. Ramakrishnan,Koyel Ghosh*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 基于2025年中期对16名跨性别者（多为跨男性及性别多元者）的焦点小组研究，本文考察印度跨性别者"证件过渡"（统一姓名、性别标记等信息）中的行政障碍与伤害，提出"trans tax"与"haunting"概念，并呼吁短期敏感化培训与长期系统性变革。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 身份证件是跨性别者与国家基础设施互动中最具后果的场域之一。作者希望填补信息研究与跨性别研究在分类、国家承认交叉领域的空白，揭示印度官方记录信息对齐过程中跨性别者遭遇的具体伤害机制。

Method: 2025年中期开展焦点小组研究，共16名跨性别参与者（多为跨男性与性别多元者），考察其在印度官方记录中统一姓名、性别标记及相关信息的"证件过渡"经历；并将发现置于信息研究与跨性别研究关于分类与国家承认的学术脉络中分析。

Result: 参与者遭遇缺乏明确程序、不断变化的证明要求及官员不当行为，根源在于系统与政策对跨性别性的理解有限，进而在生计、住房、投票、旅行、暴力与歧视救济及教育方面受到伤害。证件不匹配者承受作者称为"trans tax"的扭矩式行政暴力，包括过高税额扣除、雇主抵触与工作不稳定；而性别一致的证件反而可能重新分配伤害——行政上被识别为男性的参与者失去工作、福利与权利，同时仍面临相关项目本欲解决的歧视。性别一致数据对生活的扰乱要求重新审视跨性别性、分类与数据实践交叉处的"haunting"。

Conclusion: 文章呼吁短期内针对街头、系统与政策层面决策者开展关于跨性别身份与国家规定的敏感化工作，并建立、广泛传播成熟的证件过渡程序；长期则需广泛协调地革新系统与政策，以消除顺性别—异性恋规范预设。

Abstract: Identity documents (IDs) are some of the most consequential sites through which transgender people encounter state infrastructures. This article examines document transition, the work of aligning names, gender markers, and related information across official records in India based on focus groups with sixteen trans participants, most of whom were transmasculine and gender diverse, in mid-2025. Participants described encountering an absence of clear protocols, changing demands for proof, and objectionable conduct from officials, arising often from limited understandings of transness in systems and policies. As a result, participants faced harms related to livelihood, housing, voting, travel, redress from violence and discrimination, and education. We situate these findings within information studies and trans studies scholarship on classification and state recognition. Participants with mismatching IDs faced a form of torque or administrative violence that we call `trans tax' including disproportionate tax deductions, reluctant employers, and resultant insecure jobs. Further, gender-concordant IDs could redistribute harm: participants who became administratively legible as male lost jobs, benefits, and rights despite remaining vulnerable to the discrimination the programs sought to address. The disruption of people's lives by updated gender-concordant data calls for a re-examination of `haunting' at the intersection of transness, classification, and data practices. The article concludes by calling for sensitization efforts directed at street-, system-, and policy-level decision-makers about trans identities and state provisions together with established and widely disseminated protocols for document transition in the short term; as well as a broad and coordinated upheaval of systems and policies to undo cisgender-heteronormative assumptions.

</details>


### [165] [Knit-Structure Effects on Electromechanical Metrics and Their Correlation with Joint-Angle Estimation Error in Knitted Strain Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07416)
*Annika Eloranta,Zhuchenyang Liu,Iiro Naulapaa,Iida Arvola,Yao Zhang,Anna-Mari Leppisaari,Lulu Xu,Yu Xiao*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究考察针织结构与机器设置如何影响电阻式应变传感器的机电性能，并识别能预测弯曲传感性能的关键指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针织电阻应变传感器在运动与康复的关节运动传感中很有前景，但传感器设计与实际使用性能之间的联系尚不清晰。

Method: 制备覆盖七种常见针织结构、两种针距的传感器，进行单轴循环拉伸表征，并在模拟关节弯曲装置上评估；用机器学习模型评估关节角度估计，并分析其与机电指标的相关性。

Result: 六项常见指标中，应变系数和基线电阻主要由针织结构决定，而工作范围、线性范围、滞后和循环稳定性在各设计间差异较小；应变系数与关节角度估计误差负相关，基线电阻正相关，主要见于低灵敏度设计，其余指标预测价值弱或无。

Conclusion: 单轴拉伸测试可用于筛除弱设计，但识别最优设计仍需结合关节相关评估和应用特定指标。

Abstract: Knitted resistive strain sensors show strong promise for joint motion sensing in sports and rehabilitation, but the linkage between sensor design and in situ performance remains unclear. We investigate how knit structure and machine settings (e.g., stitch size) shape electromechanical properties and which metrics predict sensing performance during bending. Sensors spanning seven common knit structures at two stitch sizes were fabricated, characterized under uniaxial cyclic tension, and evaluated on a joint emulating bending rig. Joint-angle estimation was assessed with machine learning models, and correlations with electromechanical metrics were analyzed. Experimental results show that, among six common metrics, gauge factor and baseline resistance are largely set by knit structure, while working range, linear range, hysteresis, and cyclic stability vary only modestly across designs. Gauge factor correlates negatively and baseline resistance positively with joint-angle estimation error, mainly in lower-sensitivity designs, whereas the other metrics have weak or no predictive value. These results support using uniaxial tensile tests to screen out weak designs, while underscoring the need for joint-relevant evaluation and application-specific metrics to identify top performers.

</details>


### [166] [Artifact removal improves electrodermal waveforms but not downstream classification in a virtual-reality balance task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07438)
*Haochen Chai,Qixu Zhu,Siyao Li,Fangfang Jiang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究发现，在VR平衡干扰任务中，去除EDA信号伪迹并未提升分类性能，预处理应以决策效果而非波形指标来评判。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 领域惯例认为信号越干净，分类决策越好，因此伪迹去除常被默认前置。本文旨在验证这一假设是否成立，即清洁信号是否真正改善基于EDA的分类决策。

Method: 训练一个残差门控网络（residual gating network），在专家校正过EDA的基准数据集上训练后冻结，再应用于VR记录。原始与门控信号使用五种已发表的时间序列方法，在相同的留一参与者（leave-one-participant-out）评估下进行分类。

Result: 基准测试中门控检测伪迹效果良好（中位记录AUROC 0.94），伪迹区域误差降低17.8%。但在VR任务中未改善分类：平衡准确率变化为-1.35至+0.93个百分点，无一分类器提升，两个下降，五种方法均与原始输入等价（±3.32点内）。校正降低了波形误差的同时也使全部43条基准记录的SCR检测减少。处理使92.8%预测不变，改变的预测中修正与破坏比例相近。VR记录污染较少（约4.6%样本），即使完美定位注入伪迹，最敏感分类器也只恢复3.3点。伪迹水平与准确率的合并关联（11.3点）在参与者内消失（0.1点）。

Conclusion: 预处理应以它支持的决策来评判，并需与未处理对照组比较；波形层面的改善未必转化为决策收益。

Abstract: Artifact removal routinely precedes the classification of electrodermal activity (EDA), on the assumption that a cleaner signal supports a better decision. We tested this assumption in a virtual-reality (VR) balance-disturbance task. A residual gating network was trained on a benchmark with expert-corrected EDA, frozen, and applied to VR recordings, where raw and gated signals were classified by five published time-series methods under identical leave-one-participant-out evaluation. On the benchmark the gate detected artifacts well (median record AUROC 0.94) and reduced error inside artifact regions by 17.8%. In the VR task it did not improve classification. Changes in balanced accuracy ranged from -1.35 to +0.93 percentage points, no classifier improved and two lost accuracy, and all five were equivalent to raw input within +/- 3.32 points. The benefit was lost between waveform and decision. The correction that lowered waveform error also reduced skin conductance response detection in all 43 benchmark records. Processing left 92.8% of predictions unchanged, and the predictions it did change were corrected and corrupted at similar rates. The VR recordings also carried little contamination (an estimated 4.6% of samples), and even perfect localization of deliberately injected artifacts recovered only 3.3 points in the most sensitive classifier. A pooled association between artifact level and accuracy (11.3 points) disappeared within participants (0.1 points), showing how differences between people can make cleaning look useful. Preprocessing should be judged by the decision it supports, against an unprocessed arm.

</details>


### [167] [MRPilot: Supervising and Intervening LLM-Based Multi-Robot Teams through Mixed Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07477)
*Xiaoran Yang,Xun Qian,Yang Zhan,Nathan Tran,Ziyi Liu,Qiao Jin*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: MRPilot是一个混合现实系统，通过四个监督与干预阶段，帮助非专家用户理解、审查、监控和修复大语言模型驱动的多机器人系统任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽能让用户用自然语言指挥异构多机器人系统，但任务解释、机器人分配和协调过程难以被检查和修改，用户缺乏可监督性。

Method: 基于对12名非专家用户的形成性研究，开发了MRPilot混合现实系统，将机器人团队计划与执行状态表示为结构化承诺，在同步的情境视图与总览视图中共享，围绕Forming（消解歧义指代）、Reviewing（执行前审查计划）、Following（监控分布式执行）、Repairing（问题出现时进行机器人级或团队级调整）四个阶段提供支持。

Result: 在一项20名参与者、基于虚拟现实模拟家庭的被试内研究中，与使用相同LLM规划器和机器人能力的传统对话式界面相比，MRPilot降低了工作负荷，提升了态势感知、透明度、信任和感知控制。

Conclusion: 研究为基于LLM的多机器人系统中的多尺度干预、自适应监督和校准依赖提供了设计启示。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) let users direct heterogeneous multi-robot systems (MRS) through natural language, but make task interpretation, robot assignment, and coordination difficult to inspect and change. Based on a formative study with 12 non-expert users, we developed MRPilot, a mixed reality system organized around four stages of supervision and intervention. MRPilot represents robot-team plans and execution states as structured commitments shared across synchronized situated and overview views. Across four stages, it helps users resolve ambiguous references (Forming), review plans before execution (Reviewing), monitor distributed execution (Following), and make robot-level or team-level changes when problems arise (Repairing). In a within-subjects study with 20 participants in a virtual reality-simulated home, MRPilot reduced workload, increased situational awareness, transparency, trust, and perceived control compared with a conventional LLM-based conversational interface using the same LLM planner and robot capabilities. We provide design implications for multi-scale intervention, adaptive supervision, and calibrated reliance in LLM-based MRS.

</details>


### [168] [From Written Response to Dialogue with AI: How Activity Format, Interaction Modality, and Language Impact Student Learning and Engagement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07483)
*Deepak Varuvel Dennison,Connie Zhang,Rene Kizilcec,Aditya Vashistha*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 一项在印度卡纳塔克邦305名以卡纳达语为母语的本科生中进行的实地研究，比较了写作活动与AI文本/语音对话活动，以及纯英语与双语（卡纳达语-英语）设置的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）的普及使学生能轻松生成答案而无需真正投入学习内容，对传统写作类学习活动构成挑战。对话式AI为将学习活动重构为对话提供了机会，而多语言能力可能帮助非母语学习者更好地参与其中。

Method: 在印度卡纳塔克邦英语授课高校中，选取305名以卡纳达语为母语的本科生开展实地研究。对比写作回答活动与AI文本及语音对话活动，且每种形式分别在纯英语或卡纳达语-英语双语环境下进行。

Result: 参与对话活动的学生在活动上花费更多时间、贡献更多内容，并报告更高的兴趣和自我效能感，但完成对话活动的学生总数较少。所有条件下的知识均有提升，不同活动形式或语言之间未发现可靠的增益差异。语言对参与度的影响因模态而异：双语互动在语音对话中尤为有利，可减少表达困难、增加体现理解的轮次并降低对话中断率。同时，学生也重视英语，因其与学术和职业志向相关。

Conclusion: 设计AI对话式学习不仅仅是在写作与对话、语音与文本、英语与母语之间做选择。研究强调应让学习者对模态、信息和节奏拥有更大控制权，并将母语作为跨语言支持（translanguaging）而非英语的替代品。

Abstract: The widespread availability of LLMs is challenging written learning activities, as students can increasingly generate responses without necessarily engaging with the learning content. Conversational AI creates an opportunity to redesign these activities as dialogue, while multilingual capabilities may make such dialogue more accessible to students learning through a non-native language. We conducted a field study with 305 native Kannada-speaking undergraduate students at English-medium institutions in Karnataka, India. We compared written response activities with text- and voice-based dialogic activities with AI, each conducted in English-only or bilingual Kannada-English settings. Students who completed dialogic activities spent more time on the activities, contributed more, and reported greater interest and self-efficacy than those completing written responses, although fewer students completed the dialogic activities overall. Knowledge increased across all conditions, with no reliable differences in gains between activity formats or languages. Language shaped participation differently across modalities: bilingual interaction was particularly beneficial in voice dialogue, where it reduced articulation difficulties, increased turns demonstrating understanding, and reduced conversation abandonment. However, students also valued English because of its connection to their academic and professional aspirations. These findings show that designing dialogic learning with AI requires more than choosing between writing and dialogue, voice and text, or English and students' native languages. We highlight opportunities to give learners greater control over modality, information, and pace, and to use native languages as translanguaging support rather than as a replacement for English.

</details>


### [169] [Jarvis: A Proactive Speech Agent for Multi-Party Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07506)
*Seunghyun Oh,Hirotaka Hiraki,Shuyue Stella Li,Yulia Tsvetkov,Shyamnath Gollakota*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Jarvis——一个能在多人实时对话中主动、恰当地插话的语音智能体，基于事先共享的文档跟踪讨论，仅在群体遗漏或说错事实且未自行纠正时出声干预，并给出证据引用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音智能体多为被动、双人交互模式（被叫才回应，且一次只对一人）。论文希望让语音智能体真正参与多人对话，并只在能够提供帮助时主动发言。

Method: 提出三项贡献：1）以"认知性失误"为基础的问题设定，并构建可控的多方对话合成数据集 CHI-180-proactive（内含已知的知识缺口、错误与自我纠正）；2）主动干预骨干，采用小型开源模型结合确定性检查，并将每条论断溯源到源文档句子；3）在实时语音中夺取发言权并在屏幕上展示引用证据的交互技术。

Result: 在 CHI-180-proactive 上，Jarvis 所处理的事件多数判断正确；当群体自行解决问题时，其保持沉默的比例达 97%。23 名参与者的实地研究在实时干预下验证了上述趋势。

Conclusion: Jarvis 展示了语音智能体从被动双人交互转向多人主动参与对话的可行性，能够在合适时机基于文档证据进行干预，并在群体已自行纠正时保持沉默。

Abstract: Speech agents are reactive and dyadic: they speak when spoken to, and to one person at a time. We ask what it takes for a speech agent to instead take part in a conversation among several people and speak up only when it can help. We introduce Jarvis, a real-time proactive speech agent that audibly participates in multi-party human conversations. Grounded in a document shared beforehand, Jarvis follows the discussion and intervenes when the group misses or misstates a fact and does not correct itself within a few turns. We make three contributions: a problem setting based on epistemic breakdowns that makes proactive intervention measurable, realized as CHI-180-proactive, a synthetic multi-party dataset seeded with known gaps, errors, and self-corrections; a proactive backbone that harnesses a small, open-weight model with deterministic checks and grounds every claim in a source sentence; and interaction techniques for taking the floor in live speech and showing the cited evidence on screen. On CHI-180-proactive, Jarvis is correct on most events it addresses and stays silent 97% of the time when the group resolves an issue itself. A live study with 23 participants confirms these trends with real-time interventions.

</details>


### [170] [The Relationship Between Blood Pressure and Self-Reported Stress During Sound-Based VR Relaxation Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07507)
*Md Alamin Hossain,M. Rasel Mahmud*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过VR放松视频实验发现，舒张压（DIA）比收缩压（SYS）更能反映主观压力水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VR放松环境被日益用于压力管理，但VR暴露期间自我报告压力与生理指标（尤其血压）的关联尚不明确，需要厘清二者关系以支持VR心理健康干预的设计与评估。

Method: 采用被试内设计（N=18），参与者通过HTC Vive Focus Vision头显观看360度放松视频，视频按声音设计分为四类：节奏型（RHYT）、非周期型（APRD）、连续型（CONT）和低环境音型（LOWE）。每类后使用Omron 3 Series血压计记录收缩压与舒张压，并通过0-100视觉模拟量表（VAS）在固定间隔收集自我报告压力。

Result: 整体血压在不同声音类别间无显著差异；但自我报告压力存在显著差异（Friedman χ²(4)=12.07, p=.017），节奏型声音的压力显著低于非周期型和连续型声音。关键在于，舒张压与自我报告压力在各条件下显著相关（r=.31, p=.003），而收缩压无此相关。

Conclusion: 在短时VR暴露中，舒张压可能比收缩压更能敏感地反映主观压力。研究讨论了这对VR心理健康干预设计与评估的启示，以及低成本生理传感与自我报告结合的价值。

Abstract: Virtual reality (VR) relaxation environments are increasingly proposed as accessible tools for stress management, yet the physiological correlates of self-reported stress during VR exposure remain unclear, particularly for blood pressure (BP). We conducted a within-subjects study (N = 18) in which participants viewed relaxing 360-degree videos, grouped into four sound-design categories, such as videos with rhythmic (RHYT), aperiodic (APRD), continuous (CONT), and low-ambient (LOWE) sound through an HTC Vive Focus Vision headset. Systolic (SYS) and diastolic (DIA) BP were recorded after each category using an Omron 3 Series monitor, and self-reported stress was collected via a 0-100 Visual Analogue Scale (VAS) with fixed intervals. While overall BP did not differ significantly across sound categories, self-reported stress did (Friedman chi-square(4) = 12.07, p = .017), with rhythmic sound eliciting significantly lower stress than aperiodic and continuous sound. Critically, DIA, but not SYS, correlated significantly with self-reported stress across conditions (r = .31, p = .003). This suggests that DIA may be a more sensitive physiological indicator of subjective stress than SYS in short VR exposures. We discuss implications for designing and evaluating VR mental health interventions and the value of low-cost physiological sensing alongside self-report.

</details>


### [171] [PAIR: Perceptual Affective Inference and Regulation in a Real-Time Multimodal Conversational Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07523)
*Kexin Quan,Zijian Ding,Jiaye Yong,Qinshi Zhang,Dong Wang,Jessie Chin*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出实时多模态情感智能体PAIR，通过评估支架推断效价-唤醒-支配度并生成调节指导，在14天19人部署中实现效价MAE 1.20，并揭示感知理解与实际预测误差的脱钩及两周内帮助性随时间提升的规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续的情感支持要求生成式智能体把瞬时情绪推断与调节，同跨次会话的连续性连接起来。现有系统往往缺乏对"事件如何被评价为情绪状态"的重构，也缺少跨会话的记忆与调节闭环。

Method: 提出PAIR（Perceptual Affective Inference and Regulation），一个实时多模态智能体。以评估支架（appraisal scaffolds）生成效价-唤醒-支配度（VAD）估计并选择调节指导，通过协调的语音、颜色与虚拟形象线索在对话中传递；滚动记忆跨会话携带上下文，指导后重新运行评估支架。14天部署，19名参与者，1,093次会话，每次配对指导前估计、指导后估计与非锚定自我报告。

Result: 初始效价在9点SAM量表上达到MAE 1.20（r=.68），支配度MAE 1.30，唤醒度即使粗化后一致性仍较弱。自我报告的情绪变化随初始状态变化，负效价起始的会话效价增幅最大。感知理解与效价增加正相关，但与数值预测误差几乎无对应。两周内帮助性上升而输入变短；访谈将个性化与陪伴感追溯到相关回忆、上下文更新与熟悉对话。

Conclusion: 研究通过逐事件、第一人称的评估，将推断准确性与对话及时间维度的支持模式联系起来，说明情感智能体的支持质量不仅取决于预测精度，还取决于记忆连续性、感知理解与对话熟悉度。

Abstract: Sustained emotional support requires generative agents to connect momentary emotion inference and regulation with continuity across encounters. We present PAIR (Perceptual Affective Inference and Regulation), a real-time multimodal agent that reconstructs how an event is appraised into an emotional state. Appraisal scaffolds produce a valence-arousal-dominance estimate and select regulation guidance, delivered through conversation with coordinated speech, color, and avatar cues. Rolling memory carries context across sessions, and the scaffold re-runs after guidance. In a 14-day deployment with 19 participants, 1,093 sessions paired initial and post-guidance estimates with unanchored self-reports. Initial valence reached MAE 1.20 on the 9-point SAM scale (r=.68), dominance reached MAE 1.30, and arousal showed weak agreement even after coarsening. Self-reported emotional change varied with initial state, with the largest valence increases in sessions that began at negative valence. Perceived understanding was associated with greater valence increase and showed little correspondence with numerical prediction error. Over two weeks, helpfulness increased while input shortened; interviews traced personalization and companionship to relevant recall, context updates, and familiar dialogue. These findings connect inference accuracy to conversational and temporal patterns of support through per-event, first-person evaluation.

</details>


### [172] [Emoception: Selective Affective Layer Fine-Tuning of Video Vision Transformers for Player Arousal Change Recognition From Gameplay Footage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07603)
