*Yi Xia,Ibrahim Khan,Mury Fajar Dewantoro,Wenwen Ouyang,Ruck Thawonmas*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出SALFT，一种面向游戏玩家唤醒度识别的视频ViT高效微调框架，仅更新约8%参数即可达到接近全量微调的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频视觉Transformer的全量微调计算成本高昂，需要一种高效的自适应微调方法，在保持性能的同时大幅减少参数量更新。

Method: 提出选择性情感层微调（SALFT），基于短暂适应后层参数L2范数变化的选择准则，直接度量表征偏移，比基于梯度的方法更稳定；并在AVGAN数据集上用五折交叉验证评估，还引入可解释性方法追踪注意力模式。

Result: SALFT在所有游戏上性能与全量微调相当且无统计显著退化（p>0.05），仅更新约8%参数（减少超92%）；在某一游戏中一致优于全量微调和最佳基线（p=0.0625，精确双侧Wilcoxon符号秩检验）。

Conclusion: SALFT是一种有效且高效的情感游戏计算方法，兼顾性能、效率与透明性。

Abstract: This article proposes Selective Affective Layer Fine-Tuning (SALFT), an efficient adaptation framework for Video Vision Transformers in player arousal recognition from gameplay. To bypass computationally expensive full fine-tuning, SALFT introduces a selection criterion based on the L2-norm change in layer parameters after brief adaptation, directly measuring representational shifts and providing a more stable basis than gradient-based alternatives. Evaluated via five-fold cross-validation on the Arousal Video Game AnnotatIoN dataset, SALFT achieves performance comparable to full fine-tuning across all games without statistically significant degradation ($p>0.05$), while updating only $\approx$8% of parameters (over 92% reduction). Notably, in one game, SALFT consistently outperforms both full fine-tuning and the best baseline across all metrics and folds, reaching the theoretical minimum p-value (p=0.0625, exact two-sided Wilcoxon signed-rank test). In addition, we introduce an interpretability method to trace attention patterns, enhancing model transparency. These results establish SALFT as an effective and efficient approach for affective game computing.

</details>


### [173] [Beyond screen time: Explaining cross-national differences in digital literacy through socioeconomic and psychological mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07619)
*Hyejeong Lee,Daeyoung Ham,Suyoun Kim,Tiffany Emanuel*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究基于ICILS 2023数据，采用多组结构方程模型，揭示屏幕时间与数字素养关系存在跨国差异的结构性机制，发现ICT自我效能感是跨国一致的稳健预测因子，而屏幕时间管控的作用因国而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究和ICILS等大规模测评显示屏幕时间与数字能力之间的关系不一致，但其背后的机制尚不清楚，因此需要一个结构性解释来说明跨国差异的来源。

Method: 使用ICILS 2023数据，采用多组结构方程模型（multigroup SEM），考察社会经济地位、屏幕时间管控、ICT自我效能感与数字素养结果之间的关系。

Result: 屏幕时间管控的效应在各国之间差异显著；ICT自我效能感在所有国家都是稳定且稳健的预测因子；屏幕时间管控在部分情境下通过自我效能感间接影响结果，而在另一些情境下则不存在该间接路径。

Conclusion: 研究挑战了将屏幕时间作为数字参与单一指标的做法的合理性，强调心理机制的重要性；通过整合社会经济、行为与心理因素，深化了对数字能力的理解，并推动超越基于数量取向的数字学习研究。

Abstract: This study provides a structural explanation for cross-national variation in the relationship between screen time and digital outcomes. While prior research and large-scale assessments such as ICILS have documented inconsistent associations between screen time and digital competence, the mechanisms underlying these differences remain unclear. Using ICILS 2023 data, this study employs multigroup structural equation modeling to examine the relationships among socioeconomic status, screen time regulation, ICT self-efficacy, and digital literacy outcomes. Results reveal substantial cross-country differences in the effects of screen time regulation. In contrast, ICT self-efficacy emerges as a consistent and robust predictor across all countries. Moreover, screen time regulation influences outcomes indirectly through self-efficacy in some contexts but not others. These findings challenge the use of screen time as a standalone indicator of digital engagement and highlight the importance of psychological mechanisms. By integrating socioeconomic, behavioral, and psychological factors, this study advances a more nuanced understanding of digital competence and moves beyond quantity-based approaches to digital learning.

</details>


### [174] [When the Commons Appropriates a Large Language Model: How WikiVault Reshaped Korean Wikipedia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07660)
*Inhwa Song,Sohyeon Hwang,Ted Yoo,Manoel Horta Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究以韩语维基百科社区自主采用并适配的LLM编辑工具WikiVault为案例，揭示社区自行采纳LLM如何重新平衡知识公地的生产与质量保障工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM打破了知识公地中内容生产与质量保障之间的平衡，许多社区因此禁止或限制其使用。作者关注一个被忽视的替代路径：当社区主动将LLM工具据为己用、融入自身治理时会发生什么。

Method: 以韩语维基百科社区内部开发、主要用于翻译的LLM编辑工具WikiVault为对象，结合十次访谈、平台规模分析与匹配准实验比较，考察其对知识生产的影响。

Result: 工具显著放大了少数资深编辑的生产能力，产出更长且浏览量更高的条目；工作重心转向条目审阅与内容导入；不仅引入英文维基百科的内容，还引入了其编辑判断。

Conclusion: LLM的采纳可以重新平衡支撑知识公地的相互依赖型工作，同时说明社区能够通过"在使用中学习"新兴技术并相应调整其治理方式。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have disrupted the balance between content production and quality assurance that sustains knowledge commons, leading many to prohibit or restrict their use. But what happens when a community instead appropriates an LLM-powered tool for its own needs? We investigate this question through WikiVault, an LLM-powered editing tool developed within the Korean Wikipedia community and used primarily for translation. Combining ten interviews, platform-scale analyses, and matched quasi-experimental comparisons, we examine how WikiVault reshaped knowledge production on Korean Wikipedia. We find the tool 1) drastically amplified the production capacity of a small group of experienced editors, producing longer and more widely viewed articles; 2) shifted work toward reviewing articles and importing content; 3) imported not only content but also editorial judgments from English Wikipedia. Our findings show how LLM adoption can rebalance the interdependent work that sustains knowledge commons, while illustrating how communities can learn from emerging technologies through use and adapt their governance accordingly.

</details>


### [175] [SENSE: State-aware Emotion Navigation Storytelling Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07666)
*Yi Xia,Pablo Carrasco Velo,Mudit Paliwal,Ibrahim Khan,Yifan Geng,Mustafa Can Gursesli,Juho Hamari,Ruck Thawonmas*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出SENSE——一个面向可玩分支视觉小说的状态感知生成框架，支持多轨情感导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分支视觉小说的生成需要同时兼顾结构连贯性、情感丰富度、角色一致性与叙事因果性，而现有方法难以在最小化的高层输入下生成多条交叉路线并稳健整合视觉资产。

Method: SENSE整合了三个模块：基于状态的叙事架构MIND、一个结构分析器，以及一个路径感知的上下文管理模块。系统从少量高层输入出发，生成多条相互交叉的叙事路线。

Result: 基于LLM评判、情感指标与视觉评估的实验表明，SENSE在叙事多样性与资产生成整合的稳健性上优于基线方法；初步人类试玩显示情感保真度呈方向性改善，同时趣味性相当。

Conclusion: SENSE能够在保持角色一致性与叙事因果性的同时，生成结构连贯且情感丰富的可玩分支视觉小说，整体优于基线并在情感保真度上展现改进潜力。

Abstract: This paper presents SENSE, a state-aware framework for generating playable branching visual novels with multi-track emotional navigation. Integrating a state-based narrative architecture called MIND, a structure analyzer, and a path-aware context management module, SENSE produces narratives that are both structurally coherent and emotionally rich. From minimal high-level inputs, it generates multiple intersecting routes while preserving character consistency and narrative causality. Evaluations using LLM judges, affective metrics, and visual assessments indicate SENSE outperforms baselines in narrative diversity and robust asset integration, while preliminary human trials show directional improvements in emotional fidelity alongside comparable enjoyment.

</details>


### [176] [Evaluating human-AI workflows for field research in viticulture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07669)
*Niko Carvajal Janke,Daoyuan Jin,Shivranjani Baruah,Nicholas Gunner,Jacob Maus,Yu Jiang,Kaitlin M. Gold*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究评估了两种人机交互工作流在加州葡萄园精准病害治理中的价值：多智能体系统Aleks在145分钟内建成藤级预测模型，将红叶片优先采样命中率由85.8%提升至94.1%，但存在过采样导致性能估计不可靠、缺失植株替换指示等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在检验人机交互在真实项目约束下能否有效推进田间研究目标，具体评估商业巡查记录与遥感数据能否支持红叶片症状预测，以实现优先巡查与病毒检测，并比较AI规划与人工规划在采样设计上的差异。

Method: 在加州葡萄园140公顷范围内，利用2021-2024年商业巡查记录与遥感测量数据，由多智能体研究系统Aleks v1在人工迭代修正下开发预测模型。工作流1将2024年藤级模型应用于2025年预测变量，并与独立巡查结果比对，通过回顾性模拟评估行优先级排序对自适应巡查的影响；工作流2测试高模型得分藤是否更易检出病毒，并比较Aleks与人工采样方案。

Result: 模拟巡查45%藤位时，加入模型引导的行优先级使新记录红叶片观测命中比例从85.8%升至94.1%，模型主要改善区块间巡查分配。Aleks 145分钟内建成有效藤级模型，但合成过采样先于训练/测试划分导致内部性能估计不可靠。工作流2中，Aleks方案与人工方案分别采样41/50（82%）和97/100（97%）藤株，Aleks方案缺少缺失植株替换指示，限制其可操作性。137株样本中5株检出葡萄红斑点病毒，模型得分ROC AUC为0.735。

Conclusion: 评估AI交互应关注其在真实、项目特定约束下推进田间研究目标的能力。AI可在效率与预测价值上提供支持，但需人工监督以修正方法缺陷、补充操作细节并确保可实施性。

Abstract: We assessed the value of two live human-AI interactions in a precision disease control project in California vineyards. The project tested whether 2021-2024 commercial scouting records and remote-sensing measurements across 140 hectares could support 2025 red-leaf symptom forecasting for prioritized scouting and virus testing. In Workflow 1, Aleks v1, a multi-agent research system, developed forecasting models with iterative human refinement. We applied Aleks's 2024 vine-scale model to updated 2025 predictors and evaluated red-leaf forecasts against independent 2025 scouting. In retrospective simulations surveying 45% of all vine positions, adding model-informed row prioritization to adaptive scouting increased the encountered proportion of newly recorded red-leaf observations from 85.8% to 94.1%. Within-block scouting comparisons suggested the model mainly improved scouting allocation among blocks. Despite unreliable internal 2024 performance estimates from synthetic oversampling before train/test splitting, Aleks developed an informative vine-scale model in 145 minutes, increasing throughput and answering our research questions. In Workflow 2, we assessed whether higher model-score vines had more frequent virus detection, and whether Aleks could infer this sampling goal from a general prompt with data and literature. Aleks's plan prioritized balanced vineyard and model score coverage, while our plan prioritized field efficiency and high-model-score oversampling. Aleks's and our plans yielded 41/50 (82%) and 97/100 (97%) sampled vines. Aleks's plan omitted instructions for replacing missing vines, limiting implementation and operational value. Five of 137 sampled vines tested positive for grapevine red blotch virus (model score ROC AUC 0.735). These findings support assessing AI interactions by how well they advance field research objectives under live, project-specific constraints.

</details>


### [177] [Who Is Talking to the Agent? LLMs in Multi-User 3D Virtual Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07732)
*Mohammad Al-Ratrout,Shayla Sharmin,Roghayeh Leila Barmaki*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 构建 LookAway 数据集研究多用户 3D 虚拟空间中 LLM 智能体的"是否被呼叫"判断与用户画像使用问题，发现空间朝向线索大幅提升但仍会误导模型，且更丰富的画像上下文会引发过度分享。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多人共享 3D 虚拟房间时，LLM 智能体不仅要知道说什么，还要判断某句话是否针对自己，以及可以使用哪些他人的画像信息。这两个问题此前缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 构建 LookAway 受控语料库：40 个会话、80 个不同人设、1 个 LLM 智能体，共 1200 轮对话，包含说话人朝向与真实受话人一致或冲突的歧义轮次。在 3 个开放权重模型、5 种条件下（变化可见画像与是否告知朝向）进行 18000 次决策测试，并搭建浏览器 3D 演示器。

Result: 加入说话人朝向信息后，朝向一致时受话人判断准确率从 56% 提升至 99.5%；但当说话人朝向他人时，两个模型在超过 85% 的这类轮次中仍依据朝向判断。提前警告朝向可能误导仅带来有限改善。同时提供双方画像提升了对被询问者的回答质量，但也增加了未在共享对话中透露的画像属性使用，某模型达到 45.3%。

Conclusion: 更多上下文改善了多用户交互，但也导致过度分享。共享 LLM 智能体需要具备权衡空间线索与控制在何时引入用户特定信息的机制。

Abstract: When several people share a 3D virtual room with an LLM agent, the agent must decide not only what to say, but whether an utterance was addressed to it and, if accessible, what profile information about the others present it may use. To study both problems, we construct LookAway, a controlled corpus of 40 sessions involving 80 distinct personas and an LLM agent (1,200 turns), including ambiguous-addressee turns in which speaker orientation agrees or conflicts with the intended addressee. Across three open-weight large language models and five conditions varying which profiles the agent sees and whether it is told where each person faces (18,000 decisions), adding speaker orientation increased addressee accuracy from 56% to 99.5% when orientation was congruent, but when the speaker faced someone other than the addressee, two of the models went by where the speaker faced on more than 85% of those turns. Warning one model that orientation could be misleading reduced this only modestly. A browser-based 3D demonstrator shows the effect live: the same sentence gets an answer when the speaker faces the agent and silence when they face the other person. Providing both personas' profiles improved responses about the person being asked about, but also increased the use of profile attributes not revealed in the shared conversation, reaching 45.3% of answers for one model. More context thus improves multi-user interaction but also leads to oversharing, so shared LLM agents need mechanisms for weighing spatial cues and controlling when user-specific information enters a response.

</details>


### [178] [A Pedagogically Demonstrative Model Visualizing the Pathway from Online Interactions to Personalized Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07744)
*Sushmita Khan,Connor Pennington,Bart P Knijnenburg*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文开发并评估了一个教育性工具，通过本地大模型和三层桑基图直观模拟AI如何将用户数字活动转化为个性化推荐，帮助用户理解数据与推荐之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户普遍不了解原始交互数据如何被转化为个性化推荐，缺乏相关教育，因此需要提升用户对AI个性化机制的理解与隐私意识。

Method: 开发一款教育性工具，使用本地托管的大语言模型处理用户数字活动，生成推断兴趣画像和个性化推荐；通过三层桑基图展示数据源、推断兴趣与推荐之间的映射，并提供交互式过滤器供用户探索不同数据源组合的影响。随后对6名年轻成年人开展试点出声思维研究。

Result: 该工具有效地让参与者理解了数字活动与个性化推荐之间的概念关系，并促使他们产生了隐私意识；但参与者预计自身行为改变有限，因为他们认为参与平台是不可避免的。

Conclusion: 该教育性工具在教学层面效果良好，能提升用户对个性化机制的理解和隐私意识，但仅靠认知提升不足以促成行为改变，平台参与的不可避免性感知是主要障碍。

Abstract: Personal digital activity increasingly shapes online experiences, yet few users have been educated regarding the processes transforming raw interactions into personalized suggestions. We developed an education artifact that illustratively simulates how AI leverages users' digital activities to shape online recommendations (e.g., ads). Our artifact processes users' digital activity using a locally-hosted LLM to generate user profiles of their inferred interests and personalized recommendations. A three-layered Sankey diagram maps data sources through inferred interests to personalized recommendations. Interactive filters enable users to explore how different combinations of data sources influence personalized outcomes. This paper describes the artifact and its educational value, and reports findings of a pilot think-aloud study with six young adults. We find that the artifact effectively taught participants the conceptual relationship between digital activities and personalized recommendations. While this did lead participants to develop privacy awareness, they anticipated minimal behavior change due to the perceived unavoidability of platform participation.

</details>


### [179] [Novice Reliance Calibration in AI-Assisted Decision Making: The Role of Explanations and Self-Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07800)
*Eun Jeong Kang,Peter,Duan,Swati Mishra*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究提出"依赖校准"概念，考察在缺乏即时反馈场景下，新手用户如何调整对AI的依赖，以及AI解释与元认知自我评估的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI工具广泛应用于无法获得即时性能反馈的决策任务，用户难以通过试错学习来调整依赖行为。然而，关于新手用户在无外部反馈时如何校准对AI的依赖，以及AI解释能否在反馈缺失时支持校准，现有研究知之甚少。

Method: 采用组间实验设计，110名参与者完成带AI辅助、反馈有限的临床实体抽取任务；引入"依赖校准"作为组织性构念，考察AI解释与元认知自我评估对依赖行为动态调整的影响。

Result: 新手用户在存在解释时出现系统性的"过度依赖漂移"；而自我报告的任务理解程度越高，依赖行为越具选择性。

Conclusion: 研究将依赖校准研究拓展至缺乏实时性能信号的人机协作情境，并提出了设计此类AI工具以支持适当依赖的可操作指南。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) tools are widely used to support decision making in tasks and domains where no immediate performance feedback is available. In these settings, users cannot learn to adjust their reliance behavior over time through trial and error. However, little is known about how novice users calibrate reliance on AI when external feedback is unavailable, or whether AI explanations can support calibration in its absence. We introduce reliance calibration as an organizing construct for studying how novice users dynamically adjust reliance behavior, and examine how AI explanations and meta-cognitive self-assessment shape it. Through a between-subjects study with 110 participants completing a clinical entity extraction task with AI assistance and limited performance feedback, we observe that novice users exhibit systematic drift toward over-reliance in the presence of explanations, while higher self-reported task understanding is associated with more selective reliance behavior. These results extend reliance calibration research into human-AI collaboration contexts without real-time performance signals and present actionable guidelines on designing AI tools that must support appropriate reliance in these settings.

</details>


### [180] [Frontstage Mediation Work: Invisible Work Bridging Gaps Between AI Decisions and User Expectations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08067)
*Yongjae Sohn,Daehyun Kwak,Jiyeon Amy Seo,Hyungjun Cho,Seongah Youn,Youn-kyung Lim*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文提出"前台中介工作"（Frontstage Mediation Work）这一分析视角，通过对网约拼车服务的定性案例研究，揭示一线工作者如何弥合自动化决策与用户期望之间的错位，以及这种隐形劳动如何被系统所忽视。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化服务系统越来越多地生成影响服务交付的算法运营决策，但这些决策必须经由一线工作者在真实场景中传递给最终用户。在此过程中，自动化决策可能与用户期望产生偏差，在服务接触点（service encounter）显现为摩擦。然而，一线工作者为管理这些错位所付出的劳动往往不可见，且在现有研究中缺乏分析框架。

Method: 采用定性案例研究方法，以一项网约拼车（On-Demand Ride-Pooling）服务为研究对象，考察司机在服务接触点应对算法决策与用户期望错位时的实际做法。

Result: 识别出司机在摩擦出现时维持服务接触点的四种反复出现的实践，并发现这类劳动被吸收进日常运营之中，在绩效指标、系统日志或正式岗位描述中均未留下痕迹。

Conclusion: 论文通过揭示自动化服务如何将管理系统级决策互动后果的责任转嫁给一线工作者，为以工作者为中心的HCI研究做出了贡献。"前台中介工作"为分析这种隐形劳动提供了初步的分析视角。

Abstract: Automated service systems increasingly generate algorithmic operational decisions that shape how services are delivered. However, these decisions reach end-users only through frontline workers who carry them out in real-world settings. During this process, automated decisions can diverge from user expectations, surfacing as friction at the service encounter. We propose Frontstage Mediation Work as a preliminary analytic lens for examining the often invisible labor through which frontline workers anticipate and manage such misalignments between algorithmic decisions and user expectations. Drawing on a qualitative case study of an On-Demand Ride-Pooling service, we identify four recurring practices through which drivers sustain the service encounter when frictions arise. Such labor remains absorbed into routine operations, leaving no trace in performance metrics, system logs, or formal job descriptions. This paper contributes to worker-centered HCI scholarship by illustrating how automated services shift onto frontline workers the responsibility of managing the interactional consequences of system-level decisions.

</details>


### [181] [Building A Civic Tool for Community-Police Engagement to Adapt Neighborhood Policing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08212)
*Ravinithesh Reddy Annapureddy,Staņislavs Šeiko,Natalie Higham-James,William Droz,Alessandro Fornaroli,Sarah Vollmer,Britta Elena Hecking,Daniel Gatica-Perez*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文通过Research through Design (RtD)方法，开发了一个名为Par drošu Rīgu的公民工具，用于将社区数据与警务事件数据整合，以改善里加市警察与居民之间的信任与互动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动的警务往往优先考虑事件记录，而忽视居民的生活体验。在里加这样存在不信任历史和社区-警察互动有限的城市，这可能进一步疏远公众。研究旨在弥合这一差距。

Method: 采用Research through Design (RtD)探究，与市政警察、NGO和城市工作人员合作。共同设计过程包括：揭示不同安全观念的工作坊；作为边界对象进行利益相关者协商的物质探针；以及展示定量与定性数据结合如何重塑参与和信任的试点部署。采用混合方法评估。

Result: 混合方法评估表明警民互动有所增加，但在维持利益相关者合作方面存在摩擦。

Conclusion: 论文贡献包括：(i) 与公共机构进行的实证RtD探究；(ii) 结合物理和仪表板交互的人工制品；(iii) 关于交互设计作为跨越边界的实践，用于信任和基础设施化的反思。

Abstract: Data-driven policing often prioritizes incident records over residents' lived experiences. In the Baltic city of Riga, with a history of distrust and limited community-police engagement, this can further alienate the public. To bridge this gap, we propose a Research through Design (RtD) inquiry into the development of Par drošu Rīgu, a civic tool for community-data-integrated policing. With municipal police, NGOs, and city staff, we ask how RtD enables stakeholder negotiation and which interaction qualities support trust and the use of combined community and incident data. The co-design process included workshops that surfaced divergent notions of safety; material probes designed as boundary objects to negotiate among stakeholders; and a pilot deployment showing how combining quantitative and qualitative data reshapes engagement and trust. Mixed-methods evaluation suggests increased officer-citizen interaction, but frictions in sustaining stakeholder collaboration. We contribute (i) an empirical RtD inquiry with public institutions, (ii) an artifact combining physical and dashboard interactions, and (iii) reflections on interaction design as a boundary-spanning practice for trust and infrastructuring.

</details>


### [182] [Hugging Suit: Pneumatically-Actuated System Design for Remote Haptic Experiences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08305)
*Russian,Wu,Luke Hespanhol,Marius Hoggenmueller,Hannes Waldschütz,Eva Hornecker*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文开发了"Hugging Suit"——一种气动驱动的可穿戴系统，可实现远程拥抱的实时模拟与接收，并通过设计研究方法优化触觉分辨率、可用性与舒适度，同时探索环境因素对远程拥抱情感体验的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: COVID-19疫情凸显了远程情感交流的重要性，也暴露了现有技术缺乏触觉通道的缺陷。视频通话虽能维持视觉和听觉在场感，却无法传递身体接触（尤其是拥抱）所承载的情感深度，而拥抱是普遍认可的亲密与支持形式。

Method: 研究团队开发了Hugging Suit，一种气动驱动的可穿戴系统。该系统集成了可编程气动执行器矩阵（AAM）、便携式高压气动单元以及基于织物的压力传感器层，可在保持轻便和移动性的同时提供精确的可穿戴触觉反馈。研究采用"通过设计进行研究"（RtD）方法论，迭代优化原型以提升触觉分辨率、可用性和用户舒适度，并引入模块化组件以支持灵活可扩展的触觉配置。同时，研究还探索了照明、隐私和远程伙伴可见性等情境因素对接收远程拥抱的情感与感知体验的影响。

Result: 初步反馈表明，在低光照的私密空间中，特别是当用户还能通过视频看到远程伙伴时，受介导触觉体验期间的情感投入度和舒适度有所提升。系统采用低成本、适合创客空间的方法构建。

Conclusion: 该研究为远距离恋爱关系、情绪调节和远程治疗支持等情感触觉应用领域做出了贡献，其低成本、创客空间友好的方案具有推广价值。

Abstract: The COVID-19 pandemic emphasised the importance of remote emotional communication and highlighted a gap in existing technologies that lack haptic channels. While video calls maintain visual and auditory presence, they cannot convey the emotional depth of physical contact, especially hugging, a universally recognised form of intimacy and support. To explore this challenge, we developed the Hugging Suit, a pneumatically-actuated wearable system that enables users to simulate and receive remote hugs in real time. Unlike prior haptic systems that focus solely on tactile sensation, our approach integrates both technical and experiential considerations. The system integrates a programmable Air Actuator Matrix (AAM), a portable high-pressure pneumatic unit, and a fabric-based pressure sensor layer, enabling precise, wearable haptic feedback while remaining lightweight and mobile. Guided by a Research through Design (RtD) methodology, we iteratively refined the prototype to improve tactile resolution, overall usability, and user comfort, while introducing modular components to support flexible and scalable haptic configurations. In parallel, we explored how specific contextual factors, such as lighting, privacy, and the visibility of the remote partner, might shape the emotional and perceptual experience of receiving a remote hug. Preliminary feedback suggests that low-lit, private spaces, especially when users could also see their remote partner via video, improved emotional engagement and comfort during mediated haptic experiences. Our low-cost, maker-space-friendly approach contributes to affective haptics applications in long-distance relationships, emotional regulation, and remote therapeutic support.

</details>


### [183] [Living Dashboards: Automatically Self-Updating Visualization Dashboards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08393)
*Mingyu An,Heyon Jeon,Sungbok Shin,Jinwook Seo,Eduard Gröller,Niklas Elmqvist,Vaishali Dhanoa*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文提出"活仪表盘"（Living Dashboards）概念，让仪表盘视图能根据使用情况自主"出生、枯萎、复活、消亡"，并通过用户研究与AI支持的基线系统对比，验证其在任务表现、工作负荷和可用性上的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可视化仪表盘虽被广泛使用，但其动态数据显示与静态结构之间存在脱节：终端用户无法修改仪表盘来回答新问题，往往需要手动重新配置，灵活性不足。

Method: 1）提出Living Dashboards概念，视图依据使用情况"出生、枯萎、复活、消亡"；2）通过观察交互和自然语言查询，自主让被忽视的视图枯萎、让再次使用的视图复活；3）将添加或移除视图等更重要的决策交由用户决定；4）形式化为四维设计空间，并在基于网页的原型Living Dashboard中实现；5）开展探索性组间研究（N=12），与AI支持的基线系统在分析任务上进行对比。

Result: Living Dashboard组参与者正确完成的任务更多，报告的工作负荷更低，在可用性评分上也更高；但需注意，两种实验条件之间的差异不止源于自适应行为本身。

Conclusion: 让仪表盘视图自适应地"枯萎"和"复活"能够提升分析任务表现、降低工作负荷并提高可用性。不过由于两组差异并非仅来自自适应行为，未来需进一步研究以明确自适应机制的具体贡献。

Abstract: Visualization dashboards are widely used interactive tools, but a disconnect exists between the dynamic data they display and their static structure. End-users cannot modify the dashboard to answer new questions. We introduce Living Dashboards, whose views are born, wither, revive, and die in response to how they are used. Rather than requiring manual reconfiguration, a living dashboard observes interaction and natural-language queries to autonomously wither neglected views and revive those used again. More consequential decisions, such as adding or retiring views, are deferred to the user. We formalize the concept as a four-dimensional design space and implement it in Living Dashboard, a web-based prototype. We evaluate it in an exploratory between-subjects study (N = 12) against an AI-supported baseline on analytical tasks. Living Dashboard participants answered more tasks correctly, reported lower workload, and rated the system higher on usability, though the two conditions differed in more than adaptive behavior alone.

</details>


### [184] [The Now and Then: Integrating Current and Historical Data in Small Multiple Time Series Visualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08473)
*Sydney K. Purdue,Enrico Bertini,Melanie Tory*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文针对多小图时间序列监控中"突显当前值"的设计缺乏指南的问题，提出了一套可快速扫视（glanceable）的时间序列小多图设计空间，并通过两项在线实证研究验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在医疗、制造等高风险领域中，用户常依赖小多图时间序列可视化实时监控众多实体（如患者、机器），且往往在分心状态下进行。用户既需快速评估各实体的当前值是否越界，又需观察时间趋势。然而，目前尚无关于如何在历史时间序列小多图中视觉强调当前值的设计指南，因此需要系统性地探索该设计空间。

Method: 作者通过迭代式设计流程，结合相关文献综述以及视觉通道与强调（emphasis）理论，构建了面向"可快速扫视"的时间序列小多图设计空间。随后通过两项在线实证研究评估该设计空间，分别测试非阈值（non-threshold）与阈值（threshold）快速评估任务。

Result: 在非阈值任务中，将当前值的大小编码（size encoding）空间整合进折线图中，可在"寻找较大当前值"任务上实现28%的响应时间提升，同时对趋势查找任务的干扰最小。总体而言，整合式设计优于分离式设计（当前值表示与历史趋势线空间分离）。在阈值任务中，颜色阈值编码显著优于阴影带（shaded band）编码。

Conclusion: 研究为时间序列监控中融合当前值与历史数据的快速评估可视化提供了设计洞见：非阈值场景推荐采用空间整合的大小编码；阈值场景推荐采用颜色阈值编码。补充材料见 https://osf.io/wzf6a。

Abstract: Small multiple time series visualizations are often used for real-time data monitoring tasks in high-impact domains such as healthcare and manufacturing. Effective design is critical because users rely on these visualizations to monitor data from many entities, such as patients or machines, often while distracted. Users may need to rapidly appraise current values for each entity, monitoring for those that go outside an acceptable range, while also watching temporal trends. However, no design guidelines currently exist for visually emphasizing current values in historical time series represented by small multiples. Via an iterative design process informed by a review of related literature and theory on visual channels and emphasis, we present a design space for glanceable time series small multiple displays. We evaluate this space through two online empirical studies, testing against non-threshold and threshold rapid appraisal tasks. Our results provide insights into merging current value and historical data visualizations for rapid appraisal tasks in time series monitoring. For non-threshold tasks, we found that size encodings on the current value, spatially integrated into the line chart, may provide a good compromise, with 28% response time improvement for tasks involving finding large current values and minimal interference with trend lookup tasks. More generally, integrated designs outperformed separated designs (in which the current value representation is spatially separated from the historical trend line). For threshold tasks, color threshold encodings significantly outperformed shaded band encodings.
  All supplemental materials are available at https://osf.io/wzf6a.

</details>


### [185] [Systemization of Knowledge (SoK): Human-Centered AI Safety for Youth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08554)
*Pratyasha Saha,Yaman Yu,Yang Wang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究系统综述100项实证HCI研究，梳理青少年与AI交互中的风险、对策及其实践落地与评估情况，发现多数风险仅有构想性对策，落地与评估严重不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HCI领域日益关注青少年AI安全，但文献缺乏对已识别风险、其应对方式以及保护措施实际有效性的全面认识。

Method: 系统综述100项涉及儿童与青少年接触AI（学校、家庭、照护机构、公共服务场景）的实证HCI研究，采用YAIR风险分类法与MIT缓解措施分类法，映射风险、对策、实施与评估四层关系。

Result: 风险—对策映射显示，多数风险仅对应提出/构想中的对策；少数被实施，更少被评估；已有评估多衡量技术性能而非免于伤害的保护效果。

Conclusion: 研究指出覆盖空白与未经检验的保护措施，并提出加强青少年AI安全的HCI研究具体方向。

Abstract: While HCI increasingly examines AI-safety for youth, the literature lacks a comprehensive view of what risks have been identified, how they are addressed, and whether proposed protections work in-practice. We systematically reviewed 100 empirical HCI studies involving children and youth interacting with or exposed to AI across schools, homes, care settings, and public services. Using the YAIR taxonomy for risks and the MIT Mitigation Taxonomy for countermeasures, we map which risks have been identified, whether each risk is addressed by countermeasure(s), and whether each countermeasure for that risk is implemented and even evaluated. The risk-countermeasure mapping shows that most risks are matched only with proposed/ideated countermeasures; few countermeasures have been implemented, and fewer still evaluated; and existing evaluations often measure technical performance rather than protection from harm. We identify where coverage is absent, where safeguards remain untested, and propose concrete directions for HCI research to strengthen youth AI-safety.

</details>


### [186] [A Space-Agnostic Visual Game Analytics Tool with Adaptive Spatial Reconstruction for Mixed Reality Game Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08619)
*Nahian Rifaat,Fedir Skliar,Parisa Sargolzaei,Loutfouz Zaman*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向MR游戏的空间无关可视化分析工具，集成自适应空间重建与实时目标检测，在HoloLens 2上实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 游戏开发与可视化分析对MR工具的需求日增，但针对MR游戏的分析工具稀少，原因在于MR游戏融合了物理与数字世界中的真实组件，具有独特复杂性，且现有工具难以实现空间无关的泛化。

Method: 构建面向Microsoft HoloLens 2 MR头显的空间无关可视化游戏分析工具，包含自适应空间重建与实时目标检测两个核心模块。

Result: 实现了可跨空间泛化的MR游戏可视化分析工具，能够适应不同物理空间并实时检测真实物体。

Conclusion: 该工作有望加深对玩家环境的理解，并为MR游戏开发中的可视化分析开辟新方向。

Abstract: Recent years have seen an increasing need for MR tools for game development and visual game analytics. However, the number of visual game analytics tools for MR games remains scarce due to the unique complexities in integrating real components from both the physical and the digital worlds in MR games. Existing visual analytics tools for such games are not suitable for space-agnostic generalization. To tackle this problem, we introduce a space-agnostic visual game analytics tool that incorporates adaptive spatial reconstruction and real-time object detection built for the Microsoft HoloLens 2 MR headset. We believe that the outcomes of our work will increase insights into player environment and pave a new direction for visual game analytics for MR game development.

</details>


### [187] [Juicy Interactive Visualization: Evaluating How Excessive Feedback Design Shapes Visualization Engagement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08681)
*Shano Liang,Max Chen,Lane Harrison*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出 JuicyVIS 框架，将游戏研究中的"多汁反馈"概念系统化地引入交互可视化设计，通过三个维度与 26 个原型探索该设计空间，并经三项混合方法研究验证其能提升参与度与美学体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可视化研究长期关注装饰性元素与动画，但对于"丰富的、随交互而变的反馈"这一设计空间仍缺乏系统化阐述。作者希望借鉴游戏研究中成熟的"多汁反馈"理念，将这一模糊的实践概念转化为可视化领域可操作、可探索的理论框架。

Method: 作者提出 JuicyVIS，包含三个维度：交互类型、反馈时机与反馈强度，并基于既有可视化交互分类和游戏中多汁反馈的先前研究，构建了 26 个受控原型来探索该设计空间。随后通过三项探索性混合方法在线研究对该设计空间进行评估。

Result: 结果显示，多汁反馈始终与更高的参与度和更佳的美学体验相关联；最显著的收益来自"交互后反馈"，而非简单地提高反馈强度等方式。

Conclusion: 作者主张将多汁反馈视为可视化中的一种设计材料，它能够塑造可视化交互的"手感"、影响用户对动作结果的感知，并维持用户对数据表征的持续参与。

Abstract: Visualization research has long examined embellishment and animation, while the design of rich interaction-contingent feedback remains under-articulated as a systematic design space. We introduce JuicyVIS, a theory-informed operationalization of juicy feedback for interactive visualization that translates ideas from game studies into a visualization-centered framework. JuicyVIS introduces three dimensions, interaction type, feedback timing, and feedback intensity, which we explore through 26 controlled prototypes, grounded in established visualization interaction categories and prior work on juicy feedback in games. We evaluate the juicy design space through three exploratory mixed-methods online studies. Results show that juicy feedback was consistently associated with higher engagement and aesthetic experience, while the clearest gains came from post-interaction feedback, not, for example, by simply increasing intensity. We discuss how juicy feedback can be thought of as a design material in visualizations, shaping how visualization interactions feel, how action consequences are perceived, and how users remain engaged with data representations.

</details>


### [188] [reVISit-XR: Bringing Extended Reality into Embeddable, Trackable, and Replayable Visualization Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08700)
*Shano Liang,Max Chen,Lane Harrison*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出 reVISit-XR，将可定制 WebXR 刺激作为完整研究生命周期中的可复用组件，支持嵌入、追踪与回放，并通过七个示例和一个已部署研究验证其可行性与适用范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展现实（XR）日益成为实证可视化研究的场景，研究相关状态分布在头显与控制器姿态、场景配置、选择、空间布局和 AR 锚点中。现有 XR 工具只支持工作流的部分环节（如场景创作、交互或会话分析），很少将 XR 刺激视为完整研究生命周期中的可复用组件。因此，作者希望弥补这一缺口，使 XR 刺激能够作为一等研究组件被使用。

Method: 作者提出 reVISit-XR，这是 reVISit 的扩展，使可定制的 WebXR 刺激能够在实证可视化研究中被嵌入、追踪和回放。其方法包括：将 XR 场景与标准研究组件一起排序；收集反应式任务响应；同时捕获场景创作的语义状态与通用 XR 轨迹；并重新水合（rehydrate）参与者会话，以便后续在桌面端和头显端进行分析。系统组织为可复用的刺激构建包和研究集成包两个部分。

Result: 通过七个集成的、可复用的示例以及一个已部署的研究，作者展示了 reVISit-XR 的适用范围和可行性。

Conclusion: reVISit-XR 使 XR 刺激成为一等研究组件，并讨论了其当前能力与未来扩展方向。

Abstract: Extended reality (XR) is increasingly a setting for empirical visualization research, where study-relevant state is distributed across headset and controller pose, scene configuration, selections, spatial layouts, and AR anchors. Existing XR tools support parts of the workflow, such as scene authoring, interaction, or session analysis, yet seldom treat an XR stimulus as a reusable component of a complete study lifecycle. We present reVISit-XR, an extension of reVISit that makes customizable WebXR stimuli embeddable, trackable, and replayable within empirical visualization studies. reVISit-XR sequences XR scenes alongside standard study components, collects reactive task responses, captures scene-authored semantic state together with generic XR traces, and rehydrates participant sessions for later desktop and headset analysis. Organized as a reusable stimulus build package and a study integration package, it lets XR stimuli act as first-class study components. We demonstrate its scope and feasibility through seven integrated, reusable examples and a deployed study, and discuss its current capabilities and future extensions.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [189] [TEMPEST: Temporal Embeddings for Scalable Driver Identification via Angular Margin Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06855)
*Kyle Musgrove,Dylan B. Lewis,Sarah Powers,Emma J. Reid,Hector Santos-Villalobos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TEMPEST是一种基于时序卷积网络和ArcFace角边距损失的车载驾驶员身份嵌入模型，在严格的时序评估下展现出可扩展的判别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可扩展的驾驶员身份识别需要嵌入模型在车队规模扩大时仍保持判别性能，但现有三元组损失方法随驾驶员池增大而迅速退化，并在严格时序评估下过拟合于特定会话模式。

Method: TEMPEST采用时序卷积网络（TCN）嵌入模型，以加性角边距（ArcFace）损失训练，在归一化角空间中实现全局类别级分离；将60秒多模态驾驶窗口映射为紧凑的96维嵌入，支持无需重训或分类器重拟合的动态注册。模型规模72万参数、2.80 MB，50轮收敛。

Result: 在45名驾驶员的严格时序评估中，TEMPEST达91.71% Rank-1准确率，超越最佳经典模型17.9个百分点，超越最强三元组损失基线58.4个百分点；驾驶员池从10增至45时仅退化4.3pp，而监督与非监督三元组基线分别退化22pp和32.5pp。在公开KIA Soul数据集上，会话内和跨会话分别超越最佳经典模型7.3pp和14.3pp。

Conclusion: TEMPEST为可扩展的行为驾驶员生物特征识别建立了一个严格、可复现的基线。

Abstract: Scalable driver identification requires embedding models that maintain discriminative performance as fleet size grows, yet existing triplet-loss formulations degrade rapidly with driver pool size and overfit to session-specific patterns under rigorous temporal evaluation. We introduce TEMPEST, a Temporal Convolutional Network embedding model trained with an additive angular margin (ArcFace) loss that enforces global class-level separation in a normalized angular space. TEMPEST maps 60-second multimodal driving windows to compact 96-dimensional embeddings, supporting truly dynamic enrollment without any retraining or classifier refitting. Under rigorous temporal evaluation on a 45-driver dataset, TEMPEST achieves 91.71% Rank-1 accuracy, outperforming the best classical model by 17.9 pp and the strongest triplet-loss baseline by 58.4 pp. TEMPEST degrades by only 4.3 pp when growing the subject pool from 10 to 45 drivers, compared to 22 pp and 32.5 pp for supervised and unsupervised triplet-loss baselines, and its cross-session advantage is corroborated on the public KIA Soul dataset, where it outperforms the best classical model by 7.3 pp within-session and 14.3 pp cross-session. With 720K parameters, a 2.80 MB footprint, and 50-epoch convergence, TEMPEST establishes a rigorous, reproducible baseline for scalable behavioral driver biometric identification.

</details>


### [190] [When Does External Guidance Help LLM Reasoning? A Bias-Variance Theory of Guidance-Augmented GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06861)
*Sofia Torres,Gabriel Almeida,Carter Adams,Camila Rocha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Guidance-Augmented GRPO（GA-GRPO）统一理论框架，将外部引导建模为改写问题分布的随机引导算子G，把策略梯度估计器视为有偏on-policy估计器，偏差由引导分布与策略自身分布的全变差散度δ_G界定，统一涵盖vanilla GRPO、LUFFY、ExPO、PAPO、TAPO，并给出收敛率、闭式最优引导权重及匹配的minimax下界，实验验证理论预测并显著节省算力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法（LUFFY、ExPO、PAPO、TAPO）通过引入专家轨迹、自解释或检索思维模式等外部引导提升多步推理能力，但均只报告经验增益，缺乏收敛率、偏差界以及引导信号最优加权规则的理论刻画，存在明显的理论空白。

Method: 提出GA-GRPO统一理论框架：将外部引导形式化为随机引导算子G，用于重写问题分布，并将由此得到的策略梯度估计器分析为有偏on-policy估计器，其偏差由引导增强采样分布与策略分布之间的全变差散度δ_G上界。该框架通过不同G的取法将vanilla GRPO、LUFFY、ExPO、PAPO、TAPO纳入为特例。在光滑性与有界散度假设下进行收敛性分析，推导闭式MSE最优引导权重，并证明匹配的minimax下界。

Result: 理论结果：GA-GRPO以O(1/√T)速率收敛至GRPO稳定点的O(δ√T)邻域；闭式最优引导权重λ*(T, δ, σ_0²) = σ_0² / (σ_0² + R_max² δ² T)；并证明Ω(δ²T)偏差项不可消除的匹配minimax下界。实验：在Qwen2.5-Math-7B-Base上跨九个数学与OOD基准，最优权重GA-GRPO匹配或超越TAPO、LUFFY、ExPO及vanilla GRPO，同时节省31%的GPU小时数，八项分析实验验证了各项理论预测。

Conclusion: GA-GRPO为外部引导增强的RLVR方法提供了首个统一的理论分析框架，首次给出收敛率、偏差界与最优加权规则，并以匹配的下界证明偏差项的不可避免性；理论与实验共同表明，在最优权重下引导信号可提升性能并显著降低算力开销。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become the dominant paradigm for eliciting multi-step reasoning in large language models, and a recent wave of methods (LUFFY, ExPO, PAPO, TAPO) further augments RL with \emph{external guidance} - expert traces, self-explanations, or retrieved thought patterns. Although each method reports empirical gains, none provides convergence rates, bias bounds, or an optimal weighting rule for the guidance signal. We close this gap with \emph{Guidance-Augmented GRPO} (GA-GRPO), a unified theoretical framework that casts external guidance as a stochastic guidance operator G re-writing the question distribution, and analyses the resulting policy-gradient estimator as a biased on-policy estimator whose bias is bounded by the total-variation guidance divergence delta\_G between the guidance-augmented sampling distribution and the policy's own distribution. The framework subsumes vanilla GRPO, LUFFY, ExPO, PAPO, and TAPO as special cases obtained by particular choices of G. Under smoothness and bounded-divergence assumptions we prove that GA-GRPO converges at rate O(1/sqrt(T)) to an O(delta sqrt(T))-neighbourhood of the GRPO stationary point, derive the closed-form MSE-optimal guidance weight lambda-star(T, delta, sigma\_0 squared) = sigma\_0 squared / (sigma\_0 squared + R\_max squared delta squared T), and prove a matching minimax lower bound showing the Omega(delta squared T) bias term is unavoidable. Experiments on Qwen2.5-Math-7B-Base across nine math and OOD benchmarks confirm that optimal-weight GA-GRPO matches or surpasses TAPO, LUFFY, ExPO, and vanilla GRPO while requiring 31\% fewer GPU-hours, and eight analysis experiments validate each theoretical prediction.

</details>


### [191] [Neutrosophic Ensemble Classification for Uncertainty-Aware Bearing Fault Detection: Evidence from Laboratory and Variable-Speed Industrial Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06880)
*Maikel Leyva-Vazquez,Dayron Rumbaut Rangel,Lorenzo Cevallos-Torres,Alexis Matheu Perez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将随机森林+XGBoost+逻辑回归集成模型进行精细中智学分解为四个不确定性指标(T-hat、F-hat、I1-hat、I2-hat)，在CWRU和JNU轴承数据集上评估，发现熵指标I1-hat对误差有稳健关联，并报告了逻辑回归置信度优于完整分解的边界条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轴承故障检测的机器学习分类器仅输出标量置信度分数，将自信的错误与真正模糊的预测混为一谈，且传统真/假对(F = 1 - T)在构造上代数冗余，无法区分错误类型。因此需要一种更精细的不确定性分解方法来刻画集成模型预测中的多种不确定性来源。

Method: 将RF+XGBoost+LR集成模型分解为四个中智学指标：T-hat(最高类证据)、F-hat(最佳竞争类证据)、预测熵I1-hat和决策分歧I2-hat。在CWRU和JNU两个轴承基准(600-1000 rpm)上采用留一条件协议评估。此外还融合时域和频域模型，用Jensen-Shannon散度评分；并在CWRU上使用包络谱解调的轴承故障频率提取三个可解释特征。

Result: CWRU上修正文件映射错误后，集成模型在四个留出负载中的三个达到100.00%准确率(第四个92.27%)，误差太少无法做不确定性分析。JNU上留出1000 rpm时准确率骤降至40.64%，低于多数类基线；逻辑回归(57.91%)泛化远好于树集成。I1-hat对误差的关联超越T-hat/F-hat，I2-hat贡献很小；单独逻辑回归置信度优于完整分解。时频融合的JS散度优于模型自身熵(AURC 0.29 vs 0.36；单条件复现0.54 vs 0.73)，是唯一在CWRU-vs-JNU分布偏移对比中正确移动的指标。CWRU上基于文献验证故障频率的包络谱解调，用三个可解释特征几乎完美分离大多数故障类(99.57%)。

Conclusion: 精细中智学分解中I1-hat(熵)是对误差最有信息量的指标，但单独的逻辑回归置信度仍优于完整分解，构成一个诚实的边界条件。时频融合的JS散度是分布偏移下唯一表现正确的指标。基于物理故障频率的包络谱特征在CWRU上可实现近乎完美的故障分类，代码、日志和图表已公开以供独立验证。

Abstract: Machine learning classifiers for bearing fault detection produce scalar confidence scores that conflate confident errors with genuinely ambiguous predictions, and the conventional truth/falsity pair (F = 1 - T) is algebraically redundant by construction. We operationalize a refined neutrosophic decomposition of a Random Forest + XGBoost + Logistic Regression ensemble into four indicators -- T-hat (top-class evidence), F-hat (best-competitor evidence), predictive entropy I1-hat, and decision disagreement I2-hat -- evaluated on two bearing benchmarks (CWRU and JNU, 600-1000 rpm) under a leave-one-condition-out protocol. On CWRU, after correcting a file-to-class mapping error, the ensemble reaches 100.00 percent accuracy on three of four held-out loads (92.27 percent on the fourth), leaving too few errors for uncertainty analysis. On JNU, holding out 1000 rpm, accuracy collapses to 40.64 percent, below a majority-class baseline; Logistic Regression (57.91 percent) generalizes far better than the tree ensembles. I1-hat shows a robust association with error beyond T-hat/F-hat, while I2-hat contributes little; standalone Logistic Regression confidence outperforms the full decomposition, a boundary condition we report honestly. Two further results extend this: fusing a time-domain and a frequency-domain model of the same signal and scoring their Jensen-Shannon divergence beats that model own entropy (AURC 0.29 vs. 0.36 on the standard split; 0.54 vs. 0.73 under a harder single-condition reproduction), the only indicator moving correctly under a CWRU-versus-JNU distributional-shift contrast; and, on CWRU alone, literature-verified bearing fault frequencies, correctly demodulated via the envelope spectrum, separate most fault classes almost perfectly (99.57 percent) using three interpretable features. Code, logs, and figures are released for independent verification.

</details>


### [192] [Comparative review of hybrid forecasting models for short-term prediction of building thermal load](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06881)
*Nikolaos A. Efkarpidis,Despoina Kothona,Georgios C. Christoforidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对建筑短期热需求预测的不同混合模型进行对比综述，并用苏格兰家庭实测数据评估13种混合方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑短期热需求预测精度对供暖与热水系统运行优化至关重要，而现有文献中混合模型的性能缺乏系统性对比。

Method: 第一步分析文献中已有技术，总结输入输出特征、优缺点；结论是元启发式或数据驱动模型用于辨识基础模型参数。第二步利用苏格兰家庭热需求历史数据与历史天气预报，对13种混合方法进行性能评估。

Result: EMD-LSTM-Markov模型对供暖和DHW日前功率模式预测精度最高，但高功率激增与尖峰被低估；SVM-SA与RF-ISSA-LSTM可预测平滑的需求曲线，快速变化时大多数功率峰值被低估。

Conclusion: 混合模型在建筑短期热需求预测中各有优劣，EMD-LSTM-Markov整体精度最佳，但极端功率峰值预测仍是共性难题。

Abstract: In this paper, a comparative review of different hybrid models for short-term forecasting of building thermal demand is carried out. Particularly, the assessment tackles the comparison of data-driven models enhanced with other state-of-the-art techniques. At the first step, the existing techniques reported in the literature are analysed. It is concluded that Metaheuristics or a data-driven model are used to identify the parameters of the basic model. The qualitative evaluation includes for each method the input and output features, main advantages and drawbacks. At the second step, an existing dataset of historical thermal demand from Scottish households, as well as historical weather forecasts are utilized to assess additionally the performance of existing hybrid methods. From the assessment of 13 hybrid methods, the Empirical Modal Decomposition - long short-term memory - Markov (EMD-LSTM-Markov) model can predict with the highest accuracy the day-ahead power pattern of heating and domestic hot water (DHW) demands. Though local power peaks are also accurately predicted, high power swells and spikes are underestimated. Other methods, such as Support Vector Machine - Simulated Annealing (SVM-SA) and Random Forest - Improved Sparrow Search Algorithm - LSTM (RF-ISSA-LSTM) predict a smooth pattern of heating and DHW demand profiles with rapid changes underestimating most power peaks.

</details>


### [193] [Learning When to Refine: Long-Horizon Reinforcement Learning for Budgeted Neural-Operator PDE Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06883)
*Ange Tong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"预算化自适应神经算子求解"框架：全局FNO推进全场上，局部算子提出分块修正，集合感知选择器决定在哪精修，宏观策略决定何时花多少预算；并提出rollout验证的策略改进（RV-PI）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子可快速替代时间相关PDE求解，但自回归部署存在"精修分配"问题：预测误差随空间和时间变化，而沿轨迹只能提交有限次局部修正。需要确定局部修正的价值不仅取决于即时误差削减，还取决于其对自回归轨迹的下游影响。

Method: 将问题形式化为预算化自适应神经算子求解。架构包含：全局傅里叶神经算子（FNO）推进全字段；局部算子提出分块残差修正；集合感知选择器选择精修位置；宏观策略决定何时及花多少剩余精修预算。提出RV-PI：通过所学PDE求解器的实际延续rollout评估可行精修次数，将长时域优势转换为保守策略目标，仅在留出轨迹误差改善时接受更新。

Result: 在浅水基准（32次干预预算）上，RV-PI三种子平均轨迹相对L2误差为0.6910，较仅即时策略改进提升5.37%，较RandomMacro提升2.41%。在受迫Brusselator基准（76次干预预算）上，RV-PI达到0.09954，较仅即时策略改进提升2.31%，较RandomMacro提升5.32%。

Conclusion: 在固定精修预算下，局部修正的价值取决于其对自回归轨迹的下游效应，而不仅是即时误差削减。

Abstract: Neural operators provide fast surrogates for time-dependent PDEs, but autoregressive deployment creates a refinement-allocation problem: prediction errors vary over space and time, while only a finite number of local corrections can be committed along a trajectory. We formulate this as budgeted adaptive neural-operator solving. A global Fourier neural operator advances the full field, a local operator proposes patch-wise residual corrections, and a set-aware selector chooses where to refine. A macro policy decides when and how much of the remaining refinement budget to spend. We introduce rollout-verified policy improvement (RV-PI), which evaluates feasible refinement counts through actual continuation rollouts of the learned PDE solver, converts long-horizon advantages into conservative policy targets, and accepts an update only when held-out trajectory error improves. On the shallow-water benchmark with a 32-intervention budget, RV-PI achieves a three-seed mean trajectory relative L2 error of 0.6910, improving over immediate-only policy improvement by 5.37% and RandomMacro by 2.41%. On the forcing-driven Brusselator benchmark with a 76-intervention budget, RV-PI attains 0.09954, improving over immediate-only policy improvement by 2.31% and RandomMacro by 5.32%. These results show that, under a fixed refinement budget, the value of a local correction depends on its downstream effect on the autoregressive trajectory, not only on its immediate error reduction.

</details>


### [194] [Event-Driven ML Pipeline Orchestration for Manufacturing: An AWS Industry Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06890)
*Zhengyang,Gu,Thomas Cook,Fredaljohn Rohrbaugh,Joseph E. Hernandez,Chris Couch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇工业经验报告，介绍汽车制造领域连续机器学习训练的云基础设施三年运营实践：基于事件驱动的Amazon ECS/EC2 GPU、SQS与Lambda准入控制调度器，每周触发依赖感知的再训练链，在40000+训练任务中实现72-78%的成本降低，并开源模拟器与Terraform模块骨架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 汽车制造场景中的模型需随生产事件和产品专化持续再训练，而常驻GPU基础设施成本高昂且利用率低；作者希望验证事件驱动、准入控制的云架构能否在保证调度正确性与时延的前提下大幅降低成本。

Method: 架构结合Amazon ECS与EC2 GPU容量提供者、基于SQS的消息传递（含死信队列），以及强制执行集群并发上限的准入控制Lambda调度器；ECS Fargate上的Conductor编排器按周调度依赖感知的再训练链；基础设施以模块化Terraform多账户编码。评估采用40000+生产训练任务数据，并用离散事件仿真对比准入控制、队列排空与AWS Step Functions。

Result: 相比常驻GPU基础设施，成本降低72-78%；仿真表明准入控制必不可少——朴素调度会丢失65%的任务；队列排空方案的时延与AWS Step Functions相当，同时消除了每任务的启动开销。

Conclusion: 事件驱动、准入控制的云架构可在生产规模上兼顾成本与可靠调度，作者总结实践经验并开源仿真器与Terraform模块骨架，为同类连续训练平台提供可复用参考。

Abstract: We present an industry experience report on three years of operating an event-driven cloud infrastructure for continuous machine learning training in automotive manufacturing. Our system orchestrates GPU-accelerated training of product-specialized model pairs, a physics prediction model and a reinforcement-learning control policy, across multiple plants, coordinating long-running GPU workloads triggered by manufacturing events. The architecture combines Amazon ECS with EC2 GPU capacity providers, SQS-based messaging with dead-letter queues, and an admission-controlled Lambda dispatcher that enforces cluster concurrency limits. A Conductor orchestrator on ECS Fargate initiates dependency-aware retraining chains on a weekly schedule. The entire infrastructure is codified in modular Terraform with multi-account separation. From 40000+ production training jobs we report a 72-78% cost reduction versus always-on GPU infrastructure. A discrete-event simulation confirms that admission control is necessary (naive dispatch loses 65% of jobs) and that queue-draining matches AWS Step Functions latency while eliminating per-job startup overhead. We provide lessons learned and release the simulator and Terraform module skeletons as open-source artifacts.

</details>


### [195] [Learning from Unreliable Trajectories: Adversarially-Robust Federated Q-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06918)
*Sreejeet Maity,Aritra Mitra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究存在对抗性智能体的联邦强化学习，提出 Robust Async-Fed-Q 算法，结合方差缩减的 Bellman 最优算子估计与鲁棒聚合，建立了有限时间高概率保证及信息论下界，首次为对抗鲁棒联邦强化学习给出近乎匹配的上下界，并扩展至单轨迹马尔可夫采样与异构部分覆盖，同时显著改善联邦 Q 学习的通信复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解当部分智能体表现对抗性并传输任意损坏信息时，协作带来的样本效率收益是否能被保留。已有联邦强化学习多假设智能体诚实，缺乏对对抗污染的鲁棒性与统计极限的理论刻画。

Method: 提出 Robust Async-Fed-Q：基于 epoch 的联邦学习算法，在智能体端对 Bellman 最优算子进行方差缩减估计，在服务器端进行鲁棒聚合。进一步扩展到单轨迹马尔可夫采样和异构部分覆盖场景，即不同智能体探索状态-动作空间的不同区域、依赖集体覆盖进行学习。

Result: 建立了高概率有限时间保证，显示方法在容忍对抗损坏的同时保留诚实智能体间的统计收益；对抗智能体的影响随每个诚实智能体数据量增加而减小，在无限样本极限下消失。给出信息论下界，刻画对抗损坏不可避免的统计代价，首次得到对抗鲁棒联邦强化学习近乎匹配的上下界。基于 epoch 的设计显著改善了异步采样下联邦 Q 学习的最佳已知通信复杂度。

Conclusion: 该工作为对抗鲁棒联邦强化学习提供了首个近乎紧的统计刻画，并表明鲁棒聚合与方差缩减相结合可在大幅降低通信的同时保持协作的样本效率。

Abstract: We study federated reinforcement learning in which multiple agents interact with a common Markov decision process and communicate through a central server to collaboratively learn the optimal state-action value function. Our goal is to understand whether the sample-efficiency benefits of collaboration can be retained when a fraction of the agents behave adversarially and transmit arbitrarily corrupted information. To address this problem, we introduce Robust Async-Fed-Q, an epoch-based federated learning algorithm that combines variance-reduced estimation of the Bellman optimality operator at the agents with robust aggregation at the server. We establish high-probability finite-time guarantees showing that the proposed method preserves the statistical gains of collaboration among the honest agents while tolerating adversarial corruption. In particular, the effect of the adversarial agents decreases as the amount of data collected by each honest agent grows and eventually vanishes in the infinite-sample limit. We complement these guarantees with information-theoretic lower bounds that characterize the unavoidable statistical cost of adversarial corruption, leading to the first nearly matching upper and lower bounds for adversarially robust federated reinforcement learning. We further extend our framework to accommodate single-trajectory Markovian sampling and heterogeneous partial coverage, where different agents may explore different regions of the state-action space and learning relies on their collective coverage. Finally, our epoch-based design substantially improves the best known communication complexity for federated Q-learning under asynchronous sampling.

</details>


### [196] [AttSVD:Prompt-Adaptive Low-Rank KV Cache Compression via Attention-Guided SVD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06927)
*Sara Abdali,Jongwoo Ko,Pashmina Cameron*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AttSVD：一种基于注意力几何的可解释低秩KV缓存压缩方法，沿特征轴压缩而非驱逐token，节省最多50%内存且性能不降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归Transformer的KV缓存随上下文线性增长，成为长上下文的存储瓶颈。现有免训练方法多在序列轴上不可逆地驱逐低重要性token。

Method: 保留全部token，改在特征轴上进行更廉价的存储。提出AttSVD，基于每个prompt自身注意力几何的在线逐prompt截断SVD，仅保留注意力实际读取的方向。提出累积式与流式两种解码时缓存策略，以及两项自适应改进：逐矩阵能量规则独立确定logit空间与注意力质量；注意力感知基截断仅在注意力读取的空间内截断。

Result: 在多个模型上，于agentic基准和完整LongBench评测中，AttSVD性能与稠密缓存相当，同时KV缓存内存占用最多降至50%。

Conclusion: AttSVD通过沿特征轴的低秩压缩实现高效KV缓存，兼顾性能与内存，并可提供逐头可解释性（有效秩与注意力几何）。

Abstract: The key-value (KV) cache of autoregressive transformers grows linearly with context length and dominates memory at long context. Most training-free remedies evict low-importance tokens, an irreversible choice along the sequence axis. We instead keep every token and store it more cheaply along the "feature" axis. We therefore propose AttSVD, a new "interpretable" low-rank compression whose basis is derived from each prompt's own attention geometry: an online, per-prompt truncated SVD that keeps only the directions attention actually reads, cutting persistent per-head KV memory in proportion to the retained rank. We propose two decode-time caching strategies, accumulating and streaming, for short and long generation regimes. Furthermore, we propose two refinements that make compression adaptive. A per-matrix energy rule sizes the logit space and the attention mass independently. An attention-aware basis truncates only in the spaces attention actually reads, preserving both the attention logits and the attention output. The same factors also provide free, per-head interpretability insights into the effective rank and the geometry attention consumes. Across multiple models, on both an agentic benchmark and the full LongBench suite AttSVD stays on par with the dense cache while using up to 50% of the KV-cache memory.

</details>


### [197] [Near-Optimal Sample Complexity for Recursive Entropic Risk Reinforcement Learning with a Generative Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06931)
*Amirparsa Bahrami,Oliver Mortensen,Mohammad Sadegh Talebi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对模型驱动的风险敏感Q值迭代（MB-RS-QVI）进行精细分析，为递归熵风险偏好下的有限折扣MDP推导出Q值学习和策略学习的PAC样本复杂度界，缩小了有效视界的指数差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在递归熵风险偏好的MDP中，已有工作给出了样本复杂度的上界和指数下界，但两者在有效视界1/(1-γ)上存在指数级差距，且上界对有效视界的指数依赖过强，因此需要更精细的分析来弥合这一差距。

Method: 假设可访问MDP的生成模型，对一类插件式模型驱动方法——风险敏感Q值迭代（MB-RS-QVI）进行精细化理论分析，推导同时学习最优Q值函数和ε-最优策略的(ε,δ)-PAC保证。

Result: 所得界在有效视界1/(1-γ)上的指数依赖优于该设定下现有最好结果；在|β|/(1-γ)的指数依赖上以及S、A、ε和|β|的依赖上（至多对数因子）与已有下界匹配，从而消除了此前上下界之间的指数差距，仅剩有效视界上的多项式差距。

Conclusion: 精细分析显著改进了风险敏感MDP中值学习和策略学习的样本复杂度上界，将原有指数级差距缩小为有效视界上的多项式差距，为后续进一步闭合该多项式差距提供了基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the sample complexities of value and policy learning in finite discounted Markov decision processes (MDPs) under recursive entropic risk preferences with risk parameter \(β\neq 0\), assuming access to a generative model of the MDP. We provide a refined analysis of model-based risk-sensitive Q-value iteration (MB-RS-QVI), a plug-in model-based method introduced in prior work, and derive \((\varepsilon,δ)\)-PAC guarantees for both learning the optimal \(Q\)-value function and an \(\varepsilon\)-optimal policy. Our bounds improve the exponential dependence on the effective horizon \(1/(1-γ)\) compared with the best existing guarantees for this setting. In particular, they match the existing lower bounds in their exponential dependence on \(|β|/(1-γ)\), as well as in \(S\), \(A\), \(\varepsilon\), and \(|β|\), up to logarithmic factors. Consequently, our analysis removes the exponential gap between the previously known upper and lower bounds, leaving only a polynomial gap in the effective horizon.

</details>


### [198] [QiYao-I: A Manifold Based Foundation Model for Irregular Multivariate Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06936)
*Linfeng Wang,Ruitong Zhang,Kai Zhao,Yang Shu,Zhongwen Rao,Meng Wang,Yijie Li,Bin Yang,Chenjun Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QiYao-I，一种基于流形的不规则多变量时间序列预测基础模型，通过采样条件化时间流形注意力和频率感知的动态变量交互机制，解决不规则采样与异步跨变量依赖问题，在多个基准上取得优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实应用中多变量时间序列常存在不规则采样和变量间异步记录，而现有时间序列基础模型多基于规则采样序列构建，难以泛化到不规则时间间隔与异步跨变量依赖场景，因此需要专门针对不规则多变量时间序列预测的基础模型。

Method: 提出QiYao-I基础模型。核心包含两部分：（1）采样条件化的时间流形注意力机制，将真实时间戳映射到可学习的时间流形特征空间，并向注意力层注入时间流形偏置，以捕获不规则时间间隔和局部采样结构；（2）提出具有频率感知能力的动态变量交互机制，在异步观测条件下选择性地进行跨变量消息传递。

Result: 在真实世界不规则多变量预测基准上的大量实验表明，QiYao-I相比时间序列基础模型和端到端不规则预测模型均取得更优性能，并在零样本和少样本设置下展现出强泛化能力。

Conclusion: QiYao-I通过流形建模与频率感知的变量交互有效应对不规则多变量时间序列预测难题，验证了其作为基础模型的优越性与泛化能力。

Abstract: Irregular multivariate time series forecasting is a challenging yet important problem in real-world applications, where observations are often irregularly sampled and asynchronously recorded across variables. Existing time series foundation models are mostly built on regularly sampled sequences, making them difficult to generalize to irregular time intervals and asynchronous cross-variable dependencies. To address these challenges, we propose QiYao-I, a manifold based foundation model for irregular multivariate time series forecasting. Specifically, we introduce a novel sampling-conditioned temporal manifold attention mechanism that maps real timestamps into a learnable temporal manifold feature space and injects temporal manifold biases into attention layers, enabling the model to capture both irregular time intervals and local sampling structures. Further, we propose a dynamic variable interaction mechanism with frequency awareness. It selectively performs cross-variable message passing under asynchronous observations. Extensive experiments on real-world irregular multivariate forecasting benchmarks demonstrate that QiYao-I achieves superior performance compared with both time series foundation models and end-to-end irregular forecasting models, showing strong generalization ability in zero-shot and few-shot settings.

</details>


### [199] [Learning to Remember: Distilling Memory Retention for Compact Recurrent Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06942)
*Nilushika Udayangania,Kishor Nandakishora,Marimuthu Palaniswami*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MemKD知识蒸馏框架，通过捕捉师生模型在时间序列子序列上的记忆保持差异，实现紧凑高性能循环神经网络，适配资源受限环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习尤其是LSTM等循环神经网络在时间序列分析中表现优异，但计算复杂度高、模型体积大，难以部署于可穿戴设备与边缘计算平台。知识蒸馏可将大模型知识迁移至小模型，但现有KD方法源于计算机视觉任务，忽视了时间序列模型特有的时间依赖性与记忆保持特性。

Method: 提出MemKD（Memory-Discrepancy Knowledge Distillation）框架，设计专门的损失函数来捕捉教师模型与学生模型在时间序列数据各子序列上的记忆保持差异，促使学生模型有效模仿教师行为。

Result: 在扩展的时间序列基准上，MemKD显著优于现有最先进KD方法；在多种压缩级别下可匹配教师模型性能，在参数量和内存占用显著降低的同时保持精度无明显损失。

Conclusion: MemKD弥补了现有KD方法在时间序列场景下的不足，可实现适合实时时间序列分析的紧凑高性能循环神经网络，并通过理论分析与扩展实验验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Deep learning models, particularly recurrent neural networks and their variants, such as long short-term memory, have significantly advanced time series analysis. These models capture complex, sequential patterns in time series, enabling real-time assessments. However, their high computational complexity and large model sizes pose challenges for deployment in resource-constrained environments, such as wearable devices and edge computing platforms. Knowledge Distillation (KD) offers a solution by transferring knowledge from a large, complex model (teacher) to a smaller, more efficient model (student), thereby retaining high performance while reducing computational demands. Current KD methods, originally designed for computer vision tasks, neglect the unique temporal dependencies and memory retention characteristics of time series models. To bridge this gap, we propose a novel KD framework termed Memory-Discrepancy Knowledge Distillation (MemKD). MemKD leverages a specialized loss function to capture memory retention discrepancies between the teacher and student models across subsequences within time series data, ensuring that the student model effectively mimics the teacher's behaviour. This approach facilitates the development of compact, high-performing recurrent neural networks suitable for real-time, time series analysis tasks. We provide additional experiments, in-depth theoretical analysis, and insights into the proposed framework across extended time series benchmarks. Our experiments demonstrate that MemKD significantly outperforms state-of-the-art KD methods. Additionally, we demonstrate that it can match the teacher model's performance across a wide range of compression levels, achieving notable reductions in parameter count and memory usage without a significant loss in accuracy.

</details>


### [200] [Learning to Decide, Not to Reason: Parameter-Efficient Decision Operators via Low-Rank Activation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06950)
*Ran Li,Lei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Decisionsteer，一种通过行为克隆训练的系统-1决策算子，用约330K参数即可匹配1.33M参数的强化学习算子，并将3685 token的推理压缩为6 token决策且不损失准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将技能注入冻结语言模型通常需要百万级参数和强化学习流程，成本高昂且训练困难；作者希望以更低成本实现同等或更优的技能注入效果。

Method: 采用行为克隆训练的系统-1决策算子，默认330K参数，并设计rank-4变体（23K参数）；关键在于共享低秩主干内的零初始化输出投影，使梯度在第一步即可流动，而非像先前算子那样在大因子矩阵处梯度归零。

Result: 330K参数算子匹配1.33M参数的RL算子，3685 token压缩至6 token无精度损失；23K变体为最强公开算子的1/58，适用SearchQA并接近适用LiveMath；跨5任务、3骨干迁移，OOD增益在训练后数月发布的LiveMath上仍保持；23/57 LiveMath点超过基模型best-of-8采样。

Conclusion: 性能差距源于可训练性，由初始化与架构共同决定；算子通过放大模型现有后层路径而非提前写入答案，技能可作为近似线性算子进行相加、插值与推理时热插拔，且增益与基模型headroom相关。

Abstract: Injecting skills into a frozen language model currently costs a million parameters and a reinforcement-learning pipeline. We introduce \method{}, a System-1 decision operator trained by behavior cloning that lowers this cost by roughly two orders of magnitude. The default operator uses 330K parameters to match a 1.33M-parameter operator trained with reinforcement learning, exceeds or achieve comparable performance, while collapsing 3,685-token deliberation into a 6-token decision with no loss in accuracy. A rank-4 variant with 23K parameters, 1/58 of the strongest published skill operator, suffices for SearchQA and near-suffices for LiveMath, where higher rank still helps; the same recipe transfers across five tasks and three backbones, with out-of-distribution gains persisting on LiveMath problems released months after training. The gap to prior work is trainability, and it is set jointly by initialization and architecture: the initialization of prior operators zeroes the gradient of both large factor matrices at the first optimization step, whereas our zero-initialized output projection inside a shared low-rank backbone receives a gradient immediately, which a gradient-flow probe confirms directly. The gain isn't chain-of-thought compression: 23 of 57 LiveMath points beat the base model's best-of-8 sampling, and a logit-lens probe shows the operator amplifies the answer along the model's existing late-layer pathway, not writing it earlier. Gains track the base model's headroom across 13 base--task pairs, and skills compose as approximately linear operators that can be added, interpolated, and hot-swapped at inference time. Code on https://github.com/rlisml/decisionsteer.

</details>


### [201] [Do Neural PDE Solvers Learn the Right Dynamics?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06952)
*Haonan Li,Yue Song,Bin Yang,Kaihong Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于评估神经PDE求解器动态保真度的框架，通过演化邻近初始状态集成并与直接数值模拟对比，考察误差形成、集成几何与极端事件三方面，揭示预测精度高不等于动态保真度高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经PDE求解器通常只以预测误差衡量性能，但预测分数只反映与参考解的一致程度，无法揭示误差如何累积、邻近状态如何发散、极端事件如何产生，因此需要更深入地评估其是否真正复现了系统动态。

Method: 提出一个评估框架，针对确定性与随机神经求解器，通过演化邻近初始状态的集成并与直接数值模拟进行比较，从误差形成、集成几何和极端事件三个互补方面考察学习到的动态。

Result: 在二维Kolmogorov流上的实验揭示了传统分数掩盖的局限：更小的轨迹误差可能源于更弱的误差放大而非更准确的局部更新；模型可匹配集成的整体散布与有效维度，却无法捕捉邻近状态发散的空间方向；匹配总体事件频率也可能掩盖对持续极端事件的预测失败。

Conclusion: 预测精度的提升并不必然意味着更高的动态保真度。该框架使这一区分变得可测量，为评估神经PDE求解器的进展是否更好地捕捉了底层动态提供了具体标准。

Abstract: Neural PDE solvers can achieve low prediction errors, but do they reproduce the dynamics of the systems they model? Prediction scores alone offer an incomplete answer: they measure agreement with reference solutions but provide limited insight into how errors accumulate, nearby states diverge, or extreme events arise. We propose an evaluation framework that directly examines these behaviors in deterministic and stochastic neural solvers. By evolving ensembles of nearby initial states and comparing them with direct numerical simulation, we assess three complementary aspects of learned dynamics: error formation, ensemble geometry, and extreme events. Experiments on two-dimensional Kolmogorov flow reveal limitations that conventional scores can obscure. Smaller trajectory errors can reflect weaker error amplification despite less accurate local updates. Models can match an ensemble's overall spread and effective dimension while failing to capture the spatial directions where nearby states diverge. Similarly, matching overall event frequencies can conceal failures to predict persistent extreme events. These findings show that improved prediction accuracy does not necessarily imply greater dynamical fidelity. Our framework makes this distinction measurable, providing concrete criteria for evaluating whether advances in neural PDE solvers better capture the underlying dynamics.

</details>


### [202] [EVFormer: An Egocentric Vision-EMG Bidirectional Attention Model for Bimanual Hand Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06970)
*JiaCheng Ge,SiYu Zhang,ShengJie Li,XinTong Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EVFormer融合RGB图像与前200ms双侧手腕sEMG，通过双模态编码与门控融合估计44个手部关节角度，误差11.482°，较纯视觉降低13.20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一视角双手手部姿态估计对虚拟交互、可穿戴控制与康复很重要，但自遮挡、双手接触和物体操作会导致视觉观测退化，仅靠视觉难以稳定估计。

Method: 提出EVFormer多模态框架：输入当前RGB帧与之前200ms的双侧手腕表面肌电（sEMG），分别编码视觉空间特征与sEMG时序特征；通过顺序双向交叉注意力实现跨模态信息交换；再用特征级门控融合整合两种模态，回归44个手指与手腕关节角度。

Result: 在单个公开同步EgoEMG录制上的单被试可行性研究中（按时间划分训练/验证/测试），296个测试样本上EVFormer平均绝对误差为11.482°，优于纯视觉、纯sEMG、晚期融合和训练均值基线（13.228–13.610°）；相对纯视觉降低13.20%，相对晚期融合降低14.23%，并在五类手势中的四类取得最低误差。

Conclusion: 结果初步表明，第一视角视觉与sEMG的特征级交互可提升双手手部姿态估计；但跨被试、跨录制会话、跨传感器佩戴位置及真实交互条件下的泛化性仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Egocentric bimanual hand pose estimation is important for virtual interaction, wearable control, and rehabilitation, but visual observations are often degraded by self-occlusion, hand-hand contact, and object manipulation. We propose EVFormer, a multimodal framework that combines the current RGB frame with the preceding 200 ms of bilateral wrist surface electromyography (sEMG) to estimate 44 finger and wrist joint angles. EVFormer separately encodes visual spatial features and sEMG temporal features, enables cross-modal information exchange through sequential bidirectional cross-attention, and integrates the two modalities using feature-wise gated fusion. We evaluate EVFormer in a single-participant feasibility study using one synchronized public EgoEMG recording with chronologically separated training, validation, and test splits. On 296 test samples, EVFormer achieves a mean absolute error of 11.482 degrees, compared with 13.228-13.610 degrees for vision-only, sEMG-only, late-fusion, and training-mean baselines. This corresponds to relative error reductions of 13.20% compared with the vision-only model and 14.23% compared with late fusion. EVFormer also achieves the lowest error in four of the five evaluated gesture classes. These results provide preliminary evidence that feature-level interaction between egocentric vision and sEMG can improve bimanual hand pose estimation. Further evaluation across participants, recording sessions, sensor placements, and real-world interaction conditions is required to establish the generalizability of the approach.

</details>


### [203] [Uncertainty in Representation Learning on Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06974)
*Yuqicheng Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文系统研究知识图谱嵌入（KGE）中的三类不确定性（知识不确定性、算法不确定性、预测不确定性），并提出一系列模型无关、理论可保证的方法提升KGE预测的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管KGE方法在基准测试上表现强劲，但其预测常缺乏有原则的可靠性保证，限制了在高风险场景中的应用。此外，不确定性贯穿整个KGE流程：从输入知识的不完整或概率性，到随机训练与预测。

Method: 针对算法不确定性：证明相同设置下训练的模型会产生显著不同的预测，提出基于投票的聚合框架；针对预测不确定性：将保形预测（conformal prediction）适配到KGE，构建具有无分布覆盖保证的答案集，并扩展为谓词条件可靠性保证；针对知识不确定性：为带置信度评分的三元组开发统计有效的预测区间，并提出基于嵌入的方法近似统计本体上的概率推理，具形式化可靠性保证。

Result: 这些互补的、模型无关的方法能有效缓解训练不稳定性、提供无分布覆盖与谓词条件可靠性保证，并在知识不确定性下实现统计有效的预测区间与具形式化保证的概率推理近似。

Conclusion: 该论文提供了一套实用且具理论基础的KGE不确定性处理方法，超越单纯预测精度，推动知识图谱推理向可靠且不确定性感知的方向发展。

Abstract: Knowledge graph embedding (KGE) methods represent entities and predicates in continuous vector spaces to infer missing knowledge. Despite strong benchmark performance, their predictions often lack principled reliability guarantees, limiting their use in high-stakes applications. Moreover, uncertainty arises throughout the KGE pipeline, from incomplete or probabilistic input knowledge to stochastic training and prediction. This thesis systematically investigates three sources of uncertainty in KGE: knowledge uncertainty, arising from incomplete, noisy, or probabilistic input knowledge; algorithmic uncertainty, induced by randomness in model training; and predictive uncertainty, concerning the reliability of model outputs. To address algorithmic uncertainty, the thesis demonstrates that models trained under identical settings can produce substantially different predictions and introduces a voting-based aggregation framework to mitigate this instability. To quantify predictive uncertainty, it adapts conformal prediction to KGE, constructing answer sets with distribution-free coverage guarantees and extending them to provide predicate-conditional reliability guarantees. To support reasoning under knowledge uncertainty, it develops statistically valid prediction intervals for confidence-scored triples and an embedding-based approach to approximate probabilistic reasoning over statistical ontologies with formal soundness guarantees. Together, these complementary, model-agnostic methods provide a practical and theoretically grounded approach to uncertainty in KGE, advancing beyond predictive accuracy toward reliable and uncertainty-aware knowledge graph reasoning.

</details>


### [204] [A Data-Driven Framework for Unsupervised Monitoring of Transmission Systems Using End-of-Line Testing Data: A Case Study at Ford Motor Company](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06980)
*Mohammad N. Bisheh,Mehrdad Moradi,Parinaz Farajiparvar,Colin Brady,Rajesh Gupta,Xueling Li,Javad Navaei,Milad Parvaneh,Kamran Paynabar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向高维时序数据的多元监控框架，先预处理（去除无效样本、时间对齐），再做非线性降维并由控制图进行Phase I异常检测，支持有无监督两种设置。在福特汽车制造实际生产数据上验证，准确率、召回率、F1显著优于公司现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传感技术快速发展带来高维、强非线性、强时序依赖的数据，传统统计监控难以刻画非线性结构，端线检测中常用的阈值和启发式规则难以发现有效异常特征，而深度学习/生成式模型虽预测能力强，却在数据采集昂贵、需可解释、低延迟、计算高效且面向非技术人员的场景中不适用。

Method: 提出两阶段多元监控框架。第一阶段：预处理，剔除不完整与非信息性样本，并对时序数据进行时间对齐。第二阶段：进行非线性降维，随后通过基于控制图的Phase I监控程序实现异常检测。框架依据训练时是否具备真实标签，可分别在无监督与有监督设置下使用，且模块化结构便于按不同领域与运营需求调整组件。

Result: 在福特汽车公司实际汽车制造生产数据上评估，所提方法在准确率、召回率和F1分数上均优于公司现有模型，分别从0.50、0.30、0.429提升至0.625、1.00、0.769。

Conclusion: 该框架在保证可解释性、低延迟与计算效率的同时，有效提升了高维时序数据的异常检测性能，具备在工业实际场景中落地并泛化至其他领域的潜力。

Abstract: Sensing technologies have advanced rapidly across industries ranging from energy to automotive manufacturing. These systems generate high-dimensional (HD) data characterized by complex nonlinear patterns and strong temporal dependencies. Traditional statistical monitoring methods are often limited in their ability to capture such nonlinear structure. Likewise, many analytical approaches used in End-of-Line testing rely on predefined thresholds and heuristic rules, which restrict their ability to detect informative anomaly signatures in HD temporal data. In contrast, while modern deep learning and generative AI models offer strong predictive capabilities, they are often unsuitable in applications where data are costly to collect and where the monitoring system must remain interpretable, low-latency, computationally efficient, and usable by non-technical practitioners. To overcome these limitations, we propose an advanced multivariate monitoring framework for HD data. The framework operates in two stages. In the first stage, the data are preprocessed to remove incomplete and non-informative samples and to temporally align time series data. In the second stage, nonlinear dimensionality reduction is performed, followed by anomaly detection through a control chart based phase I monitoring procedure. The framework can be used in both unsupervised and supervised settings, depending on the availability of ground truth labels during training. Moreover, its flexible and modular structure allows practitioners to adapt its components to different domains and operational requirements. We evaluate the proposed framework on real production data from an automotive manufacturing environment at Ford Motor Company. The proposed method achieves higher accuracy, recall, and F1 score than the company's existing model, improving these metrics from 0.50, 0.30, and 0.429 to 0.625, 1.00, and 0.769, respectively.

</details>


### [205] [An Information-Theoretic Evaluation Framework for Benchmark and Model Diagnosis in Knowledge Tracing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06988)
*Houru Jiang,Zixi Wang,Tengteng Cheng,Xueyi Li,Mingliang Hou,Jiaqi Zheng,Renqiang Luo,Teng Guo,Zitao Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于信息论的KT基准诊断框架，用CTW将预测投影到共享的因果不确定性坐标上，通过分熵带评估模型性能，揭示KT模型改进的局部非均匀性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识追踪（KT）模型多用AUC、准确率等聚合指标评估，这些全局分数掩盖了剩余错误的来源，也无法判断基准是否接近饱和。虽然现实KT场景中估计全局理论性能极限很困难，但可以量化局部可预测性。

Method: 提出信息论评估框架：在项目-作答历史与当前项目查询上使用上下文树加权（CTW），作为可操作的因果不确定性坐标；并将其与完整KT信息集下未观测的局部不可约不确定性（LIU）区分开。通过将预测投影到该共享不确定性坐标，按不同熵带而非全局层面评估模型性能增益。

Result: 在NIPS Task 3/4和Algebra 2005数据集上的综合评估显示，模型改进高度非均匀：现代KT模型在高熵区域取得显著增益；额外的项目感知参考、对数损失和等频分析支持这种局部化结论；框架也标示出表面增益需警惕噪声敏感行为的区域。

Conclusion: 该框架通过同时呈现局部建模失败与真实增益，为研究残余预测结构以及当前KT基准和模型的局限提供了诊断工具。

Abstract: Knowledge tracing (KT) models are predominantly evaluated using aggregate metrics such as area under the curve (AUC) and accuracy. However, these global scores obscure where the remaining errors originate and fail to indicate whether a benchmark is approaching saturation. While estimating a global theoretical performance limit is challenging in realistic KT settings, it is possible to quantify local predictability. To address this, we propose an information-theoretic evaluation framework for KT benchmark diagnosis. We use Context Tree Weighting (CTW) on item-response histories and current-item queries as an operational causal uncertainty coordinate, while distinguishing it from the unobserved Local Irreducible Uncertainty (LIU) under the full KT information set. By projecting predictions onto this shared uncertainty coordinate, we evaluate model performance gains across distinct entropy bands rather than only at the global level. Comprehensive evaluations on NIPS Task 3/4 and Algebra 2005 reveal that model improvements are highly non-uniform. Modern KT models show substantial gains in high-entropy regions, and additional item-aware references, log-loss, and equal-frequency analyses support this localization. The framework also flags regions where apparent gains require checks for noise-sensitive behavior. By surfacing these local modeling failures alongside genuine gains, this approach provides a diagnostic tool for studying both residual predictive structure and the limitations of current KT benchmarks and models.

</details>


### [206] [Repair Lot Skyline: A Weighted Constraint Satisfaction Approach to Pavement Repair Optimization from Geospatial Hazard Density](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06989)
*Takato Yasuno,Keita Kobayashi,Ryuta Sakaguchi,Takuya Okamoto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将路面病害修复计划形式化为"修复批次天际线问题"（Repair Lot Skyline），在链age空间上构建加权约束满足问题（WCSP），实现仅需单期病害调查的可执行修复批次规划，并联合调优最小批次长度与历史上下文半径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 路面管理机构需要把空间分布的病害清单转化为有界、可执行的修复批次计划：坑槽等事故关键缺陷必须处理，裂缝等低风险缺陷仅在收益高于成本时才纳入，而历史修补位置仅作为再退化风险信号而不直接触发修复。现有方法多依赖时间维度的退化预测，需多期数据且对未来退化做出假设。

Method: 将问题形式化为修复批次天际线问题：定义在链age（沿路距离）而非时间上的WCSP，仅需单期病害调查，不对未来退化作任何断言。识别143个候选危险簇（61个硬约束、82个软约束）。进一步将最小批次长度L_min与历史上下文半径κ形式化为四目标联合超参数搜索。

Result: 最终计划合并106个批次，总长1,997.4 m，为全部软候选纳入时2,385.9 m的83.7%；覆盖100%坑槽（138/138）、91.6%裂缝（404/441），捕获93.6%（542/579）总危险收益。天际线前沿显示明显递减收益：剩余37个被排除软候选仅增加6.8%收益却使修复长度增加19.4%。推荐配置（L_min=14.7 m，κ=10 m）使修复班组出动次数较未调优默认减少7.1%，代价是预算增大12.9%、裂缝覆盖率下降1.1个百分点。

Conclusion: 该WCSP框架在链age空间上为路面修复批次规划提供了无需未来退化假设的可执行方案，并通过天际线前沿揭示收益-成本权衡；联合超参数调优可在可控预算代价下显著减少班组出动次数。

Abstract: Pavement agencies must translate a spatially distributed distress inventory into a bounded, actionable repair-lot plan: accident-critical defects (potholes) must always be addressed, lower-risk defects (cracks) should be included only when their benefit justifies the repair cost, and historical patch locations signal re-degradation risk without themselves triggering repair. We formalize this as a Repair Lot Skyline problem: a Weighted Constraint Satisfaction Problem (WCSP) defined over chainage (distance along the road) rather than over time, so that it requires only a single-epoch distress survey and makes no claim about future deterioration. The WCSP identifies 143 candidate hazard clusters (61 hard, 82 soft), of which 106 are merged into a final repair plan totaling 1,997.4 m---83.7% of the 2,385.9 m that would be required if every soft candidate were included regardless of cost. This plan covers 100% of observed potholes (138/138) and 91.6% of observed cracks (404/441), capturing 93.6% (542/579) of the total hazard benefit available in the full candidate set. The skyline frontier shows pronounced diminishing returns beyond this point: the remaining 37 excluded soft candidates would add only 6.8% additional benefit for a 19.4% increase in repair length. We further formalize the minimum-lot-length $L_{\min}$ and historical-context radius $κ$ as a joint, four-objective hyperparameter search over this WCSP; on the same case study, the recommended configuration ($L_{\min} = 14.7$ m, $κ= 10$ m) reduces repair-crew mobilizations by 7.1% relative to an untuned default, at the cost of a 12.9% larger budget and a 1.1-percentage-point lower crack coverage.

</details>


### [207] [DART-ES: Difficulty-Aware Reweighting and Targeted Replay for Fine-Tuning LLMs with Evolution Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06993)
*Zhishen Sun,Hongzhan Wang,Sizhe Dang,Guang Dai,Haishan Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DART-ES，通过难度感知重加权与稀有可解样本回放改进进化策略，仅用前向计算实现大模型全参数微调，性能与效率均优于ES和GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准进化策略（ES）在微调大语言模型时，对所有问题统一平均奖励，并把问题级种群反馈压缩为单一标量，无法刻画每个问题的学习价值随模型能力变化的情况，导致扰动方向评估与训练数据分配不理想。

Method: DART-ES从扰动种群的通过率估计每个问题的局部可解性，并聚合历史观测构建动态难度状态；该共享状态同时指导连续难度重加权和稀有可解样本回放，从而改进扰动方向评估与训练数据分配，且不引入额外难度模型或反向传播。

Result: 在五个基座模型上均优于ES，平均准确率由72.07%提升至73.53%，超过GRPO在GSM8K上的73.26%；在五个数学推理基准上平均49.20%（ES为48.34%），与强7B RL模型相当。在指令微调、代码生成及14B模型的Countdown任务上均有稳定增益。效率方面，每步运行时间较GRPO减少15.2%–50.2%，单GPU峰值显存降低21.1%–51.1%，甚至比GRPO+LoRA更省时省显存。

Conclusion: DART-ES在保持ES内存高效全参数微调优势的同时，显著提升性能并改善系统效率，展现出良好的跨任务泛化性与向更大模型扩展的能力。

Abstract: Evolution Strategies (ES) enable memory efficient full parameter fine-tuning of large language models (LLMs) using only forward computation. However, standard ES uniformly averages rewards across problems and compresses problem level population feedback into a single scalar, making it difficult to capture how the learning value of each problem changes with model capability.
