To address this limitation, we propose Difficulty-Aware Reweighting and Targeted Replay for Evolution Strategies (DART-ES). DART-ES estimates the local solvability of each problem from its pass rate across the perturbation population and aggregates historical observations to construct a dynamic difficulty state. This shared state jointly guides continuous difficulty reweighting and rare solvable sample replay, thereby improving perturbation direction evaluation and training data allocation without introducing an additional difficulty model or backpropagation. Extensive experiments show that DART-ES achieves good fine-tuning performance. DART-ES outperforms ES on all five base models and improves the average accuracy from 72.07\% to 73.53\%, exceeding the 73.26\% achieved by GRPO on GSM8K. Across five challenging mathematical reasoning benchmarks, DART-ES achieves an average accuracy of 49.20\%, compared with 48.34\% for ES and remains competitive with strong 7B models trained with RL. Further experiments show consistent gains in instruction tuning, code generation and the Countdown task with a 14B model, demonstrating strong generalization across tasks and scalability to larger models. Beyond performance gains, DART-ES also shows clear advantages in system efficiency. It reduces runtime per step by 15.2\%--50.2\% and peak memory usage per GPU by 21.1\%--51.1\% compared with GRPO. Despite performing full parameter fine-tuning, DART-ES also requires less runtime and GPU memory than GRPO+LoRA.

</details>


### [208] [Mask-Guided KV Cache Eviction in Block Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06996)
*Gleb Molodtsov,Ekaterina Alimaskina,Evgeny Uskov,Artur Zagitov,Aleksandr Beznosikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MaskAhead 是一种免训练方法，通过单一掩码查询排序机制同时解决块扩散语言模型中的 KV 选择与淘汰问题，显著降低显存并加速生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 块扩散语言模型在生成过程中需维护庞大的 KV 缓存并在每个去噪步都对其做注意力，导致显存容量和生成速度受限。要降低成本，必须解决两个问题：当前块去噪应使用哪些历史 token（选择），以及哪些 token 应保留在内存中供未来块使用（淘汰）。

Method: 提出 MaskAhead，一种免训练方法，用单一基于掩码查询的排序机制同时完成选择与淘汰：当前块掩码引导选择，未来掩码块的探针引导淘汰，两者都按对注意力输出的估计贡献对 KV 条目排序。其量化变体 Q-MaskAhead 直接从低位 KV 计算选择与注意力，基本保留所选条目。

Result: 在 Fast-dLLM-v2、DreamReasoner 和 LLaDA2.0-mini 上，覆盖长生成推理、长提示问答和针检索任务。长提示问答中，MaskAhead 相对稠密推理平均减少 9.5 倍 KV 显存，平均 F1 仅损失 1.2 分；Q-MaskAhead 将减少量提升至 20.1 倍，平均 F1 损失 2.3 分。batch-32 系统测试中，MaskAhead 相比稠密推理实现 1.23 倍端到端和 1.68 倍解码阶段加速。

Conclusion: MaskAhead 以无需训练的统一掩码查询排序机制，在大幅压缩 KV 缓存的同时保持较低性能损失，并能带来实际端到端与解码阶段加速，为块扩散语言模型的高效推理提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Block diffusion language models keep a large key-value (KV) cache throughout generation and attend to it at every denoising step, limiting both memory capacity and generation speed. Reducing these costs requires deciding which past tokens to use for denoising the current block (selection) and which to keep in memory for future blocks (eviction). We propose MaskAhead, a training-free method that solves both tasks with a single mask-query-based ranking mechanism. Current-block masks guide selection, while probes of upcoming masked blocks guide eviction. Both rank KV entries by their estimated contribution to the attention output. Our quantized variant, Q-MaskAhead, computes selection and attention directly from low-bit KV, largely preserving the selected entries. Experiments on Fast-dLLM-v2, DreamReasoner, and LLaDA2.0-mini cover long-generation reasoning, long-prompt question answering, and needle-in-a-haystack retrieval. On long-prompt QA, MaskAhead reduces KV memory by $9.5\times$ on average with a 1.2-point mean F1 loss relative to dense inference. Q-MaskAhead increases the reduction to $20.1\times$ with a 2.3-point mean F1 loss. In a batch-32 systems profile, MaskAhead achieves $1.23\times$ end-to-end and $1.68\times$ decode-stage speedups over dense inference.

</details>


### [209] [Should We Skip Diffusion?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07002)
*Yiping Ji,James Martens,Simon Lucey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DDT-RFE，通过移除编码器块中自注意力与MLP操作周围的残差连接，并将输入patch嵌入与中间及最终编码器特征融合，使编码器能学习更抽象的表示，同时为解码器保留所需的多层级信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在生成图像的同时学习语义表示。在解耦扩散Transformer（DDT）中，条件编码器提供特征以引导速度解码器去噪，这些特征需在各级噪声下同时捕获高层抽象结构与低层细节。但编码器中的跳跃/残差连接使浅层特征绕过后续变换，可能限制渐进式抽象，或至少难以解耦不同抽象层次。

Method: 提出DDT-RFE：移除每个编码器块中自注意力与MLP操作周围的残差连接，同时保持训练稳定；为保留抽象所丢弃但解码器仍需的信息，将输入patch嵌入与中间及最终编码器特征融合，形成编码器输出，使解码器可访问多个编码深度的信息，而每个编码器块能学习更抽象的表示。

Result: DDT-RFE在多种视觉理解任务上相较DDT取得整体提升，包括图像分类、语义分割、目标发现和语义对应，且使用的编码器块更少；在ImageNet图像生成上也取得更低的FID。

Conclusion: 移除编码器残差连接并融合多层级特征，能有效提升编码器表示的抽象程度与解码器所需信息的可用性，在理解与生成任务上均优于DDT且更高效。

Abstract: Diffusion models learn semantic representations while generating images. In the Decoupled Diffusion Transformer (DDT), a condition encoder provides features that guide a velocity decoder in denoising. To enable effective denoising at all noise levels, these features must capture both high-level abstract structures and low-level details. However, skip/residual connections in the encoder allow shallow features to bypass successive transformations, which may limit progressive abstraction, or at least make it difficult to disentangle different levels of abstraction. We propose DDT-RFE, which removes the residual connections around the Self-Attention and MLP operations in each encoder block while maintaining stable training. To retain the information that abstraction discards but that the decoder still needs, we fuse the input patch embedding with intermediate and final encoder features to form the encoder output. The decoder thus has access to information from multiple encoder depths, while each encoder block is able to learn more abstract representations. DDT-RFE achieves overall improvements over DDT across visual understanding tasks, including image classification, semantic segmentation, object discovery, and semantic correspondence, while using fewer encoder blocks. It also achieves a lower FID for image generation on ImageNet.

</details>


### [210] [STOCK-JEPA: Prior-Anchored Latent Revision Representation Learning in Equity Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07006)
*Yizhi Luo,Jiahe Yi,Jianhui Zhang,Shuo Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Stock-JEPA框架，将经典线性金融先验与深度表征学习结合，通过"先验锚点+可预测增量修正"的联合嵌入预测学习股市表征，理论与实验均验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 股市数据信噪比低。黑盒深度模型能捕捉复杂模式，但易过拟合噪声且缺乏显式经济结构；经典线性金融模型可解释，但假设过于简化，无法捕捉非线性信号。本文旨在融合两者优势。

Method: 1) 用低复杂度金融模型生成多期收益与风险的固定统计量，经先验投影器映射到目标编码器隐空间作为锚点；2) 设计上下文条件修正预测器，从历史上下文估计未来表征相对锚点的可预测位移，两支路分别用独立损失更新（锚点学习先验统计量，修正捕捉额外可预测信息）；3) 冻结全部表征模块，训练下游读出层，通过横截面排序与组合表现评估预测。理论证明最优修正使先验锚点对同一未来表征的期望平方误差恰好降低 E[||Δ||²]。

Result: 在中国和美国大规模股票池上，Stock-JEPA在5项关键评价指标上优于13个强基线。消融实验与表征分析进一步表明所学修正在股市表征学习中具有价值。

Conclusion: 将可解释的线性金融先验与深度联合嵌入预测学习相结合，可有效学习股市表征，其带来的额外可预测信号增益具有理论保证并获实验支持。

Abstract: Learning effective representations helps characterize the structure and dynamics of equity markets from financial data with a low signal-to-noise ratio. Black-box deep models can capture complex patterns but may overfit sample noise and lack explicit economic structure. Meanwhile, classic linear financial models provide interpretable references, but their oversimplified assumptions leave non-linear signals uncaptured. To combine the strengths of these two directions, we propose Stock-JEPA, a joint-embedding predictive framework that learns predictable incremental revisions relative to a point-in-time financial prior. First, we leverage a low-complexity financial model to produce fixed statistics summarizing multi-horizon return and risk. A prior projector then maps these statistics into the target encoder's latent space as an anchor. Second, we design a context-conditioned revision predictor to estimate the future representation's predictable displacement from the anchor. Separate losses update the two branches: the anchor learns from prior statistics, while the revision captures additional predictable information from historical context. Third, we freeze all representation modules and train a downstream readout, evaluating its forecasts through cross-sectional ranking and portfolio performance. Theoretically, we prove that optimal revision reduces the prior anchor's expected squared error for the same future representation by exactly $\mathbb{E}[\|\boldsymbolΔ\|_2^2]$. This non-negative gain is the expected squared magnitude of the additional signal predictable from historical context. Experimentally, Stock-JEPA outperforms 13 strong baselines across large-scale China and U.S. equity universes on 5 key evaluation metrics. Ablation studies and representation analysis further demonstrate the value of the learned revisions for representation learning in equity markets.

</details>


### [211] [Identifiable World Models from Pretrained Diffusion Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07028)
*Ruchi Sandilya,Conor Liston,Logan Grosenick*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 冻结的预训练扩散模型可通过轻量对齐映射获得可识别、可解释的潜在坐标，无需重训练生成主干。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散世界模型预测精度高，但其潜在坐标未必能恢复真实状态变量与因果交互，作者希望在不重训练生成主干的前提下赋予其可识别坐标。

Method: 提出Contrastive Diffusion Alignment (ConDA)，在冻结扩散潜变量上学习轻量对齐映射，将辅助变量非线性ICA保证迁移其中；基于TCL/GCL/CEBRA三种方案与TDRL、CaRiNG、IDOL、temporal SuaVE、iVAE对比。

Result: TCL与GCL实现近完美的分块状态恢复与竞争性的滞后图恢复，在模拟落体系统中精确恢复；在模拟双足机器人中，学习动态可恢复对留出控制扰动的响应符号与时间结构。

Conclusion: 冻结生成扩散模型可被赋予可识别、结构可解释且有助于分析干预相关动态的坐标。

Abstract: Diffusion-based world models can generate and predict trajectories in high-dimensional dynamical systems, but predictive accuracy does not imply that their latent coordinates recover the underlying state variables or causal interactions. We ask whether a frozen pretrained diffusion model can be equipped with identifiable coordinates without retraining its generative backbone. We show that auxiliary-variable nonlinear ICA guarantees can be transferred to Contrastive Diffusion Alignment (ConDA), which learns only a lightweight alignment map on top of frozen diffusion latents. Under standard TCL/GCL assumptions, the aligned representation identifies latent dynamical states up to permutation and componentwise invertible transformations, preserves the latent dynamic structural causal model, and reduces lagged graph recovery to transition-Jacobian sparsity. We evaluate TCL-, GCL-, and CEBRA-based ConDA against TDRL, CaRiNG, IDOL, temporal SuaVE, and iVAE across physical and robotic video systems. TCL and GCL achieve near-perfect blockwise state recovery and competitive lagged graph recovery, including exact recovery in a simulated falling-body system. In a simulated bipedal robot, learned dynamics recover the sign and temporal structure of responses to held-out control perturbations. These results show that a frozen generative diffusion model can be equipped with coordinates that are identifiable, structurally interpretable, and useful for analyzing intervention-relevant dynamics.

</details>


### [212] [Shaping the Wind: Nested Potentials for Kinematically Admissible Urban Wind Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07033)
*Yidi Wang,Yunhe Zhang,Jiawei Gu,Ziyue Qiao,Pengyang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Sculpt嵌套势框架，将几何相关约束内嵌于参数化中，直接生成满足局部质量守恒与壁面不可穿透的暂态城市风场，并发布UrbanWindFlow数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测暂态城市风对理解城市微气候与设计气候韧性城市至关重要。建筑解析大涡模拟（LES）精度高但每个布局计算成本巨大，神经代理模型虽快，却仅最小化速度预测误差，无法保证局部质量守恒与壁面不可穿透这两项运动学可行性条件。其根源在于输出表示无约束：几何条件只引导预测而不限制其落在可行速度场集合内；对边界违规的修正又会改变相邻流体单元的通量平衡，进而危及局部质量守恒。

Method: 提出Sculpt——一种嵌套势框架，将耦合的、依赖几何的约束直接构建进参数化中。该嵌套参数化在原生三维交错网格上，通过体积矢量势的离散旋度生成无散速度更新；共享标量势约束矢量势的边界值，使同一算子同时施加不可穿透条件，无需逐步压力投影。针对反向传播经旋度算子会衰减大尺度梯度信号的问题，在多分辨率上参数化体积势以更好捕捉大尺度流动结构。同时构建UrbanWindFlow数据集，覆盖多种城市形态与来流条件。

Result: 通过UrbanWindFlow数据集，可同时评估预测精度与运动学可行性；框架在无逐步骤压力投影的情况下生成满足无散与不可穿透约束的速度场，并借多分辨率参数化改善大尺度流动结构的捕捉。

Conclusion: 将几何相关的物理约束直接嵌入参数化，是实现既准确又运动学可行的神经城市风场代理的有效途径；UrbanWindFlow为联合评估精度与可行性提供了基准。

Abstract: Predicting transient urban winds is fundamental to understanding urban microclimates and designing climate-resilient cities. Building-resolving large-eddy simulation produces detailed incompressible urban wind fields at substantial computational cost for each layout. Neural surrogates offer a faster alternative by learning to predict the evolution of velocity fields. However, minimizing velocity prediction error does not guarantee local mass conservation and wall impermeability, which together define kinematic admissibility. This limitation stems from an unconstrained output representation: geometry conditioning guides predictions but does not restrict them to admissible velocity fields. Correcting boundary violations in these outputs changes the flux balance in adjacent fluid cells and may consequently compromise local mass conservation. To address the challenge, we propose Sculpt, a nested potential framework that builds the coupled, geometry-dependent constraints directly into its parameterization. This nested parameterization generates divergence-free velocity updates through the discrete curl of a volume vector potential on the native three-dimensional staggered grid. A shared scalar potential constrains the vector potential's boundary values so that the same operator also enforces impermeability, without a per-step pressure projection. Because backpropagation through this curl attenuates large-scale gradient signals, we parameterize the volume potential at multiple resolutions to better capture large-scale flow structures. We introduce UrbanWindFlow, an LES dataset spanning urban morphologies and inflow conditions, to evaluate accuracy and kinematic admissibility together.

</details>


### [213] [The Premise Is the Problem: Exchangeability Failure in Self-Monitored Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07038)
*Weijia Han,Lisha Qu,Zhenda Li,Liying Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究多步时间序列预测中"用与自适应相同的预测误差来监控自身"的可靠性，指出重叠目标与误差依赖会破坏监控统计保证的假设，导致误报、响应损害预测质量，并可能掩盖持续变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代预测模型部署后常需更新以适应数据变化，但更新也可能让预测变差，因此系统需要可靠监控来检测有害变化并触发保护。一个自然的设计是用驱动更新的同一预测误差来做监控，但这一自监控设计的统计保证是否仍然成立尚不清楚，本文正是要回答这一问题。

Method: 作者在多步时间序列预测场景下，分析当监控与自适应使用同一反馈时监控统计保证的有效性，重点考察重叠目标（overlapping targets）与预测误差间依赖（dependence in forecast errors）对保证所依赖关键假设的影响，并对比自适应模型与其自身监控、以及原始冻结模型的信号。

Result: 研究表明，重叠目标和预测误差依赖会破坏保证所需的关键假设，使监控在未发生有害变化时也发出警报，且其响应会进一步损害预测质量；同时，自适应可能将持续变化从自身监控中隐藏，而原始冻结模型反而保留了更清晰的信号。

Conclusion: 这些结果揭示了自监控自适应中的一个基本失效模式。可靠的部署需要检查监控器的假设、在真实反馈下比较自适应与冻结模型，并限制每个保护性响应的影响。

Abstract: Modern forecasting models are often updated after deployment so they can respond to changing data. These updates can also make predictions worse, so practical systems need a reliable monitor that can detect harmful changes and trigger protection. A natural design is to monitor the same prediction errors that guide the updates. This paper asks whether the statistical guarantee behind such a monitor remains valid when monitoring and adaptation use the same feedback. We study this question in multi-step time-series forecasting. We show that overlapping targets and dependence in forecast errors can break a key assumption required by the guarantee. The monitor may then raise alarms even when no harmful change has occurred, and its response can further damage prediction quality. We also find that adaptation can hide sustained changes from its own monitor, while the original frozen model retains a clearer signal. These results expose a basic failure mode in self-monitored adaptation. They show why reliable deployment requires checking the monitor's assumptions, comparing adaptation with the frozen model under realistic feedback, and limiting the effect of every protective response.

</details>


### [214] [TRIAGE: Direction-Aware Mismatch Stabilization of Native NVFP4 Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07043)
*Zhen Li,Shuai Zhang,Yanggan Gu,Yiming Zhang,Yang Yu,Mingfa Feng,Congkai Xie,Shuang Yu,Junjie Lai,Hongxia Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究低精度(如NVFP4)强化学习训练中学习器与采样器执行不一致对策略优化的影响，提出方向感知的稳定化方法TRIAGE，在保持原生W4A4前向执行的同时实现稳定训练与全精度性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低精度执行可显著加速大语言模型的强化学习，但学习器与采样器之间的执行差异(不匹配)会破坏策略优化的稳定性。仅凭不匹配的量级无法识别其对策略梯度方向的影响，因此需要刻画不匹配与策略梯度方向的交互作用并据此设计稳定化方法。

Method: 作者区分了局部放大与收缩两类更新贡献，发现在原生NVFP4运行中存在放大区域间的早期不平衡，偏向负优势、负gap的更新，其尾部token在全局扩散前先集中于少数响应片段。据此提出TRIAGE：一种方向感知的稳定化方法，使用片段级诊断有选择地重新平衡策略梯度更新，并对残余严重不匹配施加有界修复。该方法修改优化目标，同时在采样器和学习器上保留原生NVFP4的W4A4前向执行。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B和Qwen3-30B-A3B上的实验表明，训练全程优化稳定，在五个数学推理基准上达到全精度水平性能；带TRIAGE的原生NVFP4相比BF16可实现最高2.3倍的rollout吞吐量。

Conclusion: 通过刻画不匹配与策略梯度方向的交互并进行方向感知的更新重平衡与有界修复，TRIAGE在保持低精度执行效率优势的同时解决了低精度RL训练的稳定性问题，兼顾了性能与吞吐。

Abstract: Low-precision execution can substantially accelerate reinforcement learning (RL) for large language models, but discrepancies between learner and sampler execution can destabilize policy optimization. In this paper, we characterize the interaction between mismatch and the policy-gradient direction, distinguishing locally amplifying from contracting update contributions that mismatch magnitude alone cannot identify. In native NVFP4 runs, we observe an early imbalance between the two amplifying regions, favoring negative-advantage, negative-gap updates. Their tail tokens become concentrated in a small fraction of response segments before mismatch spreads globally. Motivated by these findings, we introduce TRIAGE, a direction-aware stabilization method that uses segment-level diagnosis to selectively rebalance policy-gradient updates and applies bounded repair to residual severe mismatch. TRIAGE modifies the optimization objective while retaining native NVFP4 weight-and activation 4-bit (W4A4) forward execution on both the sampler and learner. Experiments on Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-30B-A3B show stable optimization throughout the evaluated training horizon and achieve full precision level performance across five mathematical reasoning benchmarks, while native NVFP4 with TRIAGE provides up to 2.3x higher rollout throughput than BF16.

</details>


### [215] [Skillful Data-Driven Subseasonal Soil Moisture Forecasting: Prospects and Limits for Flash Drought Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07060)
*Noelia Otero,Atahan Özer,Miguel-Ángel Fernández-Torres,Jackie Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将 S2S 土壤湿度预测视为一个"问题表述"问题，发现残差学习与物理单位目标表示比模型结构本身更能决定技巧，并指出骤旱爆发预测仍是共同难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管中短期天气预报进展显著，骤旱等高影响事件的早期预警与 S2S 物理预测仍很困难。作者想探究 S2S 土壤湿度预测技巧的限制究竟来自模型本身，还是来自预测问题的表述方式（目标变量表示、学习策略等）。

Method: 采用基于 Vision Transformer、具备时间与空间双路径注意力的架构；引入残差学习以超越持续性基线；直接预测物理单位下的根区土壤湿度而非标准化异常；并通过分位数头微调实现概率化预测。以 2021–2022 年欧洲地区为研究区，与深度学习基线及 ECMWF 业务 S2S 系统对比。

Result: 该模型在所有预见期上取得最高的确定性技巧与概率技巧，概率分布校准良好；能可靠识别根区异常干燥状态（低于第 20 百分位）。但按多候（pentad）强度标准定义的骤旱爆发，所有现有 S2S 系统都难以预测。

Conclusion: 对 S2S 土壤湿度预测而言，技巧高低既取决于模型，也同样取决于预测问题的表述（残差学习、物理单位目标表示）。这些发现推进了数据驱动的 S2S 土壤湿度预测，同时凸显出快速干旱发展（骤旱爆发）预测仍是未解难题。

Abstract: Despite substantial progress in short-to-medium-range weather forecasting, predicting high-impact events such as flash droughts remains a key challenge for both early warning operations and physically-based subseasonal-to-seasonal (S2S) prediction systems. Here we demonstrate that, for S2S soil-moisture forecasting over Europe, forecast skill depends as much on how the prediction problem is formulated as on the forecasting model itself. Using a Vision Transformer-based architecture with dual-pathway temporal and spatial attention, we show that residual learning is essential to outperform persistence. This advantage is realized only when forecasting root-zone soil moisture in physical units rather than standardized anomalies, revealing that the target representation itself constrains predictability. A probabilistic extension via quantile-head fine-tuning further provides well-calibrated predictive distributions. Benchmarked against deep-learning and operational ECMWF S2S baselines over 2021-2022, our model achieves the highest deterministic and probabilistic skill at all lead times and reliably detects anomalously dry root-zone states (below the 20th percentile). Yet flash drought onset, defined by multi-pentad intensification criteria, remains a fundamental challenge shared across all current S2S systems. These findings advance data-driven S2S soil-moisture forecasting while highlighting the remaining challenge of predicting rapid drought development.

</details>


### [216] [Learning to Simulate Individuals from Macro Social Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07062)
*Yining Zhao,Bushi Liu,Haofei Yu,Zhengyang Qi,Shanyong Wang,Chuyue Li,Yuxiang Liu,Jiaxuan You*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 macro2mind，从预测市场价格轨迹中学习行为推理，用 GRPO 训练语言模型，将行为推理显式分解为预测步骤，并通过回溯反思课程与难度感知采样优化训练，在用户模拟任务上取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型模拟个体对新情境的反应时，其行为推理要么继承自预训练，要么依赖个体级标注学习，导致行为多样性有限，且对推理过程本身缺乏监督。作者希望从预测市场价格轨迹这一大规模群体响应记录中学习行为推理。

Method: 提出 macro2mind：使用 GRPO 结合市场信号训练语言模型。通过社会行为分解，将行为推理变为预测的显式步骤：推断代表性市场参与者群体、预测各自如何解读新闻并更新信念、推理其相互作用，并聚合为价格。同时采用回溯反思课程与难度感知采样，将训练聚焦于回溯识别群体能显著改善预测的转变，并优先选择对当前策略仍可学习的样本。

Result: 在 SWM-Bench 上，macro2mind 在 Polymarket 上取得方向准确率和相关性的最优表现。仅用市场数据训练即可零样本迁移到四个用户模拟基准（Humanual、OvertonBench、PRISM、CAD），在零样本方法中具有竞争力。作为数据生成器，可将下游模拟器在未见用户上的准确率提升 15.5 个百分点，优于其骨干模型生成数据 13.2 个百分点。

Conclusion: 从预测市场学习行为推理可有效迁移至用户模拟任务，并在零样本场景与数据生成场景中均带来显著提升，无需额外训练即可应用。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to simulate how individuals respond to new situations, yet the behavioral reasoning behind these responses is either inherited from pretraining or learned from individual-level annotations, which offer limited behavioral diversity and little supervision of the reasoning itself. We propose to learn behavioral reasoning from prediction markets, whose price trajectories record how populations respond to real-world events at scale. We introduce macro2mind, which trains a language model with GRPO using market signals. A social behavioral decomposition makes behavioral reasoning an explicit step of forecasting: the model infers representative groups of market participants, predicts how each interprets the news and updates its beliefs, reasons about their interactions, and aggregates these responses into a price. A hindsight-regret curriculum with difficulty-aware sampling focuses training on transitions where hindsight-identified groups substantially improve the forecast while prioritizing examples that remain learnable for the current policy. The learned reasoning applies to user simulation without further training. On SWM-Bench, macro2mind achieves state-of-the-art directional accuracy and correlation on Polymarket. Trained on market data, it transfers zero-shot to four user-simulation benchmarks (Humanual, OvertonBench, PRISM, and CAD) and has competitive performance among zero-shot methods. Used as a data generator, macro2mind also raises a downstream simulator's accuracy on unseen users by 15.5 points, outperforming data generated by its backbone by 13.2 points.

</details>


### [217] [What Must Replay Preserve? Separating Correctable Bias from Class Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07077)
*BoRen Deng,Xiangyue Ma,Chenglong Li,Xiaoting Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个诊断框架，将缓存预测视为时间异质监督，分离存储时已知类与之后学到的类，并引入任务级偏移评估。结果表明，删除后续类匹配的大部分代价可由该偏移纠正，而破坏类对应的小部分代价持续存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类增量学习需在无任务标签下识别所有已见类，DER、DER++等logit replay方法通过匹配历史预测缓解遗忘。但删除这种匹配虽体现其收益，其精度代价无法说明存储分数本身是否必要，也无法判断该代价在纠正分类器偏向新类后是否仍存在。

Method: 提出诊断框架：把缓存预测当作时间异质监督，区分样本存储时已知的类与之后学到的类，分别编辑两组；在保持任务内预测不变的任务级偏移前后评估每个模型。在CIFAR-100上对DER++进行实验，用固定常数替换后续学习类未刷新的存储分数，并重分配存储时已知类的非金标签分数。

Result: 在DER++上，合适固定常数可在1个百分点等价边距内替换后续学习类的未刷新存储分数；任务级偏移将删除其匹配的代价从14.9降至1.8。重分配存储时已知类的非金分数（保持其值和每任务目标概率）在偏移前代价4.3，偏移后4.0，图像蒸馏中也存在类似代价。

Conclusion: 在测试的固定头设置中，删除后续类匹配的大代价主要可由任务级偏移纠正，而破坏类对应关系的较小代价持续存在。代码与数据已公开。

Abstract: Class-incremental learning must recognize all classes seen so far without task labels. Logit replay methods such as DER and DER++ mitigate forgetting by matching the model's past predictions on stored examples. Deleting this matching reveals its benefit, but the resulting accuracy cost cannot show whether the stored scores themselves are needed, or whether the cost survives correction of the classifier's bias toward recent classes. We propose a diagnostic framework that treats a cached prediction as temporally heterogeneous supervision: it separates classes known when an example was stored from classes learned afterward, edits each group, and evaluates every model before and after a task-level offset that leaves within-task predictions unchanged. On CIFAR-100 with DER++, suitable fixed constants replace the unrefreshed stored scores of later-learned classes within an equivalence margin of 1 percentage point, and the offset reduces the cost of deleting their matching from 14.9 to 1.8 points. Reassigning the non-gold scores of classes known at storage, which preserves their values and each task's target probability, costs 4.3 points before and 4.0 after the offset, and a parallel cost persists in image distillation. In the tested fixed-head setting, the large cost of deleting later-class matching is thus mostly correctable by this offset, whereas the smaller cost of disrupting class correspondence persists. Code and data are available at anonymous.4open.science/r/replay-preserve-E22B.

</details>


### [218] [Few-Shot Bioactivity Prediction with Meta-Learning under Assay Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07079)
*Michal Kmicikiewicz,Tommy Rochussen,Vincent Fortuin,Ewa Szczurek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MetaHeta，一个通过以相关assay的辅助数据为条件来应对assay异质性的元学习框架，提升少样本生物活性预测与化合物优先级排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期药物发现中，单个assay的测量数据往往过少，难以独立训练可靠模型；元学习虽适合这种少样本场景，但assay间的异质性可能限制其效果。作者据此提出假设并进行验证。

Method: 作者首先验证元学习性能随元训练任务异质性增加而下降。为此设计MetaHeta：在相关assay的辅助数据上条件化预测，相关性的定义可从可获得的assay信息中灵活确定。架构上结合了对大规模辅助数据的线性注意力与对稀缺任务特定上下文的精确注意力，从而在有效扩展前者的同时不牺牲对后者的精确注意力。

Result: 在来自ChEMBL和BindingDB的assay上，该方法改善了少样本生物活性预测，并在回溯式贝叶斯优化中提升了下游化合物优先级排序效果。

Conclusion: assay异质性确实会削弱元学习性能，而通过条件化于相关assay辅助数据、并采用线性注意力与精确注意力结合的架构，MetaHeta能有效缓解该问题，提升少样本生物活性预测及相关下游任务表现。

Abstract: Accurate bioactivity prediction is a central challenge in early-stage drug discovery, as individual assays often contain too few measurements to train reliable models independently. Meta-learning offers a principled approach to this few-shot setting, but assay heterogeneity may limit its effectiveness. Here, we test this hypothesis and show that meta-learning performance degrades as meta-training tasks become more heterogeneous. To address this, we introduce MetaHeta, a meta-learning framework that accounts for assay heterogeneity by conditioning predictions on auxiliary data from related assays, with relatedness defined flexibly from available assay information. The architecture of MetaHeta combines linear attention over large auxiliary datasets with exact attention over scarce task-specific context, enabling efficient scaling to the former without compromising exact attention over the latter. We demonstrate the benefits of our approach on assays from ChEMBL and BindingDB, improving few-shot bioactivity prediction and downstream compound prioritization in retrospective Bayesian optimization.

</details>


### [219] [When Attention Does Not Explain the Peak: Temporal Reference vs. Forecast Output in Attention-Based Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07080)
*Yuji Akamatsu,Takao Yamanaka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文检验了负荷预测模型中跨注意力图的峰时解释有效性：注意力虽结构化、对输入敏感且可复现，但无法选择性指向预测峰值，其作用更可能是整合未来外生信息的"地平线参照"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力图常被当作模型预测依据的证据，在本文负荷预测模型中，CLS表示通过跨注意力查询未来外生"地平线token"，容易诱发一种时间性解读——高注意力地平线被视为解释了预测峰值出现的时间。论文旨在验证这一解读是否成立。

Method: 提出地平线级注意力描述子Ψ_out；在巴拿马负荷数据集31个日对齐窗口上，对比预测峰值时间误差与Ψ_out argmax的峰值时间误差；考察±1 h内注意力相对均匀基线的集中度，以及跨窗口一致性；将被替换为训练集均值的12个未来天气特征做消融；并用三个随机种子复现。

Result: 预测峰值时间中位误差为0 h、精确匹配率51.6%，而Ψ_out argmax中位误差5 h、精确匹配率0%；31个窗口中27个预测峰值更接近观测峰值。±1 h内注意力仅达均匀基线的1.16×/1.11×/1.14×（对应观测、预测、周朴素峰值），集中度弱且无选择性。但注意力分布有结构，跨窗口一致性0.83；替换天气特征后近乎均匀；结果在三种子下可复现。

Conclusion: 结构化、输入敏感且可复现的地平线级跨注意力，并不必然构成对预测行为有效的峰值选择性解释；该行为更符合内部"地平线参照"用于整合未来外生信息的功能角色，但此功能角色尚未得到因果证实。

Abstract: Attention maps are often interpreted as evidence of what a forecasting model uses when making predictions. In our load-forecasting model, a CLS representation of historical demand queries 24 future exogenous horizon tokens through cross-attention, inviting a temporal interpretation in which highly attended horizons may appear to explain forecast peak timing. We test this interpretation using a horizon-level attention descriptor, $Ψ_{\mathrm{out}}$. Across 31 day-aligned windows of the Panama load dataset, the forecast achieves a median peak-time error of 0 h and a 51.6% exact-match rate, whereas the argmax of $Ψ_{\mathrm{out}}$ has a median error of 5 h and 0% exact match. The forecast peak is closer to the observed peak in 27 of 31 windows. This dissociation is not merely an argmax artifact: within $\pm1$ h, attention reaches only $1.16\times$, $1.11\times$, and $1.14\times$ the uniform baseline around observed, predicted, and weekly-naive peaks, respectively, indicating weak and non-selective concentration. Yet the attention profile is structured, with cross-window consistency of 0.83. Replacing 12 future weather features with their training-set means makes the profile nearly uniform, showing sensitivity to future weather variation rather than fixed horizon position alone. The dissociation is also reproduced across three random-seed runs. These results show that structured, input-sensitive, and reproducible horizon-level cross-attention need not provide a valid peak-selective explanation of forecast behavior. The observed behavior is instead consistent with an internal horizon-reference role for integrating future exogenous information, although this functional role is not causally established.

</details>


### [220] [SchemaFill: Efficient LLM Tool Calling via Slot-Parallel Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07086)
*Zhi-Kai Chen,Song-Yan Li,De-Chuan Zhan,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 SchemaFill，一个通过槽位并行投机解码来加速 LLM 工具调用的框架，在 Glaive 和 BFCL 上实现最高 4.05 倍的端到端吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体通过生成结构化工具调用来与外部系统交互。标准自回归解码逐 token 生成调用，当请求涉及多个调用或大量参数字段时会带来明显的延迟。虽然显式参数结构为实现并行生成提供了机会，但后续参数值可能依赖先前的字段和调用，因此独立生成的值可能与目标模型的输出不一致。

Method: SchemaFill 通过槽位并行投机解码实现高效工具调用。它并发地生成未来槽位值作为候选，无需预先知道实际调用序列或参数值。跨越多个字段和调用的候选被拼接起来，在真实输出前缀下由目标模型进行验证。只有通过验证的 token 才会被提交，当候选不一致时由目标模型提供修正，从而在槽位和调用之间利用并行性并施加目标验证。

Result: 在 Glaive 和 BFCL 数据集上，SchemaFill 相比自回归解码实现了最高 4.05 倍的端到端吞吐量提升。

Conclusion: SchemaFill 利用工具调用的显式参数结构进行并行投机生成，并通过目标模型验证保证正确性，有效降低了 LLM 工具调用的推理延迟。代码已开源。

Abstract: LLM agents interact with external systems by generating structured tool calls. Given a user request, conversational context, and a catalog of tool schemas, a tool-calling model must select tools and generate their arguments, potentially producing multiple calls in a single response. Standard autoregressive decoding generates these calls token by token, incurring substantial latency for requests involving multiple calls or many argument fields. The explicit argument structure offers opportunities for parallel generation, but later argument values may depend on preceding fields and calls, so independently generated values can differ from the target model's output. We present SchemaFill, a framework for efficient LLM tool calling through slot-parallel speculative decoding. SchemaFill generates future slot values concurrently as candidates, without requiring advance knowledge of the actual call sequence or argument values. Candidates spanning multiple fields and calls are concatenated for verification by the target model under the actual output prefix. Only verified tokens are committed, and the target supplies corrections when candidates disagree. This applies target verification while exploiting parallelism across slots and calls. On Glaive and BFCL, SchemaFill achieves up to a 4.05$\times$ improvement in end-to-end throughput over autoregressive decoding. Code is available at https://github.com/Czzzk/SchemaFill.

</details>


### [221] [Muon Is Theoretically Wrong For Convolutions, But Empirically Effective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07103)
*Thibaut Boissin,Thomas Massena,Mathieu Serrurier,Franck Mamalet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究Muon优化器在卷积核（四维张量）上的理论解释问题，提出在卷积算子几何中直接定义优化目标并设计Conv-NS方法，实验发现其与基于reshape的Muon性能相当，揭示理论与实践之间的错配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器对矩阵更新有清晰的理论解释，但卷积核以四维张量存储，标准实现将其reshape为矩阵，这一捷径破坏了Muon背后的理论理解。作者希望探究在卷积算子几何中直接进行优化是否更合理。

Method: 作者将优化目标形式化地定义在卷积算子几何中，并提出Convolutional Newton-Schulz (Conv-NS)，该方法在该几何下近似极因子（polar factor），同时保持卷积核的支持集（kernel support）。

Result: 在快速训练实验中，Conv-NS与基于reshape的Muon都具备计算效率，且在CIFAR-10和ImageNet分类任务上取得相当的准确率。

Conclusion: 尽管理论上对齐的Conv-NS本应优于基于reshape的Muon，但实验显示二者性能相当。作者假设精确的卷积正交化可能过度约束了更新。这些发现凸显了Muon的强实际性能，同时为其在卷积上的进一步发展开辟了方向。

Abstract: Muon, an optimizer known for its efficiency, has a clear interpretation for matrix-valued updates, but convolutional kernels are stored as four-dimensional tensors. Standard implementations reshape these tensors into matrices, a shortcut which breaks the theoretical understanding behind Muon. To investigate this, we formalize the corresponding optimization objective directly in convolutional operator geometry and introduce Convolutional Newton-Schulz (Conv-NS), which approximates the polar factor in this geometry while preserving kernel support. When applied in fast training experiments, Conv-NS and reshape-based Muon are both computationally efficient and achieve comparable accuracy on CIFAR-10 and ImageNet classification tasks. However, as one could expect a theoretically aligned Conv-NS to outperform reshape-based Muon, we investigate this mismatch between practice and theoretical understanding, with the hypothesis that exact convolutional orthogonalization may overconstrain updates. These findings highlight Muon's strong practical performance while opening directions for its further development on convolutions. Our code is publicly available at \href{https://github.com/thib-s/muonconv-cifar10-airbench}{github conv-muon}.

</details>


### [222] [LiLib: Lifelong Air-to-Ground Path-Loss Prediction on UAVs via a Drift-Triggered Model Library](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07111)
*Minh Tran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LiLib：一种面向无人机空对地信道预测的轻量级持续学习方案，用小型递归最小二乘（RLS）专家库应对环境漂移，性能优于滑动窗口基线并接近理想预测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机作为中继或基站时，需要准确的空对地路径损耗预测以进行速率自适应与部署；但随无人机在郊区、城区、高层区域间移动，传播条件不断变化且同一区域常被反复访问。在线回归器若靠遗忘来适应，需对每个环境从头重学；而单一全局模型又会平均掉互不兼容的不同工况。

Method: 提出 LiLib 轻量级持续学习方案：无人机维护一个小型递归最小二乘专家库；用加窗残差检验检测漂移；随后进入短探测阶段，要么复用库中最优专家，要么新建专家；专家库可被同伴无人机初始化共享。

Result: 在四种标准城市化剖面的仿真中，预测 RMSE 从 5.89 dB（最佳滑动窗口基线）降至 4.03 dB（p<0.001）；回到已知环境后的初始误差从 12.3 dB 降至 5.7 dB；速率自适应中恢复出工况感知理想预测器 99% 的吞吐量。库内存 4 个专家、占用不足 0.5 KB，无标签工况识别纯度达 92%。第二架无人机用同伴库初始化后，环境变化后的误差减半。局限：未达到理想预测器水平，强阴影下相似工况可能被合并。

Conclusion: 结果表明，对于反复出现的漂移场景，"记住"（复用历史专家）比"重新适应"更有效。

Abstract: UAVs that act as relays or base stations need accurate air-to-ground path-loss predictions for rate adaptation and placement, but propagation conditions change as a UAV moves between suburban, urban and high-rise areas, and the same areas are often revisited. Online regressors that adapt by forgetting must relearn each environment from scratch, whereas a single model trained on all data averages incompatible regimes. We propose LiLib, a lightweight continual-learning scheme in which a UAV maintains a small library of recursive-least-squares experts. A windowed residual test detects drift; a short probe phase then either reuses the best stored expert or creates a new one. In simulations based on four standard urbanization profiles, LiLib reduces prediction RMSE from 5.89 dB (best sliding-window baseline) to 4.03 dB (p < 0.001), lowers the error shortly after a return to a known environment from 12.3 dB to 5.7 dB, and recovers 99% of the throughput of a regime-aware oracle in rate adaptation. The library stores four experts in under 0.5 KB, and identifies regimes with 92% purity without labels. When a second UAV is initialized with the library of a peer, its error after environment changes halves. LiLib does not reach the oracle, and similar regimes may be merged when shadowing is strong. The results indicate that, for recurring drift, remembering is more effective than re-adapting.

</details>


### [223] [Sample-Optimal Estimation of the Fréchet Inception Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07114)
*Ziyun Chen,Jerry Li,Kevin Tian,Yusong Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究已知一个分布时，$d$ 维高斯分布之间 FID 估计的样本复杂度，给出了经验 plug-in 估计量的紧界，提出了任意阶外推去偏方法与最优的 RTD 算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FID 广泛用于评估生成模型，但其经验 plug-in 估计量存在有限样本偏差，现有工作缺乏对其样本复杂度的紧致理论刻画以及计算高效且最优的去偏方法。

Method: 在有界均值距离与协方差、且一个分布已知的设定下：(1) 建立经验 plug-in 估计量的紧致有限样本偏差与方差界；(2) 将 FID$_\infty$ 推广为任意阶 $k$ 外推方法并给出紧界；(3) 提出受 U-统计量启发的相对泰勒去偏（RTD）算法。

Result: plug-in 估计量偏差为 $\Theta(d^2/n)$、方差为 $\Theta(d/n+d^2/n^2)$，样本复杂度约 $d^2$；任意阶 $k$ 外推的偏差与方差分别为 $\Theta(d^{k+2}/n^{k+1})$ 和 $\Theta(d/n+d^2/n^2)$；RTD 达到 $O(d/\epsilon^2)$ 样本复杂度并被证明最优。合成高斯实验验证了残余偏差与界的紧致性；ImageNet 上 RTD 在 50K 样本下误差最低，VALE$_2$ 仅用 10K 样本即达到 FID$_\infty$ 50K 样本的精度。

Conclusion: 本文从理论上刻画了 FID 估计的样本复杂度并证明 RTD 达到最优，同时通过实验验证了理论界的紧致性与新估计器的实用优势。

Abstract: The Fréchet Inception Distance (FID) is widely used to evaluate generative models, but its empirical plug-in estimator suffers from finite-sample bias [BSAG18, CF20]. We study the sample complexity $n$ of estimating FID to error $ε$ between $d$-dimensional Gaussians with bounded mean distance and covariances, when one distribution is known. Our contributions are threefold. (1) We establish tight finite-sample $Θ(\frac{d^2}{n})$ bias and $Θ(\frac{d}{n} + \frac {d^2} {n^2})$ variance bounds for the empirical plug-in estimator, establishing a $\gtrsim d^2$ sample complexity. (2) To debias the empirical plug-in estimator, we generalize the ${\rm FID}_\infty$ estimator of [CF20] to extrapolation methods of arbitrary order $k$. We further prove tight bias and variance bounds of $Θ(\frac{d^{k + 2}}{n^{k + 1}})$ and $Θ(\frac d n + \frac{d^2}{n^2})$ for any order-$k$ extrapolation under our framework. (3) We introduce Relative Taylor Debiasing (RTD), a new, computationally efficient FID estimation algorithm using debiasing techniques inspired by U-statistics. We show that RTD achieves an $O(\frac d {ε^2})$ sample complexity, and prove that this is optimal. We provide a complementary empirical evaluation of our new estimators. Our experiments on synthetic Gaussians validate the predicted residual bias and support the tightness of our bounds. On ImageNet with Inception embeddings, RTD achieves the lowest mean estimation error at the standard 50K sample budget, while our second-order variance-aware extrapolation estimator (VALE$_2$) uses only 10K samples to achieve accuracy comparable to FID$_\infty$ at 50K samples.

</details>


### [224] [Will the Judge Flip? Predicting Position-Sensitive LLM Judgments from Residual Stream Activations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07115)
*Hashmath Shaik,Gnaneswar Villuri,Alex Doboli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 预判决残差流激活可预测LLM评判者的位置翻转，AUROC达.621-.850，优于非激活基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 候选答案呈现顺序会改变LLM评判者的裁决。传统检测位置翻转需对每对进行正反两次判断，成本翻倍，因此希望找到更高效的方法。

Method: 使用嵌套分组交叉验证，在JudgeBench的534对样本上，对三个Qwen3评判者及Llama-3.1-8B训练正则化线性探针，基于初始裁决前的残差流激活进行预测。

Result: 线性探针AUROC为.621-.850，比结合口头置信度、裁决标签logits、回答长度与评判者初始选择的基线高出.062-.113 AUROC。在JudgeBench上训练后冻结的探针，在1,802个MT-Bench对比上无需拟合或校准即达.685-.853 AUROC。

Conclusion: 判决前的激活能支持对候选顺序敏感性的预测，并优于本文评估的非激活预测指标。

Abstract: The order in which candidate responses are presented can change an LLM judge's verdict. Detecting such a position flip ordinarily requires judging each pair in both orders, which doubles the number of judgments. We investigate whether residual stream activations recorded immediately before the initial verdict can predict a flip. We use nested grouped cross-validation to evaluate regularized linear probes on 534 JudgeBench pairs for three Qwen3 judges and Llama-3.1-8B. The linear probes achieve AUROCs of .621-.850 and outperform a combined baseline that uses verbalized confidence, verdict-label logits, response lengths, and the judge's initial choice by .062-.113 AUROC. Linear probes trained on JudgeBench and then frozen achieve AUROCs of .685-.853 on 1,802 MT-Bench comparisons without MT-Bench fitting or recalibration. These results show that pre-verdict activations support prediction of susceptibility to candidate order and outperform the non-activation predictors evaluated here.

</details>


### [225] [SoloQ: Calibration-Free Quantization for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07121)
*Donghyun Lee,Arkapravo Ghosh,Varun Manjunath,Bumjoon Kyle Rhee,Hyunho Kook,Shiting Xiao,Youngeun Kim,Priyadarshini Panda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SoloQ 是一种免校准的扩散大语言模型量化框架，通过旋转到分布可预测的归一化基底，实现数据无关的权重量化，在4-bit下保持精度并显著降低内存与加速推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型（dLLM）因模型规模增大和推理成本高而难以高效部署：全序列去噪需反复前向计算，块扩散模型还引入高内存的KV-cache。低比特量化虽有吸引力，但现有dLLM后训练量化依赖校准数据，而激活分布会随掩码状态和去噪步变化而漂移，导致校准不可靠。

Method: 提出免校准量化框架SoloQ：将权重和激活映射到具有可预测边缘分布的归一化旋转基底，实现数据无关量化。方法结合结构化K-RPBH旋转与轻量重缩放校正；其可预测的旋转后分布同时支持分布匹配码本和硬件原生NVFP4。对块扩散模型，进一步采用提交时KV-cache量化，压缩持久状态而不扰动正在去噪的块。

Result: 在全序列dLLM（LLaDA、Dream）和块扩散dLLM（Fast-dLLM v2、Nemotron-Labs-Diffusion）上，SoloQ在4-bit量化下保持精度，并在知识密集和推理密集基准上优于基于校准的基线。使用NVFP4时，峰值内存最多降低2.61倍，端到端推理最多加速2.24倍。

Conclusion: SoloQ通过免校准的旋转量化方案，有效解决了dLLM激活分布漂移带来的量化难题，在保持精度的同时显著提升部署效率，适用于全序列与块扩散两类dLLM。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models dLLMs) have emerged as a promising alternative to autoregressive language models through bidirectional diffusion-based token generation. However, their growing model sizes and high inference costs make efficient deployment challenging: full-sequence denoising repeatedly invokes compute-intensive forward passes, while block-diffusion models additionally introduce a memory-intensive KV-cache. Low-bit weight-activation quantization is therefore attractive, yet existing dLLM post-training quantization methods rely on calibration data despite activation distributions shifting across masking states and denoising steps. We present SoloQ, a calibration-free quantization framework that maps weights and activations into a normalized rotated basis with a predictable marginal distribution, enabling data-independent quantization. SoloQ combines a structured K-RPBH rotation with a lightweight rescaling correction for calibration-free quantization. Its predictable post-rotation distribution supports both distribution-matched codebooks and hardware-native NVFP4. For block-diffusion models, SoloQ further applies commit-time KV-cache quantization to compress persistent states without perturbing the actively denoised block. Across full-sequence dLLMs (LLaDA and Dream) and block-diffusion dLLMs(Fast-dLLM v2 and Nemotron-Labs-Diffusion), SoloQ retains accuracy under 4-bit quantization and outperforms calibration-based baselines on knowledge- and reasoning-intensive benchmarks. With NVFP4, SoloQ reduces peak memory by up to 2.61X and accelerates end-to-end inference by up to 2.24X.

</details>


### [226] [The Implicit Bias of Hyperbolic Representation Learning for Multiclass Data: A Busemann Risk Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07131)
*Xingrun Li,Sho Kuno,Yusuke Mukuta,Xin Yang,Tatsuya Harada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究双曲空间 $\mathbb{H}^n$ 中固定类原型下多分类问题的黎曼梯度流的隐式偏置，基于 Busemann 函数分解证明半径发散与坍缩的二分性，并刻画边界方向收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解双曲表征学习中"边界饱和"与"近边界聚类"两种现象的机制，为双曲多分类的隐式偏置提供严格渐近分析。

Method: 在固定类原型的设定下，针对一般置换不变相对间隔（PERM）损失族（含交叉熵等）；在大半径处将到原型的距离分解为径向项与由 Busemann 函数描述的方�l向依赖项，据此分析黎曼梯度流的渐近行为。

Result: 证明径向二分性：漂移系数 $\mu$ 的符号决定半径被推向理想边界还是返回内部；正漂移持续时 $r(t)=\frac12\log t+O(1)$，持续负漂移则在有限时间内回到大半径阈值。进一步证明边界方向收敛到 $\partial\mathbb{H}^n$ 上 Busemann 风险的临界点。

Conclusion: 该分析为双曲表征学习中的边界饱和与近边界聚类提供了严格的渐近理论视角。

Abstract: We study the implicit bias of Riemannian gradient flow for hyperbolic multiclass classification with fixed class prototypes in hyperbolic space $\mathbb{H}^n$. Our framework accommodates general permutation invariant relative margin (PERM) losses, a class that includes cross entropy and other standard multiclass losses. Our analysis is based on a decomposition: at large radius, the distance to each prototype splits into a radial term and a direction-dependent term described by the Busemann function. This yields two main results. First, we prove a radial dichotomy: the sign of a drift coefficient $μ$ determines whether the radius is pushed toward the ideal boundary or back toward the interior; if the positive drift persists, then $r(t)=\frac{1}{2}\log t+O(1)$, while persistent negative drift returns the trajectory to the large-radius threshold in finite time. Second, we show that the boundary direction converges to a critical point of the Busemann risk on $\partial\mathbb{H}^n$. These results provide a rigorous asymptotic perspective on two phenomena we refer to as boundary saturation and near-boundary clustering in hyperbolic representation learning.

</details>


### [227] [Adversarial Training for Deep Hedging in Nonstationary Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07162)
*Philipp J. Schneider,Lukas Looser,Antoine Garin,Shuhan Liu,Daniel Kuhn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出WRAP框架，将深度对冲训练建模为双预算分布鲁棒优化问题，通过对轨迹重加权（φ-散度约束）与路径扰动（最优传输约束）的联合一阶展开构造可处理的对抗攻击，在非平稳市场中取得最大增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度对冲依赖历史或模拟轨迹学习交易策略，但在非平稳环境下，这些训练路径可能无法代表未来市场状况，导致策略泛化能力下降。因此需要一种能够感知分布漂移、对分布误设具有鲁棒性的训练方法。

Method: 提出WRAP（Wasserstein-Reweighting Adversarial Perturbation），基于双预算分布鲁棒优化（DRO）推导。以加权经验参考分布为锚点，其固定基线权重用于平衡采样不确定性与时间漂移；歧义集允许对手在φ-散度约束下重加权观测轨迹，并在最优传输（OT）约束下扰动路径。通过联合一阶展开，将分布内层上确界替换为可处理的一阶近似，得到一个显式的有限维对抗攻击。

Result: 一阶展开表明，相对名义期望损失的领先阶增量可分解为重加权贡献（由对冲损失跨轨迹的离散度决定）与传输贡献（由损失对路径扰动的敏感度决定）。在平稳与非平稳Heston动态及广义仿射扩散（GAD）上的实验显示，重加权与传输具有互补收益，联合对抗训练在非平稳条件下增益最大。

Conclusion: WRAP通过整合轨迹重加权与路径扰动两类分布误设形式，为深度对冲提供了一种漂移感知的对抗训练方法；理论展开给出可处理的攻击形式，实验验证了联合对抗训练在非平稳市场中的优越性。

Abstract: Deep hedging learns trading policies from historical or simulated market trajectories, yet under nonstationarity these training paths may not represent future market conditions. We propose WRAP (Wasserstein-Reweighting Adversarial Perturbation), a drift-aware adversarial training framework derived from a two-budget distributionally robust optimization (DRO) formulation. The formulation is anchored to a weighted empirical reference distribution whose fixed baseline weights are chosen to balance sampling uncertainty against temporal drift. Around this reference distribution, the ambiguity set addresses two complementary forms of distributional misspecification by allowing an adversary to reweight the observed trajectories subject to a $φ$-divergence constraint and perturb their paths subject to an optimal-transport (OT) constraint. We derive a joint first-order expansion in which the leading-order increase over the nominal expected loss decomposes into a reweighting contribution determined by the dispersion of hedging losses across trajectories and a transport contribution determined by the sensitivity of the loss to path perturbations. This expansion yields an explicit finite-dimensional adversarial attack that replaces the distributional inner supremum with a tractable first-order approximation. Across stationary and nonstationary Heston dynamics and a generalized affine diffusion (GAD), the experiments show complementary benefits from reweighting and transport, with joint adversarial training providing the largest gains under nonstationarity.

</details>


### [228] [A theory of platonic representations in language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07168)
*Darshil Doshi,Wenjie Zhou,Corinna Elena Wegner,Daniel J. Korchinski,Santiago Acevedo,Matthieu Wyart*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出理论解释：多语言模型内部层出现翻译句表示相似（跨语言相似）源于数据隐藏层级结构——抽象层共享、表层语言特定，可用概率上下文无关文法合成语言并以信念传播（BP）作为贝叶斯最优预测器复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言模型内部层翻译句表示相似这一现象与柏拉图表示假说相关，但缺乏理论解释。

Method: 假设数据具隐藏层级结构，抽象层跨语言共享、表层语言特定；用共享上层层级但下层产生式不同的PCFG生成合成语言，将贝叶斯最优下一词预测器视为信念传播，把BP消息编码进逐层，并与相同数据训练的Transformer对比。

Result: 分析预测与训练结果吻合良好；框架解释了跨语言相似度在中层达峰、与语言特定结构共存、并随语言接近度、模型质量和数据暴露增强；区分相似（共享邻域几何）与对齐（共享坐标），后者在代码混用数据充足时出现；预测从每层减去前一层线性可预测成分可提高跨语言相似度，并在预训练LLM中得到验证。

Conclusion: 该框架为多语言模型跨语言表示相似提供理论机制，说明其源于共享抽象层级与BP消息结构，并给出相似与对齐的区分及可验证预测。

Abstract: Representations of translated sentences are similar in the inner layers of multilingual language models -- an observation connected to the platonic representation hypothesis, yet unexplained theoretically. We provide an explanation based on the assumption that data have a hidden hierarchical structure whose abstract levels are shared across languages while surface levels are modality- or language-specific. Concretely, we generate synthetic languages from probabilistic context-free grammars sharing upper-level but not lower-level production rules. In this setting the Bayes-optimal next-token predictor is belief propagation (BP); encoding its messages in successive layers yields analytical predictions that agree well with transformers trained on the same data. The framework explains why cross-lingual similarity peaks in middle layers, coexists with language-specific structure, and strengthens with language proximity, model quality and data exposure. It distinguishes similarity (shared neighborhood geometry) from alignment (shared coordinates), showing that the latter occurs when code-switched data, i.e. mixed-language sentences, are abundant enough. It further predicts that subtracting from each layer the component linearly predictable from the preceding one increases cross-lingual similarity, which we confirm in pretrained LLMs.

</details>


### [229] [CLM-as-a-Judge: Evaluating an Open Contrastive Decision Model on Public Judge Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07177)
*Gowthamkumar Nandakishore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 一个开放对比决策模型（CLM-v0.1-8B）在硬公开基准上接近随机水平，尽管其校准置信度与顺序稳定性等属性表现良好，但用于级联时几乎无法保留任何条目。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验开放对比决策模型是否能在困难公开基准上充当有效评判者，并与同参数量生成式模型、奖励模型及平凡基线进行严格对比，采用预注册设计以避免测试项泄漏。

Method: 在五个公开偏好基准与一个带真实标签的幻觉基准上进行评估，采用预先冻结的预注册方案；对比生成式判官、奖励模型与平凡基线；使用Benjamini-Hochberg校正检验显著性；对原始置信度进行留出集温度拟合校准；并测试置信度门控级联（0.97保留阈值）与顺序翻转率、长度偏好偏移等指标。

Result: CLM得分介于0.351（四选一，随机0.250）与0.593（成对，随机0.500）之间，在RM-Bench与JudgeBench上与抛硬币统计不可区分，对HaluEval全部输出同一常量标签，等同平凡基线0.581；同参数量奖励模型达0.764–0.976，生成式判官0.611–0.778，所有差距经BH校正后显著。原始置信度过高最多+0.401，但一次温度拟合将ECE修复至≤0.062，修复后置信度在六个基准中的三个上能把自身错误排到随机之上。顺序翻转率0.0002（对比生成式0.2188），长度偏好偏移-0.023（对比-0.217）。然而置信度门控级联在0.97保留条下将0.923–1.000的条目升级给强判官。

Conclusion: CLM作为困难公开基准上的评判者接近随机，其良好校准的置信度与顺序稳定性虽真实存在，但不足以支撑级联筛选，因为对一个接近随机的判官，校准置信度几乎没有可保留的信息；结果强调在硬基准上需谨慎使用该类模型作为评判者。

Abstract: An open contrastive decision model is near chance as a judge on the hard public benchmarks: Contrastive-LM/CLM-v0.1-8B scores between 0.351 (best- of-four, chance 0.250) and 0.593 (pairwise, chance 0.500), is statistically indistinguishable from coin flipping on RM-Bench and JudgeBench, and answers every HaluEval item with one constant label, matching the trivial always-first baseline at 0.581. Judges with the same parameter count score far higher everywhere: a reward model reaches 0.764 to 0.976 and a generative judge 0.611 to 0.778, and every gap to CLM is significant after Benjamini-Hochberg correction. Two properties do work. Raw confidences are overconfident by up to +0.401, yet one pooled temperature fit on held-out calibration items repairs expected calibration error to at most 0.062, and the repaired confidence ranks the model's own errors above chance on three of six benchmarks. The decision order-flip rate is 0.0002 against 0.2188 for the generative judge, and the length-preference shift is -0.023 against -0.217. The confidence-gated cascade, however, escalates between 0.923 and 1.000 of items to the strong judge at the preregistered 0.97 retention bar: calibrated confidence about a near-chance judge has almost nothing to keep. The design: five public preference benchmarks and one hallucination benchmark with real labels, scored under a preregistration frozen before any test item was seen, against generative, reward-model, and trivial baselines, with per-item predictions released.

</details>


### [230] [Learning Scientific Exploration from Human Research Decision Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07184)
*Xuchen Gong,Shane Gu,Haokun Liu,Dixi Yao,Chenhao Tan,Tian Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 ResearchTrails 数据集，利用 Git 仓库的提交历史作为研究探索过程的代理，构建人类科研轨迹数据，用于检索经验和训练模型以改进科研决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建科研 AI 系统的一大挑战是支持“科学探索”，即通过一系列研究决策与行动来系统性地研究未知现象。然而现有科研语料（如论文）只记录最终成果，缺失了产生这些成果的探索轨迹。因此作者希望从过程层面而非结果层面为 AI 提供科研探索数据。

Method: 作者构建了 ResearchTrails，一个从 Git 仓库自动、可扩展地抽取人类研究轨迹的数据集，以提交历史作为研究探索的代理。该流程将提交提取为结构化的科研轨迹，捕捉方法、实验和消融实验的连续变化。作者对数据集进行了刻画，分析其中是否包含论文无法揭示的中间研究决策信号。

Result: 结果表明，这些轨迹蕴含关于中间科研决策的有意义信号，超出了最终论文所披露的信息。作者展示多个应用场景：在测试时检索人类研究经验作为外部技能，以及在研究轨迹上训练模型以提升对新研究决策的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该工作为 AI 系统提供了一条路径，使其不仅从科学的最终产物中学习，也能从发现过程本身的演化中学习。

Abstract: A key challenge in building AI systems for scientific research is enabling $\textit{scientific exploration}$: the systematic process of investigating unknown phenomena or ideas to gain new knowledge through sequences of research decisions and actions. Yet this process is largely missing from existing scientific corpora; for example, research papers primarily record final outcomes rather than the trajectories that produced them. In this work, we introduce $\textbf{ResearchTrails}$, a dataset of $\textbf{human research trajectories constructed from Git repositories}$, where $\textbf{commit histories}$ serve as proxies for research exploration. We develop an automated and scalable pipeline that extracts structured research trajectories from repository commits, capturing successive changes to methods, experiments, and ablations. We characterize the resulting dataset and show that these trajectories contain meaningful signals about intermediate research decisions beyond what final papers reveal. We further demonstrate utilities of ResearchTrails in multiple use cases, including retrieving human research experience as external skills at test time and training models on research trajectories to improve generalization to new research decisions. Our results suggest a path toward AI systems that learn not only from the products of science, but from the evolving process of discovery itself.

</details>


### [231] [Exact Unlearning via Quantized Sufficient Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07197)
*Ami Tavory,Shripad Gade,Tal Sarig,Noam Touitou,Ido Guy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出量化充分统计量（QSS），将模型分为冻结的模式与可变的、可求和的量化内容，使删除操作变成精确减法而非重训练，从而在保持精度的同时大幅降低精确遗忘的删除延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用精确遗忘方法通过不相交分片局部化重训练，但每次请求仍会使某个模型失效，且分片越小各子预测器可用数据越少。作者希望在不牺牲精度的情况下降低删除延迟。

Method: QSS 将小型冻结模式与可变的、按求和分解的内容分离：模式学习全局结构，内容按量化区域索引存储局部预测修正的加性统计量，删除内容即精确减法。方法区分两种保证：QSS-L 精确移除标签但保留未标注输入，QSS-E 通过不使用可删除样本学习模式来精确移除输入与标签。删除以概率 1-ρ 走算术快路径，以概率 ρ 触发完整重建，报告的期望延迟包含两者。

Result: 在 15 个视觉、文本和表格数据集上，ρ=0.5% 时，QSS-L 在 11 个任务上与 SISA 的精度差距在 2 个百分点以内，并在紧凑模式有效的低类别数任务上实现 4–483 倍的期望删除延迟降低。QSS-E 量化了彻底移除输入所需额外的精度代价。

Conclusion: QSS 通过将精确遗忘转化为加性统计量的精确减法，在低类别数任务上显著优于 SISA 的删除效率，同时保持可比精度，并明确刻画了移除输入痕迹的额外精度成本。

Abstract: Exact unlearning requires a deployed predictor to match one rebuilt without the information named by a deletion request. Existing general-purpose exact methods localize retraining through disjoint shards, but every request still invalidates a model, and smaller shards reduce the data available to each constituent predictor. We introduce Quantized Sufficient Statistics (QSS), which separates a small frozen schema from mutable, sum-decomposable content. The schema learns global structure; the content stores local prediction corrections as additive statistics indexed by quantized regions. Deleting content is therefore exact subtraction rather than optimization. We distinguish two guarantees: QSS-L exactly removes a label while retaining the unlabelled input, whereas QSS-E exactly removes both input and label by learning the schema without deletable examples. A deletion takes the arithmetic fast path with probability $1-ρ$ and triggers a full rebuild with probability $ρ$; all reported expected latencies include both events. Across 15 vision, text, and tabular datasets at $ρ=0.5\%$, QSS-L is within 2 percentage points of SISA on 11 tasks and provides 4--483$\times$ lower expected deletion latency on the low-class-count tasks where a compact schema is effective. QSS-E quantifies the additional accuracy cost of removing every trace of an input.

</details>


### [232] [Distributionally Robust Mixture-of-Experts Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07207)
*Xin Teng,Muxiao Li,Hongyi Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DRMoET 将 MoE 各层专家视为稳健性分组，优化高损失路由结果而非仅均衡流量，提升路由鲁棒性与下游性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE 通过每 token 仅激活少量专家来扩展容量，但稀疏性带来隐藏的可靠性问题：路由不完美时，负载均衡的模型可能把 token 送到对相应输入训练不足的专家。现有方法多关注负载均衡或利用率，未把路由鲁棒性作为显式优化目标。

Method: 提出 DRMoET（分布鲁棒 MoE 训练），一个可直接替换的目标。将各层专家视为内生稳健性分组，基于 EMA 平滑、激活加权的专家损失，用熵正则化 softmax 规则更新每层专家分布，强化合理的非 top 路由路径，同时保持标准 MoE 计算。

Result: 在 FLAME-MoE 配方下（746M 和 10.3B 总参数规模），DRMoET 的下游平均优于标准 FLAME-MoE 和无辅助损失均衡基线。10.3B 总参数、67B 训练 token 时，七任务平均从 0.6625 提升至 0.6767，而无辅助损失基线为 0.6431。机理分析显示专家损失方差更低、平均损失几乎不变，强制 mid-k 误路由下超额损失降低 4.3%，且域-专家特化改善。

Conclusion: 结果表明，路由鲁棒性——而非仅利用率均衡——可作为可靠稀疏 MoE 扩展的实用目标。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) transformers scale capacity by activating only a few experts per token, but this sparsity creates a hidden reliability problem: when routing is imperfect, load-balanced models may send tokens to experts that are insufficiently trained for the assigned inputs. We propose Distributionally Robust MoE Training (DRMoET), a drop-in objective that treats layer-wise experts as endogenous robustness groups and optimizes high-loss routing outcomes rather than merely equalizing traffic. DRMoET updates a per-layer expert distribution by an entropy-regularized softmax rule on EMA-smoothed, activation-weighted expert losses, strengthening plausible non-top routing paths while preserving standard MoE computation. Under the FLAME-MoE recipe at 746M-total and 10.3B-total scales, DRMoET improves downstream averages over both standard FLAME-MoE and auxiliary-loss-free balancing. At 10.3B total parameters and 67B training tokens, DRMoET improves the seven-task average from 0.6625 to 0.6767, while the auxiliary-loss-free baseline achieves 0.6431. Mechanistic analyses show lower expert-loss variance with nearly unchanged mean loss, 4.3% lower excess loss under forced mid-$k$ misrouting, and improved domain-expert specialization. These results position routing robustness-not only utilization balance-as a practical objective for reliable sparse MoE scaling. Project page and code are available at: https://drmoet.github.io/.

</details>


### [233] [Can LLM-assisted regularization increase forecast accuracy for migration flows in low data regimes?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07208)
*Nathaniel T. Hindman,Fabricio Murai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究是否可利用大语言模型从新闻文章中提取与迁移相关的推拉信号，并通过特征特定正则化惩罚将其融入加权Lasso预测框架，以改进传统引力模型的迁移流量预测。实验覆盖墨西哥—美国、乌克兰—波兰、叙利亚—土耳其等走廊，结果显示性能不一，但在特定条件下LLM辅助特征与正则化可提升预测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于引力的迁移预测模型主要依赖结构化社会经济指标（如经济差距、政治稳定性和地理距离），难以捕捉新闻中所反映的动态情境信号，因此本文探索用LLM提取非结构化文本中的迁移推拉信息以改进预测。

Method: 采用分层LLM推理流水线从新闻数据中分类提取迁移相关的推拉信号，构建推拉比率等文章衍生特征，并引入特征特定正则化惩罚，将LLM引导的正则化融入加权Lasso预测框架，与引力基线模型结合。

Result: 各迁移走廊和建模策略表现不一，没有单一正则化方法在所有实验中持续占优。墨西哥最佳配置（引力模型+推拉比率）MAPE为17.15%；叙利亚最佳配置（Direct LLM-Lasso）MAPE为29.29%；乌克兰最佳配置（LLM-Assisted Regularization）MAPE为41.05%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在特定条件下，基于文章的上下文特征与LLM引导的正则化能够改善迁移预测，但迁移走廊特征、文章数量和超参数配置对性能有显著影响。

Abstract: Predicting migration flows remains a significant challenge for traditional gravity-based forecasting models, which primarily rely on structured socio-economic indicators such as economic disparity, political stability, and geographic distance. This work investigates whether Large Language Models (LLMs) can improve migration forecasting by extracting contextual migration-related signals from news articles and incorporating them into a weighted Lasso forecasting framework through feature-specific regularization penalties. The proposed framework uses hierarchical LLM inference pipelines to classify migration-related push--pull signals from news data and evaluates the resulting forecasting performance across multiple migration corridors between November 2021 and November 2022, including Mexico--United States, Ukraine--Poland, and Syria--Turkey. Experimental results showed mixed performance across migration corridors and modeling strategies, and no single regularization approach consistently outperformed the others across all experiments. The best-performing Mexico configuration, which consisted of a gravity-based model augmented with the proposed push--pull ratios, achieved a Mean Absolute Percentage Error (MAPE) of 17.15%, while the strongest Syria configuration achieved a MAPE of 29.29% using Direct LLM-Lasso. For Ukraine, the best-performing configuration used LLM-Assisted Regularization (AR) and achieved a MAPE of 41.05%. Overall, the results suggest that contextual article-derived features and LLM-guided regularization can improve migration forecasting under certain conditions, although migration corridor characteristics, article volume, and hyperparameter configuration strongly influenced performance.

</details>


### [234] [Reward-Driven Learning under Prompt-Level Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07212)
*Jiachen Zhao,Antonia Januszewicz,Taeho Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次为可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR）提供提示级差分隐私保证，方法是将同一提示的所有回答视作隐私记录、聚合并裁剪其梯度后加高斯噪声；在ε=8预算下训练Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct，奖励信号比去掉奖励的对照分别提升MATH 2.65分、GSM8K 3.24分，并优于两种私有监督微调方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR在可能涉密的题目上训练语言模型，训练后的模型会泄露其见过的题目，因此需要在提示级差分隐私下研究RLVR，以保护单个训练问题的存在性不被泄露。

Method: 将同一提示的所有回答作为一个隐私记录，聚合它们的梯度并对该提示的贡献只裁剪一次，再加入高斯噪声，并在各次更新间组合隐私损失，使预算不依赖每个提示的回答数或裁剪范数；在ε=8的每次运行预算下用LoRA训练Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct，并在相同提示和预算下与去掉奖励信号的对照以及两种私有监督微调方法比较。

Result: 奖励信号相对对照在MATH上提升2.65分、GSM8K上提升3.24分且每个随机种子都成立；该提升在格式鲁棒的评分器下仍存在（MATH上1.3分），且不能由回答长度解释。相同预算下私有模型在MATH和GSM8K上比两种监督方案高2.3至3.8分，保留非私有GRPO增益的85%–90%，在MATH上预算收紧八倍的噪声代价至多1.2分；奖励效应还可迁移到CommonsenseQA这一探索性非数学任务。

Conclusion: 在提示级隐私约束下，验证器反馈仍是一种可用的学习信号，私有RLVR能有效提升模型能力且性能损失有限。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) trains a language model on problems that may themselves be confidential, and the trained model can reveal which problems it saw. We study RLVR under prompt-level differential privacy: the released weights must be (ε,δ)-differentially private with respect to the presence of any one training problem. Taking the group of responses to one prompt as the privacy record, our method aggregates their gradients, clips the prompt's contribution once, adds Gaussian noise, and composes the privacy loss across updates, so the budget depends on neither the number of responses per prompt nor the clipping norm; to our knowledge this is the first differential privacy guarantee for RLVR training. We train Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct with LoRA at a per-run budget of ε=8 and compare, on the same prompts and at the same budget, a control that removes only the reward signal and two private supervised fine-tuning recipes. The reward signal improves accuracy over the control by 2.65 points on MATH and 3.24 on GSM8K, in every seed; the improvement survives a format-robust scorer, at 1.3 points on MATH, and is not explained by response length. At the same budget the private model outperforms both supervised recipes on MATH and GSM8K by 2.3 to 3.8 points, retains 85--90% of the gain of non-private GRPO on these tasks, and on MATH the noise of an eightfold tighter budget costs at most 1.2 points. The reward effect also carries to CommonsenseQA, an exploratory non-mathematical task. Verifier feedback thus remains a usable learning signal under prompt-level privacy.

</details>


### [235] [Constant-Curvature Sliced Gromov-Wasserstein for Heterogeneous Cross-Curvature Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07218)
*Shanglin Li,Wenjing Lu,Muyang Li,Nicu Sebe,Ziheng Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出常曲率切片Gromov-Wasserstein（CCSGW）散度，用于对齐异构常曲率空间上的概率分布，并在多个混合曲率学习任务上取得一致性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合曲率模型整合了多种常曲率组件，但不同曲率的空间本质异构且缺乏统一度量，导致各组件空间通常独立学习，缺乏显式的几何一致性约束机制。此外，跨混合曲率空间比较概率分布的问题尚未被探索。

Method: 作者基于Gromov-Wasserstein距离（通过对齐空间内部几何来比较异构空间上的分布），提出CCSGW散度。具体地，先引入球面空间中缺失的基于测地线的一维投影，再将切片GW扩展到常曲率空间，从而在保持内在几何关系的同时避免高计算成本。

Result: 理论分析表明CCSGW能够控制异构空间间的内在几何差异，促进分布层面的几何一致性。将其集成到图异常检测、图节点分类和多模态学习等混合曲率学习任务中，在多种设置下均观察到一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: CCSGW为异构常曲率空间上的分布对齐提供了有原则且高效的框架，能够增强混合曲率模型的几何一致性，并在多个下游任务上验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Recent advances in representation learning have highlighted the utility of constant-curvature models, such as hyperbolic and spherical spaces, for modeling complex data. Mixed-curvature models further enhance this by integrating multiple constant-curvature components. However, these models typically learn each component space independently because spaces with different curvatures are inherently heterogeneous and lack a unified metric. Consequently, they lack explicit mechanisms to enforce geometric consistency across various spaces. Moreover, the problem of comparing probability distributions across mixed-curvature spaces remains unexplored. To compare distributions on heterogeneous spaces, Gromov-Wasserstein (GW) distances provide a principled framework by aligning their intra-space geometries. Building on this, we propose constant-curvature sliced Gromov-Wasserstein (CCSGW), a novel divergence for aligning distributions supported on heterogeneous constant-curvature spaces. We first introduce the missing geodesic-based one-dimensional projections for spherical spaces, and then extend sliced GW to constant-curvature spaces, enabling efficient and principled comparison across manifolds with different curvatures. This formulation preserves intrinsic geometric relationships while avoiding the high computational cost. We provide theoretical analysis showing that CCSGW controls intrinsic geometric discrepancy across heterogeneous spaces, promoting distribution-level geometric consistency. By integrating CCSGW into existing mixed-curvature learning tasks, including graph anomaly detection, graph node classification, and multimodal learning, we observe consistent performance gains across diverse settings.

</details>


### [236] [Data, Numbers, and Geometry: Three Tutorials on Numerical Methods, Machine Learning, and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07220)
*Jessica N. Howard,Yidi Qi,Tomás S. R. Silva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍三个面向数学研究的数值计算与机器学习实践教程，分别涉及外微分的数值方法、数学结构指导神经网络设计，以及机器学习结果的评估与呈现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为2026年4月在班夫国际研究站举办的DANGER（数据、数值与几何）研讨会开发实践教程，帮助研究者在数学研究中应用数值计算与机器学习方法，并区分数值一致性、预测精度、结构保证与严格界等不同形式的证据。

Method: 教程一：基于微分形式的逐点求值，用通量形式的外微分发展外微分的数值方法；教程二：通过椭圆曲线、箭图与边值问题示例，考察数学结构如何指导神经网络设计，并用区间算术对训练网络在边值问题整个区间上的残差进行界定；教程三：讨论机器学习结果的评估与呈现，涉及性能指标、统计不确定性、分类阈值、ROC曲线与易读图表设计。

Result: 教程一在欧氏空间与球面上展示了几何恒等式、拓扑特征及近似与有限精度的影响；教程二探讨了架构选择对学习效果的影响，并用区间算术给出残差的严格界；教程三给出了机器学习结果评估与可视化的方法。各部分可独立阅读，并配有可复现的notebook与练习。

Conclusion: 这三个教程为数学研究中的数值计算与机器学习提供了可复现的实践指南，强调区分不同类型的证据形式，并可推广到其他问题。

Abstract: We present three practical tutorials on numerical computation and machine learning for mathematical research, developed for the DANGER: Data, Numbers, and Geometry workshop held at the Banff International Research Station in April 2026. The first develops a numerical approach to exterior calculus from pointwise evaluations of differential forms, using a flux formulation of the exterior derivative. Examples in Euclidean space and on the sphere illustrate geometric identities, topological features, and the effects of approximation and finite precision. The second examines how mathematical structure guides neural network design through examples involving elliptic curves, quivers, and a boundary value problem. It explores how architectural choices affect learning and uses interval arithmetic to bound the residual of a trained network over the full interval of the boundary value problem. The third addresses the evaluation and presentation of machine learning results, covering performance metrics, statistical uncertainty, classification thresholds, receiver operating characteristic curves, and accessible figure design. Throughout, the tutorials distinguish numerical agreement, predictive accuracy, structural guarantees, and rigorous bounds as different forms of evidence. Each contribution can be read independently, with accompanying notebooks and exercises that allow readers to reproduce the examples and adapt the methods to other problems.

</details>


### [237] [Minimal Witness Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07226)
*T. Y. Tsui,Zihao Ye,Pengxiang Cai,Yanchao Li,Yuqiang Li,Zhehong Ai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文形式化"最小见证识别"问题并提出 MWRL 方法，通过覆盖度信用分配从单一验证器比特中同时实现最小性与多解恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算与科学中常见问题是"产生某结果的最小充分条件是什么"，即最小充分见证（解释、机制、原因）。这类问题通常需要多个最小见证，但标准强化学习方法往往只能找到一个解，或返回冗余解。

Method: 将问题形式化为最小见证识别，提出最小见证强化学习（MWRL）。MWRL 取策略采样成功提案所认证集合的并集，并按"去掉某提案后组并集损失的覆盖度"为每个提案分配信用。该信用分配直接源于问题定义，用单一黑盒验证器比特统一了最小性与替代解恢复的需求。在此原则下推导出可恢复整个见证族的值迭代规划器，以及可扩展到大语言模型的策略梯度方法。

Result: 在不同实验设置中，MWRL 能恢复大部分最小见证，而其他方法只返回冗余超集或单个见证。

Conclusion: 通过使见证族可从验证器反馈中学习，MWRL 将强化学习的适用范围扩展到单解优化之外。

Abstract: ``What are the irreducible conditions that are sufficient to produce an outcome?'' is one of the most common questions that recur across computation and science. Its answers, the minimal sufficient witnesses, are what we mean by explanations, mechanisms and reasons. These problems usually ask for multiple minimal witnesses, yet standard RL methods may reveal only one solution or redundant ones. We formalize this problem as minimal-witness identification and introduce Minimal-Witness Reinforcement Learning (MWRL). MWRL takes the union of the sets certified by successful proposals sampled from the policy and credits each proposal for the coverage the group union would lose without that proposal. This credit assignment, derived directly from the problem definition, unifies the demands for minimality and recovery of alternatives from a single black-box verifier bit. Under this principle, we derive a value iteration planner that recovers the entire family of witnesses and a policy gradient method that can scale to large language models. Across different experimental settings, MWRL recovers most minimal witnesses, while other methods return redundant supersets or a single witness. By making witness families learnable from verifier feedback, MWRL expands the scope of reinforcement learning beyond single-solution optimization. Our code is available at https://github.com/TSUITUENYUE/MWRL.

</details>


### [238] [Conditional Flow Matching for Transport Between Markov Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07229)
*Syamantak Kumar,Dheeraj Nagaraj,Saptarshi Roy,Purnamrita Sarkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究马尔可夫过程轨迹之间的传输问题，提出基于流匹配的算法，在保持马尔可夫结构的同时学习从源到目标轨迹分布的传输映射，并给出理论保证与实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受时间序列领域自适应中序列到序列传输问题的启发，需要在仅有有限源与目标轨迹样本的情况下，实现轨迹分布之间的传输，同时保持马尔可夫结构。

Method: 提出基于流匹配（flow matching）的算法，从源轨迹分布学习到目标轨迹分布的传输映射并保持马尔可夫结构；在混合时间假设下进行总体极限一致性与有限样本误差界分析；并构造下界说明依赖混合时间的样本复杂度不可避免。在EEG图像检索任务中，将条件流加入ENIGMA解码器的受试者特定时间映射之前。

Result: 理论上证明了总体极限下的一致性并给出有限样本误差界，同时给出混合时间依赖样本复杂度的下界；实验在合成和真实数据上验证，THINGS-EEG2上平均top-5检索准确率从43.87%提升至49.05%，相对提升11.82%。

Conclusion: 所提流匹配方法在保持马尔可夫结构下有效实现轨迹分布传输，理论上具备一致性与样本复杂度保证，并在EEG图像检索任务中显著提升性能。

Abstract: Motivated by sequence-to-sequence transport in the context time-series domain adaptation, we study the problem of transportation between trajectories of Markov processes. Given a limited number of trajectories from source distribution and the target distribution, we formulate a flow matching based algorithm which learns a transport map from the source to target trajectory distribution, while preserving the Markov structure. We show that this is consistent in the population limit and derive finite-sample error bounds under mixing time assumptions, following the analysis of classical statistical problems including regression (Nagaraj et al., 2020), principal component analysis (Kumar and Sarkar, 2023), and matrix concentration (Neeman et al., 2024) in the Markov setting. We complement that with a lower-bound construction showing that a mixing-time dependent sample complexity is unavoidable even with regular Gaussian conditional transitions. We evaluate on synthetic and real-world data. For image retrieval from electroencephalography (EEG) on THINGS-EEG2 (Gifford et al., 2022), the task is to identify the viewed image from EEG signals captured from human subjects, which suffers from high inter subject variability. We augment the ENIGMA decoder (Kneeland et al., 2026) with a conditional flow before its subject-specific temporal map. This improves mean top-5 retrieval accuracy from 43.87% to 49.05%, an 11.82% relative improvement.

</details>


### [239] [Benchmarking Time Series Foundation Models for Load Forecasting Under Covariate Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07232)
*Tomas Kaljevic,Ivan Arzola,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对四个从头训练模型与四个时间序列基础模型在三个真实电力负荷数据集上进行全面基准测试，考察不同未来协变量可用性与质量情景下的短期负荷预测表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短期负荷预测对现代电力系统可靠高效运行至关重要。时间序列基础模型虽在多种预测任务中表现优异，但其在真实运营条件下的短期负荷预测效果尚未被充分探索，尤其是未来协变量信息可用性与质量变化带来的影响缺乏系统评估。

Method: 构建涵盖四个从头训练（TFS）模型与四个时间序列基础模型（TSFM）的基准测试，在三个真实世界负荷预测数据集上，设置未来协变量信息可用性和质量不同的多种运营情景，对比零样本与微调两种设定下的预测性能。

Result: 当未来协变量可用或预测准确时，Chronos-2在零样本和微调设定下均持续取得最优性能。随着协变量预测噪声增加，其性能下降；而在协变量不确定性严重时，TimesNet表现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 结果证明协变量感知的时间序列基础模型在短期负荷预测中有效，同时强调在真实预测应用中稳健的协变量建模具有关键作用。

Abstract: Accurate short-term load forecasting (STLF) is essential for the reliable and efficient operation of modern power systems. While time series foundation models (TSFMs) have recently demonstrated remarkable performance across a wide range of forecasting tasks, their effectiveness for STLF under realistic operational conditions remains largely unexplored. In this paper, we present a comprehensive benchmark of four trained-from-scratch (TFS) models and four TSFMs across three real-world load forecasting datasets under operational scenarios that differ in the availability and quality of future covariate information. Our results show that Chronos-2 consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance in both zero-shot and fine-tuned settings when future covariates are available or accurately forecast. However, its performance degrades as covariate forecasts become increasingly noisy, whereas TimesNet exhibits greater robustness under severe covariate uncertainty. These findings demonstrate the effectiveness of covariate-informed TSFMs for STLF while highlighting the critical role of robust covariate modeling in real-world forecasting applications.

</details>


### [240] [Learning What to Distill: Bilevel Top-K Token Selection for Self-Distillation in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07247)
*Heng Liang,Xinwen Zhang,Hongchang Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 BiToK-SD，一种基于双层优化的 Top-K 令牌选择方法，用于在线自蒸馏，自适应学习在哪些位置进行蒸馏，在数学推理基准上取得最佳平均性能且额外计算开销轻量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型推理能力强但推理成本高，知识蒸馏可将能力迁移到紧凑模型。在线自蒸馏进一步降低对外部大教师模型的依赖。但现有方法要么对所有令牌位置统一蒸馏，要么用固定启发式准则选令牌，并对选中位置赋予相同蒸馏强度，无法自适应学习哪些令牌最有利于蒸馏。

Method: 将 BiToK-SD 建模为双层优化问题。下层问题把 Top-K 令牌选择建模为可微的基于阈值的松弛，使被选位置能随学生策略演化而自适应调整；上层问题在选中的位置上执行知识蒸馏。

Result: 在数学推理基准上，BiToK-SD 在所有对比方法中取得最佳平均性能，同时仅需轻量的额外计算。

Conclusion: 通过双层优化自适应选择蒸馏令牌位置，可有效提升在线自蒸馏中紧凑语言模型的推理能力，且计算开销可控。

Abstract: Large language models have shown strong reasoning capabilities, but their high inference costs make knowledge distillation an important approach for transferring such capabilities to compact models in resource-constrained scenarios. On-policy self-distillation further reduces the reliance on external large teacher models while improving the reasoning ability of compact language models. However, existing methods typically either distill all token positions uniformly or select tokens using fixed heuristic criteria, assigning the same distillation strength to the selected positions rather than adaptively learning which tokens are most beneficial for distillation. To address these limitations, we propose BiToK-SD (Bilevel Top-K Token Selection for Self-Distillation), a bilevel-optimization-based token selection method that learns where distillation should be applied during on-policy self-distillation. Specifically, BiToK-SD is formulated as a bilevel optimization problem, where the lower-level problem models Top-K token selection as a differentiable threshold-based relaxation, allowing the selected positions to adapt as the student policy evolves, while the upper-level problem performs knowledge distillation on the selected positions. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that BiToK-SD achieves the best average performance among all compared methods while requiring only lightweight additional computation.

</details>


### [241] [Neural Fields Encode Adaptation Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07253)
*Prateik Sinha,Stefania Druga*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 神经场不仅能重建观测，其适应几何还编码了"如何改变"和"从何而来"的信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上神经场仅以重建观测的好坏来评价，这忽略了拟合网络的两个有用属性：适应新观测的难易程度，以及权重从早期观测中保留了什么信息。

Method: 将上述属性作为"适应几何"研究。图像方面：元学习类别特定初始化，将每个初始化适应新图像，测量网络适应所需的变化量，并用局部线性模型预测适应成本，还进行切核替换实验。物理场方面：将同一网络反复拟合序列观测，考察权重对历史信息的保留。

Result: 简单局部线性模型能很好预测适应成本，替换网络切核会显著降低预测效果。物理场中，两个波历史终止于完全相同的观测时，最终权重恢复波速符号的准确率为68.6%，而仅凭当前观测只有50%。切核特征值同时预测哪些变化易于学习以及它们被后续拟合覆盖的速度。

Conclusion: 适应依赖拟合网络的局部几何而非仅当前重建。神经场包含超出其当前重建的信息：它可以如何改变，以及它如何演变成现在的状态，且两种现象在定量上相互关联。

Abstract: Neural fields are usually evaluated by how well they reconstruct an observation. We show that this misses two useful properties of a fitted network: how easily it can adapt to new observations, and what its weights retain from earlier ones. We study these properties as adaptation geometry. For images, we meta-learn class-specific initializations, adapt each one to a new image, and measure how much the network must change to fit it. A simple local linear model closely predicts this adaptation cost, while replacing one network's tangent kernel with another's substantially worsens the prediction. Adaptation thus depends on the local geometry of the fitted network, not only on its current reconstruction. For physical fields, we repeatedly fit the same network to observations from a sequence. Its weights then retain information about that history. When two wave histories end at exactly the same observation, the final weights recover the sign of the wave velocity with 68.6% accuracy, whereas the current observation alone contains no such information and gives 50%. These two phenomena are quantitatively linked: tangent-kernel eigenvalues predict both which changes are easy to learn and how quickly they are overwritten by later fitting. Together, these results show that neural fields contain useful information beyond what they currently reconstruct: in how they can change and in how they got there.

</details>


### [242] [Neural Algorithmic Reasoning for Graph Saddle Point Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07255)
*Samantha Chen,Jesse He,Coleman Clougherty,Gal Mishne,Chester Holtz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 GraphPDHG，一种基于 Chambolle-Pock 原始-对偶混合梯度（PDHG）方法的图消息传递框架，用于求解一般图鞍点问题，并展示其可学习加速 PDHG 算法以及与 PDHG 对齐带来的更强规模泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算法推理旨在将神经网络与算法范式对齐，以求解多项式时间可解乃至计算更难的组合优化问题。现有方法在图优化问题的求解与泛化方面仍有不足，作者希望借助经典 PDHG 算法的结构，设计能高效求解图鞍点问题的神经网络架构。

Method: 提出 GraphPDHG，一种基于 Chambolle-Pock 原始-对偶混合梯度（PDHG）方法的消息传递框架，用于求解一般图鞍点问题。理论上证明该网络可通过模拟 PDHG 高效求解一类图鞍点问题，并能学习一种加速 PDHG 算法。
