Building on this structure, we introduce TRACE, a weights-only auditing method that localizes an unknown checkpoint update relative to frozen harmful and non-harmful reference prototypes and converts this geometry into a continuous harmful-objective score. TRACE requires neither model queries nor access to the unknown SFT data, and can be evaluated directly from checkpoint updates. Across distribution shifts, unseen data, different SFT configurations, partial checkpoint access, and LoRA/full-parameter fine-tuning, the trace remains stable and is positively associated with independently measured attack success rates. TRACE remains informative even at low harmful-objective proportions, providing a complementary auditing signal when behavioral evaluation is unavailable or incomplete. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Code4TRACE-54D3.

</details>


### [277] [Activation Denoising: A Robustness View on Parallel vs Sequential LLM Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07522)
*Yan Scholten,Rachel Lawrence,James Hensman,Stephan Günnemann,Alicia Curth,Riccardo Grazzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"带激活去噪的并行量化"，从鲁棒性视角把上游量化误差建模为噪声，通过预处理加度量加权舍入进行正则化，在保持全并行效率的同时恢复了序贯量化的大部分收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练后量化是大模型压缩的重要工具。可扩展性最好的方法对每层并行量化，但量化误差会沿残差流累积，且没有层去修正前面层的误差。序贯量化通过在已量化输出上逐层重校准来应对误差累积，效果更好，但串行调度在规模化时成为瓶颈。因此需要一种既能保持并行、又能抑制误差累积的方案。

Method: 提出并行量化+激活去噪：不逐层重校准，而是把上游误差建模为噪声，通过预处理步骤与度量加权舍入（metric-weighted rounding）使每层对该噪声鲁棒。该正则化在各层叠加后形成随深度累积的平滑性惩罚，抑制量化误差沿模型放大。此外，将权重乘以更一般的线性变换（不同于量化中常用的、须保持模型函数的正交旋转），并发现二者互补、效果可叠加。

Result: 实验表明，该鲁棒性正则化在一次并行传播中即可恢复序贯量化相当一部分收益，而耗时仅为其一小部分；与正交旋转结合时效果进一步提升。

Conclusion: 将累积量化误差视为鲁棒性问题，为规模化下更高效、更准确的LLM量化提供了有原则的理论基础。

Abstract: Post-training quantization is a powerful tool for compressing large language models. The most scalable methods quantize every layer in parallel, but quantization errors then compound through the residual stream, as no layer corrects for the errors of the layers before it. Sequential quantization accounts for this error compounding by re-calibrating each layer on the already-quantized outputs of its predecessors, yielding stronger results but at the cost of a serial schedule that becomes a bottleneck at scale. As a solution, we propose parallel quantization with activation denoising, which recovers much of the sequential benefit while keeping quantization fully parallel. Rather than re-calibrating layer-by-layer, we take a robustness perspective and model the upstream error as noise, regularizing to be robust to it through a preprocessing step followed by metric-weighted rounding. Applied at every layer, this regularization forms a depth-compounding smoothness penalty that dampens how strongly quantization errors amplify through the model. Unlike orthogonal rotations commonly used in quantization, which must preserve the model's function, we multiply the weights by a more general linear transformation. We find that the two are complementary and their effects compound. Empirically, our robustness regularization recovers a significant part of sequential quantization's benefit in a single parallel pass, at a fraction of its time. Overall, by treating compounding quantization errors as a robustness problem, we offer a principled foundation for more efficient and accurate LLM quantization at scale.

</details>


### [278] [Targeted search shows that random-device testing underestimates worst-case error in a simulated wave-based neural operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07529)
*Samrendra Roy,Jason Yoo,Souvik Chakraborty,Syed Bahauddin Alam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过数值案例研究指出，波基处理器上运行的混合傅里叶神经算子在随机抽样器件上验证不足：通过搜索找到的“最差器件”其误差可达随机蒙特卡洛器件最大值的数倍，因此随机测试只能估计超阈值频率，而最差器件搜索能给出误差上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 波基处理器有望为神经算子提供快速、节能的傅里叶层，但通常仅在随机采样器件上验证。实际使用需了解在制造与对准偏差下误差可能有多大，而随机验证无法回答这一问题，因此需要探究最差器件误差。

Method: 在一个风格化数值案例中，将混合傅里叶神经算子的四个谱层运行在仿真的相干4f处理器上（32个带容差的旋钮，半宽为代表值）。对120个模型（4任务×6训练方法×5随机种子），比较N个随机合规器件的最差误差与搜索器件误差。在确定性模拟器上使用一组冻结随机静态误差抽样，并进一步用20组新静态抽样、均匀/Sobol'采样、共享旋钮、第二种串扰模型、0.25–2的盒尺度及像素级器件模型进行验证。还比较了仅用随机静态误差训练的模型，以及随机与梯度搜索器件微调的效果。

Result: 搜索器件的留出误差为200个蒙特卡洛器件最大值的1.08–3.10倍，为1000个器件最大值的1.06–2.71倍；在20组新静态抽样中，120个模型有116个仍超过200个随机器件最大值。均匀采样下抽到此类器件的概率至多0.37%/模型。该差距在多种采样、模型和尺度下持续存在。仅用随机静态误差训练的模型在搜索器件上误差达标称的3.7–39.9倍；随机加梯度搜索器件微调在全部20个任务-种子对中取得六种方法中最低的搜索误差。两个换热器量各自定向搜索在全部39个模型中超过1000个随机器件最差误差，因而也超过Wilks 95/95界限；11个模型的平均压力无随机器件超过1%误差阈值，但搜索器件超过了。

Conclusion: 随机测试只能估计误差超过阈值的频率，而最差器件搜索能给出误差可能达到多大的下界。因此，波基神经算子处理器的验证不应仅依赖随机采样器件，应结合最差器件搜索来评估误差上界。

Abstract: Wave-based processors promise fast, energy-efficient Fourier layers for neural operators. They are usually validated on randomly sampled devices, but using them requires knowing how large their error can become under fabrication and alignment variation. In a stylised numerical case study, a hybrid Fourier neural operator runs its four spectral layers on simulated coherent 4f processors with 32 toleranced knobs, whose half-widths are representative rather than calibrated. For 120 models (four tasks, six training methods, five seeds), we compared the worst of N random in-spec devices with a searched one. On a deterministic simulator with one frozen draw of the random static errors, the searched device's held-out error was 1.08-3.10 times the maximum over 200 Monte Carlo devices and 1.06-2.71 times that over 1000. With 20 fresh static draws, it still exceeded the maximum over 200 random devices in 116 of 120 models. Under uniform sampling, the probability of drawing such a device is at most 0.37% per model (two-sided 95% Clopper-Pearson), which says nothing about how large its error is. The gap persisted with uniform or Sobol' sampling at the search's budget, shared knobs, a second crosstalk model, box scales of 0.25-2 and a pixel-level device model. Models trained only with random static errors reached 3.7-39.9 times their nominal error on searched devices, and fine-tuning on random and gradient-searched devices gave the lowest searched error of the six in all 20 task-seed pairs. For two heat-exchanger quantities, a search targeted at each exceeded the worst of 1000 random devices in all 39 models, and hence the Wilks 95/95 limit (worst of 59). For the mean pressure of 11 models, no random device exceeded a 1% error threshold, but the searched device did. Random testing estimates how often errors exceed a threshold; worst-device search gives a lower bound on how large they can be.

</details>


### [279] [Preserving Unstable Modes Through Inverse Dynamics in JEPA World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07540)
*Leonardo F. Toso,Yann LeCun,James Anderson,Oumayma Bounou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出JEPA式世界模型训练中"下一步预测+防坍缩正则"无法保证保留可控不稳定模态，并提出加入动作重构（逆动力学）损失来学习控制感知表征，理论上证明可保证编码器在有限时域可达子空间上单射，实证验证于多个视觉控制任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人系统常存在不稳定模态，微小扰动若无反馈纠正会无界增长。仅凭高维视觉观测控制这类系统，需要保留这些不稳定模态的表征。JEPA可从视觉数据学习表征及其动力学，但作者发现"下一步预测+防坍缩正则"并不保证可控不稳定模态被保留：训练损失可被最小化，而这些模态却被坍缩，导致无法从所学表征中实现稳定化。因此需要能保留对控制至关重要特征的表征。

Method: 在世界模型训练中引入动作重构目标（即逆动力学损失），以鼓励"控制感知"表征。理论上：证明精确动作重构使编码器在有限时域可达子空间上单射，从而编码器无法丢弃任何在H步内可由动作序列到达的状态方向；并证明当H增大时，有限时域可控性Gramian的主特征子空间收敛到可控不稳定子空间。理论结果在线性系统中建立，并在非线性视觉控制任务（CartPole、Walker2D、PointMaze）上实验验证。

Result: 理论上确立了动作重构与可控可达子空间单射性、以及可控性Gramian主特征子空间与可控不稳定子空间收敛性的关系（线性系统）。实验上在CartPole、Walker2D、PointMaze等非线性视觉控制任务中，结果表明这些发现可推广，凸显控制感知表征学习的收益。

Conclusion: 仅靠下一步预测加防坍缩正则不足以保留可控不稳定模态，会因模态坍缩导致无法稳定化。加入逆动力学/动作重构损失可促使表征保留控制关键方向，并在理论上具备单射与收敛保证，实验亦支持其在非线性视觉控制中的有效性。

Abstract: Robotic systems often exhibit unstable modes, along which small perturbations and disturbances can cause unbounded growth unless corrected through feedback. Controlling such systems from high-dimensional visual observations requires representations that preserve these modes. Joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs) provide a natural framework for learning such representations and their dynamics from visual data. However, we demonstrate that next step prediction combined with anti-collapse regularization does not guarantee that controllable unstable modes are preserved: the training loss can be minimized while these modes are collapsed, making stabilization from the learned representation impossible. To address this, we augment world-model training with an action reconstruction objective (i.e., an inverse dynamics loss) that encourages control-aware representations, namely, visual representations that preserve crucial features for control. We prove that exact action reconstruction makes the encoder injective on the finite-horizon reachable subspace. Thus, the encoder cannot discard any state direction reachable by an action sequence within $H$ steps. Moreover, we show that, as $H$ grows, the dominant eigenspace of the finite-horizon controllability Gramian converges to the controllable unstable subspace. We establish our theoretical results for linear systems and demonstrate empirically that our findings extend to nonlinear visual control tasks (CartPole, Walker2D, and PointMaze), highlighting the benefits of control-aware representation learning.

</details>


### [280] [Foundation Model-Aided Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Wireless Random Access Network Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07550)
*Myeung Suk Oh,Zhiyao Zhang,Alvaro Velasquez,Nathaniel D. Bastian,Jia Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用基础模型（FM）提升多智能体强化学习（MARL）在随机接入网络优化中的效率，设计了FM辅助的actor-critic算法并给出收敛性分析，实验表明可显著加快训练速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机接入是处理多终端不可预测流量的基础MAC层调度方案。尽管MARL已被用于优化随机接入无线网络，但其依赖经验驱动的分布式策略学习，每个优化任务都需大量训练开销，限制了实际应用可行性。

Method: 在基于共识的去中心化MARL架构中设计FM辅助的actor-critic算法，并在局部奖励交换与非线性值函数近似条件下进行收敛性分析。

Result: 收敛性分析表明该算法达到与传统MARL（采用critic模型交换和线性近似）相同的收敛阶；数值结果证明基于FM的方法显著提升了随机接入网络优化的MARL训练速度。

Conclusion: 利用基础模型可有效降低MARL在多样随机接入网络优化任务中的训练开销，提升其实用性。

Abstract: Random access (RA) is one of the most foundational medium access control (MAC) layer scheduling schemes for handling unpredictable data traffic from multiple terminals. While multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) has been explored to optimize RA-based wireless networks, its reliance on experience-driven, distributed policy learning incurs significant training overhead for each optimization task, limiting its feasibility in real-world applications. In this work, we propose to leverage a foundation model (FM) to improve MARL efficiency across diverse RA network optimization tasks. Specifically, we design an FM-aided actor-critic algorithm within a consensus-based decentralized MARL architecture and provide its convergence analysis under local reward exchanges and nonlinear value function approximations to show that our algorithm achieves the same convergence order as the conventional MARL with critic model exchanges and linear approximations. Our numerical results show that our FM-based approach significantly enhances MARL speed for RA network optimization.

</details>


### [281] [Which and When to Admit: Gradient Admission for Data-Centric Small Language Model Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07553)
*Hongyu Cao,Yanchi Liu,Kunpeng Liu,Xujiang Zhao,Wei Cheng,Zhengzhang Chen,Yanjie Fu,Haifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GRADE数据中心框架，通过状态感知样本选择与自校准步级门控，控制进入LoRA子空间的梯度，缓解梯度冲突、静态选择与子空间饱和问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA微调在小语言模型上存在三个结构性问题：冲突梯度相互抵消、静态数据选择无法跟踪学习动态、子空间饱和导致后续更新覆盖有用方向。作者认为有效适配需要控制哪些数据诱导的梯度进入LoRA子空间以及何时进入。

Method: GRADE包含两个机制：1）状态感知选择器，持续接纳与演化中的多任务梯度场对齐的样本；2）自校准步级门控，在接近饱和时拒绝可能导致破坏性覆盖的更新。

Result: 在三个当前代骨干模型和七个异构数据集指令池上，GRADE在准确率和鲁棒性上优于强数据选择和PEFT稳定化基线，是唯一在每个架构上均一致优于标准LoRA的方法，并产生更连贯的梯度轨迹和更少的破坏性覆盖。

Conclusion: SLM适配的成功不仅取决于选择哪些数据，还取决于哪些梯度被允许进入并保留在受限更新子空间中。

Abstract: LoRA fine-tuning adapts small language models (SLMs) to heterogeneous instruction data within a low-rank update subspace, making it vulnerable to three structural problems: conflicting gradients that cancel, static data selection that cannot track evolving learning dynamics, and subspace saturation that causes later updates to overwrite useful directions. We argue that effective adaptation therefore requires controlling which data-induced gradients enter the LoRA subspace and when. We propose GRADE (GRadient-Aligned Data-centric rEcipe), a data-centric framework combining two mechanisms: a state-aware selector that continually admits samples aligned with the evolving multi-task gradient field, and a self-calibrating step-level gate that rejects updates likely to cause destructive overwrite near saturation. Across three current-generation backbones and a heterogeneous seven-dataset instruction pool, GRADE outperforms strong data-selection and PEFT-stabilization baselines in accuracy and robustness. It is the only method to improve consistently over standard LoRA on every architecture, while producing more coherent gradient trajectories and less destructive overwrite. These results show that successful SLM adaptation depends not only on which data are selected, but also on which gradients are allowed to enter and persist in the constrained update subspace.

</details>


### [282] [Global Transport Couplings for Classifier-Free Guided Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07555)
*Katarina Petrović,Zander W. Blasingame,Danyal Rehman,İsmail İlkan Ceylan,Michael Bronstein,Stephen Y. Zhang,Lazar Atanackovic,Alexander Tong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Global Transport（GT），一种无需类别标签的全局类别无关最优传输耦合。GT 在无引导生成中会降低性能，但与 CFG 结合后在离散类别与连续文本条件图像生成中均稳定提升效果，说明条件流的耦合设计应在推理所用的引导流下评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最优传输耦合已被证明能降低无条件流模型的训练方差，但其在条件生成中的作用尚不明确。为每个条件单独构造耦合在现代图像基础模型的大规模或连续条件空间中不可行，因此需要一个可扩展的耦合方案。

Method: 提出 Global Transport（GT），一种全局、类别无关的最优传输耦合，计算时不使用类别标签，可让不同条件关联到源噪声的不同区域。将 GT 与 classifier-free guidance（CFG）结合使用，并在离散类别与连续文本条件的图像生成任务上，跨模型规模和采样预算进行评测，同时分析耦合选择对引导轨迹的影响。

Result: GT 单独使用时（无引导）会因条件与源噪声区域的关联方式而降低性能；但与 CFG 结合后，在多个领域、模型规模和采样预算下均一致提升生成质量。该反转现象表明条件流的耦合应在推理时的引导流下进行实证与理论评估。

Conclusion: 引导流设定下的耦合设计构成一个简单的训练时优化维度，可在不修改现有架构、采样器或引导机制的前提下提升性能。

Abstract: Optimal-transport couplings have been shown to reduce training variance in unconditional flow models, but their role in conditional generation remains unclear. A natural approach constructs separate couplings for each condition, but this is impractical for large or continuous conditioning spaces found in modern image foundation models. We introduce Global Transport (GT), a global class-agnostic optimal-transport coupling, computed without class labels. GT can associate different conditions with different regions of the source noise, and consequently worsens performance without guidance. However, when combined with classifier-free guidance (CFG), GT consistently improves generation across domains, model scales, and sampling budgets. This reversal suggests that couplings for conditional flows should be evaluated both empirically and theoretically under the guided flow used at inference, rather than on unguided generation. We evaluate GT over both discrete class and continuous text conditioned image generation across model scales, and investigate how coupling choice alters guided trajectories. These results identify coupling design in the guided flow setting as a simple training time axis to improve performance without modifying existing architectures, samplers, or guidance mechanisms.

</details>


### [283] [TAFFY: A Task-Adaptive Tabular Foundation Model with In-Context Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07559)
*Zijian Li,Xiangchen Song,Gongxu Luo,Jie Qiao,Ruichu Cai,Zhenhao Chen,Xinshuai Dong,Fan Feng,Guangyi Chen,Kun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出表格基础模型TAFFY，通过上下文多样性先验与任务条件循环Transformer提升从上下文推断任务特定预测关系的能力，在11个基准数据集上取得最低平均排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型的性能主要取决于能否从推理时的上下文中推断出任务特定的预测关系，本文旨在强化这一能力。

Method: 提出TAFFY，包含两个组件：（1）上下文多样性先验，通过对共享因果过程施加受控干预和分布偏移，从多个相关环境采样构建合成预训练上下文；（2）任务条件循环Transformer，迭代且有选择地对共享Transformer块组进行复用，并以任务条件门控调制最终隐藏状态更新。

Result: 在6个分类和5个回归基准数据集上，TAFFY取得了最低的平均排名。

Conclusion: 上下文多样性与任务自适应迭代精炼相结合，促使模型从上下文对比中识别预测关系并为每个任务动态调制上下文整合，从而提升表格任务的上下文学习表现。

Abstract: Recent progress in tabular foundation models suggests that training on synthetic tasks can substantially improve in-context learning capabilities, with overall performance largely depending on how well models can infer task-specific predictive relationships from the available context during inference. In this paper, we introduce TAFFY, a tabular foundation model with an In-Context Diversity Prior and a Task-Conditioned Looped Transformer that strengthen this ability. Specifically, to construct each synthetic pretraining context, the In-Context Diversity Prior samples from multiple related environments derived via controlled interventions and distribution shifts on a shared causal process. This in-context diversity encourages the model to learn a more comprehensive and task-specific representation. Moreover, the Task-Conditioned Looped Transformer iteratively and selectively applies a shared group of Transformer blocks to refine contextual representations, with a task-conditioned gate modulating the final hidden-state update. This enables task-adaptive iterative refinement. Together, these components encourage the model to identify predictive relationships from contextual contrasts during pretraining and dynamically modulate context integration for each task. Across six classification and five regression benchmark datasets, TAFFY attains the lowest average rank.

</details>


### [284] [Learning a Mixture of GFlowNets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07562)
*Tiago da Silva,Amauri H. Souza,Salem Lahlou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出描述GFlowNet混合模型的一般性理论框架，统一解释连续索引与离散索引两类集成方法，并基于Doob h-变换提出分层条件GFlowNet，在不增加显著计算开销的前提下加快收敛并提升模式覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练GFlowNet集成模型虽能改善状态空间探索与收敛性，但常带来大量运行时开销，且集成方法与基础模型之间的概念联系尚不清晰。因此需要统一的理论框架来理解并改进这类集成方法。

Method: 提出描述GFlowNet混合模型的通用理论框架，并将其特化为连续索引（CI）与离散索引（DI）两类集合。CI GFlowNet通过随机特征展开进行解释，可证明提升图结构任务中的采样器表达能力，并通过谱偏移减少学习不稳定性；DI GFlowNet涵盖已有GFlowNet训练方法，并支撑新提出的分层条件（SC）GFlowNet。SC GFlowNet受马尔可夫链Doob h-变换启发，按给定模函数分解状态空间，并限制各分量从不同子集采样，支持集中式与分量级并行训练。

Result: CI GFlowNet可证明提升图结构任务的表达能力并降低学习不稳定性；DI GFlowNet统一了已有方法并催生SC GFlowNet；SC GFlowNet的集中式与分量级并行训练均显著加快学习收敛与模式覆盖，且不引入任何不可忽略的额外计算。

Conclusion: 该工作为GFlowNet集成提供了统一的理论视角，并提出的SC GFlowNet在保持计算效率的同时改善了收敛速度与模式覆盖，为高效采样离散目标分布提供了新思路。

Abstract: Learning an ensemble of GFlowNets to sample from a discrete target distribution has become a common approach for achieving better state space exploration and convergence than that of a monolithic sampler. However, these methods often add a substantial runtime overhead to the base model, and their conceptual connection remains elusive. To address this, we first propose a general-purpose theoretical framework for describing a mixture of GFlowNets, which we specialize into continuously (CI) and discretely indexed (DI) collections. On the one hand, we show CI GFlowNets can be interpreted through the lens of a random features expansion, provably boosting the sampler's expressivity in graph-structured tasks and reducing learning instability via spectral shifting. On the other hand, we demonstrate DI GFlowNets encompass prior approaches for GFlowNet training and provide the foundation for the newly proposed Stratum-Conditioned (SC) GFlowNets. This method, which is inspired by the Doob's h-transform of Markov chains, decomposes the state space according to a prescribed modular function and restricts each component to sample from a distinct subset of it. Importantly, SC GFlowNets support centralized and component-wise embarrassingly parallel training, and we show both of them significantly speed up learning convergence and mode coverage without introducing any non-negligible extra computation.

</details>


### [285] [Complementary Feature Domains: Information Preservation Does Not Imply Predictive-Contribution Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07565)
*Timothy Oladunni,Farouk Ganiyu-Adewumi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出互补特征域（CFD）理论，将预测价值刻画为由表示及其实现族共同诱导的、以上下文为索引的贡献系统，并证明Shannon信息保持并不意味着该贡献系统被保持：可逆表示变换可在目标信息不变的情况下改变受限决策族下的预测贡献。作者用CFD贡献缺陷量化这一变化，给出有界Lipschitz效用下缺陷受联盟行动集行为距离与联盟不相容性之和约束的定量转移律，并用受控ECG实验验证机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点常将信息保持等同于预测价值保持。本文动机在于指出这一等价性不成立：即使表示变换可逆、目标信息完全不变，在固定决策族（如仿射学习器）下预测贡献仍可能改变，因此需要一个能刻画表示与实现共同决定预测价值的理论框架。

Method: 建立互补特征域（CFD）理论，把预测价值定义为由表示及其实现族共同诱导的、以上下文为索引的贡献系统；形式化CFD贡献缺陷以度量受控重编码下上下文贡献的变化。对满足有界Lipschitz效用的情形，证明每个联盟效用变化受重编码前后可达行动集之间行为距离的约束，进而每个上下文贡献缺陷受相应联盟不相容性之和的约束；并分析行为闭包（不变性）与精确补偿（恢复性）两种极限情形。最后用受控ECG实验进行验证：采用非线性双射重编码，在冻结的时频表示上保持信息不变，观察固定仿射学习器的精度变化，并施加精确逆变换检验恢复。

Result: 理论上证明Shannon信息保持不蕴含CFD贡献系统保持；给出贡献缺陷的定量上界：联盟效用位移受行为距离约束，上下文贡献缺陷受联盟不相容性之和约束；行为精确闭包导致不变性，补偿越精确越能恢复贡献。ECG实验中，非线性双射重编码在冻结时频表示上保持信息，却改变了固定仿射学习器的准确率，施加精确逆变换后所有被测联盟准确率均恢复。

Conclusion: 研究将信息保持与依赖实现方式的预测贡献区分开来，为多表示预测提供了定量转移律。其意义在于：信息层面的等价不足以保证决策层面的等价，预测价值应视为表示与实现族共同作用的上下文索引贡献系统，并可通过行为距离与联盟不相容性进行定量刻画与恢复。

Abstract: Complementary Feature Domains (CFD) theory characterizes predictive value as a context-indexed contribution system induced jointly by representations and their realization family. We show that Shannon-information preservation does not imply preservation of this contribution system: an invertible representation transformation can leave target information unchanged while altering predictive contribution under a restricted decision family. We formalize the resulting transition through a CFD contribution defect that measures how contextual contributions change under controlled recoding. For bounded Lipschitz utility, we show that each coalition utility shift is bounded by the behavioral distance between the attainable action sets before and after recoding; consequently, every contextual contribution defect is bounded by the sum of the corresponding coalition incompatibilities. Exact behavioral closure yields invariance, while increasingly accurate compensation yields restoration. A controlled ECG experiment illustrates the mechanism: a nonlinear bijective recoding preserves the information in a frozen time-frequency representation but changes accuracy under a fixed affine learner; applying the exact inverse restores all tested coalition accuracies. The result separates information preservation from realization-dependent contribution and provides a quantitative transition law for multi-representation prediction.

</details>


### [286] [Mechanistic Interpretability of Atmospheric Rivers in GraphCast](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07583)
*Madelyn Mathai,Timothy B. Higgins,Kevin M. Grise,Chirag Agarwal,Antonios Mamalakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究用稀疏自编码器（SAE）分析GraphCast，揭示其内部计算了"大气河强度（IVT）"这一稳定变量，Matryoshka SAE还能按重要性排序概念并揭示其关系，干预实验证实因果性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI天气模型已能与业务预报媲美，但其内部如何表征大气仍是未解问题。特征归因只能说明哪些输入模式重要，无法揭示模型内部计算了什么、如何组合信息。作者以大气河为切入点，尝试打开模型"黑箱"。

Method: 在GraphCast上训练稀疏自编码器（含标准SAE与Matryoshka SAE）以提取其学到的概念，聚焦大气河现象；Matryoshka SAE可将概念按重要性排序并显露概念间关系；并通过干预实验验证概念的因果作用。

Result: 两种SAE均显示GraphCast将大气河强度（以积分水汽输送IVT度量）计算为一个稳定的内部变量，尽管IVT既非输入也非输出目标；大气河概念跨网络深度持续存在；干预实验确认其因果性；相比标准SAE的无结构概念检索，Matryoshka SAE能有序揭示概念重要性及关联。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一条发现模型内部变量、并判断模型实际依赖哪些变量的途径；这是进一步追问这些变量在气候变暖导致现象变化时是否仍有意义的前提。

Abstract: While AI weather models now rival operational forecasts, how they represent the atmosphere internally remains an open question: feature attribution reveals which input patterns matter, not what the model computes or how it combines information internally. We train sparse autoencoders (SAEs) on GraphCast to uncover its learned concepts, using atmospheric rivers as our phenomenon of focus. Both standard and Matryoshka SAEs show GraphCast computes atmospheric river intensity, measured by integrated vapor transport (IVT), as a stable internal variable, despite IVT being neither an input nor a target. In contrast to the unstructured concept retrieval of the standard SAE, the Matryoshka SAE orders concepts by importance and exposes their relations. Atmospheric river concepts persist across depth and direct interventions confirm causality. This method offers a way to find internal variables and determine which of them the model actually relies on, which is a prerequisite for asking whether those variables remain meaningful as the phenomenon changes under a warming climate.

</details>


### [287] [Hub for Outliers, Spokes for Inliers: Uniform Latent Space Construction for Dual-Mismatched Semi-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07610)
*Li Yuan,Yaxin Hou,Jiawei Tang,Yongbiao Gao,Yuheng Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对半监督学习中标注与未标注数据在类别分布和标签空间上的双重不匹配问题，提出了一种"轮毂-辐条"（hub-spoke）隐空间几何结构，结合基于证据的分类器，提升了特征判别性与不确定性分离能力，在多种设置下最高提升3.25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统半监督学习假设标注与未标注数据共享相同的类别分布和标签空间。但现实中未标注数据往往类别不平衡且包含未知类别样本，导致分布与标签空间的双重不匹配，进而使多数类主导隐空间、未知类样本被过度自信地误分类，损害了特征判别性与伪标签质量。

Method: 提出一种 hub-spoke 隐空间几何结构：已知类别均匀分布在中心 hub 周围，每个类别围绕其原型形成紧凑簇；hub 为高不确定性的未知类样本提供低证据区域的锚点。该结构与基于证据的分类器相结合，通过结构化特征组织缓解多数类主导问题，并引导高不确定性未知类样本向 hub 聚集。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法优于当前最先进的方法，在各种设置下最高提升3.25%。

Conclusion: 通过 hub-spoke 隐空间几何与证据分类器的结合，可有效应对类别分布与标签空间的双重不匹配，增强特征判别性和不确定性分离，为半监督学习在更现实的开放、不平衡场景下的应用提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Semi-supervised learning typically assumes that labeled and unlabeled data share an identical class distribution and label space. However, this setting is often violated: unlabeled data may be imbalanced and contain unknown class samples, causing mismatches in both class distribution and label space. Such dual mismatch leads to majority classes dominating the latent space and unknown class samples being overconfidently misclassified, degrading feature discriminability and pseudo-label quality. To address this, we propose a hub-spoke latent geometry, where known classes are uniformly distributed around a central hub and each class forms compact clusters around its prototype, while the hub provides an anchor for a low-evidence region specifically designed for high-uncertainty unknown class samples. Integrated with an evidence-based classifier, this geometry ultimately enhances feature discriminability and uncertainty separation by mitigating majority-class domination through structured feature organization and guiding high-uncertainty unknown class samples toward the hub. Extensive experiments show that our method outperforms state-of-the-art methods, with a maximum improvement of 3.25% across various settings.

</details>


### [288] [AFA-BANDIT: Provably Near-Optimal Online Multi-Feature Classification Under Budget Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07615)
*AbdAlRahman Odeh,Teng-Hui Huang,Hesham El Gamal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将在线主动特征获取(AFA)建模为带背包的组合多臂老虎机(BwK)问题，提出改进的regret上界及LP-Chain算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线AFA方法要么使用无性能保证的深度强化学习，要么优化成本调整后的奖励而非强制全局预算约束，缺乏理论保证和预算控制。

Method: 将在线AFA形式化为组合Bandits with Knapsacks问题，耦合成特征获取与预测；利用基数感知置信界和子集更新结构获得改进的regret上界；提出LP-Chain变体，通过搜索规模随特征数线性增长的成本感知特征子集链，避免指数级动作空间。

Result: 在合成数据上，LP-Chain优于基于HEDGE的BwK和深度RL的在线AFA基线，且随特征数增加具有良好扩展性；经验上达到可比的预测性能。

Conclusion: 该组合BwK框架为在线AFA提供了更优的regret上界，LP-Chain在保持预测性能的同时有效应对大规模特征场景。

Abstract: Active Feature Acquisition (AFA) is a classification problem in which an agent decides which costly features to acquire before predicting each sample's label. Unlike batch AFA, which trains a fixed policy and classifier offline on fully observed data, online AFA updates its predictor from revealed labels as samples arrive. Existing online methods either use deep reinforcement learning (RL) without performance guarantees or maximize cost-adjusted reward rather than enforce a global budget. We formulate online AFA as a combinatorial Bandits with Knapsacks (BwK) problem that couples acquisition and prediction. Unlike prior bandit-based AFA and classical BwK, our setting has combinatorial complexity, evolving rewards, a global budget, and structured side information. We obtain an improved regret upper bound over standard BwK bounds in this framework, leveraging a cardinality-aware confidence bound and the subset update structure. To avoid an exponentially large action space, we propose \emph{LP-Chain}, a variant that searches a cost-aware chain of feature subsets with a size that grows linearly with the number of features. While the regret upper bound is specific to the combinatorial framework, \emph{LP-Chain} empirically achieves comparable predictive performance. On synthetic data, \emph{LP-Chain} outperforms HEDGE-based BwK and deep RL-based online AFA baselines and scales favorably to more features.

</details>


### [289] [Stateless Language Agents: Scaling Long-Horizon Automated Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07625)
*Qizheng Zhang,Changxiu Ji,Isaac Sun,Yuetai Li,Shubhangi Upasani,Sherry Ruan,Boyuan Ma,Fenglu Hong,Vamsidhar Kamanuru,Yoonho Lee,Yuzhen Mao,Genghan Zhang,Rulin Shao,Qiuyang Mang,Andy Dimnaku,Changran Hu,Radha Poovendran,Kunle Olukotun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"无状态语言智能体"（SLA）框架，主张将研究状态从智能体对话中剥离，由外部框架持有，从而在长周期自动化研究中避免历史膨胀、重复劳动与无效消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化研究系统越来越多地让大模型智能体长时间运行，但更多推理并不等于更多进展：智能体会重复回放不断增长的历史、重复彼此的工作，或在消耗大量 token 的同时停止实验。现有评测多用短预算或过早饱和的基准，导致这些失效模式未被检验。作者将其归因于两个设计选择：研究状态存放在哪里，以及由谁决定下一步尝试什么。

Method: 提出无状态语言智能体（SLA），原则为"有状态搜索 + 无状态智能体"：智能体不跨调用携带对话，由框架持有研究状态（候选方案与实测结果），并在每次调用时重建全新且角色专属的上下文。在 SLA 框架中，无状态 Advisor 阅读框架汇总的各搜索方向证据并分配具体实验给并行的 Worker。

Result: 在软件工程、内核优化与算法设计三类任务上，以最高十亿级 token 预算对比三个近期框架：SLA 在每个任务上取得最佳最终结果，并以减少超过 84% 的 token 达到最强内核基线的最终性能。在共享检查点上的消融显示，聚焦上下文与显式任务分配各自都有贡献，且效果可在完整运行中累积；Advisor 消耗的 token 不足 0.6%。

Conclusion: 结果支持采用 SLA，即将持久的研究状态排除在智能体对话之外；同时表明短评测周期可能误判研究系统及其组件的真实能力。

Abstract: Automated research systems increasingly run LLM agents over long horizons, but more inference does not by itself produce more progress: agents replay growing histories, duplicate one another's work, or stop experimenting while token consumption continues. Yet most evaluations use short budgets or benchmarks that saturate early, leaving these failure modes untested. We trace these failures to two choices: where research state lives and who decides what to try next. We introduce Stateless Language Agents (SLAs), built on the principle of stateful search with stateless agents: no agent carries its conversation across invocations; instead, the harness owns the research state (candidate solutions and measured outcomes) and reconstructs a fresh and role-specific context for every invocation. What each agent sees becomes an explicit design choice rather than a history that grows with the run. We implement this principle in the SLA framework, where a stateless Advisor reads harness-summarized evidence across search directions and assigns concrete experiments to parallel Workers. We evaluate SLA against three recent frameworks on software engineering, kernel optimization, and algorithm design at budgets of up to one billion tokens. SLA achieves the best final result on every task and reaches the strongest kernel baseline's final performance with over 84% fewer tokens. Ablations from shared checkpoints show that focused contexts and explicit assignments each contribute to SLA's progress, with effects that can compound over full runs, while the Advisor consumes less than 0.6% of tokens. These results argue for SLAs, which keep durable research state out of agent conversations, and show that short evaluation horizons can misjudge research systems and their components.

</details>


### [290] [Complementary Supervised and Self-Supervised Representations for Out-of-Distribution Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07628)
*Qingying Hao,Zikang Chen,Chuxuan Hu,Jinyuan Jia,Bo Li,Gang Wang,Carl Gunter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究自监督表示（SSL）能否为监督式图神经网络的OOD节点分类提供互补信号，提出两个与骨干网络无关的框架：Co-Train（联合学习并自适应整合监督与SSL表示）与Dual-Space Retrieval（双空间非参数预测与置信度感知融合）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图分布会随时间与领域发生显著变化，GNN的OOD泛化仍具挑战；监督与自监督图表示学习目标不同、视角互补，因此作者探索SSL表示能否提升监督式OOD节点分类性能。

Method: 提出两个与骨干网络无关的框架：1）Co-Train在训练阶段联合学习监督与SSL表示并自适应整合；2）Dual-Space Retrieval在推理阶段于两个表示空间进行非参数预测，并通过置信度感知融合结合预测结果。两个编码器独立参数化，无需共享相同GNN架构。

Result: 在四个涵盖时间与跨域分布偏移的图基准上，评估多种GNN骨干与两种SSL目标（DGI、GRACE）。Co-Train持续优于强监督OOD基线，Dual-Space Retrieval作为灵活的非参数替代方案也取得具竞争力的性能；表示分析与消融实验进一步验证SSL表示的互补性。

Conclusion: 自监督表示能为监督表示提供互补信息，在多种骨干网络、SSL目标与分布偏移设定下均可提升OOD节点分类性能。

Abstract: Out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization remains challenging for graph neural networks (GNNs), as graph distributions can vary substantially across time and domains. Supervised and self-supervised graph representation learning are guided by distinct objectives and offer different perspectives on graph representations. In this work, we study whether self-supervised representations (SSL) can provide complementary signals to improve supervised OOD node classification. We develop two backbone-agnostic frameworks that exploit such information at different stages of learning and prediction. Co-Train jointly learns supervised and SSL representations and adaptively integrates them during training, while Dual-Space Retrieval performs non-parametric prediction in the two representation spaces and combines their predictions through confidence-aware fusion at inference time. The supervised and SSL encoders are separately parameterized and need not share the same GNN architecture.
  We evaluate multiple GNN backbones and two distinct SSL objectives, DGI and GRACE, on four graph benchmarks spanning temporal and cross-domain distribution shifts. Extensive experiments show that Co-Train consistently outperforms strong supervised OOD baselines, while Dual-Space Retrieval achieves competitive performance as a flexible non-parametric alternative. Results across different backbones and SSL objectives, together with representation analyses and ablations, demonstrate that SSL representations provide complementary information to supervised representations and can improve OOD node classification across diverse settings.

</details>


### [291] [Does On-Policy Distillation for Safety Pose Backdoor Risks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07654)
*Jian Luo,Kehan Qi,Qingqiao Hu,Meilong Xu,Jiacheng Qiu,Weimin Lyu,Jiawei Zhou,Chao Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示on-policy distillation (OPD)存在被忽视的安全威胁：安全对齐但含后门的教师模型可将隐藏恶意行为传播给原本干净的学生模型，并提出简单缓解策略Lazy Defense。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将OPD视为提升大语言模型安全性的有效工具，但普遍假设教师模型和训练数据是可信的。本文旨在揭示这一假设下被忽视的威胁，即教师模型的后门可能通过OPD传播给学生模型。

Method: 作者构建了威胁模型：使用一个安全对齐但被植入后门的教师模型，对初始干净的学生模型进行on-policy distillation。通过控制中毒率、训练轮数以及KL类型（top-k KL与sampled-token KL），系统性评估后门传播效果，并提出Lazy Defense缓解方法，通过裁剪KL奖励使更新更保守。

Result: 在仅3%中毒率下，蒸馏学生模型的攻击成功率(ASR)最高可达70%；增加训练轮数会放大风险，仅10个中毒样本在16轮后ASR达67%；top-k KL相比sampled-token KL在多数设置下更快诱发触发器条件下的有害行为；Lazy Defense可延缓低中毒率下的后门传播。

Conclusion: OPD能够传播后门，说明其安全性存在显著风险，亟需针对OPD的安全风险加以应对。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has attracted growing attention as an effective way to transfer capabilities from teacher models to student models. Recent studies further explore OPD as a tool for improving large language model safety with promising results. However, these approaches typically assume that the teacher and training data are trustworthy. In this paper, we uncover an overlooked threat to OPD for safety: a safety-aligned but backdoored teacher can propagate its hidden malicious behavior to an initially clean student. Under our threat model, a poisoning rate as low as 3% results in an attack success rate (ASR) of up to 70% on the distilled student. We further identify two training choices that can amplify this risk. First, increasing the number of training epochs can lead to high ASR even at low poisoning rates. With only 10 poisoned samples, ASR reaches 67% after 16 epochs. Second, the commonly used top-k KL can accelerate backdoor transfer, causing trigger-conditioned harmful behavior to emerge earlier than sampled-token KL in most settings. Alongside these findings, we explore a simple mitigation, Lazy Defense, which clips KL rewards to make student updates less aggressive, limiting aggressive updates and slowing backdoor learning. Experiments show that Lazy Defense delays backdoor transfer in low poisoning rate settings. Together, our findings reveal that OPD can propagate backdoors, highlighting the need to address the safety risks of OPD.

</details>


### [292] [MS-ECG-FM: Towards a More Universal Electrocardiogram Foundation Model for Health Monitoring using Multi-source Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07662)
*Robert A. Lewis,I-Min Chiu,Kyle Verrier,Karthik Jayaraman Raghuram,Francoise Marvel,Salar Abbaspourazad,Anshuman Mishra,Guillermo Sapiro,Andrew C. Miller,Joseph Futoma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出多来源对比对齐的心电基础模型MS-ECG-FM，利用多种临床报告监督，在广泛心电检测任务及缩减导联场景下全面超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有心电基础模型仅依赖心电解读报告作为监督信号，而解读报告只涵盖临床医生常规识别的部分波形信息，导致表征学习忽略心电中更广泛的诊断信号。作者希望突破这一单一监督的局限。

Method: 构建心电基础模型MS-ECG-FM，通过对比对齐同时关联多种不同临床记录类型，包括心电、超声心动图、放射学及出院报告，实现多来源对齐训练，并在扩展的心电检测基准上进行评估。

Result: MS-ECG-FM在心电可检测的全谱系病症上全面优于现有方法，在缩减导联配置下同样表现优异；不同报告对不同诊断领域有增益，多来源对齐可捕捉互补信息。

Conclusion: 多来源临床报告对比对齐能充分利用互补诊断信息，使心电表征在临床多样化任务中保持一致且强劲的表现，为心电基础模型提供了更有效的监督范式。

Abstract: Electrocardiography (ECG) records the electrical activity of the heart, aiding diagnosis by detecting abnormalities in cardiac function. ECG foundation models have demonstrated promising results, but are limited by a reliance on ECG interpretation reports as their sole supervision. Because interpretation reports only capture the subset of waveform information routinely recognized by clinicians, this constrains representation learning to overlook the broader diagnostic signals present in ECG. We introduce a new ECG foundation model --- MS-ECG-FM --- that is trained through contrastive alignment to multiple distinct clinical note types, including ECG, echocardiography, radiology, and discharge reports. We evaluate MS-ECG-FM on an extended set of ECG detection benchmarks, showing that it comprehensively outperforms existing methods on the full span of conditions that ECG can detect, including in reduced-lead configurations. Different reports improve representations for different diagnostic domains, while multi-source alignment captures their complementary information and produces consistently strong representations across clinically diverse tasks.

</details>


### [293] [Exact-Solution Volume and Length Generalization in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07676)
*Yijia Jessica Zhu,David Chiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出归一化精确解体积（NESV）来刻画Transformer解的长度泛化能力，给出四个任务的渐近界，并通过体积分析定位INDEX任务的误差来源以改进模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer表达能力研究只关注能否解决任务，却很少说明所学解能否泛化到更长的输入长度，因此需要一种量化长度泛化能力的理论工具。

Method: 定义NESV为在长度为n的每个输入上都得到精确解的参数区域占某有界参数区域的比例；对固定宽度、单层、log n缩放的注意力Transformer，推导FIRST、MAJORITY、INDEX、PARITY四个任务的渐近NESV界；并针对INDEX做误差来源的体积累分析，设计消除其中一项误差的结构化模型。

Result: NESV界为FIRST Θ(1)、MAJORITY Θ(1/(n log n))、INDEX Θ(1/n^3)、PARITY 0，与"体积衰减越快越难长度泛化"的经验一致；INDEX分析发现两个随n增长的误差源，改进模型理论上把NESV界提升到Θ(n^{-1})，在10倍训练长度上准确率85%，优于原模型的60%。

Conclusion: 体积分析可作为识别长度敏感性具体来源的有效方法，并能为任务特定的模型改进提供理论指导。

Abstract: Research on transformer expressivity shows whether a transformer is capable of solving a given task, but gives little indication of whether the solution, if learned, is generalizable to longer input lengths. We study this question through normalized exact-solution volume (NESV): the fraction of a bounded parameter region that achieves an exact solution on every input of length $n$. For fixed-width, single-layer transformers with $\log n$-scaled attention, we establish asymptotic bounds on NESV for four tasks: FIRST ($Θ(1)$), MAJORITY ($Θ(1/(n\log n))$), INDEX ($Θ(1/n^3)$), and PARITY ($0$). These results are consistent with previous empirical results: the faster the exact-solution volume decays with input length, the harder it is to length-generalize on that task. Looking deeper into INDEX, our volume analysis reveals two error sources that grow with $n$. Consequently, we study a transformer model that would structurally eliminate one of the terms, theoretically improving the NESV bound to $Θ(n^{-1})$, and empirically achieving 85% accuracy when tested at $10\times$ the training length, compared with the 60% accuracy of the original model. We conclude that volume analysis may be a useful approach to identify concrete sources of length sensitivity and thus provide insights into task-specific model refinements.

</details>


### [294] [Adaptive Model Inversion Attacks Generalize a Privacy-Robustness Tradeoff](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07677)
*Shailen Smith,Rasmus Torp,Adam Breuer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 高分辨率模型反演攻击(MIA)的标准评估显著低估了训练数据隐私泄露；经自适应攻击调整和评估器变化后，泄露率提高1.16至6.59倍，且泄露与对抗鲁棒性高度相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高分辨率MIA的标准评估可能低估隐私泄露，把优化失败和测量失败误认为隐私保护；同时隐私与对抗鲁棒性之间的关系被低估的泄露率所掩盖，需重新审视。

Method: 对攻击进行简单的自适应修改，并改变用于评估重建结果的外部分类器的特征基（如对比标准Inception与对抗训练Inception评估器），在FaceScrub数据集上重新测量多种防御、标准训练技术(MixUp、对抗训练)及无防御模型的泄露率。

Result: 在FaceScrub上泄露率提高1.16至6.59倍，防御报告隐私最强者的增幅最大；同一重建图像在不同评估器下身份识别率不同；调整攻击并变化评估器后，重建泄露与对抗鲁棒性紧密相关，跨越近期防御和标准训练范式。

Conclusion: 标准MIA评估可能将优化与测量失败误判为隐私保护；对抗鲁棒性可作为重建脆弱性的攻击无关代理，适用范围比先前理论更广。遗留问题：能否设计出在不牺牲对抗鲁棒性的前提下降低训练数据重建的实用防御。

Abstract: In this paper, we show that standard evaluations of high-resolution Model Inversion Attacks (MIAs) significantly underestimate training-data privacy leakage. State-of-the-art privacy defenses, standard training techniques such as MixUp and Adversarial Training, and undefended models all leak training images at rates 1.16 to 6.59 times higher on FaceScrub under simple adaptive changes to the attack, with the largest increases among defenses reporting the strongest privacy. We further show that measured leakage depends on the feature basis of the external classifier used to evaluate reconstructions: for the same reconstructed images, an adversarially trained Inception evaluator identifies the targeted identity at different rates than the standard Inception evaluator. Our results suggest that standard MIA evaluation can mistake optimization and measurement failures for privacy.
  These underestimated leakage rates also concealed a broader relationship between privacy and adversarial robustness. Once we adapt the attack and vary the evaluator, reconstruction leakage closely tracks adversarial robustness across recent defenses and standard training regimes, suggesting that robustness provides an attack-agnostic proxy for reconstruction vulnerability that applies far more broadly than previously theorized. This raises an open question: can a practical defense reduce training-data reconstruction without paying a corresponding cost in adversarial robustness?

</details>


### [295] [Improving Synthetic Data Generation for Argument Mining via Adversarial Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07699)
*Zhijun Zhang,Qianlong Wang,Keyang Ding,Genan Dai,Bowen Zhang,Bin Liang,Ruifeng Xu,Yongsheng Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于论辩挖掘（AM）数据合成的新型对抗强化学习框架，通过生成器与判别器的对抗优化，同时提升合成数据的结构准确性与多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论辩挖掘受限于高质量结构标注数据的稀缺。虽然大语言模型在合成数据生成方面展现出潜力，但生成结构准确且足够多样的合成论辩数据仍具挑战。

Method: 从一个新视角重新审视AM合成数据生成，提出对抗强化学习框架。该框架在对抗循环中联合优化生成器和判别器：生成器产生结构化AM实例，判别器通过区分真实数据与合成候选数据来提供学习信号。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上，无论全数据还是低资源设置下，该框架均持续提升AM性能，验证了其有效性与可扩展性。

Conclusion: 对抗反馈使生成器逐步提升合成论辩数据的结构准确性，同时借助对抗机制保持数据多样性，为缓解AM数据稀缺问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Argument Mining (AM) is fundamentally constrained by the scarcity of high-quality structure-annotated datasets. While LLMs have shown promise in synthetic data generation, producing synthetic AM data that is both structurally accurate and sufficiently diverse remains a challenging problem. To address this problem, we revisit synthetic data generation for AM from a new perspective and propose a novel adversarial reinforcement learning framework for data synthesis. The proposed framework jointly optimizes the generator and the discriminator in an adversarial loop, in which the generator produces structured AM instances, and the discriminator provides learning signals by distinguishing real data from synthetic candidates. This enables the generator to progressively improve both the structural accuracy of generated argument data while maintaining diversity through adversarial feedback. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently improves AM performance on three benchmark datasets in both full-data and low-resource settings, validating its effectiveness and scalability.

</details>


### [296] [WASD: Wasserstein-based Knowledge Distillation for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07706)
*Byeonghu Na,Donghyeok Shin,Yeongmin Kim,Mina Kang,Il-Chul Moon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于Wasserstein的知识蒸馏方法WASD，通过token语义信息改进LLM蒸馏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归大语言模型能力快速提升，但规模增大带来推理时巨大的计算和内存成本。知识蒸馏可将大模型知识迁移到小模型，但现有LLM蒸馏方法主要依赖逐词表索引处概率值的散度，未显式利用token级语义信息。

Method: 提出Wasserstein知识蒸馏WASD，通过基于Wasserstein距离并利用token嵌入构建代价矩阵来引入token级语义信息；为保证计算可行性，采用Sinkhorn散度并推导出可高效优化的梯度等价目标，无需引入额外网络。

Result: 在多种LLM家族和规模上的实验表明，WASD在指令跟随、数学推理和代码生成等多样任务上一致提升蒸馏性能。

Conclusion: 结果凸显token空间中的语义信息对LLM蒸馏中有效分布对齐的重要性，代码已公开。

Abstract: Autoregressive large language models (LLMs) have rapidly advanced in capability, but their increasing scale comes with substantial computational and memory costs at inference time. Knowledge distillation (KD) offers a practical solution by transferring knowledge from a large teacher model to a smaller student model via alignment of discrete probability distributions. However, existing KD methods for LLMs primarily rely on divergences that evaluate discrepancies through probability values at each vocabulary index, without explicitly leveraging token-level semantic information. We propose Wasserstein-based knowledge distillation (WASD) for LLMs, which incorporates token-level semantic information via the Wasserstein-based distance with a cost matrix derived from token embeddings. To ensure computational tractability, we adopt the Sinkhorn divergence and derive a gradient-equivalent objective that can be efficiently optimized without introducing additional networks. Experiments across multiple LLM families and scales show that WASD consistently improves distillation performance on diverse tasks, including instruction following, mathematical reasoning, and code generation. Our results highlight the importance of semantic information encoded in the token space for effective distribution alignment in LLM distillation. The implementation is publicly available at https://github.com/aailab-kaist/WASD .

</details>


### [297] [Neuromotor Hierarchy Network: Physiological Inductive Biases for Robust Generalization in sEMG Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07713)
*He Wang,Hongyuan Qi,Zhaoxian Zhang,Jinbin Luo,Linyi He,Mehul Motani,Changsheng Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出神经运动层级网络(NHN)，从任务监督中学习紧凑的潜在神经运动状态，实现高效、跨用户跨会话的sEMG解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: sEMG虽可提供可穿戴、非侵入的神经肌肉接口，但群体级解码困难：sEMG与神经肌肉活动的关系因用户和会话而异，任务相关动态横跨多通道与多时间尺度；仅从任务标签学习波形到输出的映射，无法显式区分记录变异与协调运动活动。

Method: NHN受神经运动组织启发构建层级结构：自适应记录统计量同时保留相对强度；时空编码器采用参数高效的通道交互，并用多时间尺度历史调制特征；由此得到所学运动基元的候选激活，经时间整合与连续加权形成潜在神经运动状态。论文还对核心机制的高效性、时间行为和优化进行了理论分析。

Result: 在emg2pose连续手部姿态估计上，相比Hadidi等人最佳任务特定变体，NHN在三种泛化划分的Regression与Tracking中均降低用户平均角度误差0.52%~2.84%，参数减少48.42%~48.51%；在emg2qwerty触控打字识别上，相比SplashNet-Upscale，零样本beam-search字符错误率降低19.40%，微调后降低30.42%，参数减少65.86%。

Conclusion: 生理学引导的潜在神经运动状态推断可支持参数高效的sEMG解码，验证了NHN架构的有效性。

Abstract: Surface electromyography (sEMG) provides a wearable, noninvasive interface to neuromuscular activity for movement decoding and human-computer interaction. Population-scale decoding remains difficult because the relationship between sEMG and neuromuscular activity varies across users and sessions, while task-relevant dynamics span channels and multiple timescales. Learning waveform-to-output mappings from task labels leaves the distinction between recording variability and coordinated motor activity implicit. We introduce the Neuromotor Hierarchy Network (NHN), which learns a compact latent neuromotor state from task supervision to represent task-relevant neuromuscular coordination. NHN constructs this latent state through a hierarchy inspired by neuromotor organization.It adapts recording statistics while preserving relative intensity.Its spatiotemporal encoder uses parameter-efficient channel interactions and modulates features with multi-timescale history. The resulting features yield candidate activations of learned motor primitives, which are temporally integrated and continuously weighted to form the state. Theoretical analysis characterizes the efficiency, temporal behavior, and optimization of NHN's core mechanisms. We evaluate the architecture for both continuous hand-pose estimation on emg2pose and touch-typing recognition on emg2qwerty. On emg2pose, NHN reduces user-averaged angular error by 0.52% to 2.84% across all three generalization splits in both Regression and Tracking relative to Hadidi et al.'s best task-specific variants, using 48.42% to 48.51% fewer parameters. On emg2qwerty, NHN reduces beam-search character error rate by 19.40% zero-shot and 30.42% after fine-tuning relative to SplashNet-Upscale, using 65.86% fewer parameters. Physiology-guided inference of a latent neuromotor state supports parameter-efficient sEMG decoding.

</details>


### [298] [Cite What You Explore: Budget-Aware LLM Reasoning over Medical KGs with Verifiable Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07739)
*Chen Chen,Dongjie Wang,Mei Liu,Zijun Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BAR框架，在医学知识图谱上进行预算感知的LLM推理，用于出院后风险预测，兼顾成本约束、证据质量区分与可引用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 出院后风险预测中，连接出院时观察与后续并发症的依赖关系（如共病级联、药物-疾病交互）常缺失于电子健康记录中。外部医学知识图谱可补充这些依赖，但追溯它们需要三个特性：图谱探索成本有界、检索证据按来源质量区分、推理依据可引用以供回溯审查。现有基于LLM的方法无法同时满足这三点。

Method: BAR将原始知识图谱精炼为疾病特异的证据图，边携带支持分数与来源记录，形成质量标注的推理空间；LLM通过"规划-导航-验证"循环在图上推理，将问题分解为步骤，在患者特定预算下检索证据，并在验证失败时修正；用对比有无证据的预测结果、结合获取成本与引用完整性项训练推理策略。

Result: 在MIMIC-III与MIMIC-IV上，覆盖8种疾病和3个预测时间跨度，BAR相比最强基线将AUPRC提升3.4个点，引用精确率从59.8%提升至77.9%，且仅消耗预算上限的62-65%。

Conclusion: BAR通过预算感知的LLM推理框架有效利用医学知识图谱，同时满足成本有界、证据质量区分与可引用三项要求，在出院后风险预测任务上取得显著提升。

Abstract: Post-discharge risk prediction from electronic health records (EHRs) is difficult because many dependencies that link discharge-time observations to downstream complications, such as comorbidity cascades and drug-disease interactions, are absent from the record. External medical knowledge graphs (KGs) can supply these missing dependencies, but tracing them demands three properties: KG exploration must remain cost-bounded, retrieved evidence must be differentiated by source quality, and the resulting rationale must be citable for retrospective review. Large language models (LLMs) can plan and verify over structured evidence, making them natural candidates for KG reasoning, but existing LLM-based methods do not satisfy these three properties jointly. In this paper, we propose BAR, a Budget-Aware LLM Reasoning framework over medical KGs with three contributions. First, BAR refines the raw KG into disease-specific evidence graphs whose edges carry support scores and provenance records, turning the KG into a quality-annotated reasoning space rather than a static feature source. Second, an LLM then reasons over this graph through a plan-navigate-verify loop that decomposes the question into steps, retrieves evidence under a patient-specific budget, and revises when verification fails. Third, a reasoning policy is trained with a reward that compares predictions with and without acquired evidence, combined with acquisition cost and citation-integrity terms. Across 8 diseases and 3 prediction horizons on MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV, BAR improves AUPRC by 3.4 points over the strongest baseline, raises citation precision from 59.8% to 77.9%, and consumes only 62-65% of the budget cap.

</details>


### [299] [Adversarially Trained Linear Transformers Are Optimal Robust In-Context Learners for Gaussian Mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07754)
*Soichiro Kumano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了对抗预训练的鲁棒性是否可以无需任务特定的对抗训练就迁移到未见任务，并在一类高斯混合分类任务上给出了肯定答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗训练虽是最可靠的防御之一，但每个任务都需重新付出高昂的计算成本。鲁棒基础模型提供了替代思路：只需一次对抗预训练，再通过轻量适配迁移鲁棒性。然而一个根本问题仍未解决——预训练阶段获得的鲁棒性能否在不追加对抗训练的情况下迁移到未见任务。

Method: 针对一类高斯混合分类任务，研究一个足够深的线性 transformer，在跨任务上进行对抗预训练，并通过上下文学习（in-context learning）从干净示例中完成下游任务；同时分析梯度流下的收敛性、准确率—鲁棒性权衡以及示例复杂度。

Result: 研究表明，规模化的单一对抗预训练模型可以在新任务上达到最优鲁棒性，而无需额外的任务特定训练；足够深的线性 transformer 经过跨任务对抗训练后，通过上下文学习可渐近达到未见任务上的鲁棒贝叶斯误差，而标准训练的模型则无法做到。

Conclusion: 对抗预训练所获得的鲁棒性确实可以在不追加对抗训练的情况下迁移到未见任务，这为鲁棒基础模型的可行性提供了理论支持，并揭示了收敛性、准确率—鲁棒性权衡和示例复杂度等性质。

Abstract: Adversarial training is one of the most reliable defenses against adversarial attacks, but its high computational cost must generally be paid anew for each task. Robust foundation models offer a promising alternative: adversarially pretrain a model once and then transfer its robustness to downstream tasks through lightweight adaptation. However, a fundamental question remains open: can robustness acquired during pretraining transfer to unseen tasks without further adversarial training? In this study, we answer this question affirmatively. A single model adversarially pretrained at scale can achieve optimal robustness on new tasks without additional task-specific training. Specifically, we show that, for a family of Gaussian-mixture classification tasks, a sufficiently deep linear transformer adversarially trained across tasks can asymptotically attain the robust Bayes error on previously unseen tasks through in-context learning from clean demonstrations. By contrast, a standardly trained model cannot. We further analyze convergence under gradient flow, an accuracy--robustness trade-off, and demonstration complexity.

</details>


### [300] [TRACE: Rollout-Guided Quantization-Aware Training for FP4 Reinforcement Learning of MoE Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07767)
*Xin Wang,Hao Yu,Zhengyang Zhuge,Bochao Mao,Zheng Li,Junda Feng,Yuyan Luo,Yi Zhang,Yizhong Cao,Mi Zhang,Dayiheng Liu,Jianwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TRACE框架，通过训练-rollout量化对齐实现MoE语言模型的高效FP4强化学习训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL后训练LLM时rollout生成的计算和内存开销巨大，促使研究者采用低精度rollout。但现有FP4 RL方法主要在训练路径和rollout路径上独立优化量化精度，未能直接减小两条量化执行路径之间的差异。

Method: TRACE包含两部分：一是rollout引导的量化感知训练，用rollout侧的量化结果指导训练侧的FP4舍入决策，直接减小训练-rollout差异；二是高效的量化信息缓存方案，选择性保留深层网络的尾数和缩放信息，以降低引导带来的存储与通信开销。

Result: 在四个大规模MoE语言模型上，针对推理、编码和长时程RL任务进行评估。TRACE支持FP4权重/激活与FP4 KV-cache联合rollout，RL性能与BF16 rollout相当，rollout最高加速5.4倍，相比对BF16训练策略进行事后FP4量化具有更强的最终FP4性能。

Conclusion: 通过直接对齐训练与rollout的量化路径，TRACE在保持RL性能的同时实现了显著的rollout加速，为MoE语言模型的高效FP4 RL训练提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for post-training large language models (LLMs) incurs substantial computation and memory overhead during rollout generation, which motivates low-precision rollout for efficient RL training. However, existing FP4 RL methods suffer from a key limitation: they primarily optimize quantization accuracy on the training and rollout paths independently rather than directly reducing the discrepancy between the two quantized execution paths. In this work, we propose TRACE (Train-Rollout Quantization Alignment via Compact GuidancE), an FP4 quantization framework for RL training of Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models that addresses the limitation of existing FP4 RL methods. TRACE incorporates rollout-guided quantization-aware training that uses rollout-side quantization outcomes to guide training-side FP4 rounding decisions, directly reducing train-rollout discrepancy. Moreover, TRACE adopts an efficient quantization-information caching scheme that selectively retains mantissa and scale information from deeper layers to reduce the storage and communication overhead introduced by rollout guidance. We evaluate TRACE on four large-scale MoE language models across reasoning, coding, and long-horizon RL tasks. Our results demonstrate that TRACE enables joint FP4 weight/activation and FP4 KV-cache rollout with RL performance comparable to BF16 rollout, while achieving up to 5.4xrollout speedup and strong final FP4 performance compared with post-hoc FP4 quantization of BF16-trained policies.

</details>


### [301] [Towards One-for-All Foundation Model for Attributed Graph Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07778)
*Yunhui Liu,Xudong Jin,Kang Zhang,Danshuo An,Yu Xing,Te Song,Jia Liu,Tieke He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OFAG，一个面向属性图聚类的"一次训练、通用适用"基础模型，基于先验数据拟合网络从合成图上学习可复用的聚类推理策略，在十个数据集上以单次前向传播取得最佳平均性能，且总耗时仅12.43分钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有属性图聚类方法通常需要为每个输入图单独训练和调参，流程昂贵且脆弱，难以在不同特征空间、结构模式和属性-结构关联的图之间迁移。作者希望探索一种"通用模型"方案：单个模型训练一次即可直接应用于多样化属性图，无需图特定训练、微调或超参数搜索。

Method: 提出OFAG基础模型。以Prior-data Fitted Networks为基础，从覆盖潜在簇、节点属性和图结构的广泛先验下生成的合成属性图中学习可复用的聚类推理策略。为处理跨图的不兼容特征空间，采用维度无关的信号级图编码器，将每个特征通道视为图信号并建模其对共享图滤波器的响应。训练使用超球面聚类目标，使推理时单次前向传播即可得到聚类友好的节点表示。

Result: 在十个数据集上，一个冻结的OFAG模型在NMI、ACC、ARI和F1上均取得最佳平均性能与平均排名，且在12.43分钟内完成全部十个数据集，比第二快的基线快6倍以上，在聚类质量上比第二好的方法快近28倍。代码与预训练权重已开源。

Conclusion: OFAG表明单一基础模型可免训练、免微调、免调参地直接用于多样化属性图聚类，兼顾质量与效率，为属性图聚类提供了实用的一站式解决方案，便于从业者直接应用于自有数据集。

Abstract: Attributed graph clustering aims to discover node groups by jointly exploiting node attributes and graph topology, yet its unsupervised nature makes model selection and adaptation inherently difficult. Existing methods typically train and tune a separate model for each input graph, leading to costly and fragile pipelines that often fail to transfer across graphs with different feature spaces, structural patterns, and attribute-structure correlations. In this paper, we study a one-for-all alternative: can a single model be trained once and directly applied to diverse attributed graphs without graph-specific training, fine-tuning, or hyperparameter search? We propose OFAG, a foundation model for attributed graph clustering. Building upon Prior-data Fitted Networks, OFAG learns a reusable clustering inference strategy from synthetic attributed graphs generated under broad priors over latent clusters, node attributes, and graph structures. To handle incompatible feature spaces across graphs, OFAG adopts a dimension-agnostic signal-wise graph encoder that treats each feature channel as a graph signal and models its response to shared graph filters. The model is trained with a hyperspherical clustering objective, producing clustering-friendly node representations in a single forward pass at inference time. On ten datasets, one frozen OFAG model achieves the best mean performance and average rank across NMI, ACC, ARI, and F1, while completing all ten datasets in 12.43 minutes total---over 6* faster than the second-fastest baseline and nearly 28* faster than the second-best on clustering quality. Our code and pretrained checkpoint are available at https://github.com/Cloudy1225/OFAG, allowing practitioners to directly apply OFAG to their own attributed graph datasets without additional training or tuning.

</details>


### [302] [Extending Pathwise Gradients to Discrete Random Variables via Finite-Order Relaxation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07786)
*Donghan He,Luhuan Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个通用框架，为泊松等常见离散随机变量构造有限阶精确的路径梯度估计器，该估计器是满足低阶多项式无偏条件下的最小范数解，保留硬前向采样、无需温度调节，并且实现简洁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 路径梯度对连续随机变量具有无偏、低方差、单样本可用等优点，但对离散变量而言，路径恒等式一般无法对每个可微函数都精确成立。此前工作通常用类别变量或增广表示来近似非类别离散变量，这会引入额外方差与计算开销，因此需要一个更直接、精确且低方差的离散变量路径梯度框架。

Method: 作者提出一个通用框架，用于构造一系列常见离散变量（如泊松）的有限阶精确路径梯度估计器。该估计器在所有对次数至多某阶多项式无偏的解中，取最小范数解，从而得到唯一解并最小化权重方差。方法保留硬前向样本，不需要温度调参，代码实现只需几行。作者还推导了针对超出指定函数类之外函数的非渐近偏差界，用于理解近似偏差。

Result: 与其他可行解相比，该估计器唯一且最小化权重方差。实验表明，低阶方法在线性、非线性和层次潜变量模型中能达到或超过经过调参的基线方法，并且在每次运行时基准测试中都优于竞争方法的速度。

Conclusion: 该工作为离散随机变量提供了有限阶精确、低方差、易实现的路径梯度估计器，避免了类别化或增广近似带来的额外方差与计算成本，并在多种潜变量模型中验证了精度与效率优势。

Abstract: Pathwise gradients are preferred for continuous random variables because they are unbiased, low variance, and work with a single sample. For discrete variables, however, the pathwise identity cannot generally be exact for every differentiable function. We propose a general framework to construct finite-order exact pathwise gradient estimators for a range of common discrete variables such as Poisson. The estimator is the least-norm solution among all solutions that are unbiased for polynomials of degree at most. The resulting estimators preserve the hard forward sample, require no temperature tuning, and can be implemented in a few lines of codes. Against other admissible solutions, our estimator is unique and minimizes weight variance; in contrast, prior works use categorical variables or augmented representations to approximate non-categorical variables that induces excess variance and computations. To understand approximation bias for functions beyond the prescribed class, we also derive a non-asymptotic bias bound. In experiments our low order methods match or improve tuned baselines across linear, nonlinear and hierarchical latent-variable models, while out-speeding competitors in every runtime benchmark.

</details>


### [303] [ServeLearnBench: How Well Can Agents Self-Improve from Serving Experience?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07792)
*Haizhong Zheng,Yizhuo Di,Ranajoy Sadhukhan,Shuowei Jin,Beidi Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出演化环境流式数据集(EESD)与ServeLearnBench基准，覆盖零售、银行、销售场景的53个环境窗口、7,718个任务，评估28组模型-学习框架组合共252次运行，发现智能体在"从服务经验中学习"上存在显著能力缺口、持续适应代价高且可能损害原有正确行为，探索不足是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实环境中正确行为所需的隐性知识往往未公开且随时间变化，现有持续学习评测基准覆盖不足：或直接给出目标知识，或假设环境静态，或规模与知识多样性有限，导致持续适应方法的有效性与局限缺乏系统刻画。

Method: 形式化演化环境流式数据集EESD，要求智能体在隐藏策略演化时从交互与结果反馈中推断、应用并修正潜在环境知识；构建ServeLearnBench，涵盖零售支持、银行与销售话术生成，含53个环境窗口与7,718个任务；评测5种学习框架（RAG、Mem0、SkillOpt、Continual Harness、Prime）与6个模型，共28组模型-框架配对、252次学习运行。

Result: 三大发现：任务能力与"从经验中学习"之间仍存在显著差距；持续适应成本高昂，并可能使原本正确的行为退化；探索不足是有效适应的关键瓶颈。

Conclusion: ServeLearnBench为诊断上述局限、追踪智能体通过服务经验持续可靠改进的进展提供了受控测试平台。

Abstract: Large language model agents are increasingly deployed to perform complex tasks in real-world environments. However, the knowledge required for correct behavior in these environments is often implicit, undisclosed, and subject to change over time. Recent continual-learning harnesses seek to address this challenge by enabling agents to improve from serving experience. Yet the effectiveness and limitations of these methods are not yet well characterized. Existing benchmarks provide only partial coverage: some explicitly provide the target knowledge, others assume a static environment, and those that support continual adaptation remain limited in scale and knowledge diversity. To enable systematic evaluation, we formalize an evolving-environment streaming dataset (EESD), in which agents must infer, apply, and revise latent environment knowledge from interaction and outcome feedback as hidden policies evolve, and introduce ServeLearnBench, spanning retail support, banking, and sales-pitch generation with 53 environment windows and 7,718 tasks. We evaluate five learning harnesses (RAG, Mem0, SkillOpt, Continual Harness, and Prime) across six models (GPT-5.6 Terra, Opus 5, Kimi K3, GLM-5.3, DeepSeek V4.1 Flash, and GLM-5.3 Flash), covering 28 model-harness pairs and 252 learning runs. Our evaluation reveals three main findings: a substantial gap remains between task capability and learning from experience; continual adaptation is costly and can degrade already-correct behavior; and insufficient exploration emerges as a key bottleneck to effective adaptation. Overall, ServeLearnBench provides a controlled testbed for diagnosing these limitations and tracking progress toward agents that continually and reliably improve through serving experience.

</details>


### [304] [The Geometry of Empowerment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07796)
*Catherine Ji,Vivek Myers,Sergey Levine,Benjamin Eysenbach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立赋能最大化与技能学习的联系，提出新的几何视角来解释和分析赋能，回答了赋能与结构中心性之间的长期开放问题，并揭示了信息几何与奖励几何的区别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 赋能（empowerment）在概念上作为信息论量具有吸引力，但其与提供广泛未来结果可达性的结构中心性状态之间的联系一直是开放问题。

Method: 将赋能最大化与技能学习（skill-learning）方法相联系，构建新的几何框架来刻画和分析赋能。

Result: 分析回答了赋能与结构中心性之间联系的长期开放问题，并揭示了信息几何与奖励几何之间的差异。

Conclusion: 研究为构建可扩展的赋能最大化方法提供了重要理论启示，相关网站与代码已公开。

Abstract: Empowerment captures the capacity for an agent to actively control its environment. While conceptually appealing as an information-theoretic quantity, the connection between empowerment and structurally central states that provide broad access to future outcomes has remained an open question. In this work, we link empowerment maximization and skill-learning methods to provide new geometries for interpreting and analyzing empowerment. Our analyses answer longstanding open questions on the connections between empowerment and structural centrality. Our analyses also reveal distinctions between information and reward geometries, highlighting important theoretical implications to build scalable empowerment-maximization methods. Website and code can be found at https://empowerment-geometry.github.io/.

</details>


### [305] [Adaptive Mean Estimation by In-Context Learning: A Gradient-Flow Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07804)
*Martin Eppert,Krishna Balasubramanian,Subhro Ghosh,Jason Klusowski,Yan Shuo Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究先验拟合网络(PFN)如何学习统计自适应性，在位置估计问题上揭示softmax注意力可计算经验累积生成函数的导数，从而提供区分数据族的特征并构造估计器。结合专家混合或GLU架构，理论上证明了所学估计器在高斯任务上渐近有效、均匀任务上接近极小极大率、混合任务上序最优，并给出风险分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PFN（如TabPFN）在预测和估计任务上已能与成熟统计方法媲美，一个自然解释是PFN具备统计自适应性，即在不知道真实数据生成模型的情况下，在异质模型集合上表现接近针对真实模型定制的方法。作者希望深入理解这种自适应性是如何被学习的，因此选择了受控的位置估计问题进行研究。

Method: 在受控位置估计任务中研究：每个任务是无标签样本，其分布族未知——高斯数据适合用均值估计（误差阶n^{-1}），均匀数据最适合用极值估计（更快阶n^{-2}），对称高斯混合可达到σ²_n/n的率。在标量输入上，softmax注意力计算经验累积生成函数的导数，该单一原语既提供区分分布族的特征，又形成在样本均值与中程值之间插值的估计器。通过softmax专家混合或门控线性单元(GLU)组合注意力专家，并分析分阶段梯度流。

Result: 在约Ω̃(n^{1+ε})个预训练任务下，所学估计器在高斯任务上渐近有效，在均匀任务上达到最小最大率的n^ε因子内，在收缩方差机制下的混合任务上序最优。这些保证可推广到新位置和更长上下文。风险分解将误差分为专家误差、路由误差和归一化误差，澄清了架构差异：softmax门控强制归一化和精确平移等变性，而GLU必须学习它，其动态分为快速偏差消除和缓慢专家选择两个阶段。端到端实验验证了预测的特化现象。

Conclusion: softmax注意力中的单一原语可同时实现分布族区分与估计器构造，从而赋予PFN统计自适应性。softmax门控与GLU在归一化和平移等变性上的架构差异导致了不同的学习动态，风险分解进一步阐明了这种对比。理论保证与端到端实验一致，验证了预测的专家特化。

Abstract: Prior Fitted Networks (PFNs) such as TabPFN now rival established statistical procedures across prediction and estimation tasks. A natural explanation is that PFNs have the property of statistical adaptivity, that is, they perform nearly as well as a method tailored to the true data-generating model for a heterogeneous set of models, while not being told which model the data comes from. We study how such adaptivity is learned in a controlled location-estimation problem. Each task is an unlabeled sample whose family is hidden: Gaussian data call for averaging, with error of order $n^{-1}$, whereas uniform data are best estimated from their extremes, at the faster rate $n^{-2}$. We also provide the example of a symmetric Gaussian mixture, for which a rate of $σ^2_n/n$ can be attained. On scalar inputs, softmax attention computes the derivative of the empirical cumulant-generating function. A single primitive therefore both supplies features that distinguish the families and forms estimators interpolating between the sample mean and the mid-range. We combine attention experts through either a softmax mixture of experts or a gated linear unit (GLU), and analyze stagewise gradient flow. With $\widetildeΩ(n^{1+ε})$ pretraining tasks, the learned estimator is asymptotically efficient on Gaussian tasks, within a factor $n^ε$ of the minimax rate on uniform tasks, and order-optimal on mixtures in a shrinking-variance regime. These guarantees extend to new locations and longer contexts. A risk decomposition separates expert error, routing error and normalization error, which clarifies the architectural contrast. Softmax gating enforces normalization and exact translation equivariance, whereas the GLU must learn it: its dynamics separate into fast bias removal followed by slow expert selection. End-to-end experiments recover the predicted specialization.

</details>


### [306] [MASKerade: Token-Routed Mask Experts for Dense-to-MoE Upcycling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07809)
*Mingyuan Zhang,Yue Bai,Zhongruo Wang,Yupin Huang,Yiyang Huang,Hailing Wang,Huimin Zeng,Yun Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MASKerade 将专家定义为冻结预训练 FFN 的可学习稀疏子网络，通过掩码与路由联合优化构建 MoE，实现高效密集到 MoE 升级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏激活 MoE 可在不显著增加每 token 计算的情况下提升模型容量，但现有的密集到 MoE 升级方法通常将 FFN 复制成独立训练的专家，参数开销较大。作者希望探索一种不依赖独立专家权重矩阵的构造方式。

Method: MASKerade 保持预训练 FFN 权重冻结，将每个专家表示为该 FFN 中的可学习二值掩码稀疏子网络；token 级路由器选择要执行的掩码 FFN 并组合输出，路由与掩码分数联合优化。该框架支持神经元级、半结构化与无结构化专家。主配置采用四个 2:4 专家与 top-2 路由。

Result: 在 Qwen 与 Gemma 骨干上的五个视觉-语言基准测试中，该配置取得对比基线中的最高性能；两个半稠密专家传递的名义 FFN 算术量相当于一次稠密传递，且无需独立专家权重矩阵。跨掩码粒度、路由干预与计算匹配对照的实验区分了学习连接性与专家激活数量的影响。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在冻结权重上进行掩码学习是构建 token 路由 MoE 专家的实用替代方案。

Abstract: Sparsely activated Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models increase model capacity without a proportional increase in per-token computation. Dense-to-MoE upcycling reuses pretrained dense models to construct such systems, commonly by copying feed-forward networks (FFNs) into independently trained experts. We introduce MASKerade, a dense-to-MoE training method that instead learns experts as sparse subnetworks of a frozen pretrained FFN. Each expert is defined by a learned binary mask, and a token-level router selects which masked FFNs to execute and combine. The router and mask scores are optimized jointly, while the underlying FFN weight values remain unchanged. This formulation supports neuron-structured, semi-structured, and unstructured experts within the same routing architecture. Our main configuration uses four 2:4 experts with top-2 routing, where two half-dense expert passes have the nominal FFN arithmetic of one dense pass, without requiring independent expert weight matrices. On five vision-language benchmarks with Qwen and Gemma backbones, this configuration achieves the highest performance among the compared baselines. Comparisons across mask granularities, routing interventions, and compute-matched controls distinguish the effects of learned connectivity from expert activation count. These results establish mask learning over frozen weights as a practical alternative for constructing token-routed MoE experts.

</details>


### [307] [SIFT: Search Intent-to-Filter Transformer for Multi-Task Personalized Filter Ranking at Airbnb](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07810)
*Shashank Dabriwal,Tanya Piplani,Hao Li,Yiwei Wang,Ashish Jain,Kedar Bellare,Stephanie Moyerman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SIFT是一个基于Transformer的排序模型，直接从原始行为序列学习用户偏好，替代手工特征工程，用于双向市场的筛选器推荐，并已全面线上部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生产级筛选器排序系统依赖ETL流水线生成的手工预聚合特征来表示用户，维护成本高，且难以为新筛选器类型或情境维度（行程长度、同行人数）扩展。

Method: 提出SIFT（Search Intent-to-Filter Transformer），用Transformer从原始行为序列学习统一的用户表示，支持多任务预测（预订可能性、筛选器互动、序数容量阈值），兼容布尔与数值范围筛选器；扩展新筛选器只需新增预测头；该用户表示以每日离线节奏计算以保证在线服务速度。

Result: 离线：预订与设施互动PR-AUC分别提升+51.9%和+62.8%。在线A/B：推荐筛选器互动+20.0%，搜索者整体筛选器使用+0.72%，新支持的卧室/浴室/床筛选器使用分别+3.9%、+10.7%、+0.52%；并快速集成酒店意向筛选器，未取消酒店预订+3.8%，整体市场预订+0.76%。

Conclusion: SIFT以统一用户表示替代手工特征工程，兼具高效果与强扩展性，已全面部署于生产环境，为数百万用户提供可扩展的个性化服务。

Abstract: Search filters help guests navigate vast catalogs in two-sided marketplaces like Airbnb, and recommending the right filters can meaningfully lift booking conversion. Many such production filter-ranking systems, however, represent the guest through hand-engineered, pre-aggregated features generated by ETL pipelines. This makes it expensive to maintain and difficult to extend for new filter types or contextual dimensions (trip length, group size). We present SIFT (Search Intent-to-Filter Transformer), a ranking model built on transformers that learns guest preferences directly from raw behavioral sequences. SIFT replaces manual feature engineering with a unified guest representation that feeds multiple prediction tasks, including booking likelihood, filter engagement, and ordinal capacity thresholds (e.g., 2+ bedrooms) -- a general framework for filter ranking in two-sided marketplaces that accommodates both boolean and numeric-range filter types. Extending SIFT to new filters requires only adding a new head, not a new feature pipeline. To keep serving fast, this guest representation is computed offline on a daily cadence rather than at request time. Offline, SIFT improves booking and amenity-engagement PR-AUC by +51.9% and +62.8% respectively over the production baseline. In online A/B testing, SIFT increased engagement with recommended filters by +20.0%, overall filter usage among searchers by +0.72%, and usage of the newly-supported bedroom, bathroom, and bed filters by +3.9%, +10.7%, and +0.52% respectively. Demonstrating the system's extensibility, we rapidly integrated a novel hotel-intent filter using the same shared representation, driving a +3.8% lift in uncancelled hotel bookings and a +0.76% lift in overall marketplace bookings. SIFT is now fully deployed in production, serving scalable personalization to millions of guests.

</details>


### [308] [$α$Transfer: Coefficient Transfer for Efficient Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07819)
*Shih-Cheng Huang,Zhi Rui Tam,Chieh-Yen Lin,Yun-Nung Chen,Hung-yi Lee,Shao-Hua Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出αTransfer：利用同一模型族内不同规模模型在合并系数上的性能分布高度一致，在小代理模型上搜索最优合并系数后直接迁移到大模型，实现高效模型合并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并需要搜索最优合并系数，但随着模型规模和数量增长，搜索空间呈组合爆炸且内存需求高，导致搜索代价极其昂贵。

Method: 发现同一模型族内不同规模模型在不同合并系数下的性能分布高度相似；基于此在小代理模型上搜索最优系数，再直接迁移到更大的目标模型。

Result: 在多种合并方法、模型族和任务上验证：视觉Transformer上实现6倍加速与70%内存降低，大语言模型上实现20倍加速与85%内存降低，且性能相当。

Conclusion: αTransfer是一种高效且可泛化的模型合并扩展方法。

Abstract: Model merging offers a promising solution for combining multiple fine-tuned checkpoints into a single model through parameter arithmetic. However, finding optimal merging coefficients requires an extensive search that becomes prohibitively expensive as models scale in both size and number, due to high memory requirements and combinatorial growth in the search space. We show that, within the same model family, models exhibit highly congruent performance distributions over merging coefficients across different model sizes. This distributional similarity enables a practical paradigm we call \textit{$α$Transfer}: searching for optimal coefficients on a small proxy model, then directly transfer them to larger target models. We verify $α$Transfer across multiple merging methods, model families, and tasks. Experimental results demonstrate a 6$\times$ speedup and 70\% memory reduction on vision transformers, and a 20$\times$ speedup and 85\% memory reduction on large language models, while maintaining comparable performance. Our findings establish $α$Transfer as an efficient and generalizable approach to scaling model merging.

</details>


### [309] [TTNet: Multi-Task Deep Learning for Table Tennis Player Analysis with Smart Racket](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07823)
*Ko-Hsun Chen,Xiang-Wei Ke,Hsien-Cheng Huang,Shang-Kuan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 TTNet 多任务深度学习模型，利用智能乒乓球拍的六轴传感器数据同时预测选手性别、持拍手、经验年限与技术水平，采用两阶段训练与数据增强策略，在 AI CUP 2025 竞赛中获得第二名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能乒乓球拍可采集大量挥拍数据，为乒乓球大数据研究提供基础，有助于分析回球技术与挥拍力量一致性、提升选手技能评估准确性，但如何从六轴传感器数据中有效提取信息并完成多属性预测仍是待解决问题。

Method: 提出 TTNet 模型，融合卷积神经网络（CNN）、残差网络（ResNet）与自注意力机制，具备多任务学习能力，同时预测性别、持拍手、经验年限和技能水平四个属性；采用两阶段训练策略，结合数据增强与针对各任务的损失函数，以提升在不平衡数据上的泛化能力。

Result: 该方法在官方竞赛排行榜上取得第二名。

Conclusion: TTNet 可有效利用智能球拍六轴传感器数据完成多属性预测，推动乒乓球数据分析及相关应用发展。

Abstract: The AI CUP 2025 Precise Analysis of Table Tennis Smart Racket Data Competition introduced smart table tennis rackets that collect extensive player swing data, enabling research on table tennis big data. These data support in-depth analysis of players' return techniques and swing-force consistency, improving the accuracy of player skill assessment. This study focuses on six-axis sensor data collected by smart table tennis rackets and proposes TTNet, a novel deep learning model with multitask learning capabilities, to advance table tennis data analysis and related applications. TTNet combines convolutional neural networks (CNNs), residual networks (ResNet), and self-attention mechanisms to simultaneously predict four player attributes: gender, playing hand, years of experience, and skill level. We adopt a two-stage training strategy that incorporates data augmentation and task-specific loss functions to improve generalization on imbalanced data. Our approach achieved second place on the official competition leaderboard.

</details>


### [310] [CANDLE: Cortical Null-Space Decomposition for Noninvasive Brain Source Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07824)
*Shuntaro Suzuki,Yuiga Wada,Komei Sugiura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CANDLE是一种基于学习的电生理源成像模型，在受试者个体化皮层几何上估计源活动，通过学习由源-传感器映射导出的零空间先验，并在模拟数据训练后于仿真和两项真实任务上优于既有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电生理源成像（ESI）本质上是病态问题：源活动的维度远高于传感器观测维度，导致解不唯一。现有基于学习的方法虽引入数据驱动的源先验来缓解歧义，但难以跨受试者个体化的皮层几何泛化。

Method: 提出CANDLE模型，直接在受试者个体化皮层几何上估计源活动。它学习一个关于零空间的先验，该零空间由T1加权MRI导出的源到传感器映射所诱导，从而将学习限制在不可观测的源成分上，同时保留几何约束。为训练该模型，构建了覆盖1100多个个体化皮层几何、源配置来自26000多张统计脑图的全脑模拟器，且在纯模拟数据上训练。

Result: 在模拟源活动估计上，CANDLE优于先前的ESI方法；并泛化到两项实证任务：（i）基于同步记录的头皮EEG进行颅内刺激定位；（ii）基于术前发作间期EEG进行致痫区估计。

Conclusion: CANDLE通过零空间先验与个体化几何建模，在仅用模拟数据训练的情况下实现了跨受试者泛化的高精度源成像，并成功迁移至真实临床相关任务，展现出实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Electrophysiological source imaging (ESI) aims to estimate cortical source activity from noninvasive electrophysiological measurements such as electroencephalogram (EEG). However, ESI is fundamentally ill-posed because source activity is substantially higher-dimensional than sensor observations, resulting in non-unique solutions. Recent learning-based approaches address this ambiguity by learning data-driven source priors, yet they often struggle to generalize across subject-specific cortical geometries. To address this, we propose CANDLE, a learning-based ESI model that estimates source activity on subject-specific cortical geometries. CANDLE learns a prior over the null space induced by the source-to-sensor mapping derived from T1-weighted MRI, restricting learning to unobservable source components while preserving geometric constraints. To train CANDLE, we develop a whole-brain simulator spanning over 1,100 subject-specific cortical geometries with source configurations derived from over 26,000 statistical brain maps. Trained exclusively on simulated data, CANDLE outperformed prior ESI methods on simulated source activity estimation and generalized to two empirical tasks: (i) intracranial stimulation localization from simultaneously recorded scalp EEG and (ii) epileptogenic zone estimation from presurgical interictal EEG. Our project page is available at https://candle-esi.pages.dev}{https://candle-esi.pages.dev.

</details>


### [311] [Retrieval Is Not Enough: Refreshing Memory for Frozen Time-Series Forecasters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07834)
*Chao He,Jianyu Xu,Xinyi Guo,Ruiqi Liu,Haobin Ding,Ruiqi He,Dongqing Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 FreshCast，一种即插即用的检索增强时间序列预测框架，在保持预测器冻结的同时持续更新非参数记忆并闭式校准其权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强方法在训练段一次性构建记忆，部署后新观测无法作为参考，且未校准检索信息对冻结预测器的影响程度。作者指出检索效用的两个决定因素：历史是否仍反映当前状态，以及记忆修正是否与预测器残差对齐（该对齐在验证到部署阶段可能漂移）。

Method: FreshCast 保持预测器冻结，用新观测持续更新非参数记忆，通过关系核回归形成记忆预测，并在验证段以闭式解校准其权重。作者还在简化生成模型下刻画最优组合增益（由预测器误差与记忆修正的二阶关系决定），并证明足够长的回看窗口可使周期性记忆信息冗余。

Result: 在七个基准和十种预测架构上，FreshCast 对每个预测器和输入长度都降低平均 MSE，输入长度 96 和 720 时分别降低 14.6% 和 5.6%，并在相同比较设置下优于检索增强与在线基线。消融显示，在训练结束时冻结记忆会消除大部分增益，说明训练后观测是主要提升来源。

Conclusion: 对冻结预测器而言，有用的历史参考必须保持时效性，并提供有助于修正其剩余误差的信息。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented time-series forecasting uses the continuations of historical segments similar to the current context as references for a forecaster.
