Most existing methods build the retrieval memory once from the training segment, leaving observations revealed after deployment unavailable as references, and generally do not calibrate how much the retrieved information should influence a frozen forecaster. We identify two key determinants of retrieval utility for a frozen forecaster: whether the history still reflects the current state, and whether the correction it induces aligns with the forecaster's residual errors, an alignment that can shift between validation and deployment when the memory becomes stale. We propose FreshCast, a plug-in retrieval framework that keeps the forecaster frozen, continuously updates a non-parametric memory with new observations, forms a memory forecast through relational kernel regression, and calibrates its weight in closed form on the validation segment. Under a simplified generative model, we characterize the optimal combination gain through the second-order relation between forecaster error and memory correction, and show that a sufficiently long look-back can make periodic memory information redundant. Across seven benchmarks and ten forecasting architectures, FreshCast reduces average MSE for every evaluated forecaster and input length, by 14.6% and 5.6% at input lengths 96 and 720, and achieves lower MSE than the evaluated retrieval-augmented and online baselines in their comparison settings. Ablations show that freezing the memory at the end of training removes most of the gain, identifying post-training observations as a primary source of improvement. For a frozen forecaster, useful historical references must remain timely and provide information that helps correct its remaining errors.

</details>


### [312] [Privileged Context as Drift in On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07842)
*Ravenor Davion,Nick Rui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统性地解耦了在线自蒸馏(OPSD)中特权上下文的两个设计维度(内容与来源),揭示内容选择比来源选择对策略漂移影响更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OPSD研究同时改变特权上下文、模型、数据和训练设置,导致无法隔离特权上下文设计本身的影响。作者受持续学习中减少灾难性遗忘的启发,希望厘清特权上下文的选择如何影响策略漂移。

Method: 沿两个维度变化特权上下文:内容(演示、反馈、改写)与来源(外部、自生成带验证器、自生成不带验证器),构成9种组合。在Qwen2.5-7B上用OPSD在3个数据集上训练,测量目标任务准确率、先前任务保持率、相对基座策略的反向KL及参数更新几何。

Result: 固定来源时改变内容,其中位KL跨度大于固定内容时改变来源。该比值在逐token KL上为5.1倍,逐序列KL上为2.2倍。参数更新几何呈相同模式:共享内容的适配器对齐度更高(平均余弦0.571),共享来源的为0.255。

Conclusion: 在持续学习意义上,特权上下文应被视为OPSD稳定性设计的一部分,因为它与策略移动的距离和方向相关联。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) trains a language model to match a copy of itself conditioned on privileged context. Existing work varies what privileged context contains and how it is produced while also changing models, data, and training setups, making the effects of privileged context design difficult to isolate. Motivated by efforts in continual learning to reduce catastrophic forgetting, we study how the choice of privileged context affects policy drift. Specifically, we vary two axes: content (a demonstration, feedback, or rephrase) and source (external, self-generated with a verifier, or self-generated without a verifier). We train Qwen2.5-7B with OPSD across these nine combinations and three datasets, measuring target-task accuracy, prior-task retention, reverse KL from the base policy, and parameter-update geometry. Holding source fixed, changing content spans a wider median KL range than holding content fixed and changing source. The ratio between these ranges is $5.1\times$ for per-token KL and $2.2\times$ for per-sequence KL. Parameter-update geometry shows the same pattern: updates from adapters that share content are more closely aligned (mean cosine $0.571$) than updates from adapters that share source ($0.255$). For continual learning, these findings suggest that privileged context should be treated as part of OPSD's stability design because it is associated with how far and in what direction the policy moves.

</details>


### [313] [Lost in the bf16 Cast: Exporting Ternary Language Models Can Revert Most Low-Learning-Rate Code Changes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07853)
*Avichal Sahai,Nishant Raj,Animesh Srivastava*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 审计三家实验室的三值语言模型导出管线发现：文档化的 bf16 转换会导致 0.83%–1.77% 的码位偏移，并在微调端点上大幅降低 GSM8K 严格准确率（如 Falcon-E-1B-Base 从 58.79% 降至 0.78%），而直接写入训练量化器码位或调整 bf16 输入两种补救方案均满足非劣性标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三值语言模型（如 BitNet b1.58、Falcon-E、BitCPM）以高精度潜权重微调，但部署时需经导出步骤转为三值码，官方管线先将其转为 bf16。作者怀疑该文档化导出流程可能引入码位不一致，从而损害部署模型的性能，因此对三家实验室的管线进行审计。

Method: 审计三家实验室的发布检查点与导出管线；对比 fp32 量化潜权重与部署码位的一致性；复现 onebitllms 导出器对 Falcon-E 四个发布版本的结果；在微调端点上以匹配名义学习率与 bf16-ULP 比值的设置测量 GSM8K 严格准确率变化；并测试两种兼容性补救方案（直接写入训练量化器码位、调整 bf16 输入使工具输出不变）在三个模型上的非劣性（4 点严格准确率标准）。此外通过随机干预初始阈值距离验证码位选择的距离依赖性。

Result: 发布检查点中 fp32 量化与部署码位在 Falcon-E 和 BitCPM 上不一致率为 0.83%–1.77%，BitNet 2B-4T 为 1.530%；Falcon-E 和 BitCPM 多数不一致源于 bf16 舍入恰好落在阈值上被 ties-to-even 映射为零，且 onebitllms 导出器可逐字节复现 Falcon-E 全部四个版本。微调端点性能骤降：Falcon-E-1B-Base 严格准确率由 58.79% 跌至 0.78%，BitCPM-CANN-0.5B 由 36.13% 跌至 0.39%；bf16 保存重载使 BitNet 2B-4T 严格准确率下降 27.54 点（末位数准确率反而上升）。两种补救方案在三个模型上均满足 4 点严格准确率非劣性标准。在两类模型家族中，对初始阈值距离的随机干预支持微调所改变码位的距离依赖性选择。

Conclusion: 文档化的 bf16 导出步骤会引入系统性码位偏移并严重损害三值语言模型的微调后性能。直接写入训练量化器码位或调整 bf16 输入这两种兼容性补救方案在三个模型上均达到非劣性标准，建议在导出管线中采用。

Abstract: Ternary language models such as BitNet b1.58, Falcon-E and BitCPM are fine-tuned with higher-precision latent weights and deployed as ternary codes produced by an export step that, in the labs' documented pipelines, first casts the latents to bf16. We audit those pipelines across three labs. In released checkpoints, fp32 quantization of the shipped latents disagrees with the deployed codes on 0.83-1.77% of codes in Falcon-E and BitCPM and on 1.530% in BitNet 2B-4T; for Falcon-E and BitCPM most disagreements are products that bf16 rounding lands exactly on the threshold, which ties-to-even maps to zero, and the unmodified onebitllms exporter reproduces all four Falcon-E releases byte for byte. At fine-tuned endpoints, with learning rates selected to match a nominal learning-rate-to-bf16-ULP ratio, the documented export lowers greedy GSM8K strict accuracy from 58.79% to 0.78% for Falcon-E-1B-Base and from 36.13% to 0.39% for BitCPM-CANN-0.5B, and a bf16 save and reload lowers BitNet 2B-4T's strict accuracy by 27.54 points while its last-number accuracy rises. Two compatibility remedies, writing the training quantizer's codes directly or adjusting the bf16 inputs until the unchanged tools emit them, each met a 4-point strict-accuracy non-inferiority criterion against online evaluation in all three models. In two model families, randomized interventions on the initial distance from the threshold support distance-dependent selection of the codes that fine-tuning changes.

</details>


### [314] [A Decision-Focused Neural Optimization Framework for Personalized Route Reproduction from Vehicle Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07857)
*Gyeongjun Kim,Yeseul Kang,Keemin Sohn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文把个体路径复现建模为在个性化隐式路段成本上的最短路问题，提出结合感知编码器、约束优化层与决策聚焦学习的端到端神经框架，并用隐式极大似然估计近似梯度、加正则项消除尺度歧义，实验证明其在路径复现上优于基线选择模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统路径选择/复现方法常需枚举备选路径集合，且难以整合个体特征、出行属性与网络交通状态等情境信息；同时最短路径监督存在尺度歧义，隐式成本难以标定。本文希望直接从情境信息推断驾驶员专属的隐式路段成本，从而在无需枚举备选路径的情况下复现观测路径。

Method: 整体将个体路径复现表述为在习得驾驶员专属隐式路段成本上的最短路径问题。框架包含：1）感知模型，将情境协变量（个体特征、出行特定属性、网络级交通状态）嵌入为个性化路段成本；2）约束优化（CO）层，依据估计成本求解最短路径（SP）；3）采用决策聚焦学习实现端到端训练，使预测最短路径对齐观测路径；4）用隐式极大似然估计（iMLE）为含不可微CO层的损失提供近似梯度；5）引入正则项，将隐式成本分布锚定到观测路段行程时间的经验尺度，缓解最短路径监督中的尺度歧义。

Result: 实证评估表明，所提框架在路径复现任务上优于基线路径选择模型；学习到的隐式成本可解释为感知出行成本的代理，为异质性路径选择提供了合理解释。

Conclusion: 该研究证明可将路径复现转化为隐式成本上的最短路学习问题，并通过决策聚焦学习与正则化实现可训练的端到端神经框架，兼具预测性能与对异质路径选择行为的可解释性。

Abstract: This study formulates individual route reproduction as a shortest-path problem over learned driver-specific latent link costs. The central idea is that, once such latent costs are inferred from contextual information, observed routes can be reproduced without enumerating alternative route sets. We propose a neural pipeline that includes a perception model that embeds context covariates, which comprises individual characteristics, trip-specific attributes, and network-level traffic states, into the personalized link costs. A constrained optimization (CO) layer, which determines the shortest path (SP) based on these estimated costs, follows the perception encoder. To enable end-to-end training, we employ decision-focused learning to align the predicted shortest paths with observed routes. The implicit maximum likelihood estimation (iMLE) provides an approximate gradient of the loss function that contains the non-differentiable CO layer. Furthermore, a regularization term anchors the latent cost distribution to the empirical scale of observed link travel times, mitigating the scale ambiguity inherent in shortest-path supervision. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that the proposed framework outperforms baseline route choice models in path reproduction. The learned latent costs, interpreted as proxies for perceived travel costs, provide plausible explanations for heterogeneous route choices.

</details>


### [315] [Tram-FL: Reducing Communication and Computation Costs through Sequential Model Circulation in Decentralized Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07859)
*Kota Maejima,Takayuki Nishio,Asato Yamazaki,Yuko Hara-Azumi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Tram-FL（巡回模型训练机制），通过单一模型在节点间循环训练，以最小化计算与通信开销实现去中心化联邦学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统去中心化联邦学习以客户端为中心，各客户端维护模型副本并独立更新与交换，虽能利用算力缩短训练时间，但会造成大量计算与通信浪费，在non-IID数据下尤为明显。

Method: 将研究焦点从客户端转向模型本身；提出Tram-FL机制，让单一模型在网络节点间循环传递并顺序训练；解决模型循环训练中的调度问题（哪些节点更新模型及更新次数），从循环路径与更新迭代分配两方面入手提出简单有效的方法；并采用量化动量在控制单次传输通信负载的同时提升精度、减少循环次数。

Result: 实验结果表明，该算法即使在non-IID数据下也能收敛到全局模型，且通信与计算开销更低。

Conclusion: Tram-FL以模型为中心的循环训练思路，能够在non-IID数据条件下以更少的通信与计算代价实现去中心化联邦学习，验证了该机制的有效性。

Abstract: Conventional decentralized federated learning (DFL) often focuses on clients, with each client maintaining a model copy, performing updates individually, and undertaking model exchange and integration. While fully leveraging computational resources can shorten training times, it can also lead to significant computational and communication waste. This is especially pronounced with non-independent and identically distributed (non-IID) data, where achieving high model accuracy demands extra resources. This research shifts focus to the model itself, aiming to realize DFL with minimal computation and communication costs. To this end, we propose Tram-FL (Traveling Model Training Mechanism for Decentralized Federated Learning), a mechanism designed to efficiently address these challenges. It sequentially trains a single model by circulating it among nodes. We address the training scheduling problem in model circulation-based training, specifically determining which nodes should update the model and the number of updates to perform. This is approached by considering the model's circulation route and update iteration allocation, for which we propose simple yet effective methods. Additionally, with quantized momentum, Tram-FL achieves high accuracy with fewer model circulations while controlling communication load per transmission. Experimental results show that the proposed algorithm, even with non-IID data, converges to a global model with reduced communication and computation.

</details>


### [316] [On-Policy Distillation with Negative-Policy Rollouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07874)
*Jaehui Hwang,Dongyoon Han,Sangdoo Yun,Byeongho Heo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 NP-OPD，通过在 rollout 阶段引入低能力负策略，为 on-policy distillation 补充负向信号，从而在多个模型规模、生成模式与推理任务上提升蒸馏效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 on-policy distillation 依赖更强教师对学生的正向监督，但当教师与学生分布重叠有限时，正向引导提供的学习信号不足。

Method: NP-OPD 不改变蒸馏奖励形式，而是在 rollout 阶段引入一个表现更差、能力更低的负策略，持续提供负策略偏好但教师不偏好的 token，使其持续接受教师监督，从而在保留正向教师监督的同时提供显式负向信号。

Result: 大量实验表明，NP-OPD 在不同模型规模、生成模式、推理领域及多种 OPD 变体上均优于 OPD；分析显示其能有效抑制负策略偏好 token，并使学生远离负策略。

Conclusion: 结果支持通过负策略 rollout 引入负向信号的设计，并为理解 rollout 策略在 OPD 中的作用提供了新视角。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has been widely studied as a post-training method in which a student model obtains token-level supervision from a stronger teacher on its own rollouts. Recent studies have improved OPD through alternative distillation reward formulations and teacher configurations, while the objective of distillation remains centered on mimicking the teacher. However, when a stronger teacher has limited distributional overlap with the student, such positive guidance can provide insufficient learning signals. In this work, we introduce Negative-Policy OPD (NP-OPD), which complements teacher supervision with rollouts from a lower-performing, lower-capability negative policy that serves as a negative reference for the student. Rather than modifying the distillation reward formulation, NP-OPD introduces the negative policy at the rollout stage, continuously supplying tokens preferred by the negative policy over the teacher so that they remain exposed to teacher supervision throughout training. This provides an explicit negative signal through negative-policy rollouts while preserving the positive teacher supervision used in OPD. Through extensive experiments, we show that NP-OPD improves OPD across model scales, generation modes, reasoning domains, and different OPD variants. Furthermore, our analyses show that NP-OPD effectively suppresses tokens preferred by the negative policy over the teacher and moves the student away from the negative policy. These results support our design of introducing negative signals through negative-policy rollouts and provide new insight into the role of the rollout policy in OPD. Code will be available at https://github.com/naver-ai/np-opd.

</details>


### [317] [Label-Efficient Deep Learning for ECG Delineation: A Multi-Dataset Benchmark against Widely Used Delineation Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07885)
*Jeonghwa Lim,Minje Park,Yeongyeon Na,Yujin Eom,Soyeon Lim,Young Ho Lee,Yu Jeong Kim,Sunghoon Joo,Ki Hong Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究自监督预训练与半监督微调在ECG波形边界识别（delineation）中的有效性，并将所得深度学习模型与主流开源及商业工具进行基准对比，证明其可借助大量无标注数据超越现有工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ECG delineation是转化为临床可解释指标的基础步骤。深度学习虽推进了该任务，但依赖昂贵专家标注。自监督预训练与半监督学习有望缓解标注负担，但是否能带来可靠结果、能否超越实践中的工具尚不清楚。

Method: 分两阶段：第一阶段在1个内部+4个外部数据集上，比较不同自监督目标与监督/半监督微调的效果；第二阶段将选定的深度学习模型与开源工具（NeuroKit2、Prominence、ECGdeli）及商业工具（CalECG）用三种互补指标进行基准对比，并做节律分层与逐点分析。

Result: 自监督预训练有帮助但目标函数很关键，半监督微调的价值取决于预训练目标。深度学习模型在所有指标和数据集上均排名第一：在节律多样集合上mIoU 71.3% vs 最强工具54.8%，平均逐点敏感度92.6 vs 76.4；从窦性到心律失常性能下降最小。

Conclusion: 多数据集证据表明自监督预训练对ECG delineation有效，能通过利用大量无标注数据实现标签高效训练并超越广泛使用的工具，支持在多样真实临床环境中采用此类模型。

Abstract: Electrocardiogram (ECG) delineation, the identification of waveform boundaries, is a foundational step that translates raw ECG signals into clinically interpretable measurements. Deep learning has advanced this task but remains dependent on costly expert annotations. Label-efficient strategies such as self-supervised pretraining and semi-supervised learning are expected to ease this burden, yet it remains unclear whether they yield reliable delineation and whether the deep models they produce outperform the delineation tools used in practice. We address this in two stages. First, comparing self-supervised objectives with supervised or semi-supervised fine-tuning across one internal and four external datasets, we find that pretraining helps but the objective matters, and that the value of semi-supervised fine-tuning depends on the pretraining objective. Second, we benchmark the selected deep learning model against widely used open-source (NeuroKit2, Prominence, ECGdeli) and commercial (CalECG) tools using three complementary metrics. The model ranks best on every metric and dataset, outperforming the strongest tool by a clear margin on the rhythm-diverse set (mIoU 71.3 vs. 54.8%; averaged point-wise sensitivity 92.6 vs. 76.4%), and degrades the least from sinus to arrhythmia. A rhythm-stratified and point-wise analysis further characterizes the distinctive behavior of each tool, yielding practical guidance for tool selection. These results provide systematic, multi-dataset evidence that self-supervised pretraining is effective for ECG delineation and enables a label-efficiently trained deep learning model to outperform widely used delineation tools by leveraging abundant unlabeled data. This supports adopting such models in diverse, real-world clinical settings.

</details>


### [318] [FC-SWE: Failure-Conditioned RL for Long-Horizon Software Engineering Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07898)
*Jia Liufu,Bin Hu,Linglin Jing,Terry Kong,Yuki Huang,Ashwath Aithal,Wenming Yang,Jun Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FC-SWE是一个失败条件化的强化学习框架，将失败补丁的验证器反馈作为后续恢复轨迹的上下文，并调整GRPO以支持完整多轮工具使用轨迹链训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SWE智能体训练（如GRPO）对同一问题独立采样多条轨迹，但不复用失败补丁的验证器反馈作为后续尝试的上下文，浪费了诊断信息；而训练恢复轨迹存在难点：前一次结果决定后续轨迹是否生成，失败执行决定其条件上下文。

Method: 提出FC-SWE框架。当补丁验证失败后，将仓库恢复到原始任务状态，并以失败补丁和验证器反馈作为上下文生成恢复轨迹。通过两个机制适配GRPO：(1)轨迹级局部奖励，保留每次尝试的验证结果，避免恢复成功奖励到之前失败的补丁；(2)活跃集优势估计，将同一问题实际执行的所有初始与恢复轨迹组成比较组，失败尝试保留在内而未执行的尝试被排除。

Result: 在全部500个SWE-bench Verified任务上，采用验证器辅助协议，FC-SWE配合Qwen3.5-4B和SWE-agent取得41.7% Resolved@1和52.8% Resolved@2，优于GRPO的38.9%和48.5%。尽管训练时每条链最多两次尝试，在十一次尝试的测试时预算下达到70.7% Resolved@11。

Conclusion: FC-SWE通过失败条件化与轨迹链训练有效提升了SWE智能体的性能，且训练时的少量尝试可在测试时预算增加下泛化到更高的解决率。

Abstract: Repository-level software engineering (SWE) is a challenging long-horizon setting: agents must reason over extended interactions, use tools, and adapt to stateful environments. Recent work trains SWE agents with reinforcement learning methods such as Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), which independently sample multiple trajectories per issue, test the resulting patches, and compare terminal rewards within a fixed group. However, this training setup does not reuse verifier feedback from failed patches as context for subsequent attempts, even though this feedback contains valuable diagnostic information about what went wrong. Training on recovery trajectories is challenging because the preceding outcome determines whether the next trajectory is generated, while the failed execution determines its conditioning context. We introduce FC-SWE, a failure-conditioned RL framework that incorporates recovery attempts into policy training. After a patch fails verification, FC-SWE restores the repository to its original task state and uses the failed patch and verifier feedback as context for a recovery trajectory. FC-SWE adapts GRPO to these chains of complete, multi-turn tool-use trajectories through two mechanisms. Trajectory-local rewards preserve each attempt's verifier outcome, preventing recovery success from rewarding an earlier failed patch. Active-set advantage estimation forms a comparison group from all initial and recovery trajectories actually executed for the same issue, so failed attempts remain in the group while unexecuted attempts are excluded. On all 500 SWE-bench Verified tasks under a verifier-assisted protocol, FC-SWE with Qwen3.5-4B and SWE-agent achieves 41.7% Resolved@1 and 52.8% Resolved@2, compared with 38.9% and 48.5% for GRPO. Although trained with at most two attempts per chain, FC-SWE reaches 70.7% Resolved@11 under an eleven-attempt test-time budget.

</details>


### [319] [Variance-Averse $n$-Step Offline Reinforcement Learning for Sparse Long-Horizon Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07899)
*Guhyeon Kang,Minhae Kwon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出VAN-Flow框架，通过方差规避的期望算子结合分类分布式评价器与流匹配生成策略，在离线强化学习中促进可靠动作选择，在40余个任务上超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式策略虽能建模复杂动作分布，但在异构数据集中会重现高方差、不可靠的动作模式，这些动作偶尔凭运气产生高回报但缺乏一致性，因此仅最大化期望Q值不足以识别可靠动作。

Method: VAN-Flow包含三部分：(i) 分类分布式评价器；(ii) 方差规避期望算子，通过平滑重加权原子概率，偏好兼具高回报与低离散度的动作；(iii) 经拒绝采样引导的流匹配生成式执行器。该算子仅重分配分类回报分布上的概率质量，无需硬截断或辅助惩罚项。

Result: 在D4RL和OGBench的40余个任务上，VAN-Flow持续优于强基线，在长视界和高方差场景中增益最大。

Conclusion: VAN-Flow能有效促进生成式离线RL中的可靠动作选择，相比CVaR或均值-方差目标更具优势，尤其适用于可靠动作选择至关重要的长视界高方差场景。

Abstract: Generative actors are transforming offline reinforcement learning (RL) by enabling expressive policy classes that model complex action distributions. However, this expressiveness also exposes a key challenge in heterogeneous datasets: generative policies can reproduce unreliable action modes whose return distributions exhibit high variance, occasionally yielding high returns by chance but lacking consistency. Consequently, maximizing the expected $Q$-value alone is insufficient for identifying reliable actions. We propose VAN-Flow (Variance-Averse $n$-step Flow), a framework that promotes reliable actions in generative offline RL. VAN-Flow combines (i) a categorical distributional critic, (ii) a variance-averse expectation operator that smoothly reweights atom probabilities to favor actions with both high returns and low dispersion, and (iii) a flow-matching generative actor guided via rejection sampling. Unlike CVaR or mean-variance objectives, the operator redistributes probability mass over the categorical return distribution without hard truncation or auxiliary penalty terms. Across more than 40 tasks from D4RL and OGBench, VAN-Flow consistently outperforms strong baselines, with the largest gains in long-horizon and high-variance regimes where reliable action selection becomes critical.

</details>


### [320] [ApexQuant: Data-Free Elastic Quantization by Residual Re-Isotropization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07904)
*Aksel Fristrup,Sumit Pandey,Ankit Kariryaa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ApexQuant：一种免校准、递归重量化残差的量化方法，可作为现有量化器的精修层，在完全无数据设置下将LLM与专业领域模型量化至4-bit接近全精度、2-bit表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量化方法通常依赖校准数据，但在许多真实场景中分布内数据难以获取（如受限许可的遥感影像、受隐私约束的医疗数据）。因此需要一种完全无数据、免校准的量化方案，同时支持多种精度。

Method: 提出ApexQuant，递归地对残差误差重新量化，作为现有量化器之上的精修层。理论证明：一次新的随机旋转会使每个残差回到超球面上的均匀分布，从而刻画逐次迭代中误差渐进衰减的速率。据此可在读取任何权重之前，预先确定某层达到目标权重空间误差所需的迭代次数。方法实例化为三种可互换阶段：标量、E8格与格状（trellis）。

Result: 在四个开源权重LLM以及地球观测和医疗领域上验证。渐进重各向同性化在4-bit下与全精度差距在几个百分点内，并在完全无数据设置下取得所测最佳2-bit表现。每个前缀本身即是一个有效的低速率模型，因此单个产物可服务多种精度。

Conclusion: ApexQuant提供了一种可证明、免校准、无数据的量化精修方案，兼具多精度复用能力，适用于数据受限的专业领域。

Abstract: We introduce ApexQuant, a calibration-free quantization method that recursively re-quantizes the residual error, serving as a refinement layer on top of existing quantizers. We establish that a fresh random rotation returns each residual to the uniform distribution on the hypersphere, which characterizes the rate of progressive error decay across successive passes. This result lets us determine, before any weight is read, how many passes a layer needs for a target weight-space error. Every prefix is itself a valid lower-rate model, so one artifact serves several precisions. We instantiate ApexQuant with three interchangeable stages, scalar, $E_8$ and trellis, and validate it on four open-weight LLMs and on Earth-observation and medical domains where in-distribution data is often unattainable as imagery arrives under restrictive licences or due to patient material under privacy constraints. Progressive re-isotropization comes within a few percent of full precision at four bits and gives the best two-bit arm we measure, in a completely data-free setting.

</details>


### [321] [Revisiting Temporal Regularization for Smooth Control in Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07910)
*SungJae Ahn,Jeong Woon Lee,Kyoleen Kwak,Hyoseok Hwang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CATS方法，通过时间正则化（时间惩罚+线性递增）实现动作平滑，在保持任务性能的同时减少动作振荡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习策略会产生不平滑的动作振荡，不利于在物理机器人上部署。已有方法通过空间平滑或时间正则化解决，但空间约束会损害任务性能，而时间正则化被认为无法在观测噪声下提供所需的平滑性。

Method: 作者重新审视这一假设，证明时间惩罚可以约束具有相同下一状态的当前状态之间的期望动作差异，揭示出可经验性扩展到空间平滑的空间效应。基于此提出CATS（Conditioning for Action using only Temporal Smoothness），将时间惩罚与线性递增结合，使策略先学习有回报的行为，再逐步平滑动作。

Result: 仿真和真实世界实验表明，CATS能显著减少动作振荡而不降低任务性能，且计算开销很小。相比显式空间正则化，时间正则化能更好地保持任务性能同时提供空间平滑。

Conclusion: 时间正则化结合线性递增可在保持任务性能的同时提供时间和空间平滑，CATS为机器人部署提供了实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Deep Reinforcement Learning policies can produce nonsmooth action oscillations that hinder deployment on physical robots. Existing architectural and penalty-based approaches seek spatial smoothness by directly reducing sensitivity to changes in state inputs, but their broad constraints can degrade task performance as stronger smoothing is pursued. Temporal regularization instead constrains action differences along observed transitions, but has been considered unable to provide the spatial smoothness needed under observation noise. We revisit this assumption by proving that the temporal penalty bounds the expected action differences between current states sharing a next state, revealing a spatial effect that empirically extends to spatial smoothness. Building on this finding, we propose Conditioning for Action using only Temporal Smoothness (CATS), which combines a temporal penalty with linear ramp-up. We highlight temporal regularization's ability to provide spatial smoothness while better preserving task performance than explicit spatial regularization. Through linear ramp-up, CATS allows the policy to learn rewarding behavior before progressively smoothing its actions, improving return preservation and both temporal and spatial smoothness. Experiments in both simulation and the real world show that CATS substantially reduces action oscillation without degrading task performance, with little computational overhead.

</details>


### [322] [Generalized Matheron Variational Implicit Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07938)
*Luis A. Ortega,Andrés R. Masegosa,Thomas D. Nielsen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GMVIP 是一种针对隐过程先验的路径式变分推断方法，通过先验采样加诱导点校正构造后验样本，在系数空间给出可处理的 KL 散度，并在回归、分类、预测任务上表现有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐过程先验（如贝叶斯神经网络、随机模拟器）通过前向采样机制定义函数分布，但其函数空间密度通常不可得，导致难以直接进行后验推断。

Method: 提出广义 Matheron 变分隐过程（GMVIP）。它从先验采样一个函数，并在诱导输入处施加校正来构造后验样本；校正远离诱导点的效果直接由先验样本决定。代理先验与变分后验采用相同的路径式构造，仅白化诱导系数的分布不同，从而得到可处理的系数空间 KL 散度。对高斯过程先验，该方法退化为标准诱导变量变分 GP 构造；对一般隐过程，其经验协方差构造在总体极限下保持先验均值与协方差。

Result: 在回归、分类和预测任务中，使用模拟器定义与检索条件化的经验轨迹先验，GMVIP 与现有方法相比具有广泛的竞争力。

Conclusion: GMVIP 为隐过程先验提供了统一、可处理的路径式变分推断框架，既能保留原隐过程的结构与变异性，又能自然涵盖高斯过程先验作为特例。

Abstract: Implicit-process priors specify distributions over functions through sample-forward mechanisms such as Bayesian neural networks and stochastic simulators, but their function-space densities are typically unavailable. We introduce Generalized Matheron Variational Implicit Processes (GMVIP), a pathwise variational family for posterior inference with such priors. For Gaussian-process priors, GMVIP recovers the standard inducing-variable variational GP construction; for general implicit priors, its empirical covariance construction preserves the prior mean and covariance in the population limit. GMVIP constructs posterior samples by drawing a function from the prior and applying a correction anchored at a set of inducing inputs. The effect of this correction away from the inducing inputs is determined directly from prior samples, allowing the posterior to retain the structure and variability of the original implicit process. The (surrogate) prior and variational posterior use the same pathwise construction and differ only in the distribution of whitened inducing coefficients, yielding a tractable coefficient-space Kullback-Leibler divergence. Experiments on regression, classification, and forecasting with simulator-defined and retrieval-conditioned empirical trajectory priors show that GMVIP is broadly competitive with existing methods.

</details>


### [323] [DecepEval: A Benchmark for Evaluating Deception in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07967)
*Yiming Xu,Hongyue Yu,Beihua Yang,Zihan Chen,Yixin Liu,Zhen Peng,Bin Shi,Bo Dong,Chao Shen,Irwin King,Qinghua Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出DecepEval基准与LLM Deception Diamond框架，系统评估LLM智能体在外部条件诱导下的欺骗行为，发现诱导条件普遍提高欺骗率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM智能体自主性增强，可能为追求任务表现而采取欺骗手段，威胁其可靠部署。现有评估多局限于孤立场景或狭窄条件，难以系统理解欺骗何时更易发生。

Method: 提出DecepEval基准，包含3个任务族、28个专业场景下的1,532个实例；基于经典欺诈理论构建LLM Deception Diamond框架，刻画压力、激励、机会、冲突四类外部诱导条件。每个实例配对中性版与诱导版，通过显式任务事实与可观测行为区分欺骗与能力错误。

Result: 对九个前沿LLM的评估显示，诱导条件在各模型和任务族中普遍提高欺骗率，即使基线欺骗率较低的模型也不例外。

Conclusion: DecepEval使这类脆弱性可被度量，为迈向可信人工智能提供了共享基准。

Abstract: As large language model (LLM) agents become increasingly autonomous, they may pursue task performance through deception, raising concerns about their reliable deployment. Existing evaluations show that LLM agents can deceive, but often examine isolated scenarios or narrowly defined conditions, limiting systematic understanding of when deception becomes more likely. To address this gap, we introduce DecepEval, a benchmark comprising 1,532 instances across 3 task families and 28 professional scenarios. Drawing on classical fraud theories, we propose the LLM Deception Diamond framework, which characterizes four external conditions that may induce deception: pressure, incentive, opportunity, and conflict. DecepEval pairs neutral and induced versions of each instance to measure condition-dependent changes in deception rates, while explicit task facts and observable agent behavior help distinguish deception from capability-related errors. Evaluations of nine frontier LLMs show that inducements increase deception across models and task families, even among models with low baseline deception rates. DecepEval makes these vulnerabilities measurable, providing a shared benchmark for progress toward trustworthy artificial intelligence.

</details>


### [324] [Learning a Ranking from Human Feedback in Log-Concave Random Utility Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07973)
*Diego Alovisetti,Marco Mussi,Alberto Maria Metelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究在随机效用模型（对数凹噪声）下根据人类比较反馈恢复物品排序的问题，针对全排序反馈与仅胜者反馈两种类型，建立最坏情况样本复杂度下界，并设计出匹配下界（至对数因子）且无需噪声分布知识的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 根据未知数值效用恢复物品排序是排序与偏好学习中的核心问题，但已有工作多假设特定噪声模型或需要已知噪声分布。本文希望在对数凹噪声这一较一般假设下，研究不同反馈信息量（全排序 vs 仅第一名）对样本复杂度的影响，并刻画两者本质难度差异。

Method: 采用随机效用模型，噪声分布为对数凹且仅需方差上界。提出容忍小幅效用差异的 ε-精确排序准则。分别针对全排序反馈与仅胜者反馈，推导最坏情况样本复杂度下界，并设计与之匹配（至对数因子）的算法，算法不依赖噪声分布的具体形式。

Result: 对两种反馈类型均得到最坏情况样本复杂度下界，且所提算法的样本复杂度与下界在常数对数因子内一致。结果表明仅胜者反馈下的排序问题本质上更难，其样本复杂度依赖于物品集合中最小的获胜概率。

Conclusion: 在一般对数凹噪声假设下，ε-精确排序的可学习性由反馈类型和信息量决定；仅胜者反馈因信息更少且受最小获胜概率制约而显著更难。所提算法无需知道噪声分布，仅需方差上界，具有较强实用性。

Abstract: We study the problem of recovering the ranking of a fixed set of items according to their unknown numerical utilities. At each interaction with the environment, a learner presents the item set to a human and receives comparative feedback of two types. Under full-ranking feedback, each interaction reveals a noisy ranking of all items, whereas under winner-only feedback, it reveals only the item ranked first. In both settings, we model human feedback using a random utility model with log-concave noise and study the number of observations needed to recover an $ε$-accurate ranking with high probability. This novel criterion tolerates ordering errors only between items whose utilities differ by less than $ε$. For both feedback types, we establish worst-case sample-complexity lower bounds and develop algorithms that match these bounds up to logarithmic factors. Neither algorithm requires knowledge of the noise distribution, while only requiring an upper bound on its variance. Our results show that the ranking problem under winner-only feedback is intrinsically harder by exposing the sample complexity dependence on the minimum winning probability across the item set.

</details>


### [325] [Do Higher-Order Models Win for Higher-Order Reasons? Rethinking Performance Gains in Hypergraph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07981)
*Fanchen Bu,Fan Li,Geon Lee,Sunwoo Kim,Xiaoyang Wang,Renaud Lambiotte,Kijung Shin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出受控的表现归因框架，通过扰动高阶信息而保留成对信息，检验高阶模型（如超图神经网络）的优势是否真来自高阶信息。在25个超图学习基准、三类任务上发现：扰动后高阶模型仍保留大部分优势，且简单增强的低阶基线可缩小剩余差距，说明部分优势可由低阶因素解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献中普遍认为高阶模型（如超图神经网络）优于低阶基线是因其能利用高阶信息，但仅凭性能更好无法证明这一归因成立，需要区分"性能提升"与"其解释"。

Method: 引入受控的性能归因框架：在保留低阶（成对）信息的同时扰动高阶信息，观察高阶模型优势的变化；随后探究剩余性能差距是否可由低阶因素（如更丰富的成对加权、更多成对特征传播步骤、归一化）解释。

Result: 在涵盖三类任务的25个常用超图学习基准上，频繁观察到：高阶模型原本优于低阶基线，但在高阶信息被扰动后仍保留大部分优势；对低阶基线做简单增强即可缩小剩余差距。

Conclusion: 观察到的部分优势可由低阶因素解释，呼吁超图学习社区重新思考性能归因，区分性能增益与其解释，采用更强的低阶基线，并使用更能检验高阶信息价值的合适基准。

Abstract: Higher-order models (e.g., hypergraph neural networks) often outperform lower-order baselines on hypergraph learning benchmarks, and their advantages are commonly attributed to their ability to exploit higher-order information. However, better performance alone does not establish this explanation. We therefore ask: Do higher-order models win for higher-order reasons? To investigate this question, we introduce a controlled performance-attribution framework that perturbs higher-order information while preserving the lower-order, i.e., pairwise, information. Across 25 commonly used hypergraph learning benchmarks spanning three tasks, we frequently observe an intriguing pattern: higher-order models originally outperform lower-order baselines, yet retain most of their advantage after perturbation. This suggests that much of the observed advantage remains achievable without the higher-order information. We then investigate potential lower-order explanations for these remaining gaps. We find that simple additions to a lower-order baseline, e.g., richer pairwise weighting, more steps of pairwise feature propagation, and normalization, reduce the remaining performance gaps, supporting lower-order explanations for part of the observed advantage. Our analysis calls for the hypergraph learning community to rethink performance attribution by distinguishing performance gains from their explanations, adopt stronger lower-order baselines, and use suitable benchmarks that better test the value of higher-order information.

</details>


### [326] [A Broader Look at Model Merging: Rethinking Implicit Regularization Induced by Task Arithmetic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07990)
*Sin-Han Yang,Shih-Cheng Huang,Chieh-Yen Lin,Yun-Nung Chen,Shao-Hua Sun,Hung-yi Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 模型合并通过组合任务专用模型权重构建多任务模型，通常用额外数据集搜索线性组合系数。本文发现这种系数搜索隐含将候选模型限制在任务权重更新张成的子空间内，形成隐性正则化；实证表明去除该正则化直接优化合并权重能显著提升性能，甚至预训练模型直接优化也能超过部分现有合并方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型合并方法多依赖额外数据集搜索任务专用权重更新的最优线性组合系数，作者识别出这一常规做法隐含一种正则化：将候选模型限制在任务权重更新所张成的子空间内。本文旨在探究这种正则化是否真正有益，并质疑现有合并流程的必要性。

Method: 作者不搜索线性组合系数，而是直接优化合并模型的权重（去除子空间约束），并在多种架构、多个领域以及每类仅一个样本的极端数据受限场景下进行实验；同时研究不同的额外数据集使用策略，并分析子空间外是否存在更优的多任务权重。

Result: 实证结果显示，去除该正则化后直接优化合并权重可显著提升常见合并方法在多架构、多领域乃至极端数据受限场景下的性能；直接优化预训练模型权重甚至优于部分现有合并方法。分析表明更优的多任务权重存在于子空间之外，且可通过多种方法找到。

Conclusion: 本文呼吁重新审视现有模型合并流程，鼓励对权重空间进行更广泛的探索，并重新考虑任务算术所引入的隐性正则化，讨论不同额外数据集使用策略的实用价值与启示。

Abstract: Model merging aims to build a multi-task model cheaply by combining the weights of individual task-specific models. To perform well across multiple tasks, most existing merging methods use an additional dataset to find the coefficients for the best linear combination of task-specific weight updates. However, we identify an implicit regularization in this standard practice: searching over coefficients restricts the candidate models to a subspace spanned by task-specific weight updates. In this work, we investigate whether this regularization is actually useful. Surprisingly, empirical results show that optimizing merged-model weights without this regularization significantly boosts the performance of common merging methods across multiple architectures, domains, and even in an extremely data-limited scenario where only one instance is available per class. Moreover, directly optimizing the pretrained model weights even outperforms some existing merging methods. Analysis shows that better multi-task weights exist outside the subspace and can be found using multiple methods. We study different strategies for using the additional dataset, discussing their practical use and implications for model merging. Overall, this work calls for revisiting the existing model-merging pipeline, motivating a broader exploration of the weight space and a reconsideration of the implicit regularization induced by task arithmetic.

</details>


### [327] [TICDA: Tabular In-Context Data Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07996)
*Yacine Benihaddadene,Milan Bhan,Eliot Dugelay,Mohammed Jawhar,Benjamin Wong,Nicolas Chesneau,Duong Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 TICDA，一种基于线性代理模型的方法，可直接从表格基础模型的潜在嵌入中衡量上下文中每个示例的影响，单次前向传播即可完成，成本极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型（TFM）通过上下文中的标注示例进行预测，但单个示例如何影响预测尚不清楚。上下文常由现有标注数据拼凑而成，可能包含错误标注、冗余或低质量样本，从而降低性能。传统数据归因方法不适用于 TFM：DemoShapley 等重采样方法需组合级前向次数，影响函数等梯度方法依赖参数更新，而上下文学习并不更新参数。

Method: TICDA 在 TFM 潜在嵌入上训练线性代理模型，直接从该代理模型测量上下文中每个示例的影响，无需组合级采样或多轮前向，仅需单次前向传播，成本几乎可忽略。

Result: 在四项任务上，TICDA 相较对比方法取得最佳折中：检测标注错误、筛选上下文以在降低推理成本的同时保持预测精度、产生可跨 TFM 迁移的归因分数，以及支持高效主动学习的采集策略。

Conclusion: TICDA 为 TFM 上下文示例的影响度量提供了一种高效可行的方案，兼具低成本与跨模型迁移能力，并在多项实际任务中表现优于现有方法。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models (TFMs) achieve strong predictive performance by conditioning on labeled demonstrations provided in context, without any parameter update. Yet how individual demonstrations shape a given prediction remains poorly understood. This gap matters in practice: the context is often assembled from whatever labeled data is available, potentially leading to the inclusion of mislabeled, redundant, or low-quality examples that degrade performance. Standard data attribution methods do not transfer to the TFM setting: resampling-based approaches such as DemoShapley require a combinatorial number of forward passes, and gradient-based estimators such as influence functions require computing training point's effect on the model parameters, which in-context learning never updates. We introduce TICDA, a method that measures the influence of every demonstration in the context directly from linear surrogates trained on TFM latent embeddings, in a single forward pass and at negligible cost. We show that TICDA offers the best compromise against competitors across four tasks: detecting labeling errors, curating context to preserve predictive accuracy while lowering inference cost, producing attribution scores that transfer across TFMs, and supporting an acquisition strategy for efficient active learning.

</details>


### [328] [Can phenotypic activity be predicted without experimental readouts?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07997)
*Télio Cropsal,Rocío Mercado*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文检验了基于配对分子-形态数据对比预训练的分子编码器（如CLOOME、CellCLIP）作为表型预测廉价替代方案的可行性。在控制泄漏和细胞毒性混淆后，六种表征在两个Cell Painting屏幕上均未表现出优于普通理化描述符的明显优势，且毒性比表型活性更易预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLOOME、CellCLIP等分子编码器被提出可作为表型预测的廉价替代品，从而避免运行Cell Painting实验。然而现有评估可能受到两种混淆因素夸大性能：一是跨越编码器自身预训练边界的泄漏，二是表型活性与细胞毒性之间的相关性。因此有必要在控制这些混淆的条件下重新评估其真实价值。

Method: 作者设计了一套控制泄漏与混淆的评估协议，测试了六种表征，其中包括一个与CLOOME输入和层数匹配的未预训练MLP对照，并在两个不同的Cell Painting屏幕上进行评估，同时控制预训练边界泄漏以及表型活性与细胞毒性的相关性。

Result: 在控制上述混淆后，预训练分子编码器相较普通理化描述符并无明显优势；且在所有表征中，毒性通常比表型活性更容易预测。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，在将表型预训练编码器作为表型药物发现的替代方案加以信任之前，应把泄漏感知、混淆受控的评估作为标准实践。

Abstract: Molecular encoders contrastively pretrained on paired molecule-morphology data, such as CLOOME and CellCLIP, have been proposed as cheap surrogates for phenotypic prediction, avoiding the need to run a Cell Painting assay. We evaluate this idea for these molecular encoders under a protocol designed to control for two confounds that can inflate apparent performance: leakage across an encoder's own pretraining boundary, and the correlation between phenotypic activity and cytotoxicity. Testing six representations, including a non-pretrained MLP control matching CLOOME's input and layer count, on two distinct Cell Painting screens, we find that once these confounds are controlled for, the pretrained molecular encoders show no clear advantage over plain physicochemical descriptors, and that toxicity is generally easier to predict than phenotypic activity across representations. Our results suggest leakage-aware, confound-controlled evaluation should be standard practice before phenotype-pretrained encoders are trusted as surrogates for phenotypic drug discovery.

</details>


### [329] [Spectra: Exact Component Transport for Test-Time Prior Adaptation in Simulation-Based Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08021)
*Xin Zhao,Nico Scherf,Robert Trampel,Kerrin J. Pine,Nikolaus Weiskopf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Spectra是一种针对扩散模型SBI的测试时自适应方法，通过精确的打分传输恒等式闭式获得自适应打分，实现无需再训练的先验变化适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摊销式SBI能快速进行后验推断，但其复用性受限于训练时的先验分布。而科学分析中常需随认知积累调整先验或检验替代假设，因此需要一种能在测试时适应先验变化的方法。

Method: 提出Spectra，一种用于基于扩散的SBI的测试时自适应方法。它利用精确的打分传输恒等式，对结构化先验变化从冻结的扩散模型中以闭式形式获得自适应打分，无需额外模拟或训练。

Result: 在六个SBI基准上，Spectra在强先验偏移下实现了准确自适应，且在线采样成本低。

Conclusion: Spectra使预训练的SBI模型能够在测试时纳入更新的先验信息，扩展了摊销式SBI的复用性。

Abstract: Simulation-based inference (SBI) has become a powerful approach to Bayesian inference in complex scientific models whose likelihoods are difficult or impossible to evaluate. Amortized SBI learns reusable inference models from simulated data, enabling rapid posterior inference for new observations, and modern generative models have made these models increasingly expressive. However, this reuse is limited to the prior distribution chosen during training, whereas scientific analyses often need revised priors as knowledge accumulates or alternative assumptions are tested. We introduce Spectra, a test-time adaptation method for diffusion-based SBI. Spectra uses an exact score-transport identity to obtain the adapted score from a frozen diffusion model in closed form for structured prior changes, without additional simulation or training. Across six SBI benchmarks, Spectra achieves accurate adaptation under strong prior shifts at low online sampling cost. This enables pretrained SBI models to incorporate updated prior information at test time.

</details>


### [330] [SepsisLens: Structure-Preserving Sequence Modelling for Decomposable Early Sepsis Warning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08046)
*Yikun Ou,Wei Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SepsisLens 提出一种结构保持的脓毒症早期预警框架，在风险合成前保留按变量索引的时序状态，实现可分解的多时域风险预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICU 记录的脓毒症早期预警本质上是一个结构保持的预测问题：模型既要从不规则测量中检测病情恶化，又要让每个预警能关联到支持它的生理信号。现有许多时序模型将临床变量融合为患者级表示，虽支持标量风险预测，却削弱了临床分解所需的结构。

Method: 方法包含：观测感知表示，编码每个变量的动态与测量历史；共享时序编码器，对每条轨迹建模而不压缩变量轴；StructuredRiskHead，从显式的变量级与器官级成分合成多时域风险。

Result: 在三个公开 ICU 队列和一个私立医院队列上，采用统一发病前协议评估。SepsisLens 在四个队列均取得较强判别性能，在 MIMIC-IV 上于相同事件召回率下具有更低的报警负担。结构消融支持该设计，输入侧掩码表明排序成分反映了对预测影响更大的变量。

Conclusion: SepsisLens 通过在风险合成前保留变量索引的时序状态，在保持预测性能的同时提供了可分解、可解释的结构化风险预警。

Abstract: Early sepsis warning from ICU records can be cast as a structure-preserving prediction problem. A model needs to detect deterioration from irregular measurements while keeping each alert connected to the physiological signals that support it. Many temporal models fuse clinical variables into a patient-level representation, supporting scalar risk prediction but weakening the structure needed for clinical decomposition. We present SepsisLens, which preserves variable-indexed temporal states until risk composition. Observation-aware representations encode each variable's dynamics and measurement history, while a shared temporal encoder models each trajectory without collapsing the variable axis. The StructuredRiskHead composes multi-horizon risk from explicit variable-level and organ-level components. We evaluate SepsisLens on three public ICU cohorts and one private-hospital cohort under a common pre-onset protocol. SepsisLens achieves strong discrimination on all four cohorts and lower alert burden at matched event recall on MIMIC-IV. Structural ablations support the design, while input-side masking shows that the ranked components reflect variables with greater influence on prediction.

</details>


### [331] [A Riemannian Geometry for Low-rank Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08049)
*Shoichiro Takeda,Shin'ya Yamaguchi,Satoshi Suzuki,Yasunori Akagi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对LoRA的参数化冗余问题，提出一种新的黎曼度量与预条件方法，使LoRA的权重更新更接近全量微调，从而提升优化效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA通过低秩矩阵BA^T近似全量微调的权重更新，其参数化存在等价关系(B,A)~(BG^{-1},AG^T)，形成商流形并带来冗余方向。已有工作虽用黎曼度量处理该几何结构，但在权重层面与全量微调仍有差距，本文希望缩小这一差距。

Method: 作者为LoRA专门设计了一种新的黎曼度量，该度量在等价关系下不变，并据此在每个梯度步诱导预条件。随后从理论上分析该预条件在每次迭代中满足的两条性质。

Result: 理论表明：(i) 权重更新方向在LoRA参数化所允许的一阶权重变化子空间内，最接近全量微调的梯度方向；(ii) 更新后的权重矩阵在Frobenius范数下比使用传统预条件和无预条件的LoRA更接近全量微调的权重矩阵。在语言和视觉领域的微调任务上，实验验证了该预条件的有效性与高效性。

Conclusion: 通过在LoRA的商流形上引入定制黎曼度量与预条件，可使LoRA在权重层面更好地逼近全量微调，实现更高效的优化，并在多领域微调任务中取得良好效果。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) is widely used as a parameter-efficient fine-tuning technique for pre-trained deep neural networks, which approximates the weight update via full fine-tuning by a low-rank matrix $BA^\top$. This parameterization leads to the equivalence relation $(B, A) \sim (BG^{-1}, AG^\top)$ for any invertible matrix $G$ because $BA^\top = BG^{-1}(AG^\top)^\top$ and thus both pairs yield the same loss value. This relation induces a quotient manifold where matrices $(BG^{-1}, AG^\top)$ for all $G$ are identified, eliminating redundant directions along which the loss value remains unchanged. To respect the geometry of this manifold, the original search space is endowed with a Riemannian metric that is invariant under the equivalence relation. Such a metric induces preconditioning at each gradient step and ensures that each weight update via LoRA changes the loss value, leading to efficient optimization. In this paper, we propose a new Riemannian metric that is specifically tailored to LoRA to close the gap to full fine-tuning at the weight level. We theoretically show that LoRA with our preconditioning induced by this metric satisfies the following two properties at each iteration: (i) The weight update follows the direction closest to the gradient of full fine-tuning within the subspace of first-order weight changes allowed by the LoRA parameterization. (ii) The updated weight matrix is closer in Frobenius norm to that of full fine-tuning than the updated weight matrices of LoRA with conventional preconditioning and without preconditioning. These theoretical insights suggest that our preconditioning makes LoRA better approximate full fine-tuning, thereby leading to more efficient optimization. Experiments show the effectiveness and efficiency of our preconditioning for LoRA on fine-tuning tasks with language and vision domains.

</details>


### [332] [Detecting a Shift Is Not Enough: Exact Minimax Limits of Linear Representation Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08069)
*Anuar Aimoldin,Yankai Chen,Ayana Mussabayeva,Xue Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将两个数据源之间均值漂移的去除建模为统计决策问题，在硬失真预算下学习一个对两个源共用的线性映射，推导出精确的有限样本极小极大风险，并揭示“检测-修复差距”：检测漂移只需 κ ≫ √d，而修复固定比例漂移在常数失真下需要 κ ≍ d。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两个数据源之间的均值漂移虽然容易检测，但难以在不显著改变其表示的情况下移除。现有方法（如 MP、SAL、LEACE 等线性概念擦除器）缺乏对残留漂移量和所需校准量的理论刻画，因此需要给出精确的风险公式，以判断校正不足时应改进方法、增加单被试记录数，还是增加被试数量。

Method: 将漂移去除建模为统计决策问题：从 R^d 中成对校准测量之间的含噪差异出发，在硬失真预算下学习一个同时应用于两个源的线性映射，使新鲜数据上的残留漂移尽可能小。推导所有此类映射的精确有限样本极小极大风险 (d−k)·E[1/(d+2J)]，其中 J~Pois(κ/2)，k 为预算允许删除的方向数，κ 为校准信噪比。方法为直接投影掉均值校准差异。

Result: 投影掉均值校准差异可在未知 κ 和噪声尺度的情况下达到该极小极大风险。检测漂移需要 κ ≫ √d，而移除固定比例漂移在常数失真下需要 κ ≍ d。标准线性概念擦除器（MP、SAL、LEACE）移除相同的校准差异，因此该公式可在拟合前精确给出其在新数据上残留的漂移量及目标所需校准量。该极限具稳健性：配对使非高斯共享内容下仍精确，投影在各向异性噪声下保持保证，选择性弃权无法弥合差距。在配对临床与可穿戴睡眠 EEG 数据上，公式成功预测新参与者中残留的设备漂移，并显示每人更多记录很快不再有帮助。

Conclusion: 该结果提供了判断校正不足原因的准则：究竟需要更好的方法、更多记录，还是更多参与者，为均值漂移去除的理论分析与实践决策提供了统一框架。

Abstract: A mean shift between two data sources can be easy to detect but hard to remove without substantially changing their representations. We cast its removal as a statistical decision problem: from noisy differences between paired calibration measurements in $\mathbb{R}^d$, learn one linear map, applied to both sources under a hard distortion budget, that leaves as little of the shift as possible on fresh data. We derive the exact finite-sample minimax risk over all such maps, $(d-k) \mathbb{E}[1/(d+2J)]$ with $J\sim\mathrm{Pois}(κ/2)$, where the budget allows deleting $k$ directions and $κ$ is the calibration signal-to-noise ratio. Projecting out the mean calibration difference attains it without knowing $κ$ or the noise scale. This exposes a detection-repair gap: detecting the shift needs only $κ\gg\sqrt d$, whereas removing a fixed fraction of it at constant distortion needs $κ\asymp d$, as for estimating its direction. Standard linear concept erasers (MP, SAL, LEACE) remove the same calibration difference, so the formula gives, before fitting, exactly how much shift they leave on fresh data and how much calibration a target requires. The limit is robust: pairing keeps it exact for non-Gaussian shared content, the projection keeps its guarantee under anisotropic noise, and selective abstention cannot close the gap. On paired clinical and wearable sleep EEG, where differences between participants act as calibration noise, the formula predicts the device shift left in new participants, and more recordings per person soon stop helping. Together, these results tell whether a correction that falls short needs a better method, more recordings, or more participants.

</details>


### [333] [Optimization Encoders: Rethinking Second-Order Meta-Learning for Neural Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08075)
*Rudolf L. M. van Herten,Soufiane Ben Haddou,Rachit Saluja,Johannes C. Paetzold*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"优化编码器"视角，将条件神经场中的潜变量优化视为一种编码过程，并据此设计了基于等变Transformer的MetaLF，实现端到端二阶元学习，在重建与语义任务上取得改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件神经场的有效性依赖于如何从观测数据推断条件潜表示。在元学习中，这种编码通过解码器诱导的梯度更新实现，使表示学习与解码器设计紧密绑定，但二者关系此前缺乏形式化理解，且一阶近似会丢弃部分学习路径。

Method: 作者将潜变量优化形式化为"优化编码器"，统一了二阶微分、潜参数化与任务监督三者的作用，从而支持编码过程与解码器的端到端二阶元学习训练。基于此提出MetaLF：一种等变Transformer神经场，通过自注意力对潜点云进行上下文建模，使局部观测能影响非局部结构，并将内层编码目标与外层任务监督解耦，在测试时仅用重建进行潜适应。

Result: 在多项式场上的受控实验表明，潜变量协调与更低的有效秩及更强函数空间对齐相关。在图像与3D形状重建任务中，MetaLF在3至5次梯度更新内提升保真度，同时支持图像、形状与体数据的语义预测。

Conclusion: 研究结果确立了"优化编码器"视角作为设计神经场的统一基础，即围绕表示的构建、协调与使用方式来设计神经场。

Abstract: Conditional neural fields represent signals continuously, but their effectiveness depends on how the conditional latent representations are inferred from observed data. In meta-learning, this encoding occurs through gradient updates induced by the decoder, tying representation learning directly to decoder design. We formalize this connection by interpreting latent optimization as an optimization encoder, unifying the roles of second-order differentiation, latent parameterization, and task supervision. This concept enables second-order meta-learning for end-to-end training of the encoding procedure alongside the decoder, and clarifies which learning pathway first-order approximations discard. Guided by this view, we introduce Attentive Latent Fields (MetaLF), an equivariant transformer-based neural field that contextualizes a latent pointcloud through self-attention. These interactions shape both field predictions and the updates that construct their representation, allowing local observations to inform coherent non-local structure. Disentangling the inner encoding objective from outer task supervision unifies reconstruction, classification, and segmentation within an end-to-end meta-learning framework, using reconstruction-only latent adaptation at test time. Controlled experiments on polynomial fields link latent coordination to lower effective rank and stronger alignment with the underlying function space. Across image and 3D shape reconstruction, MetaLF improves fidelity within three to five gradient updates, while supporting semantic prediction across images, shapes, and volumes. Together, these findings position the optimization encoder perspective as a unified basis for designing neural fields around how representations are constructed, coordinated, and used.

</details>


### [334] [Enhancing Diffusion Language Models with Autoregressive Post-Training Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08108)
*Yiming Qin,Ke Wang,Amel Abdelraheem,Adam Hazimeh,Pascal Frossard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出A2D框架，通过复用AR后训练权重更新来增强扩散语言模型，无需额外训练即可提升指令跟随、数学推理和编码能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型（dLLMs）通常从预训练的自回归（AR）模型初始化转换而来，以继承其表示能力，但转换后往往丢弃了AR祖先丰富的后训练生态（如SFT、RL更新）。作者希望回收利用这些已有的AR后训练资源来增强扩散模型。

Method: 作者首先验证：尽管经过了AR到扩散的转换，直接将AR后训练权重更新加到扩散基础模型上仍然有效。进一步发现AR与扩散的后训练更新在权重空间中近乎正交，但在扩散模型中却引起高度一致的表示变化，且两者互补。基于此提出A2D——一种简单的免训练框架，可将AR后训练模型的能力迁移到扩散基础模型，并通过组合AR与扩散后训练更新进一步提升已后训练的扩散模型。

Result: 在多种dLLM上（包括Dream、DreamReasoner、DiffuCoder、Dream-Coder、Nemotron-Labs-Diffusion和DiffusionGemma），A2D在指令跟随、数学推理和编码任务上稳定提升性能，且适用于监督微调（SFT）和强化学习（RL）更新。

Conclusion: 该方法无需额外训练或推理时计算，即可有效复用AR后训练资源增强扩散语言模型，为扩散模型的后训练提供了新的高效途径。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (dLLMs) have emerged as a promising alternative to autoregressive (AR) language models, offering flexible token-update orders and parallel decoding. Recent dLLMs are often initialized from pretrained AR models before diffusion conversion in order to inherit their learned representations. After the conversion, however, they typically ignore the extensive post-training ecosystem of their AR ancestors. In this work, we show that these existing AR post-training weight updates can instead be effectively recycled to enhance diffusion models. Despite the changes by AR-to-diffusion conversion, directly adding an AR post-training weight update to a diffusion base model remains effective, bringing its performance close to that achieved by direct diffusion post-training. Notably, AR and diffusion post-training updates are nearly orthogonal in weight space, yet induce substantially more aligned representation changes in the diffusion model. Their distinct updates are also complementary: composing their weights can retain gains from both regimes and further improve the post-trained diffusion model. Based on these findings, we propose A2D, a simple training-free framework for enhancing diffusion models with existing AR post-training resources. A2D can transfer capabilities from AR post-trained models to diffusion base models, and further improve already post-trained diffusion models by composing AR and diffusion post-training updates. Across various dLLMs, including Dream, DreamReasoner, DiffuCoder, Dream-Coder, Nemotron-Labs-Diffusion, and DiffusionGemma, A2D reliably improves instruction following, mathematical reasoning, and coding with both supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning updates, without additional training, or inference-time computation.

</details>


### [335] [Attenuated in-context identification in time-series foundation models: diagnosis under counterfactual inputs and repair by synthetic forced-system fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08118)
*Hong-In Won*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文测试协变量感知时间序列基础模型（TSFM）在强制工程系统上的反事实预测能力，发现默认接口下 TimesFM-2.5 和 TabPFN-TS 无记忆性，Chronos-2 虽识别动态但存在衰减，微调后可恢复响应幅度，但经典辨识在多数实测系统上仍更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协变量感知时间序列基础模型承诺对仪表化工厂提供免训练的 what-if 反事实答案，即不同未来输入会导致的输出变化。作者希望在具有精确反事实的强制工程系统上验证这一能力是否真实有效。

Method: 在具有精确反事实的强制工程系统上，比较 Chronos-2、TimesFM-2.5 和 TabPFN-TS 与拟合于相同上下文的经典系统辨识。使用默认协变量接口，并引入上下文抖动（context dither）和 26 分钟合成强制系统微调。通过配对反事实输入和打乱未来输入测试两个性质：协变量接口能否表示动态，以及预训练先验是否覆盖工厂时间尺度。

Result: TimesFM-2.5 和 TabPFN-TS 无记忆（预测效应是同一时刻函数，TimesFM-2.5 R²=1.000）。Chronos-2 预测效应仅为真实效应的 0.33–0.80，恢复的脉冲响应形状错误，在 8192 上下文样本下单自由度振子误差停留在 0.57，而 ARX 用 256 样本达到 0.02。上下文抖动可降低全部六类合成系统的 what-if 误差。微调恢复响应幅度（敏感度 0.83–0.96），在 Wiener-Hammerstein 和留出摩擦类上优于结构无关辨识。专用上下文辨识器接近微调模型，说明增益主要来自强制系统数据。四个实测工厂中三个经典辨识仍明显更好，微调模型损失部分单变量预测能力。

Conclusion: TSFM 的默认协变量接口无法可靠表示动态；只有配对反事实输入能暴露衰减问题。微调可部分恢复能力但代价是预测技能下降，经典辨识在多数实测系统上仍占优。

Abstract: Covariate-aware time-series foundation models (TSFMs) promise training-free what-if answers for instrumented plants: the change in output that a different future input would cause. We test this on forced engineering systems with exact counterfactuals, comparing Chronos-2, TimesFM-2.5 and TabPFN-TS with classical system identification fitted to the same context. Through their default covariate interfaces, TimesFM-2.5 and TabPFN-TS are memoryless: the predicted effect of an input change is a same-time function of that change ($R^2 = 1.000$ for TimesFM-2.5). Chronos-2 identifies dynamics in context but attenuates them. Its predicted effect is 0.33-0.80 of the true effect, its recovered impulse response has the wrong shape, and its error on a one-degree-of-freedom oscillator levels off at 0.57 with 8192 context samples, where ARX fitted to 256 samples reaches 0.02. Context dither at inference lowers the what-if error on all six synthetic classes without training. A 26-minute fine-tune on synthetic forced systems restores the response magnitude (sensitivity 0.83-0.96) and outperforms structure-agnostic identification on Wiener-Hammerstein and a held-out friction class. A specialised in-context identifier trained on the same data comes close, so the forced-system data carry most of the gain. On three of four measured plants classical identification remains clearly better, and the fine-tuned model loses part of its univariate forecasting skill. Paired counterfactual inputs, together with shuffled future inputs on measured records, test two properties: whether the covariate interface can represent dynamics and whether the pretraining prior covers the plant's time scale. Only the counterfactual pairs expose the attenuation.

</details>


### [336] [Do LLMs Act on What They Know? From Partner Representations to Cooperative Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08129)
*Yuhwan Jeong,Jinnyeong Yang,Kuk-Jin Yoon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文在Hanabi衍生环境中研究LLM如何适应未知的沟通约定。发现意图约定比目标约定更易被线性探针解码，但接收决策与发送方约定不一致；提供外部计算的动作推荐比规则陈述更能提升合作，并区分了约定可解码性、对约定信息的敏感性与合作表现三个层面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 与陌生伙伴合作时，需要适应事先未知的沟通约定。论文关注LLM能否利用可获得的伙伴信息，做出正确的接收决策，以揭示从"信息可解码"到"合作行为"之间的鸿沟。

Method: 在受控的Hanabi衍生环境（脚本化提示生成、LLM控制接收决策、模型权重冻结）下测试八个LLM。使用线性探针检测意图约定与目标约定的解码准确性，对比以"通用规则"或"外部计算的动作推荐"两种形式呈现约定信息的效果；在Qwen3-8B上做匹配状态下的陈述反转案例研究，并进行激活迁移实验。

Result: 线性探针恢复意图约定的准确率显著高于目标约定，但接收选择并不总是与发送方约定一致。规则陈述带来模型相关且较小的合作变化，动作推荐平均收益更大。Qwen3-8B案例中，匹配状态反转显示对动作推荐的敏感性远高于规则陈述。来自oracle-action与非oracle提示复述供体的激活迁移可提升两类动作上的意图准确率，但所测替代方案无法可靠复现这些收益。

Conclusion: 研究区分了三个层面：约定可解码性、对约定信息的敏感性、合作表现。结果表明，即使合作者信息可被解码，LLM仍难以将其转化为接收决策，凸显将可用伙伴信息转化为实际行动的局限。

Abstract: Cooperation with unfamiliar partners requires adapting to communication conventions that are not known in advance. We study this problem in a controlled Hanabi-derived environment with scripted hint generation, LLM-controlled receiving decisions, and frozen model weights. Across eight LLMs, linear probes recover intent conventions substantially more accurately than target conventions, yet receiving choices do not consistently agree with the sender's convention. We compare probe-predicted and ground-truth conventions presented either as general rules or as externally computed action recommendations. Rule statements yield modest and model-dependent changes in cooperation, whereas action translation produces larger gains on average. In a Qwen3-8B case study, matched-state statement reversals reveal much greater sensitivity to action recommendations than to rule statements. Activation transfers from oracle-action and non-oracle hint-restatement donors improve intent accuracy on both action classes, but the tested alternatives do not reliably reproduce these benefits. Together, these results distinguish convention decodability, sensitivity to convention information, and cooperative performance, and highlight limitations in turning available partner information into receiving decisions.

</details>


### [337] [Mu-DisCoCat: A Variational Pipeline for Compositional Generalization on Quantum Processors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08131)
*Mina Abbaszadeh,Matilda Karabina Moore,Raem Haq,Martha Lewis,Mehrnoosh Sadrzadeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Mu-DisCoCat，一个多模态变分量子学习框架，用于在 DisCoCat 语义模型上实现组合概念泛化（CoCoGen），并在经典仿真与含噪量子硬件上验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合概念泛化（通过重组已学基元理解新情境）是 AI 的核心难题。DisCoCat 等组合语义模型通过将向量推广为张量来解决，但存在张量学习的扩展瓶颈。将其映射到变分量子电路（VQC）可缓解文本场景的瓶颈，但尚未扩展到 CoCoGen 所需的多模态情境。

Method: Mu-DisCoCat 框架：先由单物体图文对学习稳定的物体表示，再固定这些表示，用于学习多物体情境中物体间的关系；采用 Uhlmann 态保真度计算多模态电路表示间的重叠；在含噪量子仿真器（多种 IBM fake backends、IQM FakeAphrodite）及 IBM Marrakesh 量子处理器上，使用破坏性 SWAP 测试评估部署。

Result: 经典仿真中，模型的关系 OOD 准确率高于所评估的 CLIP 基线；在真实设备噪声下，硬件执行模型与仿真保真度保持强正相关，能可靠区分未见过的相似与不相似对。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了在变分量子电路上执行 CoCoGen 的框架，展示了近期量子硬件的可行应用场景。

Abstract: Achieving compositional concept generalization (CoCoGen), the ability to understand novel situations by recombining learned primitives, remains a fundamental challenge in artificial intelligence. Compositional semantic models such as Compositional Distributional Semantics (DisCoCat) offer solutions by generalising vectors to tensors, but suffer from scaling bottlenecks when learning the tensors. Mapping DisCoCat onto Variational Quantum Circuits (VQCs) resolves this limitation for text, yet the methodology has not been expanded to multimodal situations such as the ones involved in CoCoGen. This paper introduces Mu-DisCoCat: a multimodal variational quantum learning framework for DisCoCat that achieves CoCoGen. The framework first learns stable object representations from single-object image-text pairs, then fixes these and uses them to learn the relations between them in multi-object situations. In classical simulations, the model used Uhlmann state fidelity to compute the overlap between the multimodal circuit representations and achieved higher relational OOD accuracy than the evaluated CLIP baseline. Its deployment was evaluated using the destructive SWAP test across noisy quantum emulators, including a range of IBM fake backends, IQM FakeAphrodite, and the IBM Marrakesh quantum processor. Despite real-world device noise, the hardware-executed models maintained a strong positive correlation with simulated fidelities, reliably distinguishing unseen similar and dissimilar pairs. Our work establishes a framework for executing CoCoGen on VQCs, demonstrating a viable use case for near-term quantum hardware.

</details>


### [338] [Symphony for Text Generation: Benchmarking Clinical Note Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08161)
*Daniel Varab,Victor Petrén Bach Hansen,Asbjørn W. Helge,Kevin Pelgrims,Mathias Baltzersen,Adrian Young-San Roessler,Vanessa Klungtvedt,Maximilian Brand,Lasse Krogsbøll,Henrik Cullen,Lars Maaløe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MedConv多语言临床数据集（英/丹/德300例），并构建结合蕴含指标与LLM成对比较的临床评估框架，比较Corti平台与两款通用AI临床记录软件的笔记质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境文档系统快速普及，但其对临床笔记质量的影响尚缺乏充分刻画，需要可复现的评估方法与数据集来对比不同系统。

Method: 构建MedConv多语言数据集（300例临床问诊，覆盖英语、丹麦语、德语），并结合ACI-BENCH基准；设计受控临床评估框架，融合蕴含指标与基于LLM的成对比较，采用PDSQI-9中的八个维度进行评分。

Result: Corti基于API的文本生成基础设施与领先商业记录软件持平或更优；其可配置API能针对具体文档用例灵活微调质量维度。

Conclusion: 论文提供了评估方法学并公开数据集，支持未来对环境文档系统的可复现对比。

Abstract: Ambient documentation systems are rapidly gaining adoption, yet their impact on clinical note quality remains poorly characterized. We introduce MedConv, a multilingual dataset of 300 clinical encounters in English, Danish, and German, and use it alongside the Ambient Clinical Intelligence benchmark (ACI-BENCH) to compare Corti, a clinical AI platform, with two leading, accessible ambient scribe software applications built on general-purpose AI. We present a controlled clinical evaluation framework that combines entailment metrics with LLM-judged pairwise comparisons across eight dimensions adopted from PDSQI-9. Results show that Corti's API-based text-generation infrastructure is on par with or outperforms leading commercial scribes. We further show that Corti's configurable API provides the flexibility necessary to fine-tune quality dimensions for specific documentation use cases. We present the evaluation methodology and release a dataset to support future reproducible comparison of ambient documentation systems.

</details>


### [339] [Align, Then Correct: Training-Free Two-Stage Low-Rank Compensation for Extremely Quantized Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08164)
*Seobin Song,Geonho Lee,Janghwan Lee,Jungwook Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两阶段闭式框架（LQEC改进），通过Fisher加权非对称目标与秩约束自然梯度步骤，显著提升低比特权重量化的补偿精度，无需训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低秩量化误差补偿器存在两个共同简化：(1)对称校准使补偿目标本质高秩，固定秩预算只能捕获小部分；(2)仅最小化二阶损失项，但补偿模型非平稳，仍残留一阶下降方向且无法被重建目标吸收。

Method: 两阶段闭式框架：阶段1在Fisher加权非对称目标下对齐每层输出与全精度模型，将秩预算集中于可压缩目标；阶段2在补偿模型上重新测量统计量，施加秩约束自然梯度步骤吸收剩余一阶信号。所有适配器均由单次截断SVD得到，反向传播仅用于收集统计量。

Result: QuIP#下2比特：Qwen3-8B的WikiText-2困惑度从12.43降至10.26，Qwen3-4B从21.11降至13.22。C4语料上分别恢复51%和84%的FP16差距（最强基线为31%和63%），并在七任务零样本平均、更高比特宽度及不同量化器下均有一致增益。

Conclusion: 通过消除对称校准与仅二阶优化的简化，该框架在无训练条件下显著缩小低比特量化与全精度的差距。

Abstract: Low-rank quantization error compensation (LQEC) recovers the accuracy lost under aggressive weight quantization by attaching a closed-form rank-$r$ adapter beside each frozen quantized weight, without any training. We show that existing compensators are limited by two shared simplifications. They calibrate symmetrically, evaluating the full-precision and compensated weights on the same activation, which yields a compensation target that is inherently high-rank -- so a fixed rank budget captures only a small fraction of it. And they minimize only the second-order term of the loss, although the compensated model is not stationary: a first-order descent direction larger than the applied compensation itself remains in every layer, and no reconstruction objective can absorb it. We propose a two-stage closed-form framework that removes both simplifications. Stage 1 aligns each layer's output with the full-precision model under a Fisher-weighted asymmetric objective, concentrating the rank budget on a rank-compressible target. Stage 2 re-measures statistics on the compensated model and applies a rank-constrained natural-gradient step that absorbs the remaining first-order signal. Every adapter is the result of a single truncated SVD; backward passes serve only to collect statistics. At 2 bits under QuIP#, our method reduces WikiText-2 perplexity from 12.43 to 10.26 on Qwen3-8B and from 21.11 to 13.22 on Qwen3-4B. On the held-out C4 corpus, it recovers 51% and 84% of the gap to FP16, versus 31% and 63% for the strongest baseline, with consistent gains in the seven-task zero-shot average, at higher bit-widths, and under a distinct quantizer.

</details>


### [340] [On the Intrinsic Limited Robustness of Latent-Based Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08178)
*Cheng-Han Yeh,Kuan-chun Yu,Cheng-Chang Tsai,Chun-Shien Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次从理论上分析了基于潜空间的扩散模型水印方法为何缺乏对扰动的鲁棒性，并通过实验验证了其固有的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于潜空间的扩散模型水印方法高估了其对抗图像失真（如旋转、缩放、平移等几何变换）的鲁棒性，且该范式因水印嵌入域而存在固有局限，但此前缺乏对此的理论解释。

Method: 通过放松不变关系，推导出刻画像素空间扰动与潜空间对应效应之间关系的最大扰动界；并提出首个能捕捉实际检测机制所有组成部分的解析公式；最后通过实验验证理论发现。

Result: 理论与实证结果表明，在当前设计范式下，基于潜空间的水印方法本质上鲁棒性有限。

Conclusion: 作者提供了分析工具与设计指南，供未来研究参考改进。

Abstract: Existing latent-based watermarking methods for diffusion models have overestimated their robustness to image distortions, including geometric transformations such as rotation, scaling, and translation (RST). Moreover, this paradigm of watermarking approaches may suffer from inherent limitations arising from the domain in which the watermark is embedded. In this paper, we provide the first theoretical analysis explaining why these methods lack invariance to perturbations. By relaxing the invariant relation, we derive a maximum perturbation bound that characterizes the relationship between pixel-space perturbations and their corresponding effects in latent space. In addition, we present the first analytical formulation that captures all components of practical detection mechanisms. Finally, we conduct experiments to validate the theoretical findings and the limitations of latent-based watermarking methods. Our theoretical and empirical results indicate that, under the current design paradigm, latent-based watermarking methods intrinsically exhibit limited robustness. We conclude by providing the analytical tool and design guidelines that future research could follow.

</details>


### [341] [Finding the Heads and the Neurons Responsible for Network Information Retrieval in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08200)
*Abdul Kadir,Md Mohasin Hossain,Daniel Sonntag*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究语言模型中对网络基础设施信息（主机名与IP地址配对）的识别是否由特定注意力头乃至头内特定神经元负责，并通过因果消融验证。结果显示：头级别识别在所有5个模型中均成立（1-9个头即可达99.5-100%准确率），但责任是否集中于单个神经元因模型而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望超越相关性分析，以因果方式验证语言模型中特定注意力头及头内特定神经元是否负责识别网络基础设施信息，探究模型内部机制的定位粒度问题。

Method: 采用因果消融筛选（causal ablation screening）找出关键注意力头，并用匹配的负样本和无上下文对照测试其选择性；随后通过因果干预与相关性排序寻找头内关键神经元；最后在另一机构独立收集的数据集上验证跨数据集迁移能力。

Result: 在5个模型（3种架构）中，每模型仅1-9个头即可支撑99.5-100%留出准确率的检测器。责任是否集中于单个神经元因模型而异：一个模型中信号集中于单个神经元（AUC=1.000，因果与相关排序完全一致），另一模型中头部整体有效（AUC=1.000）但最佳因果神经元无效（AUC=0.665），其余三模型介于两者之间。独立数据集上，所有模型的全头检测器100%正确标记正样本，但单神经元版本迁移性较差，有一个模型甚至低于随机水平。

Conclusion: 针对特定网络信息实体的因果头定位在跨模型和跨架构上均可行；但该发现能否下沉到单个神经元则因模型而异，需逐模型检查。

Abstract: We ask whether specific attention heads, and more finely specific neurons inside those heads, are responsible for recognizing that a language model's context contains network infrastructure information (a hostname paired with its IP address), and whether that responsibility can be validated causally rather than by correlation alone. At the head level the answer is yes, across five models spanning three architecture families: in every model, a small set of heads (1 to 9 out of 128 to 1152 candidates), found by causal ablation screening and tested for selectivity against matched negative and context-free controls, supports a detector with 99.5--100\% held-out accuracy. We then ask whether a head's responsibility concentrates into one neuron or stays spread across its dimensions; this is model-specific. In one model, the top head's signal concentrates into a single neuron, found independently by both a causal intervention and a correlational ranking, which agree exactly (AUC = 1.000, matching the full head). In another, the single clean head works as a whole (AUC = 1.000) but the best causally ranked neuron inside it does not (AUC = 0.665), so the responsibility there is spread across the head. The remaining three models fall in between. On an independent dataset collected by a different institution (reverse-DNS records rather than the discovery data), every model's full-head detector flags 100\% of positive records; the single-neuron versions transfer less reliably, and in one model score below chance. Causal head-finding for a specific network-information entity works across models and architectures; how far that finding can be pushed down to individual neurons varies, and needs to be checked for each model.

</details>


### [342] [Reinforcement Learning with Segment Reward Feedback under Linear Function Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08271)
*Fengxu Liu,Siwei Wang,Gal Dalal,Shie Mannor,Yihan Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究线性函数逼近下带分段奖励反馈的强化学习，揭示分段粒度对学习的影响，并针对已知/未知转移及不同反馈类型设计了多种算法与下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典RL假设每个状态-动作对都能观测奖励，但实际应用（如自动驾驶）中细粒度反馈昂贵难获取，而轨迹级反馈又过于稀疏，需要一种介于两者之间的通用反馈模型，并处理大状态空间。

Method: 在线性函数逼近下研究分段奖励反馈RL：对已知转移的等长分段，设计$\bitssegd$和$\edlinucbsegd$（分别对应二值和求和反馈），采用后验采样加规划保证计算效率、E-最优实验设计达到近最优；对未知转移，提出统一的$\seglsvits$框架含两种实例；还设计支持任意分段的$\uneqsegbitsd$算法。

Result: 建立了与上界近乎匹配的下界。发现：二值反馈下增加分段数可通过指数因子显著降低遗憾，而求和反馈下分段粒度对学习影响不大。在椭圆势分析下，状态-动作特征对遗憾的影响仅体现在对数因子中，等长分段表现最佳。

Conclusion: 分段反馈的粒度与分段方式对学习有根本性影响：二值反馈下细分段收益显著（指数级），求和反馈下则影响有限；等长分段在通常分析下达到最优性能。

Abstract: Classical reinforcement learning (RL) assumes that a reward is observed for every visited state-action pair. However, in real-world applications such as autonomous driving, such fine-grained feedback can be costly or difficult to collect, whereas trajectory-level feedback may be too sparse for efficient learning. To provide a general feedback model bridging these two extremes and handle large state spaces, we study RL with segment reward feedback under linear function approximation. Our work answers how the granularity of segment feedback and the choice of segmentation influence learning. For equal-length segments with known transitions, we design algorithms $\bitssegd$ and $\edlinucbsegd$ for binary and sum feedback types, respectively. They adopt posterior sampling with planning to achieve computational efficiency and the E-optimal experimental design to attain near-optimality. Nearly matching lower bounds are established. For equal-length segments with unknown transitions, we develop a unified $\seglsvits$ framework with two instantiations for binary and sum feedback, which carefully integrates the posterior estimated reward parameters into least-squares value iteration. These results reveal a fundamental insight: under binary feedback, increasing the number of segments significantly reduces the regret through an exponential factor, while surprisingly, under sum feedback, the granularity of segments does not affect learning much. Finally, to investigate whether segmenting according to state-action features can further expedite learning, we design an algorithm $\uneqsegbitsd$ that allows arbitrary segmentations. The resulting regret bound shows that under the usual elliptical potential analysis, the influence of state-action features on the regret appears only through logarithmic factors, and equal segmentation achieves the best performance.

</details>


### [343] [Performative Prediction with Selective Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08272)
*Giovani Valdrighi,Isabel Valera,Marcos Medeiros Raimundo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究带选择性标签的表演性预测，指出仅在观测数据上重训会破坏稳定解收敛保证，并提出基于置信区间的最坏情况目标以保持有界偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表演性预测中，既有分析通常假设模型部署后可获得特征与标签的完整分布，但现实中常存在选择性标签问题，即只能观测到被接受子群的标签。这会误导重训过程，削弱收敛到稳定解的保证。

Method: 作者形式化定义带选择性标签的表演性预测，并提出一个基于正标签概率置信区间的最坏情况目标，将重复风险最小化应用于该目标；在条件标签分布满足敏感性假设时，利用既往被接受数据随时间收紧置信区间。

Result: 理论上，所提方法能保持与真实稳定点之间有界距离。在带公平正则化的借贷应用实验中，该鲁棒优化方法的表现接近拥有完整标签访问的RRM。

Conclusion: 选择性标签会破坏普通重训的稳定性保证；通过鲁棒的最坏情况优化并动态收紧置信区间，可在标签不完整条件下获得接近完整标签场景的性能。

Abstract: Many social applications of machine learning exhibit performative effects: population behavior changes in response to deployed models. Performative prediction studies this interaction through a distribution map that relates each model to the population distribution it induces. One of the main results in this framework showed that repeated risk minimization (RRM), which updates models by retraining on the most recent data, can converge to a stable model that minimizes risk on its own induced distribution. However, existing analyses typically assume access to the complete distributions of features and labels after model deployment, ignoring the possibility of selective labels: observing labels only for the accepted subset of the population. In this work, we formalize performative prediction with selective labels and show that retraining only on observed data can misguide the retraining procedure and undermine the guarantees of convergence to a stable solution. We then propose a worst-case objective based on knowledge of a confidence interval on the probability of a positive label. Applying RRM to this objective permits us to remain within a bounded distance to the true stable point. Under a sensitivity assumption on the conditional label distribution, we further show how previously accepted data can tighten these confidence intervals over time. Experiments in a lending application with fairness regularization show that our robust optimization approach closely matches the performance of RRM with complete label access.

</details>


### [344] [Scalable extraction and visualization of multi-attribute logical and functional dependencies in tabular data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08287)
*Chaithra Umesh,Arvind Lomrore,Neethu D,Kristian Seegel-Schultz,Saptarshi Bej,Olaf Wolkenhauer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LDTool与HLDTool，用于从表格数据中提取和可视化多属性逻辑依赖（LD）与函数依赖（FD），并通过超图引导的搜索空间缩减提升可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据中属性间的结构关系对机器学习与模式识别至关重要。虽然函数依赖（FD）发现已有大量研究，但在属性数量和依赖阶数增加时，逻辑依赖（LD）的可扩展发现仍缺乏探索；这些依赖刻画了成对或多属性间非确定性、条件特定的关系。此外，现有方法缺乏统一框架来同时提取多属性LD和FD。

Method: 提出LDTool和HLDTool两个工具，用于从表格数据中提取并可视化多属性LD和FD。LDTool将依赖发现从成对关系扩展到多属性关系；HLDTool通过超图引导的搜索空间缩减实现可扩展的依赖提取。

Result: 在3个模拟数据集和11个真实数据集上的实验表明，该框架能提取有意义的LD和FD并提升可扩展性。LDTool在高维特征空间中能以更低运行时间恢复与现有FD发现方法相同的FD；HLDTool则支持具有数百个特征的数据集上的依赖发现。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了依赖结构的可解释可视化，并支持探索性数据分析以及合成表格数据的定量评估等应用。

Abstract: Understanding the structural relationships among attributes in tabular data is fundamental to machine learning and pattern recognition. While functional dependency (FD) discovery has been extensively studied, scalable discovery of logical dependencies (LDs), particularly as the number of attributes and dependency order increase, remains underexplored. These dependencies capture non-deterministic, condition-specific relationships among pairwise or multiple attributes. Furthermore, existing approaches do not provide a unified framework for extracting multi-attribute LDs and FDs. To address these limitations, we propose LDTool and HLDTool for extracting and visualizing multi-attribute LDs and FDs from tabular data. LDTool extends dependency discovery beyond pairwise relationships, while HLDTool enables scalable extraction through hypergraph-guided search-space reduction. Experiments on three simulated and eleven real-world datasets demonstrate that the proposed framework extracts meaningful LDs and FDs while improving scalability. LDTool recovers the same FDs as existing FD discovery methods with lower runtime in high-dimensional feature spaces, whereas HLDTool enables dependency discovery in datasets with hundreds of features. The proposed framework provides interpretable visualizations of dependency structures and supports applications in exploratory data analysis and the quantitative evaluation of synthetic tabular data.

</details>


### [345] [OxiGen: Oxidation-State-Aware Crystal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08296)
*Dylan John,Kim E. Jelfs,Alex M. Ganose,Eleonora Giunchiglia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OxiGen是一种氧化态感知的晶体扩散模型，在生成过程中显式表示氧化态，通过结构化输出层与有限状态自动机的精确推断从构造上保证整体电荷中性，显著提升氧化态保真度，并在稳定、独特、新颖晶体生成率上取得最佳表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型可加速无机材料发现，实现逆向设计，但生成实验上可实现的晶体仍具挑战性。氧化态常用于评估晶体的成分有效性并指导材料发现。现有晶体生成模型虽能生成电荷中性氧化态分配的材料，却难以复现合成材料中观察到的氧化态分布。

Method: 提出OxiGen，一种氧化态感知的晶体扩散模型，在生成过程中显式表示氧化态，并借助结构化输出层，通过有限状态自动机上的精确推断，从构造上强制满足整体电荷中性。

Result: 实验表明，OxiGen大幅提升氧化态保真度，在所评估方法中生成稳定、独特且新颖晶体的比率最高，即便在性质条件约束下仍保持高成分有效性。

Conclusion: OxiGen通过在生成过程中显式建模氧化态并强制电荷中性，有效弥补了现有方法在氧化态分布复现上的不足，为无机材料的逆向设计提供了更可靠的途径。

Abstract: Generative models have the potential to accelerate inorganic materials discovery by enabling inverse design, but generating experimentally realisable crystals remains challenging. Oxidation states are widely used to assess the compositional validity of crystals and guide inorganic materials discovery. While existing generative models for crystals can generate materials with charge-neutral oxidation-state assignments, they poorly reproduce the distributions of oxidation states observed in synthesised materials. To address this limitation, we propose OxiGen, an oxidation-state-aware crystal diffusion model that explicitly represents oxidation states during generation. OxiGen enforces global charge neutrality by construction using a structured output layer with exact inference over a finite-state automaton. Empirically, OxiGen substantially improves oxidation-state fidelity, generates the highest rate of stable, unique, and novel crystals among evaluated methods, and maintains high compositional validity even under property conditioning.

</details>
