### [346] [Structure-Aware Graph Abstention for Reliable Selective Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08322)
*Jianxiang Xie,Belal Alsinglawi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将选择性预测中的可靠性拆分为"实例级合理性"与"关系一致性"两个正交维度，提出用可学习稀疏图和Dirichlet型结构能量E_struct来量化后者，并以此构建结构门控（structural gating），在多个长时程预测基准上实现了在相同覆盖率下比TEM更低的选择性MSE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有选择性预测门控（如TEM）以整体方式为每条预测打分，对多变量输出而言，轨迹可能看似合理却违反变量间的依赖关系，因此单一的实例级评分不足以保证可靠性，需要显式建模跨变量结构一致性作为互补的弃权信号。

Method: 把实例级合理性与关系一致性视为两条独立的可靠性轴；用学习到的稀疏图表示变量间关系，定义Dirichlet风格的结构能量E_struct，并通过误差加权图正则化与分数-误差对齐进行训练，最终以结构一致性作为门控依据。

Result: 在7个长时程基准和4种骨干网络上，结构门控在匹配覆盖率下常能降低选择性MSE（相对TEM），在跨变量结构信息更丰富的基准上增益最大；但增益并非普遍存在，说明结构信号是互补性的弃权依据。表1为Protocol A排名诊断（seed 2024），表3为对齐子集上三seed的可部署Protocol B，完整验证-测试网格见附录A。

Conclusion: 关系一致性构成独立于实例级合理性的可靠维度，结构门控可作为互补弃权信号与现有门控结合；其收益依赖于数据中跨变量结构的可用性，尚非通用的性能提升手段。

Abstract: Selective forecasting abstains on high-risk test windows under a retained-coverage budget. Existing gates such as TEM (Brusokas et al., 2025) score each forecast as a whole; for multivariate outputs, trajectories can look plausible while violating dependencies among variables. We treat instance-level plausibility and relational consistency as distinct reliability axes and operationalize the latter via a learned sparse graph and a Dirichlet-style structural energy E_struct, trained with error-weighted graph regularization and score-error alignment. On seven long-horizon benchmarks and four backbones, structural gating often reduces selective MSE versus TEM at matched coverage, with the largest gains where cross-variable structure appears more informative in our benchmarks; gains are not universal, indicating a complementary abstention signal. Table 1 is a Protocol A ranking diagnostic (seed 2024); three-seed deployable Protocol B on an aligned subset is in Table 3 (full validation-to-test grids: Appendix A).

</details>


### [347] [Uncertainty Quantification Is Indispensable for Reliable Connectome-Based Graph Learning: A Narrative Review and Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08353)
*Mansooreh Pakravan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了面向连接组图学习的不确定性量化(UQ)框架，并通过案例研究证明未校准预测的风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN在连接组诊断分类中表现良好，但确定性模型会压制噪声与模型歧义，产生过度自信的预测；而UQ虽在体素级分割中广泛应用，在连接组图学习中仍未被充分探讨。

Method: 采用叙事性综述，梳理神经影像流程中偶然不确定性与认知不确定性的来源，回顾贝叶斯近似、集成方法、证据学习与共形预测等UQ范式；并以SUDMEX CONN数据集的动态功能连接矩阵训练时序图注意力网络(GAT)进行案例研究，通过Monte Carlo dropout进行事后不确定性审计。

Result: GAT对可卡因使用障碍的诊断准确率达80.0%（F1=0.794），但校准严重不足（ECE=0.127），被误分类的受试者预测置信度高达95%，呈现判别力与校准之间的"置信悖论"。

Conclusion: 严格的UQ、校准与选择性预测机制对于在临床神经科学中部署可信的图基生物标志物不可或缺。

Abstract: While graph neural networks (GNNs) have shown substantial promise in connectome-based diagnostic classification, deterministic models inevitably suppress pipeline-induced noise and model ambiguities, yielding overconfident predictions. Although uncertainty quantification (UQ) is widely adopted in voxel-level segmentation, its role in connectomic graph learning remains largely unaddressed. This paper presents a comprehensive narrative review of UQ frameworks tailored to connectome graph learning alongside an empirical case study demonstrating the perils of uncalibrated predictions. We delineate sources of aleatoric and epistemic uncertainty across neuroimaging pipelines and review prominent UQ paradigms, from Bayesian approximations and ensemble methods to evidential learning and conformal prediction. In our case study, a temporal Graph Attention Network (GAT) trained on dynamic functional connectivity (dFC) matrices from the SUDMEX CONN dataset achieves 80.0% diagnostic accuracy (F1 = 0.794) for Cocaine Use Disorder. However, a post-hoc uncertainty audit via Monte Carlo dropout reveals severe overconfidence (ECE = 0.127), with misclassified subjects assigned prediction confidences up to 95%. This empirical divergence between discrimination and calibration underscores the confidence paradox in deep connectomics. Our findings establish that rigorous UQ, calibration, and selective prediction mechanisms are indispensable for deploying trustworthy graph-based biomarkers in clinical neuroscience.

</details>


### [348] [Sensor Geometry as a Flow-Matching Prior for Multi-Channel Brain Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08355)
*Jaedong Hwang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出用基于传感器坐标的k近邻图与图-Matérn协方差替代各向同性高斯源，作为流匹配生成模型的起始分布；无需额外可学习参数，在八个EEG数据集和四种流匹配方法上普遍降低频谱差异（PSD-KL），并可推广至MEG、颅内EEG和交通传感器网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多通道脑记录（如EEG）中，电极位置固定，颅骨与头皮的容积传导导致邻近电极产生跨被试共享的协变结构。现有EEG生成模型通常仍从各向同性高斯噪声出发，让网络从零学习该空间结构，未利用这一先验知识。

Method: 仅利用传感器坐标构建k近邻图，取其拉普拉斯矩阵的图-Matérn函数作为源协方差，使流匹配从空间相干模式而非通道独立噪声开始。该方法不增加可学习参数，兼容任意耦合与漂移网络，并在所有数据集上使用相同的三个超参数。通过随机化空间特征向量但保留特征值谱的对照实验验证增益来源。

Result: 在八个EEG数据集和四种流匹配方法上，图-Matérn源在多数数据集上降低了五个临床频段的生成信号与真实信号的频谱差异（PSD-KL）。按数据集几何平均，PSD-KL下降12%至17%（取决于方法），在电极最密集的PhysioNet-MI上最高下降40%。随机化特征向量会消除该增益；直接拟合经验数据协方差的先验反而不如各向同性噪声。该构造同样适用于MEG、患者特异性网格的颅内EEG及交通传感器网络，且在每个网络、每种方法上均降低PSD-KL。

Conclusion: 将已知的空间结构嵌入流匹配的源分布，而非让网络从头学习，是一种零参数、通用且有效的改进方式；增益确证来自传感器位置局部图的空间特征向量。该构造可跨模态与跨领域迁移，为脑记录等具有固定空间布局的生成建模提供了简单而稳健的先验注入方案。

Abstract: Flow-matching models start from an isotropic Gaussian source, the standard choice when the correlation structure of the data is unknown in advance. For multi-channel brain recordings, however, part of this structure is known in advance. Electrodes sit at fixed positions on the head, and volume conduction through the skull and scalp makes nearby electrodes co-vary in a way that is shared across subjects. Existing EEG generative models nonetheless leave the network to learn this from scratch. We put this structure into the source instead. From the sensor coordinates alone, we build a k-nearest-neighbor graph and take a graph-Matérn function of its Laplacian as the source covariance, so the flow starts from spatially coherent patterns rather than channel-independent noise. The change adds no learned parameters, works with any coupling and any drift network, and uses the same three hyperparameters on every dataset. Across eight EEG datasets and four flow-matching methods, the graph-Matérn source lowers the spectral discrepancy between generated and real signals in the five clinical bands (PSD-KL) on most datasets. PSD-KL falls by 12% to 17% in geometric mean over datasets depending on the method and by up to 40% on PhysioNet-MI, the densest montage. We show that the improvement stems from the spatial eigenvectors of the local graph of sensor positions, since randomizing the eigenvectors while preserving the eigenvalue spectrum eliminates the gain. Furthermore, a prior fitted directly to the empirical data covariance performs worse than isotropic noise. The same construction applies unchanged to MEG, intracranial EEG with patient-specific grids, and a traffic-sensor network, lowering PSD-KL for every method on each. https://jd730.github.io/projects/GraphPrior

</details>


### [349] [Evolutionary One-Step Generators: Fast and Diverse Sampling for Discrete Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08367)
*Marcus Vukojevic,Erik Nielsen,Veronica Lachi,Andrea Passerini,Giovanni Iacca*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EGO框架，用一步推理的进化式生成器直接在离散输出上训练，兼顾可行性与多样性，在分子生成等任务上大幅提升有效且唯一的候选产出效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散设计任务（如分子发现）需要以低计算成本获得多样化且有用的候选集合。仅高有效性不足以保证候选库有用，因为反复生成相同结构会减少可选方案；而许多相关准则只能在硬解码后评估，导致同时训练可行性与多样性十分困难。

Method: EGO（Evolutionary Generators with One-step inference）：直接在离散输出上训练紧凑生成器，将分布匹配与结构约束结合，并可选地引入多样性或依赖历史的奖励；采用对偶低秩进化策略，无需针对特定准则的可微代理模型。训练完成后，生成器通过单次神经网络评估即可生成完整图结构。

Result: 在分子生成基准上，每估计稠密操作的有效且唯一产出相比近期一步流映射基线提升超过50倍，同时保持高化学有效性。在骨架补全任务中，相比MoLeR生成SMILES观察到44.3倍加速，并在相同时间预算下产生约10倍的通过过滤的候选。在NAS-Bench-101上，EGO产生的留出精英架构数量是松弛梯度训练的1.54倍。

Conclusion: 低生成成本有望支持离散设计任务中的实时候选生成，促进受限设计空间的交互式探索以及下游评估候选集的快速构建。

Abstract: Several discrete design tasks, such as molecular discovery, require diverse collections of useful candidates at low computational cost. High validity alone does not guarantee a useful candidate library: repeatedly generating the same valid structures leaves few distinct alternatives. Training for both feasibility and diversity is challenging because many relevant criteria can only be evaluated after hard decoding. To address this challenge, we propose EGO (Evolutionary Generators with One-step inference), a framework for training compact generators directly on discrete outputs. The method combines distribution matching with structural constraints and optional diversity or history-dependent rewards, using antithetic low-rank evolution strategies without requiring criterion-specific differentiable surrogates. Once trained, the generator produces the entire graph in a single neural-network evaluation. On molecular generation benchmarks, our compact generator achieves over $50\times$ the valid-and-unique yield per estimated dense operation compared to recent one-step flow-map baselines while retaining high chemical validity. In scaffold completion, EGO achieves an observed $44.3\times$ speedup over MoLeR in generation to SMILES and produces approximately $10\times$ as many filter-passing proposals within matched time budgets for generation and screening. Beyond chemistry, EGO produces $1.54\times$ as many distinct held-out elite architectures as relaxed gradient training on NAS-Bench-101. The low generation cost may enable real-time candidate generation across discrete design tasks, supporting interactive exploration of constrained design spaces and rapid construction of candidate sets for downstream evaluation.

</details>


### [350] [Accelerating the Development of PLGA In Situ Forming Depots Through AI-Driven Multi-Objective Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08368)
*Pauric Bannigan,Siddarth Chandrasekaran,Brigitte A. G. Lamers,Inge Hermsen,Gary Tom,Riley J. Hickman,Bahar Yeniad,Morgan Fox,Christine Allen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Corbion与Intrepid合作，将PURASORB生物可吸收聚合物库与AI算法ANDROMEDA 1结合，约15周内制备并表征181种原位成型储库制剂，筛选出4个先导候选，实现长效注射剂的多目标快速优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长效注射制剂开发需同时优化载药量、释放动力学、黏度、可注射性、稳定性等多个目标，这一多维设计空间难以通过传统方法高效探索，因此需要将多样化的聚合物材料库与AI驱动的优化方法结合，以加速候选处方的发现并聚焦开发空间。

Method: 将Corbion的PURASORB生物可吸收聚合物库（覆盖广泛分子量范围，含商业化牌号与在研新聚合物）与Intrepid Labs的专有AI算法ANDROMEDA 1结合，针对一种治疗性肽开发原位成型储库。约15周内制备并表征181种独特处方，载药量为6–12% w/w，采用宽域设计空间映射与定向多目标优化相结合的策略。

Result: 识别出4个先导候选处方，载药量分别为6%、9%和12% w/w。每个候选均满足预设的黏度和可注射性标准，同时提供各具特色的30天体外释放曲线。ANDROMEDA 1发现中等分子量聚合物在缓释性能与溶液黏度之间取得良好平衡。

Conclusion: 整合的聚合物专业知识与AI驱动优化能够快速识别差异化的制剂候选、聚焦开发空间，并为后续优化与体内评价建立坚实的数据驱动基础，展示了该联合方法在长效注射制剂开发中的高效性。

Abstract: Developing long-acting injectable formulations requires the simultaneous optimization of drug loading, release kinetics, viscosity, injectability, stability and other objectives. To navigate this multidimensional space, Corbion and Intrepid combined Corbion's diverse PURASORB bioresorbable polymer library with Intrepid Labs' proprietary AI algorithm (ANDROMEDA 1) to develop in situ forming depots for a therapeutic peptide. Over approximately 15 weeks, 181 unique formulations spanning drug loadings of 6-12% w/w were prepared and characterized through broad design-space mapping and targeted multi-objective optimization. Four lead candidate formulations were identified at 6%, 9%, and 12% w/w drug loading. Each met the predefined viscosity and injectability criteria while providing distinct 30-day in vitro release profiles. The study evaluated polymers spanning a broad range of molecular weights, including commercially available PURASORB grades and new polymers under development by Corbion to expand its polymer toolbox. ANDROMEDA 1 identified that polymers with intermediate molecular weights provided a favorable balance between sustained release and solution viscosity. Together, these findings demonstrate how integrated polymer expertise and AI-driven optimization can rapidly identify differentiated formulation candidates, focus the development space, and establish a strong data-driven foundation for further optimization and in vivo evaluation.

</details>


### [351] [Decision-Focused Learning in MDPs: An Occupancy Measure Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08384)
*Zihao Zhao,Ashwath K. Karunakaram,Ali Eshragh,Yuexing Li,Kai Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对决策聚焦学习（DFL）在马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）中可扩展性差的问题，提出基于占用测度的线性规划（LP）公式，并设计闭式梯度、增广拉格朗日代理、随机行草图与可学习软状态聚合层，在多个任务上以更低计算成本取得更低遗憾（regret）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 DFL 方法对 Bellman 方程的 KKT 条件求导，需要对所有状态-动作对求解线性系统，限制了可扩展性，尤其难以应对大规模或连续状态空间。

Method: 将 MDP 重新表述为基于占用测度的线性规划，其可行域由预测动态决定；通过枢轴算法识别可行多面体中的活跃约束，导出闭式梯度。针对活跃约束变化导致的梯度不连续以及反向代价随状态规模增长的问题，采用增广拉格朗日代理并对约束做随机行草图平滑边界跳变，同时引入可学习的软状态聚合层及其函数逼近泛化。

Result: 在多个任务上，该方法相比基于 KKT 的 DFL 与两阶段基线，取得更低的 regret，且计算成本显著降低。

Conclusion: 所提出的占用测度 LP 层及状态聚合机制有效提升了 DFL 的可扩展性与性能，适用于大规模有限及连续状态 MDP。

Abstract: In this work, we consider decision-focused learning (DFL) for a Markov decision process (MDP), where existing methods differentiate through the KKT conditions of the Bellman equation and require solving a linear system over all state-action pairs, limiting its scalability. We address this by reformulating the MDP as an occupancy measure-based linear program (LP), whose feasible region is induced by predicted dynamics, and we derive a closed-form gradient by identifying the active constraints in the feasible polyhedron via the pivoting algorithm. This occupancy measure-based LP layer raises two challenges: (1) LP's solution gradient is discontinuous when active constraints change, and (2) the LP backward cost still scales with the state size, which is costly for large or continuous state spaces. We address the challenges with an augmented Lagrangian surrogate and smooth the boundary jumps by random row sketching of the constraints, and a learnable soft state-aggregation layer and its function-approximation generalization that scales the LP to large finite and continuous-state MDPs. Across multiple tasks, our methods reach lower regret than KKT-based DFL and two-stage baselines with significantly lower computation cost. The source code for all experiments is available at https://github.com/A-Eshragh/State_Aggregation_Project.

</details>


### [352] [Atom-JEPA: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture for 3D Atomistic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08400)
*Kasper Helverskov Petersen,Rasmus Hannibal Tirsgaard,François R J Cornet,Mikkel Jordahn,Mikkel N. Schmidt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Atom-JEPA自监督预训练框架，通过原子级与子结构级联合嵌入预测目标从无标注3D结构中学习表征，在分子与晶体性质预测任务上取得领先或具竞争力的迁移性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模自监督预训练已在语言和视觉领域显著提升下游泛化能力，但原子尺度（分子、晶体）建模中自监督预训练尚未达到类似的下游泛化效果，因此需要针对3D结构数据设计有效的预训练范式。

Method: 提出Atom-JEPA框架，受联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）启发，在潜空间中进行预测式学习，通过互补的原子级与子结构级两个目标，从无标注3D结构数据中学习表征；在大规模分子和晶体数据集上进行预训练，随后在多样化的下游性质预测任务上微调评估迁移能力。

Result: 在分子ADMET预测和量子化学性质预测任务上达到当前最优（SOTA）性能，在晶体材料物理性质预测上表现极具竞争力，表明仅凭结构数据即可支撑广泛的下游泛化。

Conclusion: 潜空间预测式预训练能够仅依赖结构数据实现广泛的下游泛化，为原子尺度建模提供了有效的自监督预训练路径，代码与预训练权重已公开。

Abstract: Large-scale self-supervised pretraining has reshaped modern machine learning, substantially advancing the ability of language and vision models to generalize across downstream tasks. While deep learning has driven considerable progress in modeling atomistic systems in recent years, self-supervised pretraining in this domain has not yet achieved comparable downstream generalization. To address this, we introduce Atom-JEPA, a self-supervised pretraining framework that learns latent representations from unlabeled 3D structures through complementary atom-level and substructure-level objectives inspired by joint-embedding predictive architectures. We pretrain Atom-JEPA on large-scale molecular and crystalline datasets and evaluate its transfer performance by fine-tuning on a diverse set of downstream property prediction tasks. Atom-JEPA achieves state-of-the-art performance on molecular ADMET and quantum-chemical property prediction tasks, and is highly competitive in predicting the physical properties of crystalline materials. These results demonstrate the potential of latent-space predictive pretraining to support broad downstream generalization from structural data alone. Code and pretrained model checkpoints are publicly available at https://github.com/khelverskovp/atom-jepa

</details>


### [353] [VETTA: Coordinating Turn- and Token-Level Credit Assignment for Multi-Turn LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08402)
*Jiaju Chen,Min Yang,Jinghua Piao,Xiaochong Lan,Xu Xia,Xiangnan He,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出VETTA，通过共享轻量critic的分离头联合学习turn级与token级价值，并按层级协调用于PPO更新，在ALFWorld和WebShop上显著提升成功率，同时降低价值估计计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮LLM智能体面临稀疏任务反馈，且响应逐token生成，产生两个相关信用分配问题：哪些响应促成了结果，以及每个响应内部哪些生成决策重要。现有方法通常只关注一个层级：turn级方法评估完整响应但不区分内部决策，token级方法可跨轮传播反馈但未显式建模每个响应的信用。二者局限互补，因此需要同时在两个层级学习信用并在单次策略更新中协调。

Method: VETTA在共享的轻量critic上使用分离的头联合学习turn级和token级价值。它沿时间序列计算优势，并将每个turn优势与响应内中心化的token残差结合，用于PPO更新。为降低价值学习成本，critic仅保留用于初始化actor的预训练检查点中的早期Transformer块。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop两个挑战性智能体基准上，使用Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct时，VETTA相比PPO分别将成功率提升37.5%和22.3%；使用Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct时成功率分别达到95.5%和76.0%。critic深度对比进一步显示，在显著降低critic侧计算的情况下仍保持强任务表现。

Conclusion: 结果表明，紧凑的共享critic能够协调turn级和token级信用，在保持价值估计高效的同时提升智能体性能。代码已开源。

Abstract: Multi-turn LLM agents often receive sparse task feedback across several interactions, while generating each response token by token. This creates two related credit-assignment questions: which responses helped achieve the outcome, and which generation decisions mattered within each response? Existing methods typically focus on only one level: turn-level methods evaluate complete responses but do not distinguish the decisions within them; token-level methods can propagate feedback across turns but do not explicitly model credit for each response. These complementary limitations motivate learning credit at both levels and coordinating it in a single policy update. We introduce VETTA, a credit assignment method that jointly learns turn- and token-level values through separate heads on a shared lightweight critic. VETTA computes advantages along both temporal sequences and combines each turn advantage with a within-response-centered token residual for PPO updates. Furthermore, to reduce value-learning cost, the critic retains only early Transformer blocks from the pretrained checkpoint used to initialize the actor. On two challenging agent benchmarks, ALFWorld and WebShop, VETTA improves success rates over PPO by 37.5% and 22.3%, respectively, with Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct and achieves success rates of 95.5% and 76.0%, respectively, with Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct. Critic-depth comparisons further show strong task performance with substantially lower critic-side computation. These results suggest that a compact shared critic can coordinate turn- and token-level credit to improve agent performance while keeping value estimation efficient. Code is available at https://github.com/Jiaju-Chen/VETTA-official.

</details>


### [354] [SSR: Sparse Segment Reduction for Ternary GEMM Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08403)
*Adeline Pittet,Shien Zhu,Valérie Verdan,Gustavo Alonso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Sparse Segment Reduction (SSR) 三元矩阵乘法方法，通过专用数据格式与稀疏度自适应计算树，同时利用三元量化与稀疏性，加速三元大模型推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三元 LLM 虽能大幅压缩模型（50-90% 稀疏度），但现有方法存在局限：BitNet、RSR、RSR++ 等面向三元权重优化的方法未利用稀疏结构，而常规稀疏格式又忽视三元特性，无法同时获得"三元+稀疏"双重优化收益，导致推理效率未达最优。

Method: 提出 SSR（Sparse Segment Reduction）三元矩阵乘法方法，用于加速三元 LLM 及通用三元权重网络（TWNs）推理。其包含专用的优化三元数据格式，以及通过随稀疏度扩展的计算树来系统利用稀疏模式的算法。

Result: 理论上，当稀疏度高于 50% 时 SSR 推理渐近快于 RSR++；实测在所有稀疏度下均有提升。三元 GEMM 在 45-95% 稀疏度下相较 RSR++ 提速 2.1-11.3 倍；在 Llama-3 1B 模型推理中端到端提速 3.5-6.3 倍，并节省 4.9% 内存。

Conclusion: SSR 通过统一利用三元量化与稀疏结构，在理论与实测中均优于现有三元推理方法 RSR++，为资源受限硬件上部署三元 LLM 提供了高效方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) require substantial computational resources, limiting their deployment on resource-constrained hardware. Ternary LLMs mitigate these demands through weight quantization via ternary values, achieving significant compression often with 50-90% sparsity. However, existing approaches have limitations: methods optimized for ternary weights, such as BitNet, redundant segment reduction (RSR), and its improved version RSR++, do not exploit sparsity structures, while conventional sparse formats neglect ternary characteristics, foregoing dual optimization opportunities.
  In this paper, we introduce Sparse Segment Reduction (SSR), a ternary matrix multiplication method designed to accelerate the inference of ternary LLMs and general Ternary Weight Networks (TWNs). SSR has a dedicated optimized ternary data format and an algorithm that systematically exploits sparsity patterns through computation trees that scale with the sparsity. SSR provides theoretical gains with asymptotically faster inference than RSR++ for sparsity above 50%, while practical evaluations reveal performance improvements across all sparsity levels. Evaluation results show that SSR achieves 2.1-11.3x speedup over RSR++ on ternary GEMM with 45-95% sparsity. Furthermore, SSR achieves 3.5-6.3x end-to-end speedup and 4.9% of memory saving over RSR++ on the Llama-3 1B model inference.

</details>


### [355] [Symmetry-Aware Feature Learning: A Polynomial Separation for Multi-Index Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08420)
*Jivan Waber,Vanessa Piccolo,Yatin Dandi,Florent Krzakala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文证明了对称性感知与对称性无关特征学习之间的多项式样本复杂度分离：权重共享与全群数据增强均达到约 d^{p-1}（或 d）的样本复杂度，而无对称性学习需约 rd^{p-1}（或 rd）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究对称性结构（架构权重共享、数据增强）相较于无对称性学习在样本效率上的优势，并量化其差异。

Method: 研究 R^d 中高斯协变量下的增长秩多指标模型，教师方向 r=Θ(d^δ)、0<δ<1/2，构成循环对称轨道；比较对称绑定卷积网络、非绑定网络及全群数据增强的非绑定网络，使用球面在线 SGD 与相关损失，分析信息指数 p≥3 的多项式链接。

Result: 对 p≥3 的多项式链接，绑定与增强学习器以约 d^{p-1} 样本实现弱方向恢复，而无对称性学习器需约 rd^{p-1}；对纯二次 Hermite 链接，教师子空间弱恢复复杂度分别为约 d 与 rd。

Conclusion: 全群数据增强可匹配架构权重共享的样本效率，二者相对无对称性训练均有多项式优势；对 p≥3，证明揭示了先由初始化涨落选定轨道中一个方向、再经局部增长放大重叠的两阶段机制。

Abstract: We establish a polynomial sample complexity separation between symmetry-aware and symmetry-agnostic feature learning. We study growing-rank multi-index models with high-dimensional Gaussian covariates in $\mathbb{R}^d$ and $r=Θ(d^δ)$ teacher directions forming a cyclic symmetry orbit, where $0<δ<1/2$. We compare three ways of exploiting this structure: architectural weight sharing, data augmentation over the full symmetry group, and learning without access to the symmetry. In particular, we analyze a symmetry-tied convolutional network, an untied network, and the same untied network trained with full-group data augmentation, using spherical online SGD with correlation loss. For a class of polynomial links with information exponent $p\ge3$, we prove matching sample complexity bounds up to logarithmic factors: the tied and augmented learners achieve weak directional recovery in $\widetildeΘ(d^{p-1})$ samples, whereas the symmetry-agnostic learner requires $\widetildeΘ(rd^{p-1})$. For the pure quadratic Hermite link, the same separation holds for weak recovery of the teacher subspace, with sample complexities $\widetildeΘ(d)$ and $\widetildeΘ(rd)$, respectively. Thus, full-group data augmentation matches the sample efficiency of architectural weight sharing, and both provide a polynomial advantage over training without symmetry. For $p\ge3$, the proof reveals a two-stage mechanism: fluctuations at initialization select one direction in the teacher orbit, after which localized growth amplifies its overlap to the weak recovery scale while competing overlaps remain near their initialization scale.

</details>


### [356] [Climbing the Design Ladder: Sequential Knowledge Distillation for Early-Stage Circuit Timing Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08457)
*Reza Moravej,Fahad Rahman Amik,Zhanguang Zhang,Didier Chételat,Yingxue Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: STEP-KD利用中间设计阶段作为“垫脚石”，通过渐进式知识蒸馏，将后布线阶段的时序知识逐级传递到后综合阶段，从而在早期准确预测时序违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集成电路设计流程包含逻辑综合、布局规划、布局、布线等多个阶段，各阶段耗时数小时至数周。若时序违规在流程后期才被发现，就需要回退到早期阶段，造成大量计算资源浪费并延误产品上市。虽然后期时序可从早期数据预测以预防此类失败，但后综合的逻辑描述与后布线的物理布局之间存在巨大的抽象鸿沟，现有机器学习方法难以直接跨越。

Method: 提出STEP-KD（基于渐进式知识蒸馏的时序评估），不直接预测，而是利用中间设计阶段作为“垫脚石”进行渐进式知识迁移。具体地，分别在后布线、后布局、后规划阶段训练教师模型，再通过表示对齐（representation alignment），依次将其知识蒸馏到后综合阶段的学生模型。

Result: 在多种电路上的实验表明，STEP-KD相比直接蒸馏和有监督基线降低了时序预测误差，在多数设置下甚至优于工业标准的静态时序分析（STA）工具。STEP-KD将总负时序裕量（Total Negative Slack）预测的加权平均绝对百分比误差降至19.78%，而STA为74.84%。

Conclusion: STEP-KD是朝着更早识别时序问题、避免代价高昂的后期重新设计迈出的重要一步。通过渐进式知识蒸馏跨越抽象鸿沟，该方法能在设计早期提供更可靠的时序预测。

Abstract: Integrated circuit design involves multiple design stages: logic synthesis, floorplanning, placement, and routing, with each stage taking hours to weeks to complete. Discovering timing violations late in this flow forces costly iterations back to earlier stages, wasting computational resources and delaying product launches. While predicting post-routing timing from early-stage data could prevent these failures, existing machine learning approaches struggle with the massive abstraction gap between post-synthesis logical descriptions and post-routing physical layouts. We propose STEP-KD (Sequential Timing Evaluation via Progressive Knowledge Distillation), which leverages intermediate design stages as ``stepping stones'' for progressive knowledge transfer rather than attempting direct prediction. STEP-KD trains teacher models at the post-routing, post-placement, and post-floorplan stages, then sequentially distills their knowledge to a post-synthesis student model through representation alignment. Experiments on diverse circuits demonstrate that STEP-KD reduces timing prediction error compared to direct distillation and supervised baselines, and in most settings compared to the industry-standard Static Timing Analysis (STA) tool. STEP-KD reduces the weighted mean absolute percentage error of Total Negative Slack prediction to 19.78\%, compared with 74.84\% for STA. Our proposed method is step forward to identify timing problems earlier, avoiding expensive late-stage redesigns.

</details>


### [357] [Learning PDE solution operators with variable initial conditions via Latent Dynamics Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08475)
*Stefano Maria Pizzamiglio,Stefano Pagani,Francesco Regazzoni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文扩展了LDNet，使潜动力学网络能从不同初始条件出发预测PDE时空系统响应，采用自动解码与元学习两种潜状态初始化策略，实现分辨率无关的高效代理建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始LDNet假定固定初始条件，限制了其在需要从不同起始状态演化的真实多查询仿真场景中的适用性，本文旨在突破该限制。

Method: 保持原始LDNet端到端训练和无编码器特性，从小规模早期观测推断初始潜状态，将其视为适应问题，研究两种策略：自动解码形式与元学习（初始潜状态作为任务特定上下文变量）。

Result: 在平流扩散、流体动力学和固体力学等多种物理现象上验证了准确性；元学习显著加速潜状态推断，产生更平滑、条件更好的优化景观，并自发将潜空间组织成反映物理特征的结构化表示；基于坐标的解码器支持从空间下采样数据训练并在推理时恢复高分辨率解。

Conclusion: 所提方法为具有变化的初始条件的时间相关PDE多查询仿真提供了高效且与分辨率无关的代理建模框架。

Abstract: In many-query scenarios, data-driven surrogate models provide an efficient alternative to high-fidelity solvers for simulating physical systems governed by Partial Differential Equations (PDEs). In this context, the Latent Dynamics Network (LDNet) has recently demonstrated remarkable performance in predicting the response of spatio-temporal systems, combining Neural Ordinary Differential Equations with nonlinear dimensionality reduction. However, the original formulation assumes a fixed initial condition, limiting its applicability to many real-world applications where a system evolves from varying starting states. In this work, we overcome this limitation while keeping the end-to-end training procedure of the original LDNet and its encoder-free nature, which preserves its intrinsic independence from spatial resolution and grid topology. We infer the initial latent state directly from a small set of early-time observations, treating latent-state initialization as an adaptation problem, and investigate two strategies: an auto-decoding formulation and a meta-learning approach in which the initial latent state acts as a task-specific context variable. We demonstrate the accuracy of the proposed methods across diverse physical phenomena, spanning advection-diffusion, fluid dynamics, and solid mechanics. Meta-learning markedly accelerates latent-state inference and induces smoother, better-conditioned optimization landscapes, and spontaneously organizes the latent space into a structured representation that reflects physically meaningful features of the underlying dynamics. The coordinate-based decoder enables training from spatially subsampled data while recovering high-resolution solution fields at inference. The resulting approach provides an efficient and resolution-independent surrogate modeling framework for many-query simulations of time-dependent PDEs with varying initial conditions.

</details>


### [358] [MetaLearnNCA: Few-Shot Offline Meta-Learning via Interacting Neural Cellular Automata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08479)
*Etienne Guichard,Stefano Nichele*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MetaLearnNCA，一种通过耦合神经细胞自动机（NCA）的动态交互实现小样本自适应、且推理时无需计算梯度的去中心化框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统小样本元学习要么通过展开计算图进行解析梯度下降（推理时需昂贵的反向传播），要么基于扁平化一维特征向量做度量距离比较（丢弃了原生二维空间几何结构）。作者希望摆脱梯度依赖并保留2D空间信息。

Method: MetaLearnNCA将任务自适应分解为两部分：Active-NCA基于称为"空间程序"的连续2D空间记忆网格执行任务推断；Meta-NCA作为去中心化细胞优化器，通过在局部邻域扩散空间误差残差来动态更新该程序。整个推理过程不计算解析梯度。

Result: 在分布内Omniglot上达到96.12%，与经典元学习器相当；在MNIST、KMNIST、Fashion-MNIST的分布外迁移中，跨10个独立测试种子及1/5/10-shot设置均取得增益，例如10-shot MNIST上超过Prototypical Networks +10.54%，10-shot Fashion-MNIST上比FOMAML高+3.87%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，鲁棒且无梯度的"学会学习"可以从非冯·诺依曼基底上的去中心化细胞动力学中涌现。

Abstract: Few-shot meta-learning traditionally formulates task adaptation either as analytical gradient descent through unrolled computational graphs or as metric-based distance comparisons over flattened 1D fea- ture vectors, which either incur costly test-time backpropagation or discard native 2D spatial geometry. In this work, we propose METALEARNNCA, a decentralized framework that achieves few-shot adapta- tion through the dynamical interaction of coupled Neural Cellular Automata (NCAs) without computing analytical gradients during inference. MetaLearnNCA decomposes task adaptation into an Active- NCA, which executes task inference conditioned on a continuous 2D spatial memory grid termed the spatial program, and a learned Meta-NCA, which acts as a decentralized cellular optimizer by diffusing spatial error residuals across local neighborhoods to dynamically update this program. METALEARN- NCA is competitive against canonical meta-learners in-distribution (96.12% on Omniglot) with Out-Of- Distribution transfer gains on MNIST, KMNIST, and Fashion-MNIST transfer across 10 independent testing seeds across 1-, 5-, and 10-shot regimes (e.g., surpassing Prototypical Networks by +10.54% on 10-shot MNIST and a +3.87% gain on 10-shot Fashion-MNIST over FOMAML). Our results establish that robust, gradient-free learning-to-learn can emerge from decentralized cellular dynamics on non-von Neumann substrates.

</details>


### [359] [PHBA: Prefix-State Hybrid Block Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08527)
*Ruijie Li,Jiaxi Hu,Shiyu Wang,Yuxuan Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Prefix-State Hybrid Block Attention (PHBA)，用 top-k 块稀疏检索替代局部滑窗注意力，并将每个检索块与其前缀状态耦合，结合线性循环压缩与精确检索，配合硬件感知 Triton 实现，提升长上下文与检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合架构（如 NHA）在高效长上下文建模与精确 token 检索之间取得平衡，但其精确注意力被限制在固定的局部窗口内，难以覆盖长距离的精确证据。

Method: PHBA 以 top-k 块稀疏检索替换局部滑窗注意力；在块边界用门控线性循环构建紧凑的前缀状态，并将其与对应 token 块一起检索，使精确长程证据与压缩历史上下文在同一层内融合；同时开发硬件感知的 Triton 实现，流式处理路由的 token 块与前缀状态，避免生成大型中间张量。

Result: 实验表明，PHBA 在长上下文与检索任务上优于强线性与混合基线，同时保持高效的训练与推理。

Conclusion: 将块稀疏检索与前缀状态相结合，可在统一层内兼顾精确长距离检索与压缩历史建模，是一种兼顾性能与效率的混合注意力方案。

Abstract: Hybrid architectures combining linear sequence models with softmax attention provide an effective balance between efficient long-context modeling and precise token retrieval. Existing designs such as Native Hybrid Attention (NHA) combine compressed long-term states with sliding-window attention, but their exact attention is restricted to a fixed local window. In this work, we introduce Prefix-State Hybrid Block Attention (PHBA), which replaces local sliding-window attention with top-k block-sparse retrieval and couples each retrieved block with a compact prefix state summarizing its preceding context. The prefix states are constructed by a gated linear recurrence at block boundaries and retrieved together with the corresponding token blocks, allowing the model to combine precise long-range evidence with compressed historical context within a unified layer. We further develop a hardware-aware Triton implementation that streams routed token blocks and prefix states without materializing large intermediate tensors. Experiments show that PHBA improves long-context and retrieval performance over strong linear and hybrid baselines while retaining efficient training and inference.

</details>


### [360] [How Bregman Divergences Shape Shampoo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08534)
*Bing Liu,Wenjie Zhou,Chengcheng Zhao,Hongtao Zhang,Boao Kong,Felix Dangel,Wu Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出统一的Bregman散度框架，将Shampoo各类变体中的散度选择联系起来，并通过谱分析与GPT-2实验揭示散度选择如何影响预条件器对有限样本误差的补偿能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shampoo及其变体通过优化Frobenius或KL散度来学习预条件器，但散度选择如何影响预条件效果尚不明确，阻碍了进一步改进。作者希望建立统一视角来理解不同散度对应的Shampoo变体行为差异。

Method: 提出统一的Bregman散度框架，将现有流行散度纳入其中以便联合研究；对梯度二阶矩进行经验谱分析，考察散度选择如何塑造Kronecker近似，以及与预条件中有限样本误差的交互作用。

Result: 发现某些散度能更好地补偿经验二阶矩的有限样本低估问题，从而解释了相应Shampoo变体之间的行为差异；并通过GPT-2预训练实验验证了该解释。

Conclusion: 将散度选择与实际训练行为相联系，为理解Shampoo的理论基础及进一步改进提供了有原则的指导。

Abstract: Understanding the principles behind Shampoo has recently guided the development of more effective neural network optimizers. These methods learn a preconditioner by optimizing the Frobenius or Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence against the gradient second moment. In this work, we investigate how the choice of divergence shapes preconditioning, which remains unclear and blocks further improvements. To do so, we develop a unified Bregman divergence framework that connects all popular divergences, allowing us to study them jointly. Through empirical spectral analysis of gradient second moments, we examine how divergence choice shapes Kronecker approximation and interacts with finite-sample error in preconditioning. We find that some divergences can better compensate for finite-sample underestimation of the empirical second moment, helping explain the differing behavior of their corresponding Shampoo variants. We further validate this explanation through GPT-2 pretraining experiments. By connecting divergence choice to practical training behavior, we believe our framework provides principled guidance for understanding the foundations of, and further improving, Shampoo.

</details>


### [361] [FlowCF: Sparse Counterfactual Explanations for Mixed-Type Tabular Data using Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08537)
*Emmanouil Panagiotou,Eirini Ntoutsi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FlowCF，一种模型无关的生成式反事实解释方法，将反事实生成建模为从事实类到目标类的稀疏传输，通过流匹配与混合流算子求解，并利用门控网络优化稀疏性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实解释方法在稀疏性和邻近性上表现有限，尤其是针对数值特征时，无论是模型无关的摊销方法还是可完全访问模型的梯度方法，都存在不足。实用的反事实解释应尽可能少地改变特征，且改变幅度尽可能小。

Method: 提出FlowCF，将反事实生成视为从事实类到目标类的稀疏传输问题，使用流匹配（flow matching）求解该传输；为处理混合特征类型，提出新颖的混合流算子（mixed flow operator）；并利用所得到的几何结构，通过门控网络最小化传输所改变的特征数量，从而优化稀疏性。

Result: 在六个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，FlowCF实现了最优的数值稀疏性和邻近性：仅改变29%的数值特征（最优基线为89%），位移小70%，同时在其它评价指标上保持可比性能。

Conclusion: FlowCF作为模型无关的生成式方法，有效提升了反事实解释在数值特征上的稀疏性与邻近性，在多个基准数据集上优于现有方法，同时在其他期望属性上保持竞争力。

Abstract: In the field of Explainable AI (XAI), counterfactual (CF) explanations interpret a model's decision by suggesting the changes to the input that would lead to a more favourable outcome. To be useful in practice, such an explanation should change few features and change them as little as possible, properties known as sparsity and proximity. We observe that existing methods remain limited in this respect, especially for numerical features, whether they are model-agnostic and amortised, or gradient-based with full access to the model. In this paper, we propose FlowCF, a model-agnostic generative method that frames CF generation as sparse transport from the factual to the target class. We solve this transport with flow matching, which we extend to mixed feature types with a novel mixed flow operator, and exploit the resulting geometry to optimise for sparsity through a gating network that minimises the number of features the transport changes. Extensive experiments on six benchmark datasets demonstrate that FlowCF produces the best numerical sparsity and proximity, changing 29% of the numerical features where the best baseline changes 89%, at 70% smaller displacement, while remaining comparable on the other desiderata.

</details>


### [362] [From Shared Demand Patterns to Local Uncertainty: Probabilistic Load Forecasting by Mixing Compact Adaptations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08538)
*Haoran Li,Zhe Cheng,Yang Weng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出可扩展的客户感知概率负荷预测框架，通过共享模型+小型低维自适应组件库实现异构负荷的灵活建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 客户级和变压器级概率负荷预测面临可扩展性挑战：负荷不确定性受客户行为、天气和混合负荷构成影响，单一共享模型难以捕捉异构模式；而为每个负荷单独建模则训练、存储、更新和验证成本过高。

Method: 不采用每个负荷独立模型，也不将负荷分配给专门模型，而是学习一小组低维自适应组件，并允许每个负荷根据其预测特征对这些组件进行组合，从而在保留跨客户共享知识的同时提供足够灵活性。

Result: 在SMART-DS数据集的590个负荷曲线上，相较统计方法、神经网络、Transformer及预训练时间序列基线，在确定性精度和概率质量上均取得一致提升，同时保持较低的存储和推理成本。

Conclusion: 该框架在客户级和变压器级概率负荷预测中兼顾了精度与可扩展性，有效应对异构和混合负荷构成的建模难题。

Abstract: Probabilistic load forecasting has been widely studied for power-system operation and planning, but customer- and transformer-level forecasting introduces a distinct scalability challenge. At these levels, load uncertainty is strongly affected by customer behavior, weather, and mixed load composition, making it difficult for a single shared model to capture heterogeneous patterns. Using separate probabilistic models can improve local accuracy, but becomes costly to train, store, update, and validate at scale. To address this challenge, we develop a scalable customer-aware forecasting framework that learns common demand behavior through a shared model while adapting only a compact subset of parameters. Rather than using an independent model for each load or assigning each load to a specialized model, the proposed design learns a small bank of low-dimensional adaptation components and allows each load to combine them according to its forecasting characteristics. This preserves shared knowledge across customers while providing sufficient flexibility for heterogeneous and mixed load compositions. Experiments on 590 load profiles from the SMART-DS dataset show consistent improvements in deterministic accuracy and probabilistic quality over statistical, neural-network, Transformer-based, and pretrained time-series baselines, while retaining low storage and inference costs.

</details>


### [363] [DeltaTTT: Layerwise Optimization for Nonlinear Recurrent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08553)
*Yining Li,Dongchen Han,Jie Fu,Gao Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文发现非线性记忆网络中序列式测试时训练(TTT)的优势并不稳定，甚至不如固定基底的并行TTT基线，原因在于单次遍历内非线性记忆较难优化；为此提出DeltaTTT，用逐层学习替代两层记忆网络的联合内循环优化，在保留非线性读出能力的同时支持分块并行计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序列式测试时训练通过依次更新记忆网络，理论上每步更新能利用前序状态以更好地融合新信息，但这一预期优势在非线性记忆中并未稳定体现——固定基底的并行TTT基线反而优于其串行版本。作者希望探究这一反直觉现象的根源并加以改进。

Method: 受实验启示（非线性记忆在单次序列遍历中比线性记忆更难优化）驱动，提出DeltaTTT：将两层记忆网络的联合内循环优化替换为逐层学习，为每一层分配局部预测目标，并通过状态依赖的delta规则进行更新。该形式在保持非线性读出的同时支持分块并行计算。

Result: 在DeltaNet与LaCT两类主干上的实验表明，DeltaTTT相较其循环基线在语言建模和检索任务上均取得改进。

Conclusion: 状态依赖的序列更新并非非线性记忆性能的可靠来源；通过逐层、状态依赖的delta规则重构优化目标，既能缓解单次遍历的非线性优化困难，又能兼顾并行效率，从而在语言建模与检索上获得实际收益。

Abstract: Sequential test-time training adapts a memory network through successive updates, each computing an inner-loop gradient based on the network's previous state. Intuitively, this state dependence should allow each update to account for what the memory has already learned and better incorporate new information. However, we find that this expected advantage does not consistently materialize in nonlinear memories: a fixed-base parallel TTT baseline outperforms its serial counterpart. Our exploratory experiments point to a key underlying difficulty: nonlinear memories can be harder to optimize than linear ones within a single pass over the sequence. To alleviate this optimization difficulty, we introduce DeltaTTT, which replaces joint inner-loop optimization of a two-layer memory network with layerwise learning. Each layer is assigned a local prediction target and updated through a state-dependent delta rule. This formulation retains a nonlinear readout while enabling chunkwise parallel computation. Experiments on DeltaNet and LaCT backbones show improvements in language modeling and retrieval over their recurrent baselines.

</details>


### [364] [Reinforcement Learning for Hierarchical Reasoning Rewards: Minimax-Optimal Rates with Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08561)
*Naoki Nishikawa,Taiji Suzuki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上研究了强化学习后训练语言模型的有效性，通过将奖励建模为响应空间上的层级函数，证明一种基于 Transformer 的 actor-critic 算法能达到极小极大最优率，并量化了 on-policy 探索相对于离线奖励建模的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL 已成为推理任务语言模型后训练的标准工具，但理论理解有限，尤其缺乏对"为何 on-policy 探索结合神经奖励模型有效"这一问题的解释。

Method: 将奖励建模为响应空间上的层级函数，包含无穷多个局部组件，每个组件仅在前序组件解决后才相关；分析一种自然基于 Transformer 的 actor-critic 算法，该算法在采样自当前 KL 正则化策略、用 Transformer critic 拟合观测奖励、更新策略之间交替。

Result: 该算法在查询预算和正则化强度上达到极小极大最优率（至多对数因子），并在固定提示数量下为极小极大最优；相反，从固定参考分布采样（如离线奖励建模）的遗憾衰减仅能达到对数速率。

Conclusion: 结果表明 on-policy 探索会逐步聚焦到奖励集中的区域，并量化了其对 RL 后训练带来的收益。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a standard tool for post-training language models on reasoning tasks, where the policy is updated by reward feedback while exploring the space of responses. Despite its empirical success, theoretical understanding of RL post-training remains limited, in particular of why on-policy exploration combined with a neural reward model is effective. In this paper, we address this question by modeling the reward as a hierarchical function on the response space: the reward consists of infinitely many local components, each of which becomes relevant only after the preceding ones have been resolved. We show that a natural Transformer-based actor--critic algorithm, which alternates between sampling from the current KL-regularized policy, fitting a Transformer critic to the observed rewards, and updating the policy, achieves the minimax optimal rates in the query budget and in the regularization strength up to logarithmic factors, and is minimax optimal for a fixed number of prompts. In contrast, we prove that sampling from the fixed reference distribution, as in offline reward modeling, can limit regret decay to a logarithmic rate. These results show that on-policy exploration progressively zooms in on the region where the reward is concentrated, and quantify its benefit for RL post-training.

</details>


### [365] [Valid for Free: Homophily-Gated Conformal Prediction for Training-Free Node Classification with Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08564)
*Nguyen Duy Long,Phung Minh Hien,Nguyen Trong Viet,Nguyen Thai Anh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次对表格基础模型（TFM）在图节点分类上的可靠性进行研究，以TabICL为模型、图的一半节点作为带标签上下文，验证免训练上下文预测器可实现有限样本精确有效的分裂共形预测，并提出免训练的HG-DAPS方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TFM图节点分类工作只报告预测性能，未涉及共形覆盖或预测集大小，缺乏可靠性研究。

Method: 以TabICL为TFM，将节点与邻域特征作为表行、一半图作为带标签上下文；利用冻结的上下文预测器做分裂共形预测；提出HG-DAPS，一种免训练扩散分数，其同质性门控仅读取上下文标签以保持保证。

Result: 十个图审计中，免训练TabICL后验在九个图上ECE低于GCN+TS，平均ECE 0.019，约比GCN+TS的0.029低35%；HG-DAPS相比APS在六个同质图上平均集大小减少5.8%至17.1%，四个异质图上变化小于1%；两个二分类不平衡图的预注册陷阱案例显示，基于原始而非调整同质性门控会使低同质性节点覆盖率分别下降0.27和0.12，而边际覆盖率仍名义保持0.90并掩盖该下降。

Conclusion: 免训练TFM上下文预测器可实现精确有效的共形预测，校准误差优于GCN+TS，HG-DAPS能在保持保证的同时减小预测集，但门控指标选择会影响子群覆盖率。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models (TFMs) can classify the nodes of a graph without training on it, by reading node and neighborhood features as table rows next to labeled context rows. Work in this line reports predictive performance, not conformal coverage or prediction-set size. To our knowledge, we give the first reliability study of the setting, with TabICL as the TFM and half of each graph as labeled context. As for any predictor fixed before calibration, a frozen in-context predictor makes split conformal prediction exactly valid in finite samples, with no training, validation fold, or tuning on the target graph. An audit across ten graphs then shows that the training-free TabICL posterior has lower expected calibration error (ECE) than GCN with temperature scaling (GCN+TS) on nine of them. Its mean ECE over the ten graphs is 0.019, about 35 percent below the 0.029 of GCN+TS. We also introduce HG-DAPS, a training-free diffusion score whose homophily gate reads only the in-context labels, so the guarantee still holds. Relative to adaptive prediction sets (APS), it reduces mean set size by 5.8 to 17.1 percent on six homophilous graphs and changes it by under 1 percent on four heterophilous ones. On two binary, class-imbalanced graphs, a pre-registered trap case shows that gating on raw rather than adjusted homophily lowers coverage among low-homophily nodes by 0.27 and 0.12. Marginal coverage stays at the nominal 0.90 and masks this drop.

</details>


### [366] [Singular Value Decomposition: A Geometric Rediscovery, Where Proofs Become Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08565)
*Paul Agron*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文以几何方式重新发现奇异值分解（SVD），并主张其构造是许多机器学习方法背后的核心机制，将PCA、核方法与PageRank统一起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常教材用谱定理作用于A^T A来推导A=UΣV^T，虽正确但缺乏启发，且以强定理去证明一个本质关于椭圆的结论。作者希望颠倒顺序，从几何直观出发，让SVD自然地"被发现"而非被假定，并揭示其证明本身即可作为算法流程，支撑大量机器学习方法。

Method: 第一部分采用几何构造：线性映射将单位圆变为椭圆，追问哪些输入方向映射到椭圆的轴，并在二维中通过旋转标架、追踪像偏离垂直的程度、利用符号变化证明存在恰好垂直的标架（也是拉伸最大的方向）；随后将"最大化拉伸并递归"推广到n维，使奇异值按序出现，从而用该构造证明谱定理而非假设它。第二部分将每个构造付诸应用：最大化并递归对应幂法与PageRank；定位极大值的引理对应梯度下降的停止规则；A^T A与A A^T的对偶性对应核PCA中的传输。

Result: 作者展示了SVD的几何构造可自然导出奇异值及其排序，并证明谱定理；同时把该构造的不同环节分别对应到幂法、PageRank、梯度下降停止规则和核PCA，且每处连接都说明其边界与适用条件。前置知识仅为大二标准课程，示例规模小到可手算验证。

Conclusion: SVD不仅是代数结果，更是一种可执行的几何机器，其证明过程本身即为算法，统一地支撑PCA、核方法和PageRank等机器学习核心方法；这种几何化的重新发现比传统谱定理推导更具启发性和统一性。

Abstract: This article is a geometric rediscovery of the singular value decomposition, with a further claim: the construction it builds is the machinery behind much of machine learning. The same argument that answers an idle question about ellipses is the algorithm behind principal component analysis, kernel methods, and PageRank, and it is not only the results that transfer but the proofs themselves, run as procedures.
  The usual introduction states $A = UΣV^T$ and justifies it via the spectral theorem applied to $A^T A$. This is correct but unilluminating, since it assumes a powerful theorem to reach a result that is, in the end, about ellipses. Part I reverses the order. A linear map sends the unit circle to an ellipse; one asks which input directions map to its axes, and finds, example after example, that they are perpendicular. In the plane this can be watched: rotate a frame, track how far its images are from perpendicular, and a sign change forces a frame where they are exactly perpendicular, which is also where the map stretches hardest. Maximizing the stretch and recursing generalizes this to n dimensions, with singular values falling out in order, and the construction proves the spectral theorem rather than assuming it.
  Part II puts each construction to work: maximize-and-recurse becomes the power method and PageRank; the lemma locating the maximizer becomes the stopping rule of gradient descent; the duality between $A^T A$ and $A A^T$ becomes the transport at the heart of kernel PCA. Each connection is stated with its boundary, saying what the decomposition supplies and where another idea takes over. Prerequisites are the standard sophomore sequence, and the worked examples are small enough to check by hand.

</details>


### [367] [Less Is More: A Leakage-Controlled Study of Dermoscopic Preprocessing for Joint Skin Lesion Classification and Segmentation with YOLO26](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08570)
*Truong Viet Vu,Nguyen Chi Hai,Nguyen Phuc Nguyen,Ngo Hoang Tu,Vo Nguyen Quoc Bao,Nguyen Thai Anh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在严格的病灶不重叠评测协议下，极简预处理加在线增强在皮肤镜分类与分割联合任务上优于复杂确定性预处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 皮肤镜自动分析中常用手工预处理抑制伪影、增强病灶可见性，但其对现代实时模型的真实贡献不明，尤其当评测未充分控制同一病灶图像间的相关性时，结论可能被数据泄漏夸大。

Method: 基于HAM10000（10,015张），质控后得到来自7,468个独立病灶的10,013个有效图像-掩码对，并按病灶身份划分互斥集合。固定nano级YOLO26n-seg模型、分辨率、训练预算与评测协议，对比：极简处理+在线增强（基线）、离线类别平衡、DullRazor-CLAHE预处理、原图-处理混合视图，重复3个随机种子。

Result: 病灶不重叠测试集上，基线mask mAP$_{50:95}$为0.5636±0.0234，Dice为0.9356±0.0024，macro-F1为0.6917±0.0202。离线增强未提升平均性能；组合与混合策略反而降低类别感知的分割与分类精度。模型仅269万参数，推理约50 FPS。

Conclusion: 在泄漏受控、病灶不重叠且其他非输入因素固定的协议下，极简预处理搭配标准在线增强比日益复杂的确定性预处理具有更好的精度-效率权衡，且在HAM10000上三个种子间无一致的联合增益。

Abstract: Handcrafted preprocessing is widely employed in automated dermoscopic analysis to suppress imaging artifacts and enhance lesion visibility. Nevertheless, its actual contribution to modern real-time models remains unclear, particularly when evaluation protocols do not adequately control correlations among images of the same lesion. This study presents a leakage-controlled, lesion-disjoint evaluation of dermoscopic preprocessing and augmentation for joint multi-class lesion classification and instance segmentation using a fixed nano-scale YOLO26 segmentation model (YOLO26n-seg). From HAM10000 (10,015 images), quality control yields 10,013 valid image-mask pairs from 7,468 unique lesions, partitioned into mutually exclusive sets by lesion identity. With the architecture, resolution, training budget, and evaluation protocol held fixed, we compare minimally processed images plus online augmentation against offline class balancing, DullRazor-CLAHE preprocessing, and raw-processed hybrid views, over three random seeds. On the lesion-disjoint test set, the raw baseline achieves a mask mAP$_{50:95}$ of $0.5636 \pm 0.0234$, a Dice score of $0.9356 \pm 0.0024$, and a macro-F1 score of $0.6917 \pm 0.0202$. Offline augmentation does not improve the mean performance, while the combined and hybrid strategies reduce both class-aware segmentation and classification accuracy. At only 2.69 million parameters, the model runs at approximately 50 frames per second. Under a leakage-controlled, lesion-disjoint protocol with all non-input factors held fixed, minimally processed dermoscopic images combined with standard online augmentation deliver a better accuracy-efficiency trade-off than increasingly complex deterministic preprocessing, which yields no consistent joint benefit across three seeds on HAM10000.

</details>


### [368] [How Learning Governs Unlearning across the Memorization-Generalization Spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08577)
*Hwiyeong Lee,Hyelim Lim,Ingyu Bang,Hoki Kim,Taeuk Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了模型的学习方式（记忆与泛化）如何影响其后续的遗忘（unlearning）效果，发现泛化型模型在遗忘时对保留集的损害更大，且该趋势近乎单调，并在大语言模型遗忘中同样成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有遗忘研究多关注如何消除模型习得的不良能力，但很少有工作考察模型的学习方式如何塑造其后续的遗忘表现，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 利用模块加法中的grokking将模型分类为记忆型和泛化型，对比其遗忘响应；进一步提出分桶模块加法（bucketed modular addition），在记忆-泛化谱系上显式控制两种策略的贡献；并在大语言模型的逐字回忆与事实回忆场景中验证该关系。

Result: 泛化型模型在遗忘时遭受更大的保留集损害（性能下降更明显），这一趋势在分桶设置中近乎单调地持续存在，并在大语言模型遗忘中同样成立。

Conclusion: 学习动态对遗忘效果有重要影响，研究为开发更好的遗忘方法提供了两点实用启示，强调在遗忘中考虑学习动态的重要性。

Abstract: While unlearning seeks to negate undesired capabilities acquired through learning, little research has examined how the way models learn shapes their subsequent unlearning. In this paper, we investigate this connection from the perspectives of memorization and generalization, the two most representative yet competing strategies that models employ during training. We first classify memorization- and generalization-heavy models using grokking in modular addition and compare their responses to unlearning, showing that the latter suffer greater retain damage, i.e., a larger performance drop on the retain set. Furthermore, we conduct a finer-grained analysis by introducing bucketed modular addition, in which the respective contributions of the two strategies can be explicitly controlled across the memorization-generalization spectrum. In this setup, we reaffirm that the same trend persists and is nearly monotonic. We further demonstrate that this relationship also holds in LLM unlearning across verbatim and factual recall settings. Finally, we provide two practical insights for developing better unlearning methods, highlighting the importance of accounting for learning dynamics in unlearning.

</details>


### [369] [Random Feature Gaussian Process Attention: Linear-Time Probabilistic Attention with Calibrated Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08578)
*Amir Mohammad Mahfoozi,Zi Yang,Ying Li,Michael Minyi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出随机傅里叶特征高斯过程注意力（RFF-GPA）模块，用随机傅里叶特征近似平稳核，将高斯过程注意力的复杂度从三次/二次降至序列长度的线性，在提升校准性的同时保持预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer 建模能力强，但在安全关键应用中校准性差，限制其可靠性。将注意力解释为高斯过程后验可实现有原则的不确定性校准，但核矩阵求逆带来序列长度的三次复杂度；即使解耦 GP 变体将成本降至二次，实际计算仍难以承受，因此需要更可扩展的方案。

Method: 提出即插即用的 RFF-GPA 模块：把注意力建模为使用平稳核的高斯过程，并用随机傅里叶特征对该核进行低秩近似，从而以线性时间复杂度近似后验均值与方差。

Result: 在多个真实数据集上的实验表明，该注意力模块在保持预测精度的同时改善了校准性，并将计算复杂度降低为关于序列长度的线性级别。

Conclusion: 通过随机傅里叶特征近似高斯过程注意力，可在保证预测性能的同时显著提升可扩展性与校准性，为安全关键场景下更可靠的 Transformer 建模提供了实用途径。

Abstract: Transformers provide a state-of-the-art modeling framework, yet poor calibration limits their reliability in safety-critical applications. A promising direction addresses this issue by interpreting attention as a Gaussian process (GP) posterior, which enables principled uncertainty calibration but incurs cubic complexity in sequence length due to the inversion of the kernel; although decoupled GP variants reduced the cost to quadratic, the computation remains prohibitive in practice. In this paper, we propose the plug-and-play random Fourier feature Gaussian process attention (RFF-GPA) module, which represents the attention as a GP with a stationary kernel approximated by random Fourier features. This low-rank approximation results in linear-time complexity for approximating the posterior mean and variance, making it far more scalable compared to previous work. Empirical results on multiple real-world datasets show that our attention module improves calibration while maintaining predictive accuracy, and simultaneously reduces computational complexity to linear in the sequence length.

</details>


### [370] [CNet: A Complex-Valued Deep Learning Framework with Wirtinger Autodifferentiation and FFT--Hadamard Convolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08592)
*Marcel Crasmaru*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CNet是一个基于C++/CUDA的复值神经网络框架，采用Wirtinger微积分与Born规则测量，引入可学习复卷积与O(N log N)因果傅里叶混合器，在语言建模、无线电调制分类与相干衍射成像任务上验证了复值网络能学到物理正确结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习框架以实值为原生，复值网络与复值函数优化缺乏统一、物理契合的工具支持；同时复值算子在哪些任务上真正必要仍不清晰，需要系统性的瓶颈分析来定位。

Method: 提出CNet框架：网络视为作用于幅度向量的复（常为酉，如DFT）算子级联，分类用Born规则p_k=|z_k|²/‖z‖²替代实logits上的softmax；每层提供CPU参考实现与经有限差分校验的CUDA核，计算图按批次克隆以支持GPU；基于conv(x,k)=IFFT(FFT(x)·FFT(k)）构建可学习复卷积，并实现真Adam优化器与降内存推理模式；提出基于Bluestein/chirp-z分解的三角掩码DFT作为O(N log N)因果傅里叶混合器，构建全复值FNet风格因果序列模型。

Result: 三个研究：(1) 复值因果FNet在字符级语言建模上，调优后匹配或超越参数量对齐的实值因果FNet，并以不到一半的训练步数达到其实值模型的收敛质量；(2) 在RML2016.10a无线电调制分类与相干衍射成像的傅里叶相位问题上进行瓶颈分析，定位复值网络仍需新算子之处；(3) 三项任务中复值建模均可证明地学到物理正确的结构。

Conclusion: CNet表明复值/酉算子与Born规则测量是物理原生且有效的建模范式，复值网络在合适任务上不仅能达到实值基线水平，还能显著提升训练效率，并可通过瓶颈分析指明未来复值算子的发展方向。代码已开源。

Abstract: CNet is a C++/CUDA framework for building and training deep complex-valued neural networks (CVNNs) and, more generally, for optimizing complex-valued functions by gradient descent with Wirtinger (CR-calculus) derivatives. It takes a physics-native stance: a network is a cascade of complex -- and often unitary (the DFT) -- operations acting on an amplitude vector, and classification is a Born-rule measurement $p_k = |z_k|^2 / \|z\|^2$ rather than a softmax over real logits. Every layer ships a CPU reference and a CUDA kernel checked against finite differences, and the computation graph is cloned across the batch for GPU execution. On top of the base layers we add signal-processing primitives that turn the identity conv(x,k) = IFFT(FFT(x) . FFT(k)) into a learnable complex convolutional network, together with a true-Adam optimizer and a reduced-memory inference mode.
  We report three studies. First, a fully complex-valued, FNet-style causal sequence model built on a new $O(N \log N)$ causal Fourier mixer -- a triangular-masked DFT evaluated by a Bluestein / chirp-z factorization: once properly tuned it matches or exceeds a parameter-matched real-valued causal FNet on character-level language modeling, reaching the real model's converged quality in under half the training steps. Second and third, bottleneck analyses on radio-modulation classification (RML2016.10a) and the Fourier phase problem of coherent-diffraction imaging, which isolate exactly where complex-valued networks still need new operators. Across all three the complex formulation provably learns the physically correct structure.
  Code: https://github.com/crasmarum/CNet

</details>


### [371] [Multi-Label Perceptual Bug Detection in Video Games using Deep Learning on Gameplay Footage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08593)
*Nahian Rifaat,Felix Morosov,Loutfouz Zaman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于深度学习的多标签感知类bug检测模型ResNet-BiLSTM，用于游戏视频中同一帧内多种bug的自动检测，在基准数据集上F1达85.78%，并发布了包含77,969个视频片段的新数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统游戏bug检测依赖人工测试，成本高、耗时长；且现有工具难以在同一视频帧中同时检测多种感知类bug，限制了自动化检测在真实场景中的应用。

Method: 提出ResNet-BiLSTM深度模型，结合ResNet提取空间特征与BiLSTM建模时间依赖关系，并与Inflated 3D ConvNet、3D ResNet等视频分类模型进行对比。

Result: ResNet-BiLSTM在基准数据集上取得85.78%的F1分数，表明时间依赖建模有助于提升基于视频的bug检测准确性。

Conclusion: 多标签感知类bug检测可用于游戏玩法视频，有助于减少人工测试工作量；同时本文公开了一个新的多标签感知bug数据集，覆盖多类游戏、约120万帧，包含5类bug的组合。

Abstract: Traditional approaches for automated bug detection in video games, such as manual testing, can be beneficial for the improvement of quality assurance, but they can be expensive and time-consuming. The scarce number of tools available to detect multiple perceptual bugs in the same video frame introduces detection challenges for automated bug detection tools in real-world scenarios. We propose a deep learning model for multi-label perceptual bug detection and compare it against video classification models such as Inflated 3D ConvNet and 3D ResNet. Our proposed model, ResNet-BiLSTM, achieved an F1 score of 85.78% on the benchmark dataset. Our results demonstrated that temporal dependency modelling is beneficial for accurate video-based bug detection. We believe this work with multi-label perceptual bug detection on gameplay videos will help save resources spent on manual testing workloads in video games. Furthermore, we introduce a new dataset with multi-label perceptual bugs in this work. The dataset contains 77,969 video clips across different genres of games with approximately 1.2 million frames, containing combinations from 5 classes of bugs in the same video frame.

</details>


### [372] [Early Memory Selection for Balanced Adam](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08624)
*Alberto Fernández-Hernández,Cristian Pérez-Corral,Jose I. Mestre,Manuel F. Dolz,Enrique S. Quintana-Ortí*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出从短程试运行中选择Adam共享记忆参数β（β₁=β₂=β）的方法，选定后在全量训练中固定。基于Adam归一化方向的局部模型，权衡采样波动与梯度平均延迟，导出三次记忆规则，用少量试运行检查点的梯度探针估计两个系数，并联合利用分子分母保留其协方差。200次更新试运行、四个检查点各16个探针梯度下，在11个视觉与语言任务上，种子匹配回顾评估将平均相对验证差距降低40.7%，最差四分之一平均差距降低44.3%，且比跨全部工作负载选出的最佳常数β低32.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Adam中共享记忆参数β通常凭经验固定（如0.95），缺乏针对具体工作负载的自适应选择依据。本文希望用极短的试运行数据自动选取合适的共享β，避免昂贵的全量网格搜索，同时兼顾采样波动与梯度平均延迟之间的权衡。

Method: 构建Adam归一化方向的局部模型，用以平衡采样变异性与平均过去梯度带来的延迟，从而得到形如三次方的记忆规则；其两个系数通过若干试运行检查点上的梯度探针估计。估计器同时使用分子与分母，以保留二者协方差。流程为200次更新试运行，在四个检查点各取16个探针梯度。

Result: 在11个视觉与语言工作负载上做种子匹配的回顾性评估：相较代表共享β=0.95的网格，平均相对验证差距降低40.7%，最差四分之一平均差距降低44.3%；平均差距也比在全部11个负载上选出的最佳常数β低32.3%。

Conclusion: 共享β可通过短程试运行与少量梯度探针稳健估计，所得固定β优于常用默认值与跨工作负载的最优常数β，为Adam超参数选择提供了一种低成本、可泛化的方案。

Abstract: We propose a method for choosing the shared memory parameter $β_1=β_2=β$ in Adam from a short pilot training. The selected $β$ remains fixed during the subsequent full training. A local model of Adam's normalized direction balances sampling variability against the delay introduced by averaging past gradients. This balance gives a cubic memory rule, whose two coefficients are estimated from gradient probes at a few pilot checkpoints. The estimator uses the numerator and denominator jointly, preserving their covariance. With a 200-update pilot and sixteen probe gradients at each of four checkpoints, a seed-matched retrospective evaluation on eleven vision and language workloads reduces mean relative validation gap by 40.7% and worst-quarter mean gap by 44.3% against the grid representative of shared $β=0.95$. The mean gap is also 32.3% lower than that of the best constant $β$ chosen across all eleven workloads.

</details>


### [373] [MemFLoRA: Memory-Floor LoRA for CNN Adaptation at the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08669)
*Mehmet Emre Akbulut,Johannes Geier,Ulf Schlichtmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 MemFLoRA，一种以激活内存为优先设计原则的低秩 CNN 适配器，可将保存的激活内存降低 98.5-98.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设备端学习在模型部署后遇到用户、传感器或环境特定偏移时是必要的。虽然 PEFT（尤其是 LoRA 变体）实现了边缘端高效适配，但 CNN 适配的瓶颈往往不是可训练参数量，而是反向传播前必须保留的激活状态。

Method: 提出 Memory-Floor LoRA (MemFLoRA)，围绕“内存优先”原则设计而非直接套用面向 Transformer 的 LoRA。定义激活内存下限准则：可训练的反向计算不得依赖全宽层输入。适配器冻结下投影、训练尺度匹配的上投影，并将 eval 模式骨干归一化与激活最小化反向规则结合，将保存状态缩减至低秩分支。

Result: 在三个人体活动识别（HAR）数据集和两个 CNN 骨干上，针对受试者、身体位置和传感器放置偏移进行评估，MemFLoRA 相比全量微调减少保存激活内存 98.5-98.7%、峰值训练状态内存 94.9-97.3%，同时匹配或超过 CNN PEFT 基线。

Conclusion: MemFLoRA 通过以激活内存为优先的设计，在显著降低 CNN 设备端适配内存开销的同时保持了与现有 CNN PEFT 基线相当或更优的性能。

Abstract: On-device learning is necessary when the model encounters user-,sensor-, or environment-specific shifts after deployment. Although parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods, particularly Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) variants, enable efficient adaptation at the edge, the limiting resource for Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) adaptation is often not the number of trainable parameters but the activation state that must be retained until the backward pass. This paper introduces Memory-Floor LoRA (MemFLoRA), a low-rank CNN adapter built around a memory-first design principle rather than a direct application of transformer-oriented LoRA. Instead of merely reducing trainable weights, we define an activation-memory-floor criterion: trainable backward computations must not depend on full-width layer inputs. The resulting adapter freezes the down-projection, trains a scale-matched up-projection, and combines eval-mode backbone normalization with activation-minimal backward rules, reducing saved state to the low-rank branch. Evaluated on three Human Activity Recognition (HAR) datasets and two CNN backbones under subject, body-location, and sensor-placement shifts, MemFLoRA reduces saved-activation memory by 98.5-98.7% and peak training-state memory by 94.9-97.3% relative to full fine-tuning, while matching or exceeding CNN PEFT baselines.

</details>


### [374] [Principled Under Pressure: Post-Training Decides Whether LLMs Act on Their Own Moral Judgment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08670)
*Orion Reblitz-Richardson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过预注册的248个情境面板，测量语言模型"口头判定错误但仍执行"的价值-行动缺口，并发现该缺口取决于后训练配方而非预训练权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型日益充当智能体，而现有对"陈述价值观"的评估无法察觉"说某事错、却照做不误"这类独特失败。作者希望区分"明知故犯"与"不知者不罪"，并为后训练提供一个可测量的目标。

Method: 构建预注册的248个情境面板，涵盖五类压力。每个情境对同一模型提问两次：一次作为智能体选择行动，一次以第三人称问哪一选项正确，以模型自身判断为参照；每个情境都配有去除压力的孪生版本，并加入"操作者命令违规行动"的阳性对照，以区分"缺失缺口"与"仪器失灵"。同时进行聊天模板、推理引导等干预。

Result: OLMo-3-7B-Instruct 在约五分之一施压情境中执行了自己判定错误的行为，显著高于无压力版本。四个instruct模型中缺口依赖后训练配方：OLMo-3与Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct存在缺口；Tulu 3在全面板及其最具压力情境上均无缺口；Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct全面板无缺口。Tulu 3与Meta配方同源自Llama-3.1权重，仅Meta配方携带缺口。脱离聊天模板会反转缺口符号。行动前进行利害推理可将选择拉回模型自身判断，命名规范约贡献三分之一。

Conclusion: 价值-行动缺口是后训练配方的可测量目标，而非预训练权重的固定属性。这为评估与改进模型的智能体对齐提供了新方向。

Abstract: Language models increasingly act as agents. An agent that says an action is wrong and then takes it anyway is a different failure from one that does not know better, and evaluations of stated values cannot see it. We build a pre-registered panel of 248 scenarios across five kinds of pressure. Each scenario is posed twice to the same model, once as the agent choosing what to do and once in the third person asking which option is right, so the model's own judgment is the reference. Every scenario has a twin with the pressure removed, and every model gets a positive control in which its operator orders the violating action, so that a missing gap can be told apart from a blind instrument. On OLMo-3-7B-Instruct, the model takes the action it judged wrong on about one in five pressuring scenarios, more often than on the same scenarios with the pressure removed. Across four instruct models the gap depends on the post-training recipe: OLMo-3 and Meta's Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct carry it; Tulu 3 shows none on the whole panel (above about 0.01 in probability) or on its own most-pressuring scenarios; Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct shows none on the whole panel (above about 0.02) and is unresolved on its own (0.083, -0.028 to 0.195). Meta's recipe and Ai2's Tulu 3 start from the same Llama-3.1 weights, and only Meta's carries the gap. Reading a chat model outside its chat template reverses the sign of its gap with nothing at stake (-0.038 against +0.055 under the template on OLMo-3), a distortion present on two of three recipes. On both models that carry it, reasoning about the stakes before acting moves the choice back toward the model's own judgment, against a same-length non-moral task, with or without the pressure; on OLMo-3, naming the norm at stake does about a third of that. The gap is a measurable target for post-training recipes, not a fixed property of pretrained weights.

</details>


### [375] [Variance-Optimal Off-Policy Evaluation with Conjunct Effect Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08677)
*Nicolò Felicioni,Michael Benigni,Maurizio Ferrari Dacrema,Paolo Cremonesi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VOCEM估计器，在OffCEM与DR之间构造无偏插值族，通过闭式选择最优插值系数最小化方差，其方差不超过两端点，并在23个评估条件下全面优于两者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文赌博机策略的离策略评估中，动作级重要性加权会带来过大的方差。双重稳健（DR）估计虽在共同支撑下无偏，但仍保留高方差的动作级权重；而OffCEM用更稳定的簇级权重替代，却依赖奖励模型的局部正确性。作者希望在两者之间找到兼顾稳定与无偏的更优方案。

Method: 在DR与OffCEM所需的假设下，证明存在一个在两者之间插值的无偏估计族；据此提出VOCEM估计器，通过选择插值系数最小化方差，并推导出总体最优系数的闭式解。

Result: 理论证明VOCEM的方差不超过OffCEM或DR任一端点。在受控合成实验与两个大规模动作基准上，VOCEM在全部23个评估条件中均优于两个端点，表现出更强的稳定性与经验鲁棒性。

Conclusion: VOCEM通过方差最优插值统一并改进了OffCEM与DR，为高方差场景下的离策略评估提供了更稳定且无偏的实用估计方法。

Abstract: Off-policy evaluation (OPE) for contextual bandit policies becomes challenging when action-level importance weighting incurs excessive variance. Doubly robust (DR) estimation remains unbiased under common support but retains these high-variance action-level weights. A prior estimator, Off-policy evaluation with Conjunct Effect Model (OffCEM), replaces them with more stable cluster-level weights, at the cost of relying on local correctness of the reward model. In this paper, we show that, under the assumptions required by DR and OffCEM, there exists an unbiased family of estimators that interpolates between OffCEM and DR. Building on this result, we propose the Variance Optimal-CEM (VOCEM) estimator, which selects the interpolation coefficient to minimize variance. We derive the population-optimal coefficient in closed form and show that the resulting estimator has variance no larger than either endpoint, OffCEM or DR. Experiments in controlled synthetic settings and on two large-action benchmarks show that VOCEM improves upon both endpoints in all 23 evaluated conditions, exhibiting greater stability and empirical robustness.

</details>


### [376] [A Systematic Study of Small Language Models on Abstract Reasoning Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08680)
*Nur A Zarin Nishat,Jens Lehmann,Andrei Aioanei,Sahar Vahdati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在小语言模型上研究抽象推理中"可迁移规则"与"分布特定规律"的区别，发现准确率高度依赖于模型、适配方式、评测分布与回答格式，分布外性能显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抽象推理基准的端点准确率无法揭示模型是真正习得了可迁移规则，还是仅拟合了特定分布规律。作者希望在可控任务族上厘清这一区别，并系统刻画技能习得的效率、稳定性与鲁棒性。

Method: 在 ARC-TGI 基准上，将抽象网格变换组织为可控任务族，支持重采样、空间平移与跨基准迁移。对 decoder-only、encoder-decoder 及混合专家（MoE）模型族进行监督微调，累计超过 1000 次运行，考察技能习得效率与稳定性、分布外鲁棒性、模型族与任务表述的交互，以及伴随行为差异的逐层注意力特征。

Result: 分布内准确率可达较高水平，但习得对优化设置敏感，且在各任务族间分布不均。分布外性能急剧下降，即便规则被保留、仅网格尺度改变亦如此。训练集深度与广度的增加带来不均衡收益；额外上下文示例的作用因模型族而异。可执行规则归纳能产生直接网格生成未曾出现的正确解。注意力诊断在部分任务上显示差异明显的集中度与上下文依赖特征。

Conclusion: 抽象推理得分并非模型能力的单一指标，而是条件依赖于模型、适配方式、评测分布与回答格式。注意力诊断未能确立普遍的因果机制。研究提示应以分布外与规则保持性测试来评估抽象推理，而非仅看端点准确率。

Abstract: Endpoint accuracy on abstract-reasoning benchmarks does not reveal whether a language model has acquired a transferable rule or fit distribution-specific regularities. We study this distinction in small language models on the ARC-TGI benchmark, which organizes abstract grid transformations into controllable task families and supports resampling, spatial shifts, and cross-benchmark transfer. Across more than 1,000 runs, we profile decoder-only, encoder--decoder, and mixture-of-experts model families under supervised fine-tuning. We examine the efficiency and stability of skill acquisition, robustness beyond the training distribution, interactions with model family and task formulation, and layer-wise attention signatures that accompany behavioral differences. Substantial in-distribution accuracy is attainable, but acquisition is sensitive to optimization and unevenly distributed across task families. Performance deteriorates sharply outside the training distribution, including when the rule is retained but grid scale changes. Greater training-set depth and breadth yield uneven gains, while the effect of additional in-context examples depends on model family. Executable-rule induction also yields correct solutions not observed under direct grid generation. On selected tasks, attention diagnostics show distinct concentration and context-dependence profiles, but do not establish general causal mechanisms. Overall, abstract-reasoning scores are conditional on the model, adaptation regime, evaluation distribution, and response format.

</details>


### [377] [Probabilistic Counterfactual Inference for Discrete Outcomes in Gaussian-Process Causal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08689)
*Juliette Sinnott,Amir-Hossein Karimi,Mohammad Kohandel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出统一概率框架，将GP预测器与显式外生噪声机制配对，实现含离散子节点的GP-SCM反事实推断，并揭示耦合类型误配的严重后果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GP-SCM反事实推断主要针对连续内生变量，难以处理含离散子节点（父节点连续）的因果图，适用性受限。

Method: 为离散结果设计精确的条件噪声溯因程序：二元变量用均匀阈值，名义类别用Gumbel-max竞争，有序变量用潜高斯切点模型；将溯因噪声经干预传播，并纳入GP潜函数的后验不确定性。

Result: 证明所得机制能重现拟合模型的观测分布与干预分布；在已知反事实真值的合成SCM上评估精度、一致性与耦合误配稳健性；发现对有序数据误用类别耦合会使反事实误差约增至三倍且不随数据增加而降低，反向强制名义数据有序则损害拟合方程本身。

Conclusion: 耦合方式的选择须基于结构性依据，而不能仅凭拟合优度判断。

Abstract: Counterfactual inference in Gaussian-process structural causal models (GP-SCMs) has been developed primarily for continuous endogenous variables, limiting applicability to causal graphs that contain discrete child nodes with continuous parents. We introduce a unified probabilistic framework for counterfactual inference with heterogeneous variable types by pairing GP predictors with explicit exogenous noise mechanisms. For discrete outcomes, we derive exact conditional noise-abduction procedures using a uniform threshold for binary variables, a Gumbel-max race for nominal categories, and a latent Gaussian cut-point model for ordinal ones. In each case, we propagate abducted noise through interventions while accounting for posterior uncertainty in the GP latent functions, and prove that the resulting mechanisms reproduce the fitted model's observational and interventional distributions. On synthetic SCMs with known ground-truth counterfactuals, we evaluate estimation accuracy, consistency, and robustness to coupling misspecification. A key finding is that applying a categorical coupling to ordinal data inflates counterfactual error roughly threefold even when observational fit remains comparable, and that this error does not diminish with more data. As the training set grows, the fitted structural equation converges to the truth while the counterfactual error flattens onto a floor. In the reverse direction, forcing a false order onto nominal data instead degrades the fitted equation itself. The choice of coupling must therefore be justified on structural grounds rather than read off the fit.

</details>


### [378] [GeneICL: A Tabular Foundation Model for Bulk Transcriptomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08694)
*Michael Bohl,Alexander Theus,David Wissel,Valentina Boeva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GeneICL 是一个仅 4.2M 参数的表格基础模型，通过转录组感知的半合成预训练先验与参数高效循环架构，在 80 个临床结局预测任务上取得最佳平均排名，参数最多减少 387 倍且推理无需梯度更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基因表达数据在生物医学中广泛测量，但临床结局预测受高维度、强特征相关性和标注数据有限困扰。大规模自监督转录组基础模型常不如简单监督基线；表格基础模型虽提供上下文学习替代方案，却多基于通用合成数据预训练，缺乏转录组结构。因此作者追问：缺失的要素是否是转录组感知的预训练而非模型规模。

Method: 提出 GeneICL，一个 4.2M 参数的表格基础模型，结合由实测批量表达谱构建的半合成预训练先验与参数高效循环架构；并通过 Cox 偏似然残差实现免训练的回归化处理，从而支持右删失生存预测。

Result: 在涵盖分类、回归与生存的 80 个临床结局预测任务上评估：表格基础模型一致优于自监督转录组模型；GeneICL 在评估的基础模型与调优基线中取得最佳整体排名，参数量最多减少 387 倍，推理无需梯度更新，可在笔记本 CPU 上数秒内完成预测。

Conclusion: 转录组感知的预训练（而非单纯扩大规模）是提升临床结局预测性能的关键，小型高效模型即可超越大型自监督方法。

Abstract: Gene expression is widely measured in biomedicine, yet clinical outcome prediction remains challenging due to high dimensionality, strong feature correlations, and limited labeled data. Large self-supervised transcriptomic foundation models often fail to outperform simple supervised baselines. Tabular foundation models offer an alternative through in-context learning, but are typically pretrained on generic synthetic data rather than transcriptomic structure. We ask whether transcriptomics-aware pretraining, rather than scale, is the missing ingredient. Towards this end, we introduce GeneICL, a 4.2M-parameter tabular foundation model combining a semi-synthetic pretraining prior built from measured bulk expression profiles with a parameter-efficient recurrent architecture. We further enable right-censored survival prediction via a training-free reduction to regression using Cox partial-likelihood residuals. We evaluate GeneICL on 80 clinical outcome-prediction tasks spanning classification, regression, and survival. Tabular foundation models consistently outperform self-supervised transcriptomic models, while GeneICL achieves the best overall rank among evaluated foundation models and tuned baselines. GeneICL does so with up to 387$\times$ fewer parameters, no gradient updates at inference, and predictions within seconds on a laptop CPU.

</details>


### [379] [Optimal and Efficient Online Inverse Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08735)
*Anupam Gupta,Guru Guruganesh,Honghao Lin,Vahab Mirrokni,Renato Paes Leme,David P. Woodruff*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个多项式时间的确定性算法用于在线逆向线性优化，达到最优遗憾 $O(\sqrt d)$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sakaue 此前用随机算法取得了最优遗憾 $O(\sqrt d)$，但每轮需要 $(dT)^{O(d)}$ 次线性优化，计算代价极高，因此其遗留问题是在多项式时间内能否达到同样的最优遗憾。本文正面回答了这一问题。

Method: 作者提出一种确定性算法，是对 Sakaue 等人和 Cai 等人的变量度量（variable-metric）算法的变体：当查询点移动得离更新发生的位置足够远时，就撤销该度量更新。该算法对任意时间跨度 $T$ 都达到 $O(\sqrt d)$ 遗憾，并在 $d$ 和 $T$ 的多项式时间内运行。

Result: 所提确定性算法取得 $O(\sqrt d)$ 遗憾（对每个 $T$），且运行时间为 $d$ 和 $T$ 的多项式时间，显著优于此前随机算法的 $(dT)^{O(d)}$ 代价。

Conclusion: 该工作解决了 Sakaue 关于最优遗憾能否在多项式时间内实现的公开问题，给出了肯定答案。

Abstract: In online inverse linear optimization, a learner recommends an action and then observes the choice of an expert who maximizes a fixed, unknown linear objective on $\mathbb{R}^{d}$; the goal is to learn to optimize this objective without observing it. Sakaue recently obtained the optimal regret $O(\sqrt d)$ with a randomized algorithm making $(dT)^{O(d)}$ linear optimizations per round, and asked whether it can be attained in polynomial time. We answer positively: our deterministic algorithm has regret $O(\sqrt d)$ for every horizon $T$ and runs in time polynomial in $d$ and $T$. It is a variant of the variable-metric algorithms of Sakaue et al.\ and Cai et al., in which a metric update is revoked once the query point moves far enough from where the update was made.

</details>


### [380] [On the Computational Tractability of Robust Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08740)
*Vanessa Kosoy,Vinayak Pathak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对不精确/鲁棒赌博机中不可实现学习缺乏计算保证的问题，识别出一个可多项式时间求解的特例，达到 Õ(√T) 遗憾，并证明其若干小推广为 NP-hard，表明该特例处于可处理性边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当环境不属于学习者的假设类时，通常使用不可知（agnostic）学习保证，但在监督学习之外，不可知保证难以获得。近期提出的不精确赌博机（后称鲁棒赌博机）为赌博机场景下的不可实现学习提供了另一途径，并已有 Θ(√T) 遗憾的学习器，但缺乏计算保证。本文旨在为该问题寻找计算上可行的学习算法。

Method: 识别出不精确/鲁棒赌博机问题中的一个特殊情形，并为其设计多项式时间学习算法，实现 Õ(√T) 遗憾；同时通过归约证明该特殊情形的若干小型推广是 NP-hard 的。

Result: 该特殊情形存在多项式时间、Õ(√T) 遗憾的学习器；而其若干小推广被证明为 NP-hard，说明该特殊情形恰好位于可处理性的边界上。

Conclusion: 该特例是可计算求解的极限情形。鉴于近期有观点认为不可实现学习问题的高效计算学习器对解决 AI 对齐问题至关重要，本文是该方向的一小步进展。

Abstract: Learning when the environment does not belong to the learner's hypothesis class is typically handled using agnostic learning guarantees. However, for anything beyond supervised learning, agnostic guarantees are difficult to come by. Recently, imprecise bandits (Kosoy, 2025) (later renamed to robust bandits in Appel and Kosoy, 2025) were introduced as another approach to unrealizable learning in the bandits setting and a $Θ(\sqrt{T})$ regret learner was shown for a large class. However, no computational guarantees were provided. In this paper we identify a special case that admits a polynomial-time learner with $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$ regret. We also show that several small generalizations of this special case are NP-hard thus indicating that the special case is at the boundary of what is tractable. It has been recently suggested (Kosoy, 2018) that computationally efficient learners for unrealizable learning problems are crucial for solving the AI alignment problem. This work is a small step in that direction.

</details>


### [381] [Reinforcement Learning with Conformal Action Sets: An Application to Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08743)
*Wenwen Si,Honghao Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 RLCP，利用 critic 分数与在线阈值自适应调整保留动作集合，实现可变组块大小的序列推荐，并给出理论界与多样性提升的实证结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序列推荐通常采用固定组块大小，但一次会话中有用替代项的数量是变化的，固定大小会造成冗余或不足，因此需要自适应地裁剪候选动作集合。

Method: RLCP 基于 critic 评分与在线阈值自适应调整保留动作集；阈值根据"集合是否包含代理目标动作"的二值反馈更新。理论部分证明自适应轨迹上代理漏检率的确定性界，并将价值损失精确分解为过滤损失与选择损失；在代理与 critic 近似条件下给出有限会话奖励界，无需学习参数收敛。实验在 KuaiRand-Pure 与 MovieLens 1M 上比较两种 RLCP 实现与四种 RL 基线。

Result: 在全部 19 种配置中，至少有一种 RLCP 变体取得最高目类多样性，为最强基线的 1.11× 至 5.21×，同时会话深度具有竞争力且保留集合规模不更大。

Conclusion: RLCP 通过校准裁剪实现自适应候选集大小，在理论上有漏检率与奖励损失保证，在实验中显著提升多样性而不牺牲会话深度与集合规模。

Abstract: Sequential recommenders typically use a fixed slate size even though the number of useful alternatives changes within a session. We propose Reinforcement Learning with Calibrated Pruning (RLCP), which adapts the retained action set using critic scores and an online threshold. The threshold is updated from binary feedback indicating whether the set contains an action in a proxy target. We prove a deterministic bound on the observed proxy miss rate along adaptive trajectories. To quantify the effect of pruning on reward, we derive an exact decomposition of value loss into filtering and selection losses. Under explicit proxy and critic approximation conditions, this decomposition yields a finite session reward bound that also accounts for imperfect selection and set truncation, without requiring the learning parameters to converge. Experiments on KuaiRand-Pure and MovieLens 1M compare two RLCP implementations with four RL baselines. In each of the 19 configurations, at least one RLCP variant achieves the highest catalog diversity, reaching $1.11\times$ to $5.21\times$ that of the strongest baseline, with competitive session depth and no larger retained sets.
