</details>


### [382] [Linear Bandits under Exact Sliding-Window Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08745)
*Seyed Mohammad Hadi Hosseini,Yasin Abbasi-Yadkori,Sattar Vakili*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究精确滑动窗口约束下的线性bandit问题，提出离线最优解结构、在线学习算法及后悔界，并验证其可行性保持与低策略更新优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实决策中常要求每连续窗口内的动作属于某可行集合，且几何结构本身不足以保证可学习性，需要新工具刻画可行可达性与历史通信。

Method: 离线分析凸性与循环平移不变性；在线引入转移直径τ与稀有切换OFUL算法；进一步用有限记忆控制建模动作历史，提出历史状态直径D，并结合乐观剩余 horizon 规划与稀有策略更新。

Result: 离线在w|T时平稳解最优，否则有O(w)加性间隙；在线子线性后悔可能不可能；后悔界分别为Õ(d√T+τd+w)与Õ(d√T+dD+w)；真实与合成基准中保持精确可行性，奖励和后悔与基线相当且策略更新显著更少。

Conclusion: 滑动窗口线性bandit需借助转移直径与历史状态直径刻画可学习性，所提算法在精确可行性、后悔和低更新频率间取得良好平衡。

Abstract: We study linear bandits under exact sliding-window constraints, where every consecutive block of actions must belong to a prescribed feasible set. In the offline setting, where the reward function is known, we show that convexity and cyclic-shift invariance make a stationary solution optimal when $w\mid T$ and within an additive $O(w)$ gap otherwise. In the online setting, we show that geometric structure alone is insufficient for learning, and sublinear regret can be impossible. We introduce a transition diameter $τ$ that quantifies feasible reachability and develop a rare-switching OFUL algorithm with regret $\widetilde{O}(d\sqrt{T}+τd+w)$ against the offline-optimal feasible trajectory. Finally, we remove cyclic invariance and consider general sliding-window constraints, where optimal behavior may be non-stationary. We represent recent action history as the state of a finite-memory control problem and introduce a history-state diameter $D$ that measures feasible communication between viable histories. Combining optimistic remaining-horizon planning with rare policy updates, we obtain a regret bound of $\widetilde{O}(d\sqrt{T}+dD+w)$. We evaluate our approach on real-world and synthetic benchmarks, showing that it maintains exact feasibility while achieving reward and regret comparable to baselines with substantially fewer policy updates.

</details>


### [383] [Neural Petri flows for chemical reactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08750)
*Jose Eduardo Escrig Molina,Daniel Probst*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究何种架构对权重任意取值都保持Petri网性质，提出Neural Petri Flow（NPF），将反应速率定律参数化为可学习部分，其余Petri网语义硬编码为无参数层，在原子映射、反应分类和正向预测等任务上取得领先结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Petri网的化学反应学习模型或神经网络仅将网作为消息传递的脚手架，其语义（守恒性、使能规则等）不被权重保证。作者希望找到一种架构，使其对任意权重取值都严格保持Petri网语义。

Method: 从理论出发，证明守恒性强制给定firing形式m'=m+Cσ，非负性在局部速率律下强制使能规则，从而使速率律（转移激发倾向）成为唯一自由部分。据此提出Neural Petri Flow（NPF），仅学习速率律或分类读出，其余为无参数层。在价键网（valence net）上，原子映射、反应分类、正向预测成为同一firing向量上的三个任务。

Result: 无需训练，最小firing向量在Golden集上映射88.8%（RXNMapper 85.6%），在EnzymeMap酶反应上88.7%（77.9%）。在USPTO-480K上，NPF预测87.7%产物，仅用1%训练子集时达67.4%。ECREACT的EC号第三层预测90.2%，领先最佳已发表方法5.6个点。以电子为token，同一token game首次预测FlowER基元步骤的90.5%，且所有top-1预测均为有效分子，无需过滤。

Conclusion: 通过将Petri网语义硬编码并仅学习速率律，NPF在保持严格Petri网语义的同时，在原子映射、反应分类与正向预测上超越现有方法，并可直接推广到电子转移的基元步骤预测。

Abstract: Petri nets have been used to describe chemical processes such as reactions.They map well to chemistry: Places are the bonds between atoms and the free valence of each atom, a token is a unit of bond order, a transition forms or breaks a bond, the conserved quantities are the valence budgets of the atoms, and the enabling rule is the valence rule. These semantics are not guaranteed by learned models of reactions or neural networks that are built on Petri nets that use the net as a scaffold for message passing. Here, we ask what architecture remains a Petri net for every value of its weights. We find the answer in the theory, where all semantics of a net share the firing form $m^\prime=m+Cσ$, locality, as enabling reads only the inputs of a transition, and the enabling rule, and we prove that conservation forces the firing form and that non-negativity forces the enabling rule on local rate laws. This leaves free the rate law, which is the propensity of each transition to fire. We introduce Neural Petri Flow, which learns this rate law, or a readout for classification, and hard-wires the rest as parameter-free layers. On what we denote a valence net, atom mapping, reaction classification, and forward prediction become three tasks on one firing vector. Without training, the minimum firing vector maps 88.8% of the curated Golden set against 85.6% for RXNMapper, and 88.7 against 77.9% of the enzymatic reactions of EnzymeMap. On USPTO-480K, NPF trained on these firing vectors predicts 87.7% of the products and 67.4% when trained on a 1% subset of the training reactions. EC numbers of ECREACT are predicted at the third level for 90.2% of reactions, 5.6 points ahead of the best published method. With electrons as tokens, the same token game predicts 90.5% of the elementary steps of FlowER first, ahead of the published baseline, and every top-1 prediction is a valid molecule without a filter.

</details>


### [384] [Conformal Prediction Sets Quantify Information Gain: A Theoretical Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08785)
*Kevin Zhang,Stephen Bates*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为"共形预测集大小作为不确定性度量"提供信息论基础，引入基于集合大小与覆盖率的广义信息度量族，证明Shannon互信息可由其精确积分表示，并给出集合大小缩减的夹逼界与数据处理不等式，最后通过11个分类实验验证理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形预测能给出带有限样本覆盖保证的预测集，其集合大小常被当作不确定性的启发式度量，但这种解释缺乏信息论依据。本文旨在正式建立共形预测与经典信息论量之间的联系。

Method: 采用面向集合值预测的决策论熵推广，构造一类基于共形预测集大小与覆盖率的广义信息度量；证明Shannon互信息可由该度量族精确积分表示；在标准分类情形下，推导额外信息带来的共形集大小缩减的夹逼界（介于依赖校准的度量族成员之间），并证明其满足数据处理不等式（均到有限样本校准与模型误差项为止）。

Result: 理论上给出互信息的积分表示、集合大小缩减的夹逼关系与数据处理不等式；实证上在11个分类设定中验证理论，并在贪心特征选择实验中发现集合大小缩减与Shannon互信息对特征的排序可能不同。

Conclusion: 本文将共形预测与经典信息论量形式化关联，为以预测集大小缩减作为信息增益度量提供了理论基础，同时提示该启发式指标与互信息并不总是等价。

Abstract: Conformal prediction is a popular tool for uncertainty quantification that outputs prediction sets with finite-sample coverage guarantees. While prediction set size is commonly used as a heuristic measure of uncertainty, the information-theoretic basis for this interpretation remains poorly understood. In this work, we provide such a foundation using a decision-theoretic generalization of entropy tailored to set-valued prediction. In particular, we introduce a family of generalized information measures based on the size and coverage of conformal prediction sets. Notably, Shannon mutual information admits an exact integral representation in terms of these measures. We then show that, in standard classification settings, the reduction in conformal set size from additional information (i) is sandwiched between calibration-dependent members of this family and (ii) obeys a data processing inequality, both up to finite-sample calibration and model error terms. Together, our results formally relate conformal prediction to classical information-theoretic quantities and justify using set-size reduction as an information gain metric. Empirically, we validate our theory across 11 classification settings and show that set-size reduction and Shannon mutual information can rank features differently in a greedy feature selection experiment.

</details>
